### [1] [TAPDreamer: Transferable Adversarial Patches for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06814)
*Xuanyu Lu,Fengqing Jiang,Kaiyuan Zheng,Yichen Feng,Yaorui Ding,Yuetai Li,Zhen Xiang,Bhaskar Ramasubramanian,Basel Alomair,Luyao Niu,Radha Poovendran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TAPDreamer 是一种针对世界动作模型（world action models）的迁移性局部补丁攻击：仅利用公开视觉编码器构造固定扰动，无需查询目标策略，即可跨任务与动作架构迁移，并将多个基准成功率降至接近 0。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型依赖相机输入，其共享视觉表示一旦被扰动，会同时影响多任务与动作策略。现有攻击通常需访问目标模型输出（动作或预测未来），因此提出无需目标策略查询、可迁移且持久的攻击需求。

Method: 关键洞察是：补丁引起的注意力权重与 value vector 交互会在补丁区域之外广播近乎相同的表示偏移，且该偏移跨任务观测稳定。TAPDreamer 仅用源任务六帧，最大化干净与加补丁编码器表示之间的全局 L1 距离，从而构造固定局部扰动，无需目标策略查询。

Result: 闭环评估中，每个基准一个冻结补丁覆盖约 6.5% 输入，使 FastWAM 在 40 个 LIBERO 任务上成功率从 97.7% 降至 0.0%，在 50 个 RoboTwin 任务上从 90.8% 降至 0.0%；匹配随机补丁仍保持 81.5% 与 79.2%。同一补丁使两个 DreamWAM 配置成功率降至 2.1% 与 0.8%，使 Motus 降至 10.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅保护下游动作生成不足以防御此类攻击；世界动作模型的防御还必须保护共享视觉编码器，使其免受持久局部扰动的影响。

Abstract: World models learn to predict how their environment will evolve, making them an important foundation for general-purpose robotic control. Yet world action models depend on camera inputs whose manipulation can corrupt the visual representations used across tasks and action policies. Existing attacks on these models optimize against the victim's actions or predicted futures and therefore require access to target-model outputs. In this paper, we propose an attack, TAPDreamer, against world action models that instead uses a public encoder alone to construct a fixed local perturbation that transfers across tasks and action architectures. TAPDreamer requires no target-policy queries. Our key insight is that interactions between patch-induced changes in attention weights and value vectors broadcast a nearly identical representation shift far beyond the patch footprint, and this shift remains stable across task observations. Guided by this insight, TAPDreamer uses six frames from one source task to maximize the global L1 distance between clean and patched encoder representations. In closed-loop evaluation, one frozen patch per benchmark, covering about 6.5% of the input, reduces FastWAM's success rate from 97.7% to 0.0% across 40 LIBERO tasks and from 90.8% to 0.0% across 50 RoboTwin tasks; matched random patches retain 81.5% and 79.2% success. The same patches reduce success to 2.1% and 0.8% on two DreamWAM configurations and to 10.0% on Motus. These results show that protecting downstream action generation alone is insufficient: defenses for world action models must also secure shared visual encoders against persistent local perturbations.

</details>


### [2] [RealtimeWAM: One-Step Asynchronous World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06617)
*Chengtao Lv,Jinyang Du,Shuyi Feng,Yang Yong,Shiqiao Gu,Shunzi Yang,Ruihao Gong,Shen Ren,Tianwei Zhang,Wenya Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 RealtimeWAM，一种高效的世界动作模型（WAM）变体，通过一步动作生成与异步推理，缓解动作去噪多步迭代和视频-动作专家串行等待两大瓶颈，在多个基准上保持近无损性能并显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高效 WAM 常采用 Mixture-of-Transformers（MoT）架构，一次性计算视频表征供动作专家复用，但动作专家内部的多步去噪迭代，以及视频专家与动作专家之间的串行执行等待，仍严重限制推理效率。

Method: 方法包含两部分：1）Teacher-Anchored Consistency Distillation（TACD），针对局部一致性误差低不等于最终动作准确的问题，在局部一致性监督外引入冻结教师多步 rollout 终点的显式监督，实现准确的一步动作生成；2）Cross-Expert Wavefront Pipelining（CEWP），通过分块共享视频 KV cache 重叠两个专家的执行，仅在动作注意力即将消费对应缓存前同步，从而消除专家级等待。

Result: 在 LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus、RoboTwin 等多样基准，以及 Fast-WAM、Faster-WAM 等模型变体上的实验表明，RealtimeWAM 性能优越，保持近无损精度（下降 <1%），并实现显著端到端加速（如在 H100 上约 25 倍）。

Conclusion: RealtimeWAM 通过一步动作生成与跨专家异步流水线，有效解决 WAM 推理效率瓶颈，在精度基本无损的前提下大幅提升推理速度，验证了其作为高效 WAM 方案的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) incorporate visual representations from video generation backbones to guide action prediction. Recent efficient WAMs adopt Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) architectures and compute video representations once for reuse by the action expert. However, intra-expert iteration (\ie, multi-step action denoising) and inter-expert waiting (\ie, sequential execution of the video and action experts) still limit inference efficiency. To this end, we present RealtimeWAM, an extremely efficient WAM variant with one-step action generation and asynchronous inference, addressing these two bottlenecks. To reduce intra-expert iteration, we propose Teacher-Anchored Consistency Distillation (TACD) to address a local-global error gap: low local consistency error alone does not guarantee accurate final actions. TACD supplements local consistency with explicit supervision from the frozen teacher's multi-step rollout endpoint, enabling accurate one-step action generation. Additionally, we propose Cross-Expert Wavefront Pipelining (CEWP) to eliminate unnecessary expert-level waiting. It overlaps the two experts through block-wise sharing of the video KV cache, synchronizing only immediately before the corresponding action attention consumes it. Extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks (\eg, LIBERO, LIBERO-Plus and RoboTwin) and model variants (\eg, Fast-WAM and Faster-WAM) demonstrate the superiority of RealtimeWAM. Notably, RealtimeWAM maintains near-lossless performance (\ie, $<1\%$ drop) across these benchmarks while delivering significant end-to-end speedup (\eg, $\sim25\times$ on H100). Our code and checkpoints are available via this \href{ this https URL }{link}.

</details>


### [3] [KineWorld: Action-Induced Transport Fields for Embodied World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06349)
*Ziying Song,Yuchen Liu,Zhuoran Xu,Ziyang Liu,Jian Jin,Jiangtao Su,Haibao Yu,Lei Yang,Yuanpei Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 KineWorld，一种将机器人运动学从动作条件扩展到生成监督空间分配的运输感知世界模型框架，以改善具身动作后果预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动作条件世界模型常采用均匀加权的视觉生成目标，与具身预测需求不一致；即使引入显式运动条件，也容易低估对交互至关重要的空间稀疏变化。

Method: KineWorld 包含 Kinematic Transport Lifting（KTL）与 Transport-Aware World Diffusion（TAWD）。KTL 根据指令机器人运动构建渲染器导出的、相机对齐的运输场；TAWD 在视频潜变量网格上校准其运动支持，并通过均匀分布与运输聚焦分布的归一化混合来重加权未来 RGB 的 flow matching。模型使用 RoboTwin 2.0 的 ALOHA-AgileX 双臂操作数据训练。

Result: 单视角评估中 EWMScore-P 为 68.95，多视角评估中 TWB-Score 为 54.82。

Conclusion: 结果表明，面向具身决策的世界模型应从外观拟合转向动作后果建模。

Abstract: Embodied world models predict the visual consequences of candidate actions before execution. However, existing action-conditioned world models often adopt uniformly weighted visual generation objectives that can be misaligned with embodied prediction needs. Even with explicit motion conditioning, these objectives can underemphasize spatially sparse changes that are critical to interaction. We propose KineWorld, a transport-aware world-modeling framework that extends robot kinematics from motion conditioning to the spatial allocation of generative supervision. Kinematic Transport Lifting (KTL) constructs renderer-derived, camera-aligned transport fields from commanded robot motion. Transport-Aware World Diffusion (TAWD) calibrates their motion support on the video-latent grid and reweights future-RGB flow matching through a normalized mixture of uniform and transport-focused distributions. We train KineWorld using ALOHA-AgileX bimanual manipulation data from RoboTwin 2.0. KineWorld achieves an EWMScore-P of 68.95 in single-view evaluation and a TWB-Score of 54.82 in multi-view evaluation. These results support a shift from appearance fitting toward action-consequence modeling for embodied decision-making.

</details>


### [4] [CentriQ: Calibration-Free Quantization of Diffusion Transformers via Exact Mean Centering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06260)
*Nataša Jovanović,Mathieu Salzmann,Saqib Javed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CentriQ 是一种无需校准的 DiT 量化方法，通过 token 中心化与秩-1 均值恢复解决 Hadamard 旋转失效问题，在 4-bit 下匹配 SVDQuant，并首次在 2-bit 激活下保持可用图像质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DiT 生成质量高但采样成本高，4-bit 权重量化可降本；现有校准法依赖特定 checkpoint 和 prompt 分布，而数据无关 Hadamard 旋转在 DiT 上质量下降。

Method: 作者指出结构原因：AdaLN 条件会给激活加入 per-token 均值，而在所评估 DiT 宽度下，数据无关方法使用的 Hadamard 旋转无法将该均值均匀分散到各坐标，导致单一主导方向存活并决定量化范围。CentriQ 在旋转前对每个 token 去中心化，并通过 rank-1 全精度分支精确恢复均值，使 per-token scale 可在无数据下闭式求解；权重采用鲁棒 ℓ_p 目标拟合，跟踪每组密集模式并抑制重尾。

Result: 在三个 DiT 上，CentriQ 4-bit 匹配校准 SVDQuant；而无校准权重量化器配普通 per-token 激活量化会崩溃或显著退化。它在 2-bit 权重上优于目前最强无校准方法，并是首个在 2-bit 激活下仍保持可用图像质量的无校准方法。

Conclusion: CentriQ 通过针对 DiT 激活结构的无校准量化，在极低比特下兼顾部署成本与生成质量，为 DiT 低比特推理提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Diffusion transformers (DiTs) achieve state-of-the-art image generation, but their sampling cost limits deployment. Quantizing both weights and activations to 4 bits reduces this cost, yet existing methods fall short in one of two ways. Calibration-based methods are tied to a specific checkpoint and prompt distribution, whereas data-free Hadamard rotation, effective for LLMs, loses quality on DiTs. We show that this loss has a structural cause. Adaptive layer-norm conditioning adds a per-token mean to the activations, and at the widths of the evaluated DiTs, the Hadamard rotations used by data-free methods cannot spread this mean uniformly across coordinates. A single dominant direction therefore survives the rotation and sets the quantization range. We introduce CentriQ, a calibration-free quantizer that centers each token before rotation and restores the mean exactly through a rank-1 full-precision branch, so that per-token scales follow in closed form without data. Weights are fitted under a robust $\ell_p$ objective that tracks the dense mode of each group and discounts heavy tails. Across three DiTs, CentriQ matches the quality of calibrated SVDQuant at 4 bits, whereas calibration-free weight quantizers with plain per-token activation quantization collapse or degrade substantially. CentriQ outperforms the strongest calibration-free method reported to date at 2-bit weights. It is also the first calibration-free method to retain usable image quality at 2-bit activations.

</details>


### [5] [Controllable Road Marking Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05771)
*Zhiyu,Yufan Zhang,Ruichen Tan,Zengxiang Lei,Satish Ukkusuri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出可控道路标线生成框架：从可行驶区域掩码、可选外环标线和文本描述合成中心区域标线布局，并在Argoverse 2和Waymo上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 道路标线对车辆导航与多智能体协同至关重要，但大规模制作仍依赖人工，限制了量化分析与场景测试，因此需要可控、自动化的标线生成方法。

Method: 将任务定义为可控道路标线生成，给定可行驶区域掩码、可选外环标线和文本描述，生成缺失的中心区域标线，输出车道分隔线、道路分隔线、人行横道三个通道。构建基于确定性元数据提示的基准，并提出条件BEV管线：文本条件潜在整流流DiT，配合拓扑感知辅助损失；使用高斯模糊训练目标稳定薄而稀疏标线的学习；提出免训练后处理SGR，通过提取折线、拟合三次贝塞尔曲线并以各向异性超高斯基元重渲染，恢复清晰分隔线几何。

Result: 在Argoverse 2的4,597个测试tile上，Buffered F1为80.8、clDice为50.2，而适配的SOTA掩码细化基线分别为38.8和24.6；在Waymo上达到88.0 Buffered F1和66.2 clDice。组件消融显示拓扑感知监督与SGR在连通性上具有互补增益。文本编辑实验表明，更强引导提高编辑成功率，但也增加对非目标结构的改变。

Conclusion: 该框架有望推动仿真就绪的道路标线变化、自动化地图补全以及早期基础设施设计探索。

Abstract: Lane and road markings provide critical guidance for vehicle navigation and multi-agent coordination, yet authoring them at scale remains a manual workflow that limits quantitative analysis and scenario testing. We introduce Controllable Road Marking Generation, which synthesizes a missing center-region marking layout from a drivable-area mask, optional outer-ring markings, and a textual description. Our benchmark uses deterministic, metadata-derived prompts and three output channels: lane dividers, road dividers, and pedestrian crossings. We develop a conditional bird's-eye-view (BEV) pipeline that combines (i) a text-conditioned latent rectified-flow DiT trained with a topology-aware auxiliary loss, (ii) Gaussian-blurred training targets that stabilize learning of thin, sparse markings, and (iii) Structured Gaussian Render (SGR), a training-free post-process that recovers crisp divider geometry by extracting polylines, fitting cubic Bézier curves, and re-rendering them as anisotropic super-Gaussian primitives. On 4,597 Argoverse~2 test tiles, our system achieves Buffered F1 of 80.8 and clDice of 50.2, compared with 38.8 and 24.6 for an adapted state-of-the-art mask-refinement baseline. On Waymo dataset, it yields 88.0 Buffered F1 and 66.2 clDice. Component ablations show complementary connectivity gains from topology-aware supervision and SGR. Text-editing experiments reveal that stronger guidance improves edit success but also increases changes to non-target structures. We see this framework as a step toward simulation-ready road-marking variation, automated map completion, and early-stage infrastructure design exploration.

</details>


### [6] [StageVLN: Spatial and Trajectory Auxiliary Guidance for Efficient Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05664)
*Anh Dao,Quan-Dung Pham,Le Danh Vinh,Anh Nguyen,Nguyen Viet Tri Pham,Yiyu Chen,Pham Tuyen Le,Van-Truong Nguyen,Quan Nguyen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StageVLN 是一种仅在训练阶段引入特权空间与轨迹引导的 VLN 训练框架，可在不改变推理路径、不增加几何编码器的情况下提升导航性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLN 策略虽受益于大型视觉语言模型提供的语义先验，但标准动作监督并未显式促使中间表征保留场景几何、相对朝向或全局回合进度。若在推理时引入深度估计器、显式地图、点云或几何基础模型，虽能提供结构信息，却会带来额外计算、内存开销和架构依赖。

Method: 提出 StageVLN 训练框架：训练时利用冻结的几何基础模型为分层导航器状态提供多级空间引导，并通过相对朝向目标与专家路线进度目标提供轨迹状态监督；所有辅助组件仅用于训练，部署时移除，从而保持原始推理路径不变。

Result: 在 R2R-CE validation-unseen 上，使用 4B 参数骨干达到 56.3% SR 和 51.4% SPL，且推理时无需额外几何编码器；在 RxR-CE 上达到 54.3% SR，且无需额外导航训练数据或推理几何编码器。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过训练期的特权空间与轨迹引导即可将几何结构和全局进度信息注入 VLN 表征，无需在部署时增加几何模块或额外数据，从而实现高效且有效的视觉语言导航。

Abstract: Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) policies increasingly benefit from strong semantic priors provided by large vision-language models (VLMs). However, standard action supervision does not explicitly encourage intermediate representations to preserve scene geometry, relative orientation, or global episode progress. Incorporating depth estimators, explicit maps, point clouds, or geometry foundation models at inference can provide such structure but introduces additional computation, memory overhead, and architectural dependence during deployment. We introduce StageVLN, a training framework that shapes navigation representations through privileged spatial and trajectory guidance while preserving the original inference pathway. A frozen geometry foundation model provides multi-level spatial guidance to hierarchical navigator states, while relative-heading and expert-route progress objectives provide complementary trajectory-state supervision. All auxiliary components are used only during training and removed at deployment. On R2R-CE validation-unseen, StageVLN achieves 56.3\% SR and 51.4\% SPL with a 4B-parameter backbone, without an additional geometry encoder at inference. On RxR-CE, it achieves 54.3\% SR without additional navigation training data or a geometry encoder at inference.

</details>


### [7] [Deep Prior Learning for Embodied Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05531)
*Yimou Wu,Jiaxin Guo,Yun-hui Liu,Zheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VPGGT，一个基于VGGT并扩展OmniVGGT的先验感知具身感知框架，通过先验残差连接（PRC）和Metric Global Attention解决噪声位姿、先验稀释及物理尺度恢复问题，并在四个数据集上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身系统需要利用图像之外的几何观测进行几何感知。现有前馈3D模型虽引入相机位姿、内参和深度等几何先验，但面对噪声位姿、保持先验准确性以及恢复物理尺度时，不能仅简单接受这些输入。

Method: VPGGT基于VGGT并扩展OmniVGGT。其从真实轨迹构造传感器启发的相机位姿损坏用于训练，且仅对相机位姿加噪，内参和深度不额外损坏；引入无参数的先验残差连接（PRC）以缓解先验稀释，即预测精度低于所给位姿先验的问题；提出Metric Global Attention，用全局尺度token对可用位姿和深度尺度进行条件化，为几何输出预测共享的度量缩放因子。

Result: 在四个数据集上的实验表明，当所有视图均提供相机先验时，在精确位姿和损坏位姿下，PRC相比匹配的训练基线提升了平移方向精度和联合位姿AUC。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持在细化阶段显式访问先验，认为这是对特征级条件化的一种有用补充。

Abstract: Embodied systems need geometric perception that exploits available observations beyond images alone. Recent feed-forward 3D models incorporate geometric priors, including camera poses, intrinsics, and depth. However, handling noisy poses, preserving accurate priors, and recovering physical scale require more than simply accepting these inputs. We introduce \emph{Vision-Prior Geometry Grounded Transformer} (VPGGT), a VGGT-based framework that extends OmniVGGT for prior-aware embodied perception. We formulate sensor-motivated pose corruptions from ground-truth trajectories for training and introduce a parameter-free \emph{prior residual connection} (PRC) to mitigate \emph{prior dilution}, where predictions are less accurate than their supplied pose priors. Our noise formulation targets camera poses; supplied intrinsics and depth receive no additional corruption. We further introduce \emph{Metric Global Attention}, which conditions a global scale token on available pose and depth scales and predicts a shared metric scaling factor for the geometric outputs. Experiments across four datasets show that \emph{PRC} improves translation-direction accuracy and joint pose AUC over a matched training baseline when camera priors are provided for all views, under both exact and corrupted poses. These results support explicit prior access during refinement as a useful addition to feature-level conditioning.

</details>


### [8] [When and What to Prune? Stage-Aware Visual Token Pruning for Efficient VLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05273)
*Tianjun Shi,Haotian Xiong,Ziyu Gong,Qi Lu,Li Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAPrune是一种无需训练的视觉token剪枝框架，用于加速VLA推理。它通过校准集观察动作到视觉注意力随层的变化，在注意力模式可靠后再选择剪枝层，并采用双路径剪枝规则保护高注意力token和有用上下文。在LIBERO、SIMPLER及真实机器人任务上，剪除87.5%视觉token，最高获得1.718倍推理加速，同时保持有竞争力的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法多依赖注意力分数或特征多样性，并采用固定剪枝计划，如预设层剪枝或均匀间隔层剪枝。这类固定计划对VLA模型风险较大，因为模型在浅层可能尚不知道哪些视觉区域对动作重要；早期看似不重要的token，在结合语言指令与视觉观测后可能变得有用。

Method: 提出SAPrune，一种面向高效VLA推理的免训练视觉token剪枝框架。它不固定剪枝层，而是利用小型校准集观察动作到视觉注意力在各层间的变化，待注意力模式更可靠后再选择剪枝层。在每个选定的剪枝层，采用双路径剪枝规则：一路保护被强关注的视觉token不被剪除，另一路防止有用的周围上下文被丢弃。

Result: 在LIBERO、SIMPLER和真实世界机器人任务上的实验表明，SAPrune可剪除87.5%的视觉token，实现最高1.718倍推理加速，并保持有竞争力的任务成功率。

Conclusion: SAPrune通过基于动作到视觉注意力可靠性的动态层选择与双路径保护式剪枝，避免了固定剪枝计划在VLA中的过早剪枝风险，在显著减少视觉token和加速推理的同时维持任务性能。

Abstract: Visual token pruning is an effective way to accelerate vision-language models and is especially useful for vision-language-action (VLA) inference, where many visual tokens must be processed before predicting robot actions. Existing pruning methods usually estimate which tokens can be pruned based on attention scores or feature diversity, retaining tokens that are either highly attended or visually different from others. However, most of them use fixed pruning schedules, such as pruning once at a preset layer or pruning at uniformly spaced layers. Such schedules can be risky for VLA models, because the model may not know which visual regions matter for the action in early layers. Tokens that look unimportant at first may become useful after the model combines visual observations with the language instruction. In this work, we propose SAPrune, a training-free visual token pruning framework for efficient VLA inference. Instead of pruning at fixed layers, SAPrune uses a small calibration set to observe how action-to-visual attention changes across layers, and chooses pruning layers only after the attention pattern becomes more reliable. At each selected layer, SAPrune applies a dual-path pruning rule: one path protects strongly attended visual tokens from pruning, while the other prevents useful surrounding context from being discarded. Experiments on LIBERO, SIMPLER, and real-world robotic tasks show that SAPrune prunes 87.5% of visual tokens and achieves up to 1.718x inference speedup while maintaining competitive task success rates.

</details>


### [9] [GeoBridge-VLA: Geometry-Aware Residual Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05026)
*Hyun Song,Kangmin Kim,Loren Jinsoo Um,Minhui Han,Jaehyeok Park,Taewan Cho,Andrew Jaeyong Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoBridge-VLA 是一种两阶段方法：先从预训练 VLA 的冻结视觉编码器中学习几何特征，再通过门控残差接口增强视觉 token 以进行动作预测；部署仅需 RGB、机器人状态和语言，无需深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型虽从视觉-语言预训练中编码语义信息，但机器人操作还需要精确的空间推理能力。

Method: 第一阶段：在预训练 VLA 的冻结视觉编码器上，用深度监督训练特征桥和几何解码器。第二阶段：冻结这些模块，训练门控残差接口以及动作侧投影和动作专家；残差增强已有视觉 token，不增加第二个图像编码器，也不增加 token 数量。部署使用 RGB、机器人状态和语言，不使用深度观测。

Result: 匹配评估条件下，LIBERO 成功率 70.9%，SmolVLA 为 60.0%。在同一训练检查点中禁用残差，成功率从 70.90% 降至 69.85%，各子套件效果不一。在物理 ROBOTIS OMY 机器人上，四个任务共 200 次试验成功 148 次（74.0%），SmolVLA 为 108 次（54.0%）。

Conclusion: GeoBridge-VLA 通过从冻结 VLA 视觉编码器中提取几何特征并注入门控残差，提升了操作任务的空间推理与成功率，且部署无需深度输入；残差消融显示其有贡献但各套件效果存在差异，物理机器人实验进一步验证了有效性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models encode semantic information from vision-language pretraining, but manipulation also requires precise spatial reasoning. We present GeoBridge-VLA, a two-stage method for learning geometric features from a pretrained VLA's frozen visual encoder and using them for action prediction. Stage I trains a feature bridge and geometry decoder with depth supervision. Stage II freezes these modules and trains a gated residual interface together with the action-side projections and action expert. The residual augments the existing visual tokens without adding a second image encoder or increasing the token count. Deployment requires RGB, robot state, and language, but no depth observations. Under matched evaluation conditions, GeoBridge-VLA achieves 70.9% success on LIBERO, compared with 60.0% for SmolVLA. Disabling the residual in the same trained checkpoint reduces success from 70.90% to 69.85%, with mixed effects across suites. On a physical ROBOTIS OMY robot, GeoBridge-VLA succeeds in 148 of 200 trials (74.0%) across four tasks, compared with 108 of 200 (54.0%) for SmolVLA.

</details>


### [10] [LightVLN: Efficient Aerial Vision-and-Language Navigation with Compact Memory and History-Guided Local Aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05024)
*Yiming Zhao,Tianshun Li,Jingle He,Ruonan Chai,Xinhu Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LightVLN 是一种轻量、历史感知的空中视觉-语言导航框架，通过 0.5B 语言骨干与高度压缩的历史/当前视觉 token 表示，在降低计算与内存开销的同时取得优于 7B 基线的导航性能，并实现机载实时闭环部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中 VLN 要求无人机在复杂三维环境中根据长程自然语言指令和视觉观测进行导航。现有方法通常依赖大规模视觉-语言骨干和密集视觉历史，计算与内存成本高，难以在机载平台上部署。

Method: 提出 LightVLN：采用紧凑的 0.5B 语言骨干；利用策略已计算的视觉特征将每个历史帧压缩为 1 个 token；引入由历史和指令条件驱动的局部聚合，将当前观测从 256 个视觉 token 压缩至 32 个，同时保留导航相关空间信息；最多使用 16 个历史帧，观测相关 token 总数不超过 48。

Result: 在 OpenFly 数据集上达到 50.93% Test-Seen 和 36.14% Test-Unseen 成功率（SR），在多数指标上优于所评测的 7B 语言骨干基线；在 AerialVLN-S Val-Seen 上达到 25.83% SR。在重建的未见校园环境中，部署于 DJI M350 RTK 与外接 Jetson Orin NX 16 GB，进行闭环机载计算 real-to-sim 硬件在环（HIL）评估，实现 14.61 Hz 模型推理和 11.13 Hz 端到端决策更新。

Conclusion: 结果表明 LightVLN 在空中导航任务中兼具有效性与高效性，具备支持机载部署的潜力。

Abstract: Aerial vision-and-language navigation (VLN) enables unmanned aerial vehicles to execute long-horizon natural-language instructions from visual observations in complex three-dimensional environments. However, recent aerial VLN models often rely on large-scale vision-language backbones and dense visual histories, imposing substantial computation and memory costs that hinder onboard deployment. We propose LightVLN, a lightweight history-aware aerial VLN framework that combines a compact 0.5B language backbone with compact representations of both historical and current observations. LightVLN compresses each historical frame into a single token using visual features already computed by the policy. It further introduces history- and instruction-conditioned local aggregation to reduce the current observation from 256 to 32 visual tokens while preserving navigation-relevant spatial information. With up to 16 historical frames, the policy uses at most 48 observation-derived tokens. On the public OpenFly dataset, LightVLN achieves 50.93% Test-Seen and 36.14% Test-Unseen success rates (SR), outperforming the evaluated 7B language-backbone baselines on most reported metrics. It also achieves 25.83% SR on AerialVLN-S Val-Seen. In a reconstructed unseen campus, we deploy LightVLN on a DJI M350 RTK with an external Jetson Orin NX 16 GB for closed-loop onboard-compute real-to-sim hardware-in-the-loop (HIL) evaluation, achieving 14.61 Hz model inference and 11.13 Hz end-to-end decision updates. These results demonstrate the effectiveness and efficiency of LightVLN for aerial navigation.

</details>


### [11] [Probabilistic Pedestrian Forecasts from a Handheld Phone: World-Frame Heat Maps, Visual-Inertial Height Drift, and Evaluation without Ground Truth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04736)
*Danial Safaei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出并评估仅用单目相机与VIO的手持行人轨迹预测系统：将检测行人投影至地面、在重力对齐度量坐标系跟踪，并用小U-Net输出逐步概率图，在公开数据与ADVIO手持视频上验证，但手持自洽评估的增益有限且敏感于评估方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让手机用户预见附近行人未来数秒位置，需地面稳定、标定良好且仅依赖单目相机与视觉惯性里程计，无需外部设施或俯视视角。

Method: 检测行人；通过射线-平面交点将其提升到地面；在重力对齐的度量帧中跟踪；用小型U-Net以NLL损失在鸟瞰SDD和第一人称EgoTraj-Bench轨迹上训练，输出逐步概率图。针对VIO垂直漂移与楼层变化导致的单目地面位置缩放，采用低通滤波高度并保持相机离地高度恒定，但在扶梯上滞后。

Result: SDD与EgoTraj-Bench测试分片上，4.8秒NLL较拟合常速高斯降低1.51和1.37 nats。ADVIO手持录像缺乏行人真值，以跟踪器后续原始测量评分；7个留出片段的预注册评估中，1.2/2.4/4.8秒NLL分别降低0.17/0.23/0.47 nats（95%区间排除零），但降幅不足开发片段一半。探索性分析：按片段重采样使1.2和2.4秒区间含零；两预测器在开发片段重校准后网络仅在1.2秒显著更优；两片段用ADVIO参考位姿比手机自身位姿更利于网络。

Conclusion: 系统证明手持单目+VIO的地面稳定行人预测可行，但手持场景的自洽评分证据有限且对重采样、重校准与位姿来源敏感；此类自洽指标不能等同于真值精度，需谨慎解释其能证明与不能证明的内容。

Abstract: A pedestrian with a phone could be shown where nearby people will be in the next few seconds, if the forecast stays on the ground while the phone moves, is calibrated, and needs only a monocular camera and visual-inertial odometry (VIO). We build and evaluate such a system. People are detected, lifted onto the floor by ray-plane intersection, tracked in a gravity-aligned metric frame, and forecast as per-step probability maps by a small U-Net trained with a negative log-likelihood (NLL) loss on bird's-eye (SDD) and first-person (EgoTraj-Bench) trajectories. On handheld ADVIO recordings, vertical VIO drift and the user's own changes of level silently rescale monocular ground positions (by 87% within 90 s on one clip; on another, all tracks are lost for the last 31% of the clip); keeping the camera's height above the floor constant under a low-pass-filtered altitude avoids this, though it lags on escalators. On the SDD and EgoTraj-Bench test splits, the final forecaster lowers the NLL at 4.8 s by 1.51 and 1.37 nats relative to a fitted constant-velocity Gaussian. Lacking ground truth for people in handheld video, we score forecasts against the tracker's own later raw measurements. In an internally pre-registered evaluation on seven held-out clips, the final forecaster's NLL is lower than the benchmark-fitted baseline's at 1.2, 2.4 and 4.8 s (by 0.17, 0.23 and 0.47 nats; 95% intervals over people exclude zero), but by less than half as much as on the development clips. Exploratory analyses cut both ways: resampling clips instead of people widens the intervals to include zero at 1.2 and 2.4 s, and once both forecasters are recalibrated on the development clips the network is significantly better only at 1.2 s; but two clips run with ADVIO's reference poses favour the network much more when re-run with the phone's own poses. We discuss what such self-consistency scores can and cannot show.

</details>


### [12] [Video2World: Benchmarking Coding Agents for Interactive World Modeling from Embodied Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04432)
*Jinzhou Tang,Zijun Zhang,Jing Yang,Yuchen Yan,Kun Zhou,Lingjun Mao,Ruobing Han,Jinglin Cao,Wenpeng Xu,Lukun He,Minghao Fu,Fan Feng,Biwei Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Video2World 基准，将“自主视频到仿真”（autonomous video-to-simulation）形式化为软件工程任务，用 222 个重建实例评测前沿编码智能体能否从具身视频端到端自动构建可交互仿真环境，并揭示视觉保真度与功能正确性之间的不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从真实世界观测构建交互式仿真器是规模化扩展具身数据的可行途径，但现有流程仍严重依赖人工环境搭建与标定，成本高、难以扩展。本文探究前沿基础模型与编码智能体能否将这一流程端到端自动化。

Method: 将自主视频到仿真定义为软件工程任务：智能体观测具身视频，构建对应的仿真环境与机器人行为，并借助执行反馈迭代精化结果。作者提出 Video2World 基准，包含源自 189 个机器人及人类演示视频的 222 个重建实例，从几何保真度、动态保真度和功能正确性三个维度进行评估，分别对应空间感知、物理推理与可执行交互能力。

Result: 对 9 个前沿编码智能体系统的评测显示，自 Claude Opus 5 起任务成功率出现明显跃升，由不足 5% 提升至 15% 以上，但与人类辅助重建之间仍存在显著差距。此外发现“看起来更好的世界未必更好用”：更高的视觉保真度并不总能带来更高的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，感知真实感与事实正确性之间存在鸿沟，这与生成模型中观测到的更广泛现象相呼应；自主视频到仿真虽已取得进展，但距离人类辅助的重建水平仍有较大空间。

Abstract: Building interactive simulators from real-world observations is a promising way to scale embodied data, but current pipelines still rely heavily on manual environment construction and calibration. We study whether frontier foundation models and coding agents can automate this process end to end. We formulate \emph{autonomous video-to-simulation} as a software engineering task in which an agent observes an embodied video, constructs the corresponding simulated environment and robot behavior, and iteratively refines the result through execution feedback. To evaluate this capability, we introduce \textbf{Video2World}, a benchmark comprising 222 reconstruction instances derived from 189 robot and human demonstration videos. Video2World measures reconstructed worlds along geometric fidelity, dynamic fidelity, and functional correctness, capturing spatial perception, physical reasoning, and executable interaction. Evaluating 9 frontier coding-agent systems reveals a sharp improvement in Task success beginning with Claude Opus 5, rising from below 5\% to over 15\%, while substantial gaps to human-assisted reconstruction remain. We further find that worlds that look better could work worse: better visual fidelity does not always lead to higher task success. This echoes the broader gap between perceptual realism and factual correctness observed in generative models.

</details>


### [13] [SelectOccFlow: Selective Spatiotemporal Aggregation for 3D Occupancy and Scene Flow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04356)
*Yuhang Wang,Kai Luo,Yuanfan Zheng,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SelectOccFlow，一种选择性时空聚合框架，通过语义引导采样、状态条件时序聚合和范围感知空间聚合，逐步优化图像、时序与体素域证据，用于自动驾驶中的3D占用与场景流预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于相机的占用与场景流预测易受不可靠时空聚合影响，主要源于图像特征语义不兼容、历史观测错位以及体素结构不完整，因此需要选择性聚合机制来提升几何、语义与运动建模的可靠性。

Method: SelectOccFlow在图像、时序和体素三个域逐步细化上下文证据。具体包括：语义引导采样（SGS），利用语义先验调控特征采样以获得语义兼容的图像证据；状态条件时序聚合（SCTA），根据体素状态选择性检索历史证据以缓解时序不一致；范围感知空间聚合（ESA），利用方向性结构支撑优化前景几何，增强体素表示的结构完整性。

Result: 在OpenOcc上取得44.9的SOTA OccScore，较此前最佳提升+4.2%。在Occ3D-nus上保持有竞争力的占用性能，并在nuScenes-C损坏场景下将平均OccScore提升+11.1%，表明对视觉损坏具有更强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SelectOccFlow通过选择性时空聚合有效提升了相机-based占用与场景流预测的精度和鲁棒性，验证了跨图像、时序与体素域渐进式证据优化的有效性，源代码将公开。

Abstract: Comprehensive 3D scene understanding for autonomous driving requires modeling geometry, semantics, and motion. However, camera-based occupancy and scene flow prediction are sensitive to unreliable spatial and temporal aggregation, caused by semantically incompatible image features, misaligned historical observations, and incomplete voxel structures. To address this issue, we propose SelectOccFlow, a selective spatiotemporal aggregation framework that progressively refines contextual evidence across image, temporal, and voxel domains. To obtain semantically compatible image evidence, we design Semantic-Guided Sampling (SGS) to regulate feature sampling with semantic priors. Since reliable image evidence alone cannot resolve temporal inconsistency, we then present State-Conditioned Temporal Aggregation (SCTA) to selectively retrieve historical evidence according to voxel states. To further enhance the structural completeness of voxel representations, we introduce Extent-Aware Spatial Aggregation (ESA), which exploits directional structural support to refine foreground geometry. Experiments on OpenOcc demonstrate that SelectOccFlow achieves a state-of-the-art OccScore of 44.9, improving the previous best by +4.2%. It also maintains competitive occupancy performance on Occ3D-nus and improves the mean OccScore under nuScenes-C corruptions by +11.1%, demonstrating improved robustness to visual corruptions. The source code will be made publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [14] [WAMJET: A Harness for World Action Model Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03797)
*Le Chen,Lixin Liu,Jan Schneider,Zeju Qiu,Simon Guist,Bernhard Schölkopf,Dieter Büchler*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WAMJET 是一种面向 World Action Models（WAMs）推理加速的智能体化框架，通过编码智能体与可复用优化指导、测量和验证工具，实现瓶颈驱动、迭代式加速栈构建，在多个 WAM、编码智能体和 GPU 架构上取得最高 9.95 倍无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: World Action Models 利用预训练视频基础模型进行机器人操作，但其大骨干网络和视频-动作联合预测导致推理成本高昂。现有加速技术虽多，却需针对每个模型和硬件平台进行大量工程化选择与组合，成为部署瓶颈。

Method: 提出 WAMJET，一种 agentic harness：为编码智能体配备可复用优化指导以及测量、验证工具，采用瓶颈驱动工作流。智能体先分析推理性能，定位并修改目标代码，验证优化效果，并随瓶颈迁移迭代精炼加速栈，同时保持动作质量。

Result: 实验覆盖 6 个 WAM、3 种编码智能体和 2 种 GPU 架构。相比上游实现，WAMJET 实现最高 9.95 倍无损加速；近似优化与硬件感知优化进一步降低延迟，且成功率相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明，WAMJET 能够为 WAM 部署生成有效的加速栈。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) leverage pretrained video foundation models for robot manipulation, but their large backbones and video-action co-prediction are expensive. Although existing acceleration techniques offer many ways to reduce this cost, selecting and composing them requires substantial engineering for each model and hardware platform. To tackle this bottleneck, we present WAMJET, an agentic harness that accelerates WAM inference by equipping coding agents with reusable optimization guidance and measurement and validation tools. WAMJET follows a bottleneck-driven workflow where the agent profiles inference, modifies targeted code, validates effects, and iteratively refines the acceleration stack as bottlenecks shift, while preserving action quality. Experiments span six WAMs, three coding agents, and two GPU architectures. WAMJET achieves up to 9.95x lossless speedup over upstream implementations. Approximation and hardware-aware optimization yield additional latency reductions, with comparable success rates. The results show that WAMJET can produce effective acceleration stacks for WAM deployment.

</details>

### [15] [CurveCodec 2: Skeleton-agnostic animation compression with a learned entropy model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04211)
*Mingyi Shi,Huancheng Lin,Xuelin Chen,Taku Komura*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 研究骨骼运动压缩中冗余所在及学习模型应承担的部分，提出CurveCodec 2：在ACL生产精度下，以0.37x字节满足最坏情况契约、0.22x满足平均误差契约，单CPU核心解码且可跨物种迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 骨骼运动被存储为每帧每个关节的变换，但大部分信息由身体结构隐含；压缩需回答在给定骨架与误差界下运动仍必须表达什么。先前CurveCodec以稀疏锚点学习先验匹配ACL平均误差，但未达最坏情况，且以浮点数而非比特计量载荷。因此需定位骨骼运动冗余并确定学习模型在编解码器中的角色。

Method: 测量给出三点结论：生产精度下最大节省来自各量化曲线利用自身历史的时序预测；其次是通过层级闭环按关节选择不编码的采样；编码器留下的空隙上，百万训练样本的最近邻oracle不优于线性插值，学习式插值不划算。网络真正学习的是需发送残差的分布。CurveCodec 2将每条子轨迹表示为log map中的曲线，闭环量化并按率失真选择关键帧抽稀，残差由小型学习模型熵编码，其整数推理跨平台比特精确。

Result: 对每个解码片段验证两种契约：ACL逐关节最坏情况误差在给定容差内，或ACL逐片段平均误差。在33个数据集、4,472个保留测试片段上，CurveCodec 2在ACL默认精度0.01 cm的最坏情况契约下仅需0.37x字节，在0.1 cm平均契约下需0.22x字节；可在单CPU核心解码，且无需重训练即可迁移到训练中未出现的物种。

Conclusion: 学习模型应负责残差分布的熵编码，而非运动插值；主要增益来自曲线时序预测与闭环关键采样选择。CurveCodec 2满足生产级误差契约并显著降低码率，具备单核解码与跨物种泛化能力。

Abstract: Skeletal motion is stored as every joint's transform at every frame, yet most of it is implied by the body rather than by what the motion is about. Compression is one way to ask what a motion must still say once the body is known, and a production codec must answer it for any skeleton with a stated error bound. Our earlier codec, CurveCodec, matched the mean error of ACL, the production library of modern game engines, with a learned prior over sparse anchors, but not ACL's worst case, and it counted its payload as floats rather than bits. Here we ask where the redundancy of skeletal motion lies and which part of a codec a learned model should take over. Measurements give three answers. At production precision the largest saving comes from predicting each quantized curve from its own past, the second from choosing per joint, in closed loop through the hierarchy, which samples not to code. On the gaps such an encoder leaves, a nearest-neighbour oracle over millions of training samples is no better than linear interpolation, and no learned in-betweener we tried paid for itself. What a network does learn is the distribution of the residuals the codec must send. CurveCodec 2 codes every sub-track as a curve in the log map, quantized in closed loop and thinned to rate-distortion-selected keys, with residuals entropy-coded under a small learned model whose integer inference is bit-exact across platforms. Two contracts are verified on every decoded clip: ACL's own worst case per joint within a stated tolerance, or ACL's mean error per clip. On a held-out test side of 4,472 clips from 33 datasets, CurveCodec 2 needs 0.37x ACL's bytes at ACL's default precision of 0.01 cm under the worst-case contract and 0.22x at 0.1 cm under the mean contract, decodes on one CPU core, and transfers without retraining to a species absent from training. Project page: this https URL

</details>

### [16] [Structural Foundations of Nonlinear Systems with Unknown Inputs: The UID-Induced Normal Form and Minimal-Sensing Structure-from-Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05939)
*Agostino Martinelli*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文首次给出未知输入驱动的非线性系统状态估计的一般结构解：基于非线性未知输入可观性理论，证明任意此类系统可化为 UID 诱导标准形，将未知输入信息分解为与可观测动态结构解耦的方向和完全表征影响可观测动态的未知输入的可观量，从而在不对未知输入作模型或随机假设下统一实现未知输入解耦与重构，并在三特征点+单轴陀螺仪的最小 Structure-from-Motion 配置中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决未知输入作用下非线性系统状态估计缺乏一般结构框架的问题，并避免依赖未知输入的模型或随机假设，同时探索最小感知配置下 Structure-from-Motion 的可行性。

Method: 基于非线性未知输入可观性理论，构造结构等价的 UID 诱导标准形；该表示将未知输入信息分解为：与可观测动态结构解耦的未知输入方向，以及完全表示影响可观测动态的未知输入的可观量。

Result: 该标准形为未知输入解耦与未知输入重构提供了统一结构解，无需未知输入的模型或随机假设；在仅三个点特征和单轴陀螺仪的最小 Structure-from-Motion 配置中，实现递归状态估计，并可恢复三维结构和相机运动至未知全局尺度因子。

Conclusion: 真实数据实验验证了该框架，并证明该最小感知配置的可行性；表明所提 UID 诱导标准形为未知输入非线性系统状态估计提供了通用且实际有效的结构方案。

Abstract: This paper establishes the first general structural solution to the problem of state estimation for nonlinear systems driven by unknown inputs. Building upon nonlinear unknown-input observability theory, we show that every such system admits a structurally equivalent representation, referred to as the UID-induced normal form. The proposed representation decomposes the information carried by the unknown inputs into two complementary components: unknown-input directions that are structurally decoupled from the observable dynamics and observable quantities that completely represent the unknown-input information affecting the observable dynamics. As a consequence, the UID-induced normal form provides a unified structural solution to unknown-input decoupling and unknown-input reconstruction, without requiring any model or stochastic assumption on the unknown inputs. The practical significance of the proposed framework is demonstrated through a previously unexplored minimal Structure-from-Motion configuration. The proposed representation enables recursive state estimation from only three point features and a single-axis gyroscope, allowing the recovery of the three-dimensional structure and camera motion up to an unknown global scale factor. Experiments on real-world data validate the proposed framework and demonstrate the feasibility of this minimal sensing configuration.

</details>

### [17] [A Safe Action Is Not Enough: Feasible-Future Decoding for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05166)
*Tu Nguyen,Matthieu Zimmer,Vu Anh Vu,Ziyi Wang,Jannik Hammel Nielsen,Xuebing Zhou,Haitham Bou Ammar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出冻结视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略下安全动作未必可行，提出可行性-似然差距，并基于安全完成任务受限轨迹律的精确下一块边缘推导出免训练、报警触发的重排序器 VICS-G，在 Safety-CHORES 上降低安全代价且基本保持成功率与回合长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在冻结 VLA 策略中，动作可能概率高且局部安全，却导致后续不存在策略支持的通往安全任务完成的路径；现有按似然排序无法区分这种不可行性，因此需要一种针对未来可行性的选择准则。

Method: 作者从历史条件策略-环境轨迹律中推导出受限安全完成任务下的精确下一块边缘分布，得到候选相关的可行未来质量：其支撑集反映冻结续行过程下安全完成是否仍可能，其大小度量保留的加权安全完成质量。由于在线精确评估不可行，进一步提出选择性有限候选近似，并给出恢复最佳保留可行候选的条件；最终实例化为报警触发、免训练的重排序器 VICS-G。

Result: 在 Safety-CHORES 上，VICS-G 在六个设置中将平均累计安全代价降低 1.9%-57.5%，同时成功率与策略采样相差不超过 2.5 个百分点，平均回合长度相差不超过 0.82 步。

Conclusion: 该解码器与精确的策略相对安全完成目标相联系，且无需重新训练基础策略，也无需在线轨迹展开，为冻结 VLA 策略的安全可行动作选择提供了实用方案。

Abstract: A safe action is not necessarily a viable one. Under a frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policy, an action can be likely and locally admissible yet leave no policy-supported route to safe task completion. We call this the feasibility-likelihood gap: likelihood ranks the current action, whereas feasibility depends on the futures that remain after it. We derive the exact next-block marginal of the history-conditioned policy-environment trajectory law restricted to safe task completion. The derivation exposes a candidate-dependent feasible-future mass with two roles: its support records whether safe completion remains possible under the frozen continuation process, and its magnitude measures how much weighted safe-completion mass is preserved. Exact evaluation is impractical online, so we develop a selective finite-candidate approximation, derive conditions for recovering the best retained viable candidate, and instantiate it as an alarm-triggered, training-free reranker. On Safety-CHORES, VICS-G lowers mean cumulative safety cost by 1.9%-57.5% across six settings while remaining within 2.5 percentage points of policy sampling in success and 0.82 steps in mean episode length. The resulting decoder is tied to an exact policy-relative safe-completion target, yet requires neither retraining of the base policy nor online trajectory rollouts.

</details>


### [18] [PermVLA: Factorization Order as a Regularizer for VLA Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04659)
*Yanqiao Chen,Yuhan Rui,Dongsheng Hou,Zijie Nie,Yutong Wan,Qi Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出因果锚定排列（CAP）：通过采样带可调时间前缀的动作揭示顺序，构造条件集增强辅助目标，使单个共享VLA策略从同一专家动作块的不同已知子集学习，部署仍用确定性LTR。CAP在LIBERO、LIBERO-Plus及CALVIN上优于标准LTR，并可推广到扩散动作生成器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略通常按固定从左到右（LTR）链式分解学习动作块，但同一专家轨迹分布存在多种合法的链式分解。作者指出，分解顺序是被忽视的正则化选择；固定LTR会使策略过度依赖普通教师强制中的单一时间前缀，限制泛化。

Method: CAP采样动作揭示顺序，并保留可调的时间顺序前缀。其辅助目标训练一个共享策略，在给定同一专家动作块的不同已知子集条件下预测动作，从而在不增加演示数据的情况下生成多个条件预测问题，称为条件集增强。部署时仍保留确定性LTR控制。该方法被进一步扩展到扩散动作生成器。

Result: 受控实验表明，CAP在LIBERO和LIBERO-Plus上一致优于标准LTR训练，在跨数据集CALVIN评估中也出现相同优势。诊断指标度量同一动作块在两种揭示顺序下联合对数似然的期望平方差，验证CAP训练内化了不同揭示顺序之间的一致性。

Conclusion: 采样得到的子集条件辅助目标可作为构建VLA正则器的通用配方；CAP以分解顺序为切入点验证了该思路，并展示其可扩展至扩散动作生成器。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies commonly learn action chunks through a fixed left-to-right (LTR) factorization, although the same expert trajectory distribution admits many valid chain-rule factorizations. We identify factorization order as an overlooked regularization choice and introduce causally anchored permutation (CAP), which samples action reveal orders with a tunable chronological prefix. Its auxiliary objective trains one shared policy to predict actions from different known subsets of the same expert chunk, while deployment retains deterministic LTR control. We call this conditional-set augmentation: it creates multiple conditional prediction problems from one expert chunk without adding demonstrations. This discourages reliance on the single chronological prefix used by ordinary teacher forcing. Controlled experiments show that CAP consistently outperforms standard LTR training on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus, with the same advantage appearing in cross-dataset CALVIN evaluation. A diagnostic that measures the expected squared difference between a chunk's joint log likelihood under two reveal orders verifies that CAP training internalizes agreement across reveal orders. These findings position sampled subset-conditioned auxiliary objectives as a general recipe for constructing VLA regularizers, illustrated by an extension to diffusion action generators.

</details>


### [19] [RAGrasp: Geometry-Semantic Template Retrieval and Grasp Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04438)
*Shenzhe Zhu,Chengxiao He,Jan Harder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAGrasp 是一种检索增强的平面平行夹爪抓取流水线，仅用少量本地采集的带抓取标注 RGB-D 模板即可部署，无需端到端重训；真实试验中已见物体 20/20 成功，未见物体 19/20 成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务特定抓取预测器通常依赖大型公开或合成数据集训练，且面对新任务需端到端重训；其数据与部署时的相机、机器人、夹爪及工作空间条件不一致，因此需要一种面向具体部署、仅需有限本地标注的抓取适应方法。

Method: RAGrasp 使用部署相机、机器人和夹爪在目标工作空间采集的观测构建模板记忆；利用自监督 DINOv2 视觉特征结合外观与深度线索提示 SAM2 分割查询物体；通过两阶段几何-语义检索级联选择模板；由置信门选择两种抓取迁移估计器之一；再经掩码支撑与轮廓接触约束细化迁移抓取，并进行校准的 2D 到 3D 转换。

Result: 真实世界试验中，RAGrasp 在已见物体上达到 20/20 抓取成功，在未见物体上达到 19/20 抓取成功；在测试的视角和光照变化下表现出容忍度。

Conclusion: 在评估设定内，结果表明 RAGrasp 可基于有限本地标注实现部署特定的抓取适应，并对所测试的视角与光照变化具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: We present RAGrasp, a retrieval-augmented pipeline for planar parallel-jaw grasping from a compact set of locally collected, grasp-annotated RGB-D (color and depth) templates. Unlike task-specific predictors trained primarily on large public or synthetic grasp datasets, RAGrasp requires no end-to-end retraining for a new this http URL template memory is constructed from observations collected with the deployment camera, robot, and gripper in the target workspace, thereby aligning stored examples with the local sensing and embodiment conditions. The system uses self-supervised DINOv2 visual fea- tures together with appearance and depth cues to prompt the Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2), which isolates the query object. A two-stage geometry-semantic retrieval cascade then selects a template, and a confidence gate chooses one of two grasp- transfer estimators. The transferred grasp is refined using mask- support and silhouette-contact constraints before calibrated 2D- to-3D conversion. In real-world trials, RAGrasp achieves 20/20 successful grasps on seen objects and 19/20 on unseen objects. Within the evaluated setting, the results demonstrate deployment- specific grasp adaptation from limited local annotation and tolerance to the tested viewpoint and illumination changes.

</details>


### [20] [Towards Safer Autonomous Driving in an Open World: A Dual-Process Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04088)
*Simon Janssen,Michiel Braat,Chris van der Ploeg,Serge Thill,Jan-Pieter Paardekooper*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种受双过程理论启发的混合自动驾驶规划框架，结合神经网络直觉式规划与模型预测控制推理式规划，并通过知识图谱和风险场驱动的元认知组件进行切换；在CARLA的应急车辆闯红灯等分布外场景中，相比仅用神经网络规划器减少89%碰撞并提升特殊路权规则遵从性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统部署前必须符合安全标准、交通规则和社会规范；尽管基于大量驾驶数据训练的神经网络在常规任务中表现良好，但在数据中未充分表征的新颖或分布外场景中往往表现不佳。

Method: 提出融合神经网络与模型预测控制的框架：神经网络负责常规驾驶中的直觉式、学习型规划，模型预测控制负责不熟悉场景中的推理式规划；设计元认知组件，利用知识图谱基于显式感知信息及相关交通规则和社会规范推理上下文风险；上下文风险以风险场表示，用于指导元认知切换机制以及推理式规划器对安全标准、交通规则和社会规范的遵从。

Result: 在CARLA中针对典型分布外场景及其变体进行测试，包括应急车辆闯红灯等情形；与仅使用神经网络的规划器相比，该架构将场景中的碰撞数量减少89%，并提升了对特殊路权规则的遵从性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合双过程理论、知识图谱推理与风险场的混合规划架构，可有效提升自动驾驶在分布外场景中的安全性、交通规则和社会规范合规性，优于单一神经网络规划器。

Abstract: Before autonomous driving systems can be deployed on public roads, it is vital that these systems comply with safety standards, traffic rules, and social norms. Although neural networks trained on large amounts of driving data perform well in routine driving tasks, these models often struggle in novel situations that are not well-represented in the data. In this work, we propose a novel framework that combines a neural network for intuitive, learning-based planning in routine driving tasks with model predictive control for reasoning-based planning in unfamiliar situations, inspired by Dual Process Theory. A meta-cognitive component is designed to switch between the two, using a knowledge graph to reason about contextual risk based on explicit perceptual information and relevant traffic rules and social norms. Contextual risk is represented through risk fields, guiding both the switching mechanism in the meta-cognitive component and compliance with safety standards, traffic rules, and social norms in the reasoning-based planner. The effectiveness of our framework is tested in CARLA for variations of a typical out-of-distribution situations involving (emergency) vehicles running a red light. We show that the novel architecture reduces the number of collisions in the scenarios by 89% and improves compliance with the special right-of-way rules, compared to the NN-only planner.

</details>

### [21] [Differentiable Ternary Temporal Logic Semantics with Polynomial Surrogate Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04662)
*Ryan Matheu,Sai Sandeep Damera,John S. Baras,Calin Belta*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种三元时序逻辑的概率松弛方法，可端到端计算值与梯度，同时在实际数据上保持精确判定，并用于离线与在线机器人控制合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有控制合成大多局限于布尔时序逻辑，无法量化弃权或不确定性；三元时序逻辑虽可用 Unknown 表示不确定性，但仍处于早期，主要限于线性系统合成与监控。

Method: 将三元时序逻辑概率松弛为可微网络，使值与梯度可在同一网络内计算，并在实际数据上仍给出精确判定；该松弛网络可作为梯度控制合成目标，支持离线与在线机器人控制。

Result: 通过两个机械臂任务（离线与在线）验证，任务涉及子任务的中间检查与排序，表明方法在正确性与及时执行方面具有优势。

Conclusion: 该框架扩展了三元时序逻辑的可微控制合成能力，适用于需要处理不确定性与时序子任务的机器人应用。

Abstract: Temporal logic provides designers a formal reasoning tool for specifying complex spatio-temporal behaviors and tasks for autonomous systems. Many techniques exist for control synthesis according to temporal logic specifications, spanning a large variety of systems, including robotics. However, the vast majority of implementations are limited to Boolean temporal logics. Boolean temporal logic does not have an innate mechanism for quantifying abstention, rather verdicts are either definitively \textit{True} or \textit{False}. Recent work demonstrates that ternary logic is a capable formalism for reasoning about robotic behaviors, with the natural ability to classify uncertainty with the \textit{Unknown} literal. Ternary temporal logic is still in its infancy, with work limited to synthesis for linear systems and monitoring. This work proposes a probabilistic relaxation of ternary temporal logic that allows for value and gradient computation through the same network while retaining exact verdicts on actual data. The relaxed network can be used as an objective in gradient-based control synthesis for both offline and online robotic control applications. We demonstrate the utility of our framework with two robotic manipulator tasks, one offline and one online, involving intermediate checking and sequencing of subtasks to demonstrate the benefit of our approach in terms of correct and timely execution.

</details>


### [22] [Latent Safety Filters: When a Lossy Encoder Admits a Transferable Certificate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04297)
*Johannes Mootz,Zahra Nili Ahmadabadi,Reza Akhavian*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文研究有损编码器下的潜在安全滤波器，证明安全证书能否迁移到物理系统取决于被丢弃安全相关动态的可检测性，并给出显式迁移裕度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜在安全滤波器在低维学习表示上认证安全，但有损编码器可能丢失安全信息，导致潜在信号报告安全而物理状态不安全且无模型误差可检测；现有迁移条件未显式刻画被丢弃安全信息的影响。

Method: 构造潜在模型精确、潜在信号恒安全的系统，使物理状态任意不安全且编码器下游监视器无法检测；分析被丢弃动态收缩时的潜在障碍证书；在线性情形下结合有界性与非退化条件，研究可检测性与迁移裕度；在学习的 cartpole 编码器上验证理论。

Result: 存在反例系统：无证书且下游监视器无法检测失效；当被丢弃动态收缩时，潜在障碍证书以两个显式裕度保证真实安全，分别对应潜在模型误差和编码器不可区分状态间安全变化；线性且满足有界/非退化条件时，安全相关子空间可检测则所有校准障碍以有限裕度迁移，否则均不迁移；cartpole 上模型误差不能指示估计界是否非空，而第二裕度可以。

Conclusion: 有损编码器安全证书的迁移由被丢弃安全相关动态的可检测性决定；需显式考虑不可区分状态间安全变化裕度，不能仅依赖模型误差；第二裕度可用于判断表示下界是否非空。

Abstract: Latent safety filters certify safety on a learned low-dimensional representation of the state, enabling constraints that resist analytic description. Because the encoder is lossy, a filter can report safe while the physical state is unsafe, with no detectable model error. Existing transfer conditions leave the effect of discarded safety information implicit. We ask when a lossy encoder admits a safety certificate that transfers to the physical system, and show the answer is governed by the detectability of the discarded safety-relevant dynamics. We construct a system whose latent model is exact and whose latent signals always report safe, while the physical state becomes arbitrarily unsafe. For this system no certificate exists and no monitor downstream of the encoder can detect the failure. When the discarded dynamics contract, a latent barrier certifies true safety up to two explicit margins, one for the latent-model error and one for the variation of safety across states the encoder cannot distinguish. In the linear case and under boundedness and non-degeneracy conditions, every calibrated barrier transfers with a finite margin when the safety-relevant subspace is detectable, and none does otherwise. On learned cartpole encoders, the model error does not indicate for which representations the estimated bound is non-vacuous, while the second margin does.

</details>


### [23] [Barrier-Shaped Recurrent Reinforcement Learning for Autonomous Landing on a Heaving Ship Deck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03924)
*Ritwik Shankar,Chiranjeev Prachand,Abhishek,Soumya Ranjan Sahoo*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出用非对称actor-critic强化学习训练递归策略，实现无人旋翼机在起伏船甲板上的自主降落：训练时critic可见未来6秒甲板高度，actor仅用机载传感器；引入控制屏障函数停止裕度作为奖励和运行时安全滤波，并在触地时直接基于接近度切断推力。在平台模拟器和四旋翼上验证，31次动捕与20次视觉试验均成功降落。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无人机在运动/起伏船甲板上自主降落的难题，尤其是甲板垂荡运动、机载感知受限、触地安全以及飞控disarm逻辑无法检测飞行器停在移动甲板上的问题。

Method: 将降落建模为强化学习问题；采用非对称actor-critic：critic训练时使用未来6秒甲板高度，actor仅接收17维相对甲板状态，包括位置、速度和姿态，输出世界坐标系速度与偏航角速率指令。先在4096个并行仿真环境中训练，再在16个环境中结合机载视觉闭环微调。将控制屏障函数停止裕度用于两处：训练奖励项，以及在部署时每个控制步评估的运行时安全滤波器，若违反则中止并重试下降。由于autopilot的disarm逻辑无法检测飞行器停在移动甲板上，在降落流程中直接命令基于接近度的推力切断。验证采用并联机械臂平台模拟甲板垂荡运动，缩放为0.70 m峰峰值、7.5 s平均周期，并使用带机载相机的四旋翼。

Result: 31次动捕试验和20次视觉试验中无人机均成功降落；中位触地时间分别为6.5 s和7.3 s；首次尝试成功率为77%和50%；推力切断时平均甲板相对速度分别为0.29 m/s和0.27 m/s。训练中加入CBF奖励项后，模拟中位接触速度相比未加入时减半。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在真实起伏甲板上实现可靠的自主降落，非对称actor-critic训练与CBF安全机制有效，且在视觉闭环条件下仍具鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper addresses autonomous landing of unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) rotorcrafts on a heaving ship deck using a recurrent policy trained via asymmetric actor-critic reinforcement learning: the critic sees 6 s of future deck height during training, while the actor sees only what the onboard sensors provide in flight, a 17-dimensional state made of the vehicle's position, velocity and attitude relative to the deck and outputs world-frame velocity and yaw-rate commands. Training is performed across 4096 parallel simulated environments, followed by fine-tuning in 16 environments with an onboard vision pipeline in the loop. A control-barrier-function (CBF) stopping margin on the deck-relative vertical state is incorporated at two stages: as a reward term during training, where it halves the median simulated contact speed relative to a policy trained without it, and as a runtime safety filter at deployment, evaluated at every control step to abort and retry the descent when the margin is violated. Because the autopilot's disarm logic cannot detect the vehicle resting on a moving deck, proximity-based thrust cutoff at touchdown is commanded directly in the landing pipeline. The proposed approach is validated using a parallel-manipulator-platform-based deck emulator that reproduces the heaving motion of the ship deck (scaled to 0.70 m peak-to-peak, 7.5 s mean period) and a quadcopter UAV with an onboard camera. Across 31 motion-capture and 20 vision-based trials, the UAV landed every time, with median times to contact of 6.5 and 7.3 s; 77% and 50% landed on the first attempt, with mean deck-relative speeds of 0.29 and 0.27 m/s, respectively, at the instant of thrust cutoff, which is the last speed under the policy's control. Supplementary video: this https URL

</details>

### [24] [TACET: Context-Appropriate Acoustic-Social Navigation for Quadrupeds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03828)
*Sungsan Park,Young-Sik Shin,Sanghyun Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TACET：一种面向四足机器人的情境适配声学-社会导航方法，利用视觉语言推理与行为令牌联合决定去哪里以及以多大声移动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人进入医院、养老院和安静办公室时，其运动噪声（主要来自足地碰撞）本身是社会变量。现有社会导航尊重人际空间但将机器人视为声学上无差别，而静音运动方法仅将噪声降至操作者指定、与情境无关的水平。

Method: TACET从机器人第一视角推断社会情境，将慢速微调视觉语言推理器与快速反应控制器通过单一紧凑行为令牌<gait, speed, social_cost>耦合；该令牌同时条件化社会代价地图（去哪）和静音运动策略（多大声），并用结构化视野外记忆在人物离开相机视野后保留其上下文。

Result: 在真实四足机器人上，情境条件化运动在相同速度下最多降低9.3 dBA噪声；在评估场景中，完整方法保持100%个人空间合规，声学侵入低（≤2.9 dBA），联合改善空间与声学性能。

Conclusion: TACET表明社会导航可将声学影响纳入情境感知决策，实现空间合规与低噪声的联合优化，适用于医院、护理院和安静办公室等场景。

Abstract: Quadruped robots entering hospitals, care homes, and quiet offices must be context-appropriate not only in where they move but in how loudly they move: a legged robot's locomotion noise, dominated by foot-ground impacts, is itself a social variable. Prior social navigation respects human space but treats the robot as acoustically uniform, while quiet-locomotion methods reduce noise to an operator-specified, context-blind level. We present TACET, a context-appropriate acoustic-social navigation method that infers social context from the robot's egocentric view and decides both where it walks and how loudly, coupling a slow fine-tuned vision-language reasoner to a fast reactive controller through a single compact behavior token, <gait, speed, social_cost>. The same token conditions both a social costmap (where to go) and a quiet locomotion policy (how loudly to move), while a structured out-of-view memory keeps recently seen people in the reasoner's context after they leave the camera view. On a real quadruped, context-conditioned locomotion lowers locomotion noise by up to 9.3 dBA at matched speed, and across our scenarios the full method keeps personal-space compliance at 100% with low acoustic intrusion (<=2.9 dBA), jointly improving spatial and acoustic performance in the evaluated scenarios. The project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [25] [RMMBench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Robotic Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05414)
*Huapeng Li,Fuxiang Feng,Jinqiu Fan,Shuo Yang,Fengjiao Chen,Xuezhi Cao,Ran Song,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出RMMBench，一个面向视觉语言模型在机器人导航与操作中具身能力评估的综合基准，涵盖70个从局部操作到长程复合导航的典型任务场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器人领域虽受益于视觉语言模型带来的环境理解与任务推理能力，但缺乏综合评估方法来评测多样化机器人任务，且评估指标较为受限，难以全面、细粒度地衡量VLMs的具身能力。

Method: 提出RMMBench评估基准，要求机器人理解语言指令并在连续空间中执行长程任务；将高层与低层具身任务统一整合到同一框架中，构建“导航-操作”任务套件，包含70个典型任务场景，覆盖从局部操作到长程复合导航的任务范围。

Result: 实验结果表明，领先的VLMs在移动操作任务中仍面临显著的空间定位挑战，同时凸显了在长程交互过程中增强机器人空间感知能力的必要性。

Conclusion: RMMBench为视觉语言模型在机器人导航与操作中的具身能力提供了更综合、细粒度的评估方式，有助于推动VLMs在机器人任务中的集成与应用，并可通过论文给出的链接访问。

Abstract: Although the advancement of vision-language models (VLMs) has endowed robots with enhanced environmental understanding and task reasoning, a comprehensive evaluation methodology is important to advance the integration of VLMs in robotic navigation and manipulation. However, current benchmarks lack a comprehensive method to evaluate diverse robotic tasks, and evaluation metrics remain relatively constrained, making it difficult to assess the embodied capabilities of VLMs in a thorough and fine-grained manner. To address this issue, we propose RMMBench, an evaluation benchmark that requires robots to understand language instructions and perform long-horizon tasks in continuous spaces. RMMBench seamlessly integrates high- and low-level embodied tasks into a unified framework, constructing a "navigation-manipulation" task suite comprising 70 canonical task scenarios that range from localized manipulation to long-horizon composite navigation. The results reveal that leading VLMs still face major challenges in spatial localization when performing mobile manipulation tasks, and also highlight the necessity of enhancing the spatial perception capability of robots during long-horizon interactions. RMMBench can be accessed at this https URL

</details>


### [26] [TUCO: Curating Simulation Demonstrations for Sim-to-Real Robot Policy Co-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05407)
*Ning Zhu,Mengfei Zhao,Yikai Tang,Zhangyujie Sun,Peihao Li,Dongyue Ni,Jindou Jia,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究面向sim-to-real机器人策略联合训练的数据策展，提出TUCO，通过影响函数统一衡量轨迹级效用与集合级覆盖，并优化选择互补仿真演示，在多个设置下达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真演示可补充稀缺真实数据以用于机器人策略联合训练，但如何主动策展这些演示仍研究不足；现有方法也缺乏从闭环目标行为出发统一衡量轨迹级效用和集合级覆盖的准则。

Method: 提出TUCO（Trajectory-level Utility and set-level Coverage Optimization）。利用影响函数追踪每个源演示对目标域评分rollout的影响，并将其分解为目标回报总体贡献与跨rollout变化，作为闭环基础衡量轨迹效用和集合覆盖；进一步提出性能对齐子集优化器，在统一策展目标中结合两类度量，减少冗余并选取互补演示。

Result: 在RoboMimic和OmniReset上的大量实验证明了主动仿真数据策展对sim-to-real策略联合训练的价值，且TUCO在单模拟器、sim-to-sim和sim-to-real设置中均取得SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 数据策展能有效提升sim-to-real策略联合训练，TUCO为主动选择仿真演示提供了统一的闭环效用与覆盖优化框架，并验证了其优越性。

Abstract: Simulation demonstrations can supplement scarce real-world data for robot policy co-training. However, the value of using data curation to actively select these demonstrations for sim-to-real co-training remains underexplored. Existing curation methods also lack a unified criterion for measuring trajectory-level utility and set-level coverage from closed-loop target behavior. To address these gaps, we present the first systematic study of data curation for sim-to-real robot policy co-training and propose Trajectory-level Utility and set-level Coverage Optimization (TUCO). TUCO uses influence functions to trace how each source demonstration affects target-domain scoring rollouts. Our key insight is that these effects can be decomposed into an overall contribution to target return and variation across rollouts, providing a common closed-loop basis for measuring trajectory utility and set coverage. We further propose a performance-aligned subset optimizer that combines these measures in a unified curation objective to reduce redundancy and select complementary demonstrations. Extensive experiments on RoboMimic and OmniReset establish the value of active simulation data curation for sim-to-real policy co-training and show that TUCO achieves state-of-the-art performance across single-simulator, sim-to-sim, and sim-to-real settings.

</details>


### [27] [Optimal Control with Learned Critics under Unmodeled State Dependencies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05359)
*Philipp Schoch,Markus Ryll*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于学习的 MPC 框架，将局部基于模型的规划与学习到的残差动力学和价值评论器结合，并用 GPU 加速批量 iLQR 实现可扩展的基于模型强化学习，以应对接触等难以建模的动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPC 具有结构化和约束感知的决策能力，但依赖便于优化的解析动力学模型，难以处理接触等难以建模的状态依赖；无模型强化学习虽避免显式建模，却通常需要大量交互数据。因此需要兼顾数据效率、模型结构和对不完整动力学的补偿能力。

Method: 在标称解析模型上增加残差动力学网络，从数据中学习缺失的状态依赖效应；将所得规划器与学习到的动作价值评论器结合，把长时域 MDP 结构注入局部 iLQR 优化；开发 GPU 加速的批量 iLQR 求解器，在最优控制循环内评估学习到的动力学和评论器网络，并行求解数千个轨迹优化问题；系统集成到机器人模拟器中。

Result: 在有偏差和建模不完整的控制任务上，该方法提升了闭环控制性能，同时保留了基于模型的结构，从而支持高效的约束轨迹优化。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，结合残差动力学学习、价值评论器和 GPU 加速批量 iLQR，可在不完整动力学下实现可扩展的基于模型强化学习，并保持 MPC 的结构化与约束优化优势。

Abstract: Model Predictive Control (MPC) provides a structured and constraint-aware mechanism for decision-making, but its reliance on optimization-friendly analytical dynamics models limits its use in tasks with contacts and other hard-to-model state dependencies. Model-free reinforcement learning avoids explicit modeling assumptions but typically requires large amounts of interaction data. We present a learning-based MPC framework that combines the data efficiency and structure of local model-based planning with learned components that compensate for incomplete dynamics and finite-horizon myopia. The method augments a nominal analytical model with a residual dynamics network that learns missing state-dependent effects from data and combines the resulting planner with a learned action-value critic that injects long-horizon MDP structure into the local iLQR optimization. To make this practical at reinforcement-learning scale, we develop a GPU-accelerated batched iLQR solver that evaluates learned dynamics and critic networks inside the optimal-control loop and solves thousands of trajectory-optimization problems in parallel. The complete system is integrated into a robotics simulator, enabling scalable model-based reinforcement learning under incomplete dynamics. Experiments on biased and incompletely modeled control tasks show that the approach improves closed-loop control performance while preserving the model-based structure needed for efficient constrained trajectory optimization.

</details>


### [28] [VAMPS: Visual and Motor Policies from Sampling-Based Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05331)
*Mohamed Yassine Kabouri,Pietro Noah Crestaz,Quang-Nam Nguyen,Qilong Cheng,Ludovic Righetti,Nicolas Mansard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VAMPS 利用 MPPI 控制生成监督数据，无需人类演示，既能通过仿真迭代训练可迁移的本体感觉策略，也能直接利用真实机器人自主采集数据训练视觉运动策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接在物理系统上学习机器人策略仍然困难，主要原因是数据采集成本高，且策略探索可能不安全。该工作希望避免人类演示，并降低真实系统上试错学习的代价与风险。

Method: VAMPS 使用 Model Predictive Path Integral（MPPI）控制训练可复用策略，支持两种模式。其一，一步本体感觉策略在仿真中迭代训练：策略为 MPPI 提供热启动，MPPI 优化后的轨迹随策略变化提供新监督；学习到的终端价值函数改善短视界规划，Implicit Q-Learning（IQL）critic 指导策略更新。其二，视觉运动策略直接从真实机器人数据学习：MPPI 使用任务相关状态估计规划并执行轨迹，同时在 Flexiv Rizon 10S 上记录 RGB 与传感器观测；Action Chunking with Transformers 预测动作块，从而缩短有效预测视界，并在固定数据集上离线训练。

Result: 迭代细化优于仅在冻结的 MPPI 数据上训练一次；学习到的运动策略成功迁移到 Unitree Go2。视觉运动任务展示了 pick-and-place 与力感知白板擦拭；后者额外观测测得的 6-D wrench 和期望法向力。

Conclusion: 结果表明，VAMPS 既可以在仿真中学习策略后迁移到硬件，也可以直接从机器人自主采集的真实数据中学习策略。

Abstract: Learning robot policies directly on physical systems remains difficult because data collection is costly and policy exploration can be unsafe. We introduce Visual and Motor Policies from Sampling-Based Planning (VAMPS), a framework that uses Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) control to train reusable policies without human demonstrations. VAMPS supports two training modes. For one-step proprioceptive policies, it operates iteratively in simulation: the policy warm-starts MPPI, and the refined trajectories provide new supervision as the policy changes. A learned terminal value improves short-horizon planning, while an Implicit Q-Learning (IQL) critic guides the policy update. Iterative refinement outperforms training once on frozen MPPI data, and we transfer the learned locomotion policy to a Unitree Go2. For visuomotor policies, VAMPS operates directly from real-robot data. MPPI uses task-specific state estimates to plan and execute trajectories while recording RGB and sensor observations on a Flexiv Rizon 10S. Action Chunking with Transformers predicts action chunks, reducing the effective prediction horizon, and is trained offline on this fixed dataset. We demonstrate visuomotor pick-and-place and force-aware whiteboard erasing. In the latter task, the policy additionally observes the measured $6$-D wrench and desired normal force. These results show that VAMPS can learn policies either in simulation followed by hardware transfer or directly from autonomously collected real-robot data.

</details>


### [29] [CoDance: Learning Reactive and Compliant Human-Humanoid Interaction from Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05324)
*Zhuoqun Chen,Shucheng Jia,Boyuan Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoDance：从双人舞蹈视频学习反应式、柔顺的人-人形伙伴交互，实现人形机器人与人类伙伴持续双手接触的协同舞步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人-人形伙伴交互需将行走与持续物理接触耦合；人形机器人须在应对交互力时与伙伴运动协调，并保持稳定自然的运动。伙伴舞蹈是其中极具挑战的实例：机器人需与移动伙伴协调步态并维持双手持续接触。

Method: 给定一段两人舞蹈视频，CoDance将舞蹈者运动重定向为机器人参考运动与移动伙伴；提出多链接柔顺增强，通过在双手施加结构化力并调整机器人参考，把运动学演示转化为力感知训练数据；由此训练策略跟随伙伴、保留演示步态风格并柔顺响应物理交互。

Result: 仿真中，策略能根据伙伴变化调整步伐，并复现增强演示中约80%的手腕位移；在实体人形机器人上，CoDance实现与人类伙伴持续双手共舞，包括前进与后退动作的反复切换。

Conclusion: 结果表明，从单段视频学习可让人形机器人实现反应式且柔顺的伙伴交互，为需要持续物理接触的人-人形协同运动提供可行框架。

Abstract: Partnered human-humanoid interaction couples locomotion with continuous physical contact. A humanoid needs to coordinate with a person's motion while responding to interaction forces and maintaining stable and natural movement. We present CoDance, a framework for learning reactive and compliant human-humanoid interaction from video. We study partnered dancing as a challenging instantiation, where a humanoid coordinates its footsteps with a moving partner and maintains continuous two-hand contact. Given a single video of two human dancers, CoDance retargets their motions into a robot reference and a moving partner. We introduce a multi-link compliance augmentation that transforms the kinematic demonstration into force-aware training data by adapting the robot reference under structured forces at both hands. Policies trained on this data follow the observed partner while preserving the demonstrated locomotion style and responding compliantly to physical interaction. In simulation, the policies adapt their footsteps to changes in the partner and reproduce approximately 80% of the wrist displacement encoded by the augmented demonstrations. On a physical humanoid, CoDance enables sustained two-hand dancing with a human partner including repeated transitions between forward and backward motions.

</details>


### [30] [Safe Ergodic Control for Multi-Robot Systems via Quadratic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05258)
*Yo Toyomoto,Mohamed Elobaid,Bryce L. Ferguson,Alberto Quattrini Li,Shinkyu Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于二次规划（QP）的安全遍历控制方法，将遍历性与安全性联合优化，并通过高斯核可微遍历度量、时变控制屏障函数（CBF）及松弛变量实现实时安全空间覆盖，仿真与三架无人机实验验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全遍历控制器多依赖离线轨迹优化或分层架构，难以满足实时应用，且将遍历性目标与安全约束解耦；因此需要一种能同时保证遍历覆盖与安全性的在线控制框架。

Method: 引入高斯核遍历度量替代传统指示函数度量，使其对时间可微；将遍历度量的指数衰减建模为带松弛变量的时变CBF约束，并与区域保持、机器人间避碰的硬CBF约束共同构成QP控制器求解控制输入。

Result: 理论证明该QP从任意安全初始构型出发均可行，且除松弛项外遍历度量呈指数衰减；仿真性能优于基线方法，并在三架空中飞行器硬件实验中验证。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了遍历性与安全约束的联合、在线、实时控制，为密集时空环境监测等任务提供了可证明安全且可实际部署的遍历控制方案。

Abstract: Ergodic control drives robots to spend time in each region in proportion to a spatial distribution of interest, making it well suited for dense spatiotemporal environmental monitoring. Existing safe ergodic controllers rely on offline trajectory optimization or hierarchical architectures, which limit real-time applicability and decouple the ergodicity objective from the safety constraint. This paper presents a quadratic programming (QP)-based controller that treats ergodicity and safety jointly. We first introduce a Gaussian-kernel ergodic metric that, unlike the classical indicator-based metric, is time differentiable. This allows the exponential decay of the metric to be imposed as a time-varying control barrier function (CBF) constraint, relaxed by a slack variable, alongside hard CBF constraints for region containment and inter-robot collision avoidance. We establish that the resulting QP remains feasible from any safe initial configuration and that, up to the slack term, the ergodic metric decays exponentially. Simulations show improved performance over baseline methods, and experiments with three aerial vehicles validate the work on real hardware.

</details>


### [31] [Vela: Scaling Vision-Language-Action Models with Adaptive Action Curve Parametrization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05230)
*Yifan Li,Jiaxu Wang,Dongming Wu,Yicheng Jiang,Ryan Ji,Xiangyu Yue,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Vela视觉-语言-动作基础模型，将未来机器人行为表示为连续轨迹，结合样条动作表示、运动相关时间支撑与共享动作接口，在仿真和真实长时程任务上取得良好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多数VLA模型将未来运动表示为固定频率动作块，使时间分辨率与预测时域受固定输出预算约束；逐点表示在高度相关的相邻动作上浪费容量，时间连续性与平滑性需隐式学习，并在长时程覆盖与接触丰富操作所需的局部精度之间形成权衡。

Method: Vela将未来机器人行为建模为连续轨迹，采用紧凑的基于样条的动作表示，引入与运动相关的时间支撑，并为异构具身提供共享动作接口，使固定输出预算可随运动自适应调整时间分辨率；在大规模多具身机器人数据上预训练。

Result: 在LIBERO-X、EBench及两个真实世界长时程任务（蛋饼烹饪与土豆切丝）上评估，在仿真与物理操作中均取得有前景的结果。

Conclusion: 结果表明连续动作表示有望成为未来具身基础模型的基础。

Abstract: Most vision-language-action models represent future motion as fixed-rate action chunks, tying temporal resolution and prediction horizon to a fixed output budget. This pointwise representation wastes capacity on highly correlated neighboring actions, leaves temporal continuity and smoothness to be learned implicitly, and forces a tradeoff between long-horizon coverage and the local precision required for contact-rich manipulation. To address these limitations, we introduce Vela, a vision-language-action foundation model that represents future robot behavior as continuous trajectories. Vela combines a compact spline-based action representation with motion-dependent temporal support and a shared action interface for heterogeneous embodiments, allowing a fixed output budget to adapt its temporal resolution across motions. We pretrain Vela on large-scale multi-embodiment robot data and evaluate it on LIBERO-X, EBench, and two real-world long-horizon tasks, egg-cake cooking and potato shredding, obtaining promising results across simulation and physical manipulation. These results highlight the potential of continuous action representations as a foundation for future embodied foundation models. Project page and more results: this https URL .

</details>


### [32] [VICON: Visual-Inertial-Contact based Hand-Object Tracking for Manipulation Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05180)
*Yubin Jeon,Uiseong Shin,Hwanchul La,Jaeseong Kang,Hyelim Choi,Yongseok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 VICON 框架，融合视觉、惯性与接触信息，在严重遮挡下联合追踪手物运动、接触点和法向力，并构建演示数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作学习依赖包含自然手物交互的人体演示数据，其中接触点和力无法仅由运动轨迹充分表达；现有方法难以联合捕获手物运动、接触点和力，且手物交互造成严重遮挡，影响手与物体跟踪。

Method: 采用视觉-惯性手套与 RGB-D 相机进行精确手部跟踪，并重新设计手套：依据人类抓取频率放置力敏电阻（FSR），同步记录接触状态和经标定的法向力。无需预存 CAD 模型，利用 RGB-D 图像和单目视频重建网格估计物体位姿；提出基于因子图的物体轨迹估计，融合按手物遮挡可见性加权的物体位姿估计、FSR 测量和手部运动先验。

Result: 在 5 个物体、40 次动作捕捉会话中，VICON 失败帧率为 2.5%，基线为 50.9%-64.6%；遮挡下中位误差为 3.9 mm 和 3.0°。基于 VICON 构建了包含同步手物运动、接触点和法向力的数据集，并将公开覆盖 10 类物体的扩展数据集。

Conclusion: VICON 能在严重遮挡下整体捕获手物运动与接触信息，为灵巧操作学习提供高质量的演示数据采集方案。

Abstract: Learning dexterous manipulation benefits from human demonstration datasets that capture diverse and natural hand-object interactions. In particular, contact points and forces provide supervision on where and how strongly to interact, which cannot be fully captured by motion trajectories alone. However, methods for jointly capturing hand and object motion, contact points, and forces remain limited. Moreover, severe occlusion from hand-object interaction challenges accurate tracking of both hands and objects. To address these limitations, we present a Visual-Inertial-CONtact based hand-object tracking (VICON) framework. It holistically captures both hand and object motion along with contact information during manipulation, even under severe occlusion. First, we adopt a visual-inertial glove and an RGB-D camera for accurate hand tracking, and redesign the glove to incorporate contact sensing. Specifically, force-sensitive resistors (FSRs) are placed on the glove based on human grasp frequency to synchronously record contact states and calibrated normal forces. Second, without requiring pre-existing CAD models, we estimate object poses using RGB-D images and a mesh reconstructed from a monocular video. We propose factor-graph-based object trajectory estimation that fuses object-pose estimates weighted by visibility under hand-object occlusion, FSR measurements, and a hand-motion prior. Across 40 motion-capture sessions with five objects, VICON achieves a 2.5% failed-frame rate compared with 50.9-64.6% for the baselines, with median errors of 3.9 mm and 3.0 degrees under occlusion. Using VICON, we construct a dataset containing synchronized hand-object motion, contact points, and normal forces, and will publicly release an expanded dataset covering 10 object categories at this https URL .

</details>


### [33] [Recursive Self-Improvement of Visuomotor Policies through Local Recovery Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05151)
*Yuzhi Zhang,Xinyu Liu,Yu Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种通过局部恢复监督实现递归自我改进的框架：每轮审计当前策略、在支持的失败状态生成纠正示范并更新策略，最终仅部署学生策略，初步实验显示成功率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉运动策略能执行熟悉任务，但在自身犯错后缺乏纠正行为，因此需要一种能针对失败状态生成恢复示范并持续改进策略的方法。

Method: 框架每轮先审计当前策略，在支持的失败状态生成纠正示范，并用其更新驱动下一轮采集的策略。离线审计器利用粗粒度和密集时间证据定位未解决失败，并指定可观测修复目标。固定多模态智能体作为工具使用教师，通过观察、计算、执行和反馈生成恢复动作。冻结学生检验教师终点是否支持继续推进；若继续失败，则恢复该终点并扩展示范。动作级质量评估定义连续训练窗口，包含对齐观测、质量权重和有效性掩码。最终仅部署学生。

Result: 在初步LIBERO-Goal研究中，恢复增强后训练在100个验证场景中成功88次，而同一π0检查点用原始数据继续训练成功78次。早期在robomimic Can上的BC-RNN研究使用26个局部恢复片段，成功率从102/130提升到112/130。两组比较均匹配额外2000步优化。

Conclusion: 局部恢复监督能通过失败审计与教师生成恢复示范实现递归自我改进，在相同优化预算下优于原始数据继续训练，且仅需部署学生策略。

Abstract: Visuomotor policies can execute familiar tasks yet lack the corrective behavior needed after their own mistakes. We present a framework for recursive self-improvement through local recovery supervision. Each round audits the current policy, generates corrective demonstrations at supported failure states, and uses them to update the policy that drives the next round of collection. An offline auditor locates unresolved failures using coarse and dense temporal evidence and specifies observable repair goals. A fixed multimodal agent acts as a tool-using teacher, generating recovery actions through observation, computation, execution, and feedback. The frozen student tests whether each teacher endpoint supports further progress. If continuation fails, the system restores that endpoint and extends the demonstration. Action-level quality assessment then defines continuous training windows with aligned observations, quality weights, and validity masks. Only the student is deployed. In a preliminary LIBERO-Goal study, recovery-augmented post-training achieves 88 successful episodes out of 100 validation scenes, compared with 78 for original-data continuation from the same $\pi_0$ checkpoint. An earlier BC-RNN study on robomimic Can improves success from 102/130 to 112/130 using 26 local recovery segments. Both comparisons match 2,000 additional optimization steps.

</details>


### [34] [PB-STDG: A Prediction-Based Short-Term Decentralized Greedy Guidance Algorithm for a Drone Road System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05122)
*Zhouyu Qu,Andreas Willig,Xiaobing Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向结构化无人机道路系统（DRS）的基于预测的短时分散贪婪制导算法（PB-STDG），通过预测邻居无人机的决策来缓解原STDG算法过度保守的问题，从而在保持安全性的同时提升城市空域交通效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来无人机在城市环境中广泛用于包裹配送、基础设施巡检、应急响应和灯光秀等任务，不同实体运营的无人机数量不断增加，如何使其协同避免碰撞并确定无冲突的短期飞行路径成为不可忽视的问题。原STDG算法存在过度保守行为，导致交通效率受限。

Method: 在结构化Drone Road System（DRS）中提出PB-STDG制导算法。该算法扩展原始STDG，引入预测机制：无人机利用信标包中共享的额外信息来预判邻近无人机的决策，从而减少不必要的保守避让。算法属于分散式短时贪婪制导方法。

Result: 通过多个仿真场景对所提算法与STDG进行对比评估。结果表明，PB-STDG在保持与STDG相当的安全水平的同时，提高了交通效率。

Conclusion: PB-STDG通过基于预测的邻居决策机制有效改善了STDG的过度保守问题，能够在城市空域中实现更高效且安全的短期无冲突飞行路径引导。

Abstract: In recent years, Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) or drones have been increasingly adopted in urban environments for applications such as parcel delivery, infrastructure inspection, emergency response, and drone light shows. A non-negligible issue is how to manage the increasing number of drones operated by different entities, to enable them to cooperatively avoid potential collisions and determine conflict-free short-term flight paths in urban airspace. This paper presents a Prediction-Based Short-Term Decentralized Greedy (PB-STDG) guidance algorithm for a structured Drone Road System (DRS). PB-STDG extends the original STDG algorithm by introducing a prediction mechanism that enables drones to anticipate the decisions of neighboring drones using additional information shared in beacon packets, aiming to address the over-conservative behavior observed in the STDG algorithm. Several simulation scenarios are conducted to evaluate and compare the proposed algorithm with STDG. The results show that PB-STDG improves traffic efficiency while maintaining a safety level comparable to that of STDG.

</details>


### [35] [Now You Feel It, Now You See Me: Digital-Twin-based Teleoperation Interface for Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05081)
*Youngchan Shim,Kyutae Lee,JooYun Kim,Jaeseong Hwang,Harim Ji,Yongseok Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于数字孪生的AR遥操作界面，结合裸手追踪、双臂机械手及虚拟表征，通过力可视化和遮挡缓解视觉反馈，改善机器人灵巧操作示教采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 裸手追踪遥操作无需手套或外骨骼即可控制机械手并演示协调手指运动，但存在两大反馈局限：缺乏力反馈，操作者只能视觉推断接触力，难以精确、安全操作；缺乏遮挡区域视觉反馈，物体或机器人部件遮挡会影响手部位置、接近距离及适配物体形状的预抓取构型评估。

Method: 构建数字孪生AR遥操作界面，集成裸手追踪、双臂机械手以及机器人和任务物体的虚拟表示；将触觉测量渲染到机械手网格上，形成视-力反馈；提供用户控制的虚拟视角面板或遮挡感知透明渲染，以提供缓解遮挡的视觉反馈。

Result: 通过包含Task 1和Task 2的用户研究评估该界面，考察力可视化与视觉辅助如何在需要精细力调节和遮挡下操作的任务中支持示教采集；摘要未报告具体定量结果。

Conclusion: 该界面旨在弥补裸手遥操作中力反馈缺失和遮挡视觉受限的问题，并通过用户研究探索其在灵巧操作示教采集中的辅助作用。

Abstract: Teleoperation is becoming increasingly important for collecting high-quality demonstrations to teach robots dexterous manipulation skills. For dexterous manipulation, bare-hand tracking provides a practical way to control robotic hands and demonstrate coordinated finger movements without gloves or exoskeletons. However, this type of teleoperation faces two key feedback limitations: a lack of force feedback and visual feedback of occluded region. The absence of force feedback hinders precise and safe manipulation, as operators must infer contact force visually rather than feel them directly. Occlusion by objects or other robot parts impairs the assessment of hand positions, approach distances, and pre-grasp configurations suited to the object's shape.
