To address these limitations, we propose a digital-twin-based augmented reality (AR) teleoperation interface that integrates bare-hand tracking, bimanual robotic hands, and virtual representations of the robot and task objects. The interface renders tactile measurements on robot hand meshes as visuo-force feedback. Furthermore, it offers a user-controlled virtual view panel or occlusion-aware transparency rendering to provide occlusion-mitigating visual feedback. We evaluated the interface through user studies involving Task 1 and Task 2, examining how force visualization and visual assistance support demonstration collection in tasks requiring careful force regulation and manipulation under occlusion.

</details>


### [36] [FreeLoc: Online Floorplan Localization via Diffusion-Aided Pose Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05011)
*Haocheng Peng,Boyang Zhou,Jiarui Hu,Xiyue Guo,Ziyang Zhang,Boming Zhao,Yifan Gao,Xiao Li,Hujun Bao,Zhaopeng Cui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FreeLoc 是一个基于 RGB 的在线室内定位框架，将楼层平面图直接作为可查询的几何地图，避免构建密集的场景专属离线数据库，并支持单帧与序列定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高性能楼层平面图定位方法通常将平面图转换为密集的场景专属离线数据库，导致定位精度、存储开销和运行时间受离散化位姿空间的采样分辨率制约。

Method: FreeLoc 引入高效的在线几何查询与扩散辅助细化方案：通过即时楼层平面图射线查询检索合理位姿锚点，并细化得到连续位姿估计。针对序列定位，提出在线似然构建策略，从粗采样候选与细化位姿假设中构建似然，从而在无需离线数据库的情况下实现基于直方图滤波的时序融合。

Result: 实验表明该方法可实现实时在线推理，在单帧和序列定位中均达到最先进性能；真实场景结果验证了其在室内机器人定位中的实际可部署性。

Conclusion: FreeLoc 证明将楼层平面图视为可直接查询的几何地图是有效的，能够摆脱密集离线数据库对精度、存储和运行时的限制，并具备实际室内机器人定位应用价值。

Abstract: Floorplans provide compact and widely available geometric maps for indoor localization, but existing high-performing floorplan-based methods still convert them into dense scene-specific offline databases, tying accuracy, storage, and runtime to the sampling resolution of the discretized pose space. We present FreeLoc, an online RGB-based floorplan localization framework that treats the floorplan as a directly queryable geometric map. FreeLoc introduces an efficient online geometric querying and diffusion-aided refinement scheme, which retrieves plausible pose anchors through on-the-fly floorplan ray querying and refines them into accurate continuous pose estimates. For sequential localization, FreeLoc develops an online likelihood construction strategy that bridges single-frame localization and probabilistic temporal fusion by constructing likelihoods from coarse-sampled candidates and refined pose hypotheses, enabling histogram-filter-based temporal fusion without offline databases. Experiments demonstrate real-time online inference and state-of-the-art performance in both single-frame and sequential localization, while real-world results validate practical deployability in indoor robotic localization scenarios.

</details>


### [37] [A Unified Dynamics Framework for Reinforcement Learning and Classical Control of a Six-DOF Pipeline-Tracking ROV in NVIDIA Isaac Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04949)
*Cheng Siong Chin,M. Venkateshkumar,Jianhua Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文针对水下ROV强化学习控制器训练与部署物理模型不一致的问题，提出基于同一USD场景参数、在NumPy Fossen方程与NVIDIA Isaac Sim PhysX力实现之间保持动力学一致性的6自由度管线跟踪架构；系统比较5种RL算法与6种经典控制器，并考察Coriolis-向心项的消融影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下航行器RL控制常在一个物理表示上训练、在另一个上部署，导致报告性能无法可靠反映训练分布外的行为；需要统一训练与部署动力学，并在相同环境、奖励、随机评估体系下公平比较学习型与经典控制方法。

Method: 以真实BlueROV2-Heavy的USD场景统一提供质量、附加质量、阻尼、浮力与推进器参数；训练端使用矢量化NumPy实现Fossen船体运动方程，部署端在Isaac Sim中将相同方程作为PhysX力逐步施加。以Coriolis-向心项为核心动力学模型，在PPO/TRPO上做受控消融。通过检查点兼容层，用同一代码在统一环境和随机评估框架下训练与评估PPO、SAC、TD3、DDPG、TRPO；并扩展PID、滑模、模糊逻辑、反馈线性化、MPC、ANFIS六种经典基线，使其使用与学习策略相同的制导几何和推力分配。

Result: Coriolis项消融表明其不破坏PPO或TRPO稳定性，并小幅提升跟踪性能，TRPO的standoff RMS误差约降低19%。在启用Coriolis动力学时，PPO、TRPO和反馈线性化达到100%成功率且精度具竞争力；PID、模糊控制和ANFIS也达100%成功率但跟踪更松；DDPG与TD3各自出现特定且可解释的失效模式，而非离策略学习普遍弱点；经典控制仍是最优学习策略的有力基线。

Conclusion: 通过单源USD场景和相同Fossen/PhysX动力学实现，可减少训练-部署物理差异并支持公平基准测试；Coriolis-向心项应保留；学习型与经典控制各有优势，离策略算法的失败更可能源于算法/实现特因而非范式缺陷。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning controllers for underwater vehicles are usually trained against one physics representation and deployed against another, so reported performance does not always describe behavior outside training. This paper presents a pipeline-tracking architecture for a six-degree-of-freedom remotely operated vehicle (ROV) in which one Universal Scene Description (USD) scene supplies the real BlueROV2-Heavy mass, added-mass, damping, buoyancy, and thruster parameters to both halves of the system: a vectorized NumPy implementation of Fossen's marine-craft equations, and an interactive NVIDIA Isaac Sim deployment applying the identical equations as PhysX forces at every step. The Coriolis-centripetal term is the primary dynamics model in both branches; a controlled ablation on PPO and TRPO shows that including it does not destabilize either algorithm and modestly improves tracking, about 19 percent lower standoff RMS error for TRPO. Five reinforcement learning algorithms, PPO, soft actor-critic, TD3, DDPG, and TRPO, are trained against one environment, reward, and randomized evaluation harness through a checkpoint-compatibility layer scoring any policy with the same code. The pipeline is extended with six classical baselines, PID, sliding-mode, fuzzy logic, feedback linearization, model predictive control, and an adaptive neuro-fuzzy inference system, driven by the same guidance geometry and thruster allocation as the learned policies. Under Coriolis-enabled dynamics, PPO, TRPO, and feedback linearization reach the strongest combination of 100 percent success and competitive accuracy; PID, fuzzy control, and the neuro-fuzzy baseline also reach 100 percent success with looser tracking; DDPG and TD3 each show a specific, explainable failure mode rather than a general weakness of off-policy learning; and classical control remains a strong baseline against the best learned policies.

</details>


### [38] [DiVeR: Decision-Critical Verifier Learning for VLA Test-Time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04933)
*Seongheon Park,Heecheol Kim,Shulin Tian,Lilika Makabe,Namiko Saito,Katsushi Ikeuchi,Sharon Li,Yasuyuki Matsushita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DiVeR 通过采样动作表示的离散度估计决策关键性，并据此对验证器学习进行重加权，使测试时动作选择更有效，从而提升 VLA 策略任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩展机器人数据与模型容量可改进 Vision-Language-Action（VLA）策略，但机器人数据成本高。验证器引导的测试时扩展通过在推理时采样多个候选动作并选择最可能成功的动作，提供了高效替代方案。现有基于分类的验证器虽从轨迹级结果学习，却同等对待所有访问状态，而轨迹中只有稀疏的决策关键状态能提供有意义的动作区分信号。

Method: 提出 DiVeR，从采样动作表示的离散度估计决策关键性，并利用该信号将验证器学习重加权到动作选择后果最大的状态；无需步级标注或额外环境交互。

Result: 在 LIBERO、RoboCasa 以及 Franka Research 3 真实机器人实验中，DiVeR 通过更有效的验证器引导动作选择一致提升任务成功率，同时仅增加可忽略的验证器推理开销。

Conclusion: DiVeR 无需额外标注或交互即可聚焦决策关键状态，提升验证器引导测试时扩展的效率与 VLA 策略任务表现。

Abstract: Scaling robot data and model capacity has improved Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies, but further progress is constrained by the high cost of robotic data. Verifier-guided test-time scaling offers an efficient alternative by sampling multiple action candidates and selecting the one most likely to lead to task success at inference time. Existing classification-based verifiers learn from trajectory-level outcomes but treat all visited states equally, even though their value for candidate discrimination can vary across a trajectory. At many states, plausible actions are similar and provide limited discrimination signal, while only a sparse set of decision-critical states admits meaningfully different actions that can substantially affect downstream outcomes. To address this, we propose DiVeR, which estimates decision criticality from the dispersion of sampled action representations. DiVeR then uses this signal to reweight verifier learning toward states where action selection is most consequential, without requiring step-level annotations or additional environment interaction. Across LIBERO, RoboCasa, and real-world experiments on a Franka Research 3 robot, DiVeR consistently improves task success through more effective verifier-guided action selection, while adding negligible verifier inference overhead.

</details>


### [39] [RobotUse: Allocating Computation, Context, and Decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04929)
*Junhoo Lee,Injun Baek,Seungyeon Kim,Suhyun Jeon,Minkyu Kim,Baekseung Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RobotUse：一种围绕物理动作指定与修正来组织计算、上下文和决策的机器人智能体框架，在 RoboLab 上取得 45% 任务成功率，并支持从真实执行中持续学习与迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人智能体需要把预期动作与观测结果联系起来，并在重复尝试中保留用于修正决策的上下文；现有接口常把选择限制在预定义工具内，或要求智能体管理详细执行代码和不断增长的历史。

Method: RobotUse 让智能体通过视觉方式选择目标与位姿，后端负责几何、运动规划和控制；子智能体在每个子目标内保留详细交互，并返回后续决策所需信息；通过持续 harnessing 从执行中学习，更新持久化 playbook。

Result: 在 RoboLab 上达到 45% 任务成功率，比 CaP-X 高 6.7 个百分点，同时保持紧凑的决策上下文并减少对预定义动作抽象的依赖；还证明其能在不完美反馈下从真实世界执行中学习，并迁移到后续任务。

Conclusion: RobotUse 表明，将动作指定、执行细节与决策上下文分层组织，并持续积累经验，可提升机器人智能体的任务表现、学习能力和跨任务迁移能力。

Abstract: Robot agents must connect their intended actions to observed outcomes while retaining the context needed to revise their choices over repeated attempts. Existing interfaces often leave these choices inside predefined tools or require agents to manage detailed execution code and its growing history. We introduce RobotUse, a robot agent harness that organizes computation, context, and decisions around specifying and revising physical actions. Agents visually select targets and poses, while the backend handles geometry, motion planning, and control. Subagents retain detailed interactions within each subgoal and return the information needed for subsequent decisions. Continual harnessing lets agents learn from execution by updating a persistent playbook. On RoboLab, RobotUse achieves 45% task success, outperforming CaP-X by 6.7 percentage points while maintaining compact decision contexts and reducing reliance on predefined action abstractions. Furthermore, we show that RobotUse learns from real-world execution despite imperfect feedback and transfers what it learns to subsequent tasks. Project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [40] [InterMimicGen: Scaling Humanoid Loco-Manipulation through Self-Evolving Motion Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06850)
*Yucheng Zhang,Sirui Xu,Jinhong Li,Liuyu Bian,Anatulya Nandi,Derek Zhang,Xiangchen Liu,Xueting Li,Umar Iqbal,Yu-Xiong Wang,Liang-Yan Gui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: InterMimicGen 是一个自进化运动模仿框架，通过接触保持重定向、物理仿真通用跟踪器和数据飞轮，将稀疏异构的人-物交互演示转化为持续扩展的人形机器人全身移动操作运动资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 捕获的人-物交互数据虽能为类人机器人全身移动操作提供丰富监督，但这些数据稀疏、异构且无法被机器人直接执行，因此需要一条从人类演示到机器人可执行运动的统一路径。

Method: 首先整合运动捕捉的人-物交互数据集，并在保留全身协调与灵巧手-物关系的前提下重定向为类人机器人参考；其次在仿真中训练基于物理的通用跟踪器，使带灵巧手的人形机器人执行这些参考；最后构建数据飞轮：每轮对交互发生位置和身体执行方式做小的任务保持型编辑，微调跟踪器，仅保留仿真执行后成功完成任务的变体，并用于下一轮种子。

Result: 实验表明：重定向能在不同机器人构型间保持接触，单一通用策略可实现广泛跟踪，可执行运动随增强轮次持续增长，并能迁移到真实机器人。

Conclusion: InterMimicGen 提供了从异构人类演示到持续扩展运动资源的人形机器人学习统一路径，可作为灵巧全身移动操作的可扩展运动数据生成与策略训练框架。

Abstract: Captured human-object interactions provide rich supervision for humanoid loco-manipulation, but they are sparse, heterogeneous, and not directly executable by robots. We introduce InterMimicGen, a self-evolving motion-imitation framework in which robot motion data and a tracking policy improve each other. First, we consolidate motion-captured human-object interaction datasets and retarget them into humanoid robot references while preserving whole-body coordination and dexterous hand-object relationships. This produces a large and diverse humanoid robot reference collection for dexterous whole-body loco-manipulation. Second, we train a physics-based generalist tracker that executes these references in simulation on a humanoid with dexterous hands, covering a scale and diversity beyond prior humanoid tracking systems for loco-manipulation. Third, we close a data flywheel: each round makes small, task-preserving changes to where an interaction takes place and how the body performs it, fine-tunes the tracker on them, and keeps only the variants whose simulated execution completes the task, which seed the next round. With more iterations, these small edits compound into broader coverage around the sparse original demonstrations while preserving task semantics and motion quality. Experiments show contact-preserving retargeting across robot configurations, broad tracking with a single generalist policy, executable motions that keep growing over augmentation rounds, and transfer to real robots. InterMimicGen provides a unified path from heterogeneous human demonstrations to a continually expanding motion resource for humanoid robot learning.

</details>


### [41] [Recursive Video In-Context Learning for Agentic Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06843)
*Wenrui Bao,Xinxin Liu,Bingxin Xu,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练方法 RV-ICL，将演示视频组织成可递归导航的层次结构，让 LLM agent 按需读取多粒度视频上下文，从而更好地编排冻结的 VLA 策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM agent 通过文本记忆记录做过什么，却缺少任务如何完成的信息；演示视频虽包含该信息，但完整视频会拖慢每轮推理，固定关键帧又会丢失决定抓取是否成功的接触细节。同时，agent 在规划时需要任务结构，在执行接触相关步骤时需要局部帧。

Method: RV-ICL 免训练，把一条演示转化为 agent 可导航的层次结构，而非一次性 prompt。层次由抓取、释放等子事件构建，从全任务关键帧到阶段、时刻和短片段逐级细化，并通过只读工具暴露。agent 规划前读取粗粒度层级；执行中需要更多细节时重新进入层次，只加载当前子目标对应的短片段。每任务一条演示即可，基于 RPent 实现。

Result: 在 LIBERO-PRO 上成功率从 92.6% 提升到 96.5%，在 LIBERO-Plus 上从 86.7% 提升到 95.8%。

Conclusion: 递归、按需、多粒度的视频上下文能有效弥补文本记忆的不足，无需训练且每任务仅需一条演示，即可提升 LLM agent 编排冻结 VLA 策略的任务成功率。

Abstract: LLM agents that orchestrate frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policies improve across episodes through text memory, which records what the agent did but not how the task is done. A demonstration video shows it, but fits poorly into an agent's context. The full video slows every turn, fixed keyframes lose the contact detail that decides whether a grasp holds, and what the agent needs shifts from the task's structure while planning to the frames around each contact. We introduce Recursive Video In-Context Learning (RV-ICL), a training-free method that turns a demonstration into a hierarchy the agent navigates rather than a prompt it receives. The hierarchy is built from the sub-events of the demonstration, such as grasps and releases. Its levels grow finer, from keyframes of the whole task to phases, moments and short clips, and are exposed through read-only tools. The agent reads the coarse levels before planning. During execution it re-enters the hierarchy whenever a step needs more detail and loads only the clip of its current sub-goal. One demonstration per task is enough. Built on RPent, RV-ICL raises success from 92.6% to 96.5% on LIBERO-PRO and from 86.7% to 95.8% on LIBERO-Plus.

</details>


### [42] [AffordCraft: Scalable Construction of Task-Ready Simulation Assets from Single Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06643)
*Haoyun Yang,Xueyang Zhou,Ziyi Xie,Yongchao Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: AffordCraft 通过检索而非生成，从单张 RGB 图像和任务指令构建物理有效的铰接物体资产，用于机器人仿真学习，并在成功率和计算效率上显著优于生成式方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真机器人学习依赖仿真器提供的物体，许多任务需要具有独立部件、可实现所需运动的关节以及接触下仍有效的物理属性。现有方法需为每张图像重新恢复结构：生成模型预测的部件和关节大多无法在仿真中稳定或运动，通用智能体则需为每张照片进行长时间模型调用。

Method: AffordCraft 定位图像中的物体和待操作部件，从铰接资产库中选择匹配条目，并将其拟合到图像，同时保持部件和关节完整；无需边界框或掩码标注，且扩展资产库不需要修改方法。

Result: 在来自31个类别的2000张照片中，AffordCraft 为1703张生成物理有效资产；五种生成方法最多通过45%的相同照片，且每个有效资产的中位GPU时间为AffordCraft的10至78倍。在50张杂乱图像中，自动检测后237个标注物体有162个通过相同物理测试。资产库从141增至11372条目时，类别覆盖率从46%升至100%，具有请求标签的选择比例从18%升至51%。作者还用构建资产创建单物体和组合场景操作任务，基于脚本演示训练的策略能从训练中未见的初始状态完成两类任务。

Conclusion: 基于检索的资产构建能有效从单张图像生成物理有效且可操作的仿真资产，具备良好可扩展性，并可支持机器人操作策略学习。

Abstract: Robot learning in simulation depends on the objects the simulator offers. Many tasks need objects with separate parts, joints that allow the required motion, and physical properties that remain valid under contact. Existing methods recover this structure anew for every image: generative models predict parts and joints that mostly fail to settle or move in simulation, and general-purpose agents need a long session of model calls for each photograph. AffordCraft builds such an asset from a single RGB image and a task instruction by retrieval instead of generation: it locates the object and the part to operate, selects a matching entry from a library of articulated assets, and fits it to the image while keeping its parts and joints intact. Without any box or mask marking the object, AffordCraft produces a physically valid asset for 1,703 of 2,000 photographs from 31 categories. Five generative methods pass on at most 45% of the same photographs and, at the median, need 10 to 78 times our GPU time per valid asset. On 50 cluttered images, 162 of 237 annotated objects pass the same physical test after automatic detection. Growing the library from 141 to 11,372 entries needs no change to the method and raises category coverage from 46% to 100% and the share of selections with the requested label from 18% to 51%. We also build manipulation tasks from the constructed assets, both with single objects and in composed scenes; policies trained on scripted demonstrations complete both kinds of tasks from initial states unseen in training.

</details>


### [43] [Physics Residual Dynamics and Reduced Order Whole-Body Planning for Obstacle Aware Human Robot Cloth CoTransportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06641)
*Moein Forouhar,Kosar Behnia,Anirvan Dutta,Hamid Sadeghian,Ville Kyrki,Sami Haddadin,Gokhan Alcan,Eckehard Steinbach*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种分层规划框架，将学习式布料模型与双机械臂移动操作器的降阶全身模型结合，用于人-机器人协同搬运可变形物体，兼顾避障间隙与变形控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同搬运可变形物体时，障碍物间隙不仅取决于抓取点，还受未驱动内部构型影响，因此需要在运动规划中预测布料变形，并保证移动机械臂全身运动的实时可执行性。

Method: 采用物理残差条件循环变分自编码器（p-cRVAE），以抓取点观测预测完整布料构型，并学习对高效线性化物理模型的残差修正，以限制40步规划时域内的误差累积；将预测布料动力学嵌入模型预测路径积分（MPPI）规划器，使用保留非完整底座约束和机械臂工作空间限制的双臂移动操作器降阶表示；随后由MPC层细化采样运动，生成平滑、可执行的全身控制参考。

Result: 降阶模型在跟踪性能上与完整17-DoF模型相当，计算时间减少约80%；在四种协同搬运场景和两种搬运速度下，该方法保持布料-障碍物间隙，而沿角点跟踪基线会发生碰撞；全身细化将最终布料变形从0.93 m降至0.28 m。

Conclusion: 该分层规划框架通过结合学习式布料变形预测与降阶全身MPPI/MPC规划，实现了可变形物体协同搬运中的有效避障、计算效率提升和变形抑制，优于基线方法。

Abstract: Human--robot co-transportation of deformable objects requires predicting object deformation during motion, since obstacle clearance depends on both the grasp points and the unactuated interior. We present a hierarchical planning framework that combines a learned cloth model with a reduced-order whole-body model of a dual-arm mobile manipulator. A physics-residual conditional recurrent variational autoencoder (p-cRVAE) predicts the full cloth configuration from grasp-point observations by learning a residual correction to a computationally efficient linearized physics model, limiting error accumulation over 40-step planning horizon. The predicted cloth dynamics are embedded in a model predictive path integral (MPPI) planner using a reduced-order representation of a dual-arm mobile manipulator that preserves the non-holonomic base constraint and arm workspace limits. An MPC layer subsequently refines the sampled motion into smooth, executable references for whole-body control. The reduced-order formulation achieves tracking performance comparable to the full 17-DoF model while reducing computation time by approximately 80%. Across four co-transportation scenarios and two carrying speeds, the proposed framework maintains cloth-obstacle clearance where a corner-following baseline results in collisions, while whole-body refinement reduces final cloth deformation from 0.93\,m to 0.28\,m.

</details>


### [44] [SimForcing: Distilling Simulation Motion Priors into Real-Domain Robot World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06598)
*Xiaodong Wang,Tianle Li,Chuanxin Song,Junliang Xie,Zhanmi Zhong,Suiying Wu,Peixi Peng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SimForcing：一种仿真引导的机器人世界模型框架，通过潜在运动蒸馏与多块仿真条件化/条件丢弃，将仿真作为可迁移运动知识与可控参考，在无需外部具身预训练下提升真实机器人视频生成，并能初始化VLA模型改善下游策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件机器人世界模型需精确响应机器人轨迹并保持真实视觉动态，但从异构机器人视频中同时学习二者困难。仿真可提供结构化运动监督，然而外观差异阻碍直接迁移，且不准确的仿真预测会误导真实视频生成。

Method: SimForcing包含两点：1）通过潜在运动蒸馏从仿真教师迁移运动知识，在潜空间对齐时序变化以内化运动先验，并减轻外观差异影响；2）引入多块仿真条件化与条件丢弃，在利用预测仿真轨迹时避免过度依赖其准确性。二者由仿真条件化无分类器引导统一，平衡基于内化运动知识的预测与额外由仿真潜变量引导的预测。联合训练的学生模型同时生成仿真条件和真实域视频，推理时无需额外世界模型。

Result: 在Bridge数据集上，SimForcing在无外部具身预训练情况下取得相比对比方法最优的PSNR、SSIM、LPIPS和FVD。在InternData-A1上的评估进一步支持其跨机器人数据集的适用性。此外，用训练好的世界模型初始化视觉-语言-动作模型可提升LIBERO成功率，表明其对下游策略学习有用。

Conclusion: SimForcing有效利用仿真应对真实机器人视频世界建模中的外观差异与仿真预测不准确问题，提升生成质量并展示跨数据集泛化与下游策略初始化价值。

Abstract: Action-conditioned robot world models must respond precisely to robot trajectories while preserving realistic visual dynamics, yet learning both from heterogeneous robot videos remains challenging. Simulation offers structured motion supervision, but appearance differences hinder direct transfer, and inaccurate simulation predictions can misguide real-video generation. We present SimForcing, a simulation-guided framework that uses simulation both as a source of transferable motion knowledge and as a controllable reference for prediction. First, we transfer motion knowledge from a simulation teacher through latent-motion distillation, aligning temporal changes in latent space to internalize motion priors while mitigating the influence of appearance differences. Second, we introduce multi-block simulation conditioning with condition dropout to exploit predicted simulation trajectories without relying excessively on their accuracy. Our simulation-conditioning classifier-free guidance scheme unifies these two ideas by balancing predictions based on internalized motion knowledge with those additionally guided by simulation latents. The jointly trained student generates both simulation conditions and real-domain videos, requiring no additional world model at inference. On Bridge, SimForcing achieves the best PSNR, SSIM, LPIPS, and FVD among the compared methods without external embodied pretraining. Evaluation on InternData-A1 further supports its applicability across robot datasets. Moreover, using our trained world model to initialize a vision-language-action model improves LIBERO success, suggesting its utility for downstream policy learning. \url{ this https URL }

</details>


### [45] [ArtifactArena: Evaluating Models by What They Build in the Physical World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06511)
*Kushagra Tiwary*,David Mayo*,Nikhil Behari,Xiangzhou Sun,Abdulrahman Alabdulkareem,Isaac Galatzer-Levy,Boris Katz,Brian Cheung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ArtifactArena：一个让模型在物理仿真环境中设计、迭代并让机器人 artifact 对战的开源评测平台，用 Elo 排名持续衡量前沿模型的物理世界开放式智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估前沿模型不应只看其语言输出，而要看其能在真实物理环境中工程实现和构建什么；同时需要开放、非饱和、可持续演进的测试平台来追踪开放式智能在物理世界中的边界。

Method: 构建 ArtifactArena 开放平台，设置物理落地的软硬件协同设计挑战：模型需工程化完整机器人并在模拟竞技场中比赛。通过三类 harness 评估零样本、验证器引导细化和开放式物理设计能力，分别基于文本描述、物理模拟器反馈和游戏数据迭代优化机器人 artifact；并以 artifact 间头对头锦标赛的 Elo 排名对前沿模型进行基准测试。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数值，仅说明建立了基于 artifact 头对头锦标赛的 Elo 基准，并发布框架与赛事基础设施，支持社区持续提交。

Conclusion: ArtifactArena 被定位为持续运行、非饱和的活体测试平台，用于长期衡量开放式智能在物理世界中不断扩展的能力上限。

Abstract: To evaluate the frontier, we must measure models not by what they say, but by what they can engineer and build in grounded physical environments. We introduce \textsc{ArtifactArena}, an open-ended platform where models face a physically grounded hardware-software co-design challenge: engineering fully functional robots to compete in a simulated arena. We evaluate a frontier model's zero-shot, verifier guided refinement, and open-ended physical design capabilities through three harnesses that refine their bot artifacts based on text descriptions, physics simulator feedback, and gameplay data. We benchmark these capabilities with an Elo ranking of frontier models derived from head-to-head tournaments between their artifacts. By releasing this framework and tournament infrastructure for ongoing community submissions, we establish a living, non-saturating testbed to continuously measure the expanding limits of open-ended intelligence in the physical world. Please visit \href{ this https URL }{ this https URL } for more information.

</details>


### [46] [MarvisNav: Making Memory Visible on Route Choices for Zero-Shot Object Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06510)
*Jincheng Wang,Chi Pui Chan,Wei Zeng,Shuyang Zhang,Jianhao Jiao,Dimitrios Kanoulas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MarvisNav，一种零样本物体导航（ZSON）框架：将拓扑图中的候选节点及其探索状态直接投影到第一视角，形成“带记忆的视觉路径选择”，使VLM无需额外融合或重排即可联合评估目标相关性与探索状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ZSON方法中，VLM通常从自中心图像推断有希望的搜索区域，而探索历史以文本或地图等形式单独表示。这种分离要么需要额外融合步骤，要么让VLM隐式恢复记忆与路径选择之间的对应关系。作者希望让探索记忆直接在视觉路径选择上可见。

Method: MarvisNav维护一个拓扑图，将候选节点连同其探索状态投影到自中心视图上，作为承载记忆的视觉路径选择。这些状态不仅编码二值访问信息，还表示局部探索进度。通过将探索状态直接绑定到每个视觉候选，VLM可联合评估目标相关性与探索状态，无需事后融合或重排序，且不进行策略训练。

Result: 在HM3D上达到81.2% SR和42.5% SPL，取得最先进性能；在MP3D上也具有竞争力。相比代表性VLM方法，VLM调用次数大幅减少，例如仅为WMNav的7.5%。真实机器人跨多样场景实验进一步验证了实际可部署性。

Conclusion: 研究表明，记忆表示方式会塑造VLM决策并影响ZSON性能；有效利用记忆不仅取决于记忆是否可用，还取决于其如何被表示。

Abstract: When searching for an object, people choose their next move by considering both likely target locations and places already explored. The current view can cue place-associated memories, bringing target relevance and prior exploration into the same spatial context. In many zero-shot object navigation (ZSON) methods, however, vision-language models (VLMs) infer promising search areas from egocentric images, while exploration history is represented separately, e.g., as text or maps. This separation either requires an additional fusion step or leaves the correspondence between memory and route choices implicit for the VLM to recover. We instead make exploration memory directly visible on visual route choices. We propose MarvisNav, a ZSON framework that maintains a topological graph and projects candidate nodes together with their exploration states onto egocentric views as memory-bearing visual route choices. These states capture local exploration progress beyond binary visitation. By binding exploration state directly to each visual candidate, MarvisNav enables the VLM to jointly evaluate target relevance and exploration state without a separate post-hoc fusion or reranking stage. Without policy training, MarvisNav achieves state-of-the-art performance on HM3D (81.2% SR and 42.5% SPL), while remaining competitive on MP3D. It also outperforms representative VLM-based methods with far fewer VLM calls (e.g., 7.5% of WMNav). Real-robot experiments across diverse scenes further validate its practical deployability. Beyond MarvisNav, our study shows that memory representation shapes VLM decisions and ZSON performance, highlighting that effective memory use depends not only on its availability, but also on how it is represented. Code and project page will be available at \url{ this https URL }.

</details>


### [47] [Traversability-Aware Cooperative Path Planning for Human-UGV Casualty Evacuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06487)
*Kristian Dalland,Prithvi Poddar,Souma Chowdhury,Karthik Dantu,Ehsan T. Esfahani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作将人类建模为具有代谢成本和疲劳约束的物理协作者，与UGV组成双智能体团队，通过优化伤员交接点（switch points）进行联合路径规划，在搜救伤员转运中兼顾任务时间与人类能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多异构多机器人路径规划通常将所有智能体视为机器人，其成本模型偏机械、通行性依赖传感器；在人机团队中，人类往往仅承担指挥监督角色，未被建模为具有独特移动约束和动态能量储备的物理协同导航者。本文面向搜救伤员转运，研究人-UGV双智能体联合路径规划。

Method: 人类采用Pandolf-Santee代谢成本模型，速度受疲劳调制；UGV采用滚动阻力能量模型，速度受地形限制。利用两者通行能力互补（人可穿越密集植被和浅水，UGV在开阔地形和道路上更快），优化伤员转运位置即switch points，以最小化总任务时间。在多个合成1 km²环境（程序化生成高程和土地覆盖）中评估。

Result: 相比人类单独基线，优化策略使平均任务时间减少5.3%，人类能耗减少17.8%；相比未优化switch points的朴素人-UGV策略，任务时间减少7.0%。朴素策略虽使人类能耗减少22.4%，但任务时间比基线增加2%。

Conclusion: Switch point优化是实现人-UGV团队时间收益的必要条件；将人类建模为物理协作者并利用异构通行互补性，可在搜救场景中同时改善任务效率并降低人类能耗。

Abstract: Heterogeneous multi-robot path planning is a well-studied problem in which agents with disparate kinematic and dynamic models must coordinate to achieve shared objectives. These formulations, however, treat all agents as robotic-their cost models are mechanical and their traversability is sensor-derived. In human-robot teaming, the human partner remains relegated to command and supervisory roles rather than being modeled as a physical co-navigator with distinct mobility constraints and dynamic energy reserves. This work investigates joint path planning for a two-agent human-UGV team in search-and-rescue casualty retrieval scenarios. We model the human agent using the Pandolf-Santee metabolic cost model with fatigue-modulated speed, and the UGV using a rolling-resistance energy model with terrain-dependent speed limits. By exploiting the complementary traversability of each agent-the human's ability to traverse dense vegetation and shallow water versus the UGV's superior speed on open terrain and roads-we optimize casualty transfer locations, termed switch points, to minimize total mission time. Evaluated across multiple synthetic 1km2 environments with procedurally generated elevation and land-cover data, the optimized strategy reduces mean mission time by 5.3% relative to a human-only baseline and by 7.0% relative to a naive human-UGV strategy without switch point optimization, while reducing human energy expenditure by 17.8% relative to baseline. Notably, the naive strategy reduces human energy expenditure by a larger margin (22.4%) but incurs a 2% increase in mission time relative to baseline, illustrating that switch point optimization is necessary to realize time savings from human-UGV teaming.

</details>


### [48] [Odyssey: A Closed-Loop Benchmark for Long-Horizon Real-World Driving with Explicit Navigation Routes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06469)
*Jungho Kim,Hongjae Shin,Seunghoon Yu,Heecheol Yoo,Myeongjun Kim,Jiyong Oh,Donghyuk Kwak,Seunghyeop Nam,Haesung Oh,Hyunju Kim,Hyungchan Cho,Jaehyun Park,Soo Won Seo,Jun Won Choi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Odyssey 是一个面向端到端驾驶的长时程闭环基准，包含100个由100秒 nuPlan 日志重建的场景，并用显式SD地图路线替代方向指令，提出RouteDS等指标评估规划器的路线遵循与变道准备能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环评测只评估短片段，无法揭示早期决策对后续驾驶的长期影响；同时，模糊的方向指令会掩盖真正的导航目标，难以一致地评估规划器是否按预期路线行驶并为后续机动做准备。

Method: 构建Odyssey闭环基准：100个场景，每个重建自100秒nuPlan驾驶日志，以保留导航机动与交通交互上下文；用显式SD地图路线指定应行驶道路，局部驾驶动作由基于传感器的规划决定；在 rollout 中用基于扩散的精修方法减少沿自车轨迹的3DGS渲染伪影；提出SD Route Compliance和Pre-Lane Change Score，并扩展Driving Score为RouteDS，对SD路线偏离和变道准备失败进行惩罚；适配包括VLA模型在内的先进规划器并评估其导航性能。

Result: 在Odyssey上评估适配后的先进规划器（包括视觉-语言-动作模型）的导航表现，结果凸显了端到端驾驶在路线表示与集成方面仍存在开放问题。

Conclusion: Odyssey通过长时程闭环、显式SD路线目标和面向导航的RouteDS等指标，提供了更一致的端到端驾驶导航评估方式；基准代码和适配基线将公开释放。

Abstract: Closed-loop evaluation of end-to-end driving requires continuous rollouts that reveal how earlier decisions affect subsequent driving. However, existing benchmarks evaluate only short segments and fail to capture later consequences. Ambiguous directional commands also obscure the intended navigation objective. We introduce Odyssey, a closed-loop benchmark for long-horizon driving comprising 100 scenarios, each reconstructed from a 100-second nuPlan driving log to preserve the context of navigation maneuvers and traffic interactions. To provide a consistent navigation objective, Odyssey replaces directional commands with explicit standard-definition (SD) map routes that specify which roads to follow, while sensor-based planning determines local driving actions. Throughout these rollouts, diffusion-based refinement of 3DGS-rendered images reduces rendering artifacts along the ego trajectory. To assess how effectively planners follow these routes and prepare for upcoming maneuvers, we introduce SD Route Compliance and Pre-Lane Change Score. These assessments are complemented by RouteDS, which extends the Driving Score with penalties for SD-route deviations and failed lane preparation. We adapt state-of-the-art planners, including vision-language-action (VLA) models, and evaluate their navigation performance using these metrics. Odyssey highlights open questions in route representation and integration for E2E driving. Benchmark code and adapted baselines will be released publicly.

</details>


### [49] [A Taxonomy on Collective Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06427)
*Guillermo GP-Lenza,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Martín Molina,Ricardo Sanz,Pascual Campoy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文主张多机器人系统文献中的互意识（MA）、共享情境意识（SSA）与团队情境意识（TSA）不可互换；三者构成包含层级，并在意识分布谱上从个体化理解到统一理解，其中TSA处于混合位置。通过二维分类法与搜救案例研究，为集体意识的形式化描述、设计与比较提供概念基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态非结构化环境中的机器人团队协调，要求智能体对环境、队友和任务状态保持准确且对齐的表征；但现有文献常将MA、SSA、TSA混用为同义词，掩盖了每种概念对机器人系统施加的不同协调要求，因此需要澄清其关系与差异。

Method: 作者开展概念分析，提出沿“意识对象”和“意识分布”两个维度的分类法；论证三者构成包含层级与分布谱系；并以异构空中与地面机器人的搜救（SAR）案例，将分类法中的每个位置对应到具体协调需求。

Result: MA、SSA、TSA形成包含层级，范围逐一扩展；分布谱从MA的完全个体化理解到SSA的完全统一理解，TSA占据混合位置；SAR案例验证并具象化了各分类位置的协调要求。

Conclusion: 该工作为多机器人系统中集体意识的原则性规范、设计和比较提供了概念基础，并强调不能将三种意识概念视为同义。

Abstract: As robotic teams tackle increasingly complex tasks in dynamic and unstructured environments, effective coordination requires agents to maintain accurate, aligned representations of their environment, teammates, and mission state. We argue that Mutual Awareness, Shared Situational Awareness, and Team Situational Awareness --- three concepts widely invoked in the multi-robot systems literature --- are not interchangeable: they form a containment hierarchy in which each type subsumes the previous in scope, and span a distribution spectrum from fully individualized understanding (MA) to fully uniform understanding (SSA), with TSA occupying a mixed position. We establish this through a two-dimensional taxonomy organized along the object of awareness and awareness distribution axes. Treating these terms as synonyms obscures the precise coordination requirements each imposes on a robotic system. A Search and Rescue case study with heterogeneous aerial and ground robots grounds each taxonomy position in concrete coordination requirements. These findings provide a conceptual foundation for principled specification, design, and comparison of collective awareness in multi-robot systems.

</details>


### [50] [Visual Swarm Navigation via Deep Reinforcement Learning and Evolutionary Hybrid Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06400)
*Álvaro Díez,Fidel Aznar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种结合多智能体强化学习与神经进化策略的混合方法，用于自动合成仅依赖单目视觉的群体机器人分散式导航控制器，并在仿真中实现高效、可扩展的集体探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群体机器人在搜索救援和环境监测等复杂动态场景中具有鲁棒、低成本优势，但如何数据驱动地设计能产生涌现集体行为的分散式控制器仍是核心难题；同时，实际部署要求控制器在资源受限平台上具备计算与能耗效率。

Method: 提出AI驱动的混合合成方法，将多智能体强化学习与神经进化策略结合，具体使用交叉熵方法（CEM）和协方差矩阵自适应进化策略（CMA-ES）优化预训练的个体导航策略；底层采用紧凑神经网络，仅依赖单目相机图像，面向低成本、资源受限平台，并在高保真物理仿真器中训练与评估。

Result: 实验表明控制器可实现多种室内环境中鲁棒且可扩展的集体探索；CEM训练的控制器探索覆盖优于CMA-ES，访问区域多36.20%；最佳视觉策略的探索性能与传统更昂贵距离传感器方法统计相当，同时平均能耗降低31.40%。

Conclusion: 研究验证了一种有效且经济可行的自主控制系统，为在真实工程应用中部署高效群体智能提供了路径。

Abstract: Swarm robotics presents a robust and cost-effective paradigm for advanced automation in complex, dynamic environments, such as those encountered in search and rescue or environmental monitoring. A fundamental challenge for this field is the data-driven design of decentralized controllers capable of generating emergent collective behaviors. This paper proposes a novel, AI-driven hybrid methodology for the automatic synthesis of swarm robotic controllers for autonomous visual navigation. This approach synergistically combines multi-agent reinforcement learning with neuro-evolutionary strategies, specifically leveraging implementations of the cross-entropy method and the covariance matrix adaptation evolution strategy to optimize a pre-trained individual navigation policy. The underlying deep architecture is engineered for low-cost, resource-constrained platforms, utilizing a compact neural network that relies exclusively on monocular camera imagery. This vision-based design emphasizes computational and energy efficiency, a critical requirement for practical swarm deployments. Experiments, performed in a high-fidelity physics simulator, demonstrate that the resulting controllers enable robust and scalable collective exploration of diverse indoor environments. The controller trained using our cross-entropy method achieves superior exploration coverage, visiting 36.20% more regions compared to the covariance matrix adaptation evolution strategy. Critically, our best vision-based policy achieves exploration performance statistically comparable to traditional methods relying on more expensive distance sensors, while delivering a significant 31.40% average reduction in energy consumption. These findings validate an effective and economically viable autonomous control system, establishing a path for deploying highly efficient collective intelligence in real-world engineering applications.

</details>


### [51] [Towards Robust Prehensile Manipulation in Open-Ended Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06376)
*Mathilde Kappel,Mahdi Khoramshahi,Louis Annabi,Faïz Ben Amar,Stéphane Doncieux*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用质量-多样性(QD)算法解决开放环境中的机器人抓取操作：先发现鲁棒抓取构型，再生成多样操作轨迹，以提升泛化与适应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在开放且持续演化的环境中，机器人抓取操作需要鲁棒且多样的行为来应对未知物体与任务变化，传统单一最优策略难以泛化。

Method: 提出基于QD算法的机器人抓取操作框架；通过QD高效发现广泛且鲁棒的抓取构型，并将其作为可靠初始点，为铰接物体生成多样的抓取操作轨迹。

Result: 方法能够高效发现大量鲁棒抓取构型，并由此产生多样的抓取操作轨迹；操作行为的多样性增强了泛化性和适应性，支持在持续演化的开放世界场景中有效部署。

Conclusion: QD算法可有效支持开放环境中的机器人抓取操作，且行为多样性是提升泛化与适应能力、实现开放世界持续部署的关键。

Abstract: We propose to use Quality-Diversity (QD) algorithms to solve robotic prehensile manipulation tasks in open-ended environments. Our approach enables the efficient discovery of a wide range robust grasp configurations, which serve as reliable starting points for generating diverse prehensile manipulation trajectories on articulated objects. The resulting diversity in manipulation behaviors enhances generalization and adaptability, enabling effective deployment continuously evolving open-world settings.

</details>


### [52] [DexForge: High-Fidelity Physics-Informed Dexterous Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06331)
*Meizhong Wang,Kun Cao,Ruiqi Ni,Lihua Xie,Yiguang Hong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DexForge 是一个基于可微物理的框架，可将人类视频演示转换为高保真机器人灵巧操作轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类演示能提供丰富的精细灵巧操作样本，是机器人训练数据的重要来源；但受物理约束与不同机器人本体差异影响，高保真复现演示动作及手-物交互仍很困难。

Method: 该方法从视觉观测中重建球形高斯物体模型与手-物运动，并构建结合高效高斯碰撞检测与现有可微动力学的可微模拟器；在此基础上，DexForge 结合接触感知运动学重定向与力感知动力学重定向，利用机器人适配后的稳定接触引导运动学参考构建，再通过基于梯度的控制精化实现精确物理运动复现。

Result: 在130个DexYCB和HOT3D演示、7种灵巧手上的实验显示，成功率较基线提升约35-53个百分点；成功轨迹上物体位置与姿态跟踪误差分别降低约34-67%和71-78%；进一步实验展示了向MuJoCo的开放环迁移与真实机器人执行。

Conclusion: DexForge 能有效缩小人类演示到机器人执行的差距，实现高保真、物理一致的机器人轨迹复现，并具备仿真到真机迁移潜力；项目页面已公开。

Abstract: Human demonstrations offer rich examples of precise dexterous manipulation and a promising source of robot training data. However, high-fidelity reproduction of demonstrated motions and hand-object interactions across robot embodiments remains challenging under physical constraints. We present DexForge, a differentiable physics-grounded framework for converting human video demonstrations into high-fidelity robot trajectories. We reconstruct spherical-Gaussian object models and hand-object motion from visual observations, then build a differentiable simulator combining efficient Gaussian collision detection with existing differentiable dynamics. Based on this simulator, DexForge combines contact-aware kinematic retargeting with force-aware dynamics retargeting: robot-adapted stable contacts guide kinematic reference construction and subsequent gradient-based control refinement for precise physical motion reproduction. Experiments on 130 DexYCB and HOT3D demonstrations across seven dexterous hands show success-rate gains of approximately 35-53 percentage points over the baseline, with object position and orientation tracking errors on successful trajectories reduced by approximately 34-67% and 71-78%, respectively. Further experiments demonstrate open-loop transfer to MuJoCo and real-robot execution. Our project page is available at this https URL

</details>


### [53] [Dual Variational Autoencoders for Efficient Sim-to-Real Transfer in Low-Cost Robotic Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06327)
*Álvaro Díez,Fidel Aznar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种融合域随机化与特征级域适应的混合迁移学习框架，通过双卷积VAE共享解码器对齐仿真与真实图像分布，实现低成本机器人视觉导航的sim-to-real迁移，真实室内导航分类成功率约91%，并可在树莓派4与Jetson Nano部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低成本机器人视觉自主导航受仿真到现实差距制约；仅仿真训练迁移效果差，仅真实数据训练不现实，需要高效桥接两域的方法。

Method: 采用域随机化与特征级域适应结合的混合迁移学习；构建双卷积变分自编码器并共享解码器，在45225张仿真图像和4556个真实样本上训练，学习对齐两域分布的紧凑公共隐空间；并使用两种互补数据增强扩充有限真实数据。

Result: 在真实室内导航图像分类任务中平均成功率达近91%，显著优于仅仿真训练和仅真实数据训练；通过真实部署验证，策略可引导低成本机器人完成反应式探索任务；计算评估确认其适用于Raspberry Pi 4和NVIDIA Jetson Nano等资源受限嵌入式平台。

Conclusion: 该工作为低成本机器人系统提供了实用、有效且高效的导航策略开发方案，有效缓解仿真到现实的迁移差距。

Abstract: Vision-based autonomous navigation for low-cost robots remains a fundamental challenge, primarily due to the significant gap between simulated training environments and real-world operational conditions. Direct policy transfer from simulation is often ineffective, while training exclusively on real data is impractical. We propose a hybrid transfer learning framework that effectively bridges the sim-to-real gap by combining domain randomization with feature-level domain adaptation. Our method employs a dual convolutional variational autoencoder architecture with a shared decoder, trained on an extensive set of 45225 simulated images and a minimal set of only 4556 real-world samples. This architecture learns a compact, common latent representation space that aligns the distributions of both domains. The adaptation process is further enhanced by two complementary data augmentation techniques designed to expand the limited real-world data. Experimental evaluation demonstrates that our method achieves an average success rate of almost 91% on image classification tasks for real-world indoor navigation, significantly outperforming both simulation-only and real-world-only training. We validate these findings through a direct, real-world deployment, where the proposed policy successfully guides a low-cost robot in a reactive exploration task. Furthermore, we validate the model's efficiency through a rigorous computational estimation, confirming its suitability for resource-constrained embedded platforms such as the Raspberry Pi 4 and NVIDIA Jetson Nano. This work presents a practical solution for developing effective and efficient navigation policies for low-cost robotic systems.

</details>


### [54] [Inspect Robots: Evaluating the Capabilities and Safety of Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06306)
*Christopher Leet,Achu Menon,Sravanthi Machcha,Sabrina Zou,Aayushya Patel,Aditya Kumar Singh,Anish Kr Singh,Galaba Vamsi,Javin Ahuja,Sai Asish Yamani,Tushar Anand,Vedang Alle,Zihan Jack Zhang,Tzu Kit Chan,Jay Chooi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 我们推出了 Inspect Robots，一个用于评估具身智能体的模块化开源框架，并展示了其评估基于前沿语言模型的六种策略的能力与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用语言模型越来越多地能够控制机器人硬件，因此理解这些模型在具身化时的能力和安全性对于理解其社会影响和风险至关重要。

Method: 我们引入了 Inspect Robots，这是一个模块化、开源的框架，用于开发和运行具身智能体的评估。它提供了可定制、可重用的抽象来指定物理评估并分析结果，并配有自动化评估设置、执行和终止的基础设施。

Result: 我们使用 Inspect Robots 评估了六种基于前沿语言模型的策略的能力和安全性。该框架在发布后三个月内获得了近10万次下载，显示出显著的早期采用。

Conclusion: Inspect Robots 为具身智能体的评估提供了有效工具，并已被广泛采用，有助于理解和应对具身语言模型的社会影响和风险。

Abstract: General purpose language models are increasingly able to control robotic hardware. Understanding the capabilities and safety of these models when embodied is therefore increasingly important for understanding their societal impact and risks. To this end, we introduce Inspect Robots, a modular, open-source framework for developing and running evaluations of embodied agents. Inspect Robots pairs customizable, reusable abstractions for specifying physical evaluations and analyzing their results with infrastructure that automates evaluation setup, execution and termination. We demonstrate Inspect Robots by using it to evaluate the capabilities and safety of six policies based on frontier language models. Inspect Robots has seen significant early uptake, receiving nearly 100,000 downloads in the three months since its release.

</details>


### [55] [GAMBIT: Learning to Plan Continuous Multi-Robot Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06290)
*Rishabh Jain,Akmaral Moldagalieva,Lorenzo Magnino,Michael Amir,Keisuke Okumura,Ajay Shankar,Wolfgang Hönig,Amanda Prorok*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: GAMBIT 将多机器人协调类比为国际象棋中的弃子策略，通过模仿学习与强化学习学习运动基元选择，并利用安全回退轨迹保证无碰撞，实现大规模高效协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人协调中，个体机器人常需放弃局部奖励最大化行为以提升团队整体性能；这类自我牺牲行为难以用人工启发式规则刻画，尤其在密集且交互丰富的环境中。

Method: 针对双积分连续动力学，GAMBIT 先通过模仿学习学习协同运动基元选择，再用强化学习微调策略；同时引入带备份轨迹的安全 rollout 机制，确保执行全过程无碰撞。

Result: 实验表明，GAMBIT 显著优于集中式运动规划器和分散式反应式规划器等一系列基线，并具有强可扩展性；可在连续域中协调超过一千个机器人，规划延迟低于几百毫秒。

Conclusion: GAMBIT 证明结合学习式协同启发式与安全回退机制，能有效解决大规模多机器人轨迹执行中需局部牺牲以换取全局性能的协调问题。

Abstract: GAMBIT is an opening chess move in which a player sacrifices a piece, typically a pawn, to gain a positional advantage later in the game. Analogously, in multi-robot coordination, individual robots may need to forgo locally reward-maximising behaviours to improve overall team performance. Such self-sacrificial behaviours are difficult to capture with manually designed heuristics, particularly in dense, interaction-rich environments. Focusing on double-integrator continuous dynamics, this work studies how to learn such coordinated heuristics over motion primitives for multi-robot trajectory execution. Our framework, GAMBIT, first learns coordinated motion-primitive selection through imitation learning and subsequently fine-tunes the policy through reinforcement learning. We further introduce a safeguarded rollout mechanism with backup trajectories that guarantees collision-free execution at all times. Experiments demonstrate that GAMBIT substantially outperforms a range of baselines, including centralised motion planners and decentralised reactive planners, while exhibiting strong scalability. In particular, it coordinates over a thousand robots with planning latency below a few hundred milliseconds in continuous domains.

</details>


### [56] [Future Anchored Verification and Online Recovery for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06280)
*Zhibin Qin,Zhenxiong Tan,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FAVOR：利用WAM在执行前预测的未来帧作为锚点，进行在线验证与恢复，在不修改策略的前提下提升机器人操作任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型（WAM）先预测未来再解码动作，但执行偏离预测后剩余动作会失效；从已分布外状态重规划难以恢复任务，现有执行监控只能决定何时停止，不能决定恢复什么。

Method: FAVOR保留WAM预测的未来帧作为锚点。Anchor Verifier将观测、锚点与已执行动作比较，检测破坏任务的偏差；Anchor-Guided Recovery用视觉语言模型将异常锚点转为简短纠正指令，在增强指令引导下由WAM执行以回到预期未来，再继续任务。

Result: 在LIBERO上，基础WAM任务成功率从97.85%提升至98.10%；在LIBERO-Plus上从72.60%提升至72.98%，且未修改策略。

Conclusion: FAVOR是一种轻量、即插即用的验证与恢复框架，表明WAM预测的未来可直接作为锚点用于在线纠错，从而提升执行鲁棒性。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) have emerged as a promising paradigm for robotic manipulation. They act by first predicting how a task should be performed and then decoding the actions from that future. However, the remaining actions are invalid once execution drifts from the prediction. Simply replanning from the already out of distribution state rarely restores what the task still requires; existing execution monitors decide when to stop, but not what to restore. We observe that the answer is already in hand: the future the WAM predicted before acting depicts exactly the states it intended to pass through. We introduce FAVOR (Future Anchored Verification and Online Recovery), a lightweight framework that keeps these predicted frames as anchors and uses them for verification and recovery. An Anchor Verifier compares each observation with its anchor, together with the executed actions, to flag deviations that break the task. Anchor-Guided Recovery uses a vision-language model to turn the flagged anchor into a short corrective instruction. Under strengthened instruction guidance, the WAM executes this instruction to return to the intended future. It then resumes the task. FAVOR raises the task success of the base WAM from 97.85% to 98.10% on LIBERO and from 72.60% to 72.98% on LIBERO-Plus without modifying the policy.

</details>


### [57] [VLA-ZO: Fast Zeroth-Order Adaptation for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06271)
*Jaemin Kim,Jiahn Kim,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA-ZO 通过冻结视觉-语言前缀并复用动作侧条件状态，实现快速零阶适应，在 LIBERO 相机视角偏移下大幅缩短适应时间并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型适应到部署时分布偏移对可靠机器人操作很重要，但传统一阶适应可能超出面向推理的部署平台内存预算。零阶（ZO）优化仅需前向计算且内存开销接近推理，但准确梯度估计需要大量扰动查询，导致朴素 ZO 对大型 VLA 模型过慢。

Method: 提出 VLA-ZO 框架，利用 VLA 计算结构加速零阶适应：将适应限制在动作侧，冻结昂贵的视觉-语言前缀，并在不同扰动查询与优化器步骤间复用其条件状态；同时采用调度感知预取隐藏状态传输开销。

Result: 在 LIBERO 相机视角偏移上，VLA-ZO 在 q=16 时将端到端适应时间相对基线 ZO 减少 25.59 倍，在 q=64 时减少 32.54 倍；平均任务成功率从无适应的 48.27% 分别提升至 58.17% 和 63.58%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，加速零阶优化可使更大的查询预算变得可行，为在部署平台上实现资源高效的 VLA 适应提供了有前景的路径。

Abstract: Adapting vision-language-action (VLA) models to deployment-time distribution shifts is important for reliable robotic operation, but conventional first-order adaptation can exceed the memory budget of inference-oriented deployment platforms. Zeroth-order (ZO) optimization offers a forward-only alternative with inference-level memory, but accurate gradient estimation requires many perturbation queries, making naive ZO prohibitively slow for large VLA models. We present VLA-ZO, a framework for fast ZO adaptation that exploits the structure of VLA computation. By confining adaptation to the action side, VLA-ZO keeps the expensive vision-language prefix frozen and reuses its conditioning states across perturbation queries and optimizer steps, while schedule-aware prefetching hides state-transfer overhead. On LIBERO camera-viewpoint shifts, VLA-ZO reduces end-to-end adaptation time by 25.59$\times$ at $q=16$ and 32.54$\times$ at $q=64$ relative to baseline ZO, while improving average task success from 48.27% without adaptation to 58.17% and 63.58%, respectively. These results show that making ZO faster can make larger query budgets practical, providing a promising path toward resource-efficient VLA adaptation on deployment platforms.

</details>


### [58] [Encoded but Not in Control: Revealing the Grounding Gap in Vision-Language Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06235)
*Shaohan Jiang,Jiahang Cao,Qiduo He,Fengting Deng,Kun Wu,Jingkai Sun,Jiaxu Wang,Qiang Zhang,Qihao Zheng,Chunfeng Song,Ping Luo,Andrew F. Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文揭示语言条件机器人策略的“指令跟随”可能只是场景偏置：任务成功不等于遵循指令，并提出场景保持干预的诊断框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言应决定同一场景下多个有效动作中的选择，但成功执行无法区分策略是遵循指令，还是仅根据场景推断任务；需研究该歧义。

Method: 采用场景保持的指令干预，包括有效目标替换、任意名词和无关句子，同时固定场景；在仿真与真实实验中评估视觉-语言-动作策略（VLA）和世界动作模型（WAM），并用逐层动作透镜、线性探针、注意力分析、UMAP和共享非负矩阵分解进行分析。

Result: (a) 当指令要求不同可见物体时，所有评估策略仍主要接近并抓取场景原目标；(b) 指令扰动会影响任务表现，中间动作预测也响应扰动，线性探针能准确恢复指令目标，说明修改后的语言已被编码，但很少决定目标选择；(c) 指令token对动作生成贡献弱，目标token注意力仍聚焦原物体，目标编码与视觉接地不匹配，表征越来越按场景身份组织，但目标信息仍可访问。

Conclusion: 研究发现名义成功掩盖了接地差距，并提供诊断框架；提出明确进展标准：策略应可靠跟随用户意图的有效变化，即使其与场景偏好行为冲突。

Abstract: Instruction following is central to language-conditioned robot policies: language should determine what to do when the same scene permits multiple valid actions. Yet successful execution alone cannot establish whether a policy follows the instruction or infers the task from the scene. We study this ambiguity through scene-preserving instruction interventions, using valid target substitutions, arbitrary nouns, and unrelated sentences while holding the scene fixed. We evaluate vision-language-action (VLA) policies and world-action models (WAMs) in simulation and in real-world experiments. Our analysis addresses three questions: (a) Does task success imply instruction following? When instructions request a different visible object, all evaluated policies predominantly approach and pick up the incorrect original target associated with the scene. (b) Is this failure caused by language insensitivity? Instruction perturbations affect task performance. A layerwise action lens shows intermediate action predictions respond to these perturbations. Linear probes accurately recover instructed targets, indicating modified instructions are encoded despite rarely determining target selection. (c) Why does encoded language fail to control action? Attention analysis indicates weak instruction-token contributions to action generation. Target-token attention can remain focused on the original object, revealing a mismatch between target encoding and visual grounding. UMAP and shared non-negative matrix factorization show target information remains accessible within representations increasingly organized by scene identity. Our findings expose a grounding gap concealed by nominal success and provide a diagnostic framework. They further establish a concrete criterion for progress: policies should reliably follow valid changes in user intent, even when they conflict with scene-favored behavior.

</details>


### [59] [AUTOPILOT An Advanced Perception, Localization and Path Planning Techniques for Autonomous Vehicles Using YOLOv7 and MiDaS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06232)
*Harshkumar Devmurari,Gautham Kuckian,Prajjwal Vishwakarma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文实现并评估了一种结合YOLO目标检测、MiDaS深度感知、透视变换与决策路径规划的自动驾驶系统，仿真和实验表明YOLO与MiDaS的组合能提供鲁棒的目标检测与深度感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶技术的发展需要在感知、定位、决策和控制等多个领域取得显著进展；本文旨在通过融合目标检测、深度感知、定位与路径规划模块，提升自动驾驶车辆对环境中障碍物的感知与定位能力。

Method: 基于项目实现，将YOLO用于目标检测，MiDaS用于深度感知及障碍物定位与环境感知，结合透视变换和决策模块进行路径规划，并通过仿真与实验评估该组合系统的性能。

Result: 仿真和实验结果显示，YOLO与MiDaS的组合为对象检测和深度感知提供了一种新的鲁棒系统，能够支持自动驾驶中的障碍物感知与定位。

Conclusion: 该研究推动了自动驾驶技术的进步，并为环境中障碍物的感知与定位提供了新的创新性方法。

Abstract: Self driving vehicles have emerged as a reliable technology that has the capability to transform transportation and mobility. The development of self driving cars requires significant advances in a number of areas, including perception, localization, decision making, and control. This research paper is based on the project implementation of the combination of object detection using YOLO (You Only Look Once), depth sensing using MiDaS for the localization and perception of obstacles, perspective transform, and decision making for path planning in self driving cars. The contemporary state of the technology for object detection, depth sensing, localization, and path planning evaluates the performance of the combined system through simulations and experiments. The results show that the combination of YOLO and MiDaS provides a new robust system for object detection and depth sensing. This research paper contributes to the advancement of self driving car technology and provides new and innovative approaches to the perception and localization of obstacles in the environment. Keywords: YOLO, MiDaS, perception, localization, decision making

</details>


### [60] [Towards Quadruped-Provided Localization and Active Tracking for Micro-UAVs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06215)
*Alejandro Lorite Mora,Andrés Faíña*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出将微型无人机的定位任务从空中平台卸载到带7自由度机械臂的四足机器人上：末端相机识别无人机上的AprilTag，并结合四足机器人自定位，为无人机提供共享地图中的6-DOF外部位姿，从而实现微无人机自主飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型无人机可进入大型机器人无法到达的狭小空间，但尺寸和载荷过小，无法搭载自主飞行所需的感知与计算资源。

Method: 四足机器人搭载7自由度机械臂，臂端相机检测无人机上的AprilTag基准标记，并将观测与四足机器人自身定位融合，把无人机定位到共享地图坐标系中；机械臂作为主动观测器，随两机器人运动调整位置以保持无人机在视野内；无人机仅携带IMU，并融合外部位姿来跟踪指令设定点。

Result: 实验室飞行中外部位姿精度为12–16 mm，足以支持无人机自主飞行，其控制效果与动捕提供位姿时相差2–5 cm；四足机器人主动跟随无人机后，通过将相机保持在标记精度最高的近距离范围，将跟踪误差从11.0 cm降低到6.9 cm。

Conclusion: 地面机器人可为微型无人机提供高精度外部定位并主动跟随，从而以极低机载载荷实现受限空间内的自主飞行。

Abstract: Micro unmanned aerial vehicles (micro-UAVs) are small enough to reach confined spaces that larger robots cannot access, but too small to carry the sensing and computing power required for autonomous flight. We move the localization stack entirely off the aerial platform onto a quadruped robot with a 7-degree-of-freedom (DOF) arm, which supplies the micro-UAV (27 g bare, 42 g with fiducial markers) its full 6-DOF pose. A camera at the arm's end-effector detects AprilTag fiducial markers on the drone and composes that observation with the quadruped's own self-localization to place the drone in a shared map frame, so the ground robot localizes its partner, rather than only tracking it relative to the camera. The arm acts as an actively-controlled observer, repositioning to keep the drone in view as both robots move; the drone carries only an inertial measurement unit and fuses the external pose to fly commanded setpoints. In lab flights the external pose is accurate to 12-16 mm, enough to fly the drone autonomously within 2-5 cm of motion-capture-fed control. Having the quadruped actively follow the drone reduces the tracking error from 11.0 cm to 6.9 cm by holding the camera in the close range, where the markers are most accurate.

</details>


### [61] [STC-MPM: Coupled Deformation, Progressive Damage, and Cut Formation in Soft-Tissue Cutting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06198)
*Kewei Zuo,Jiahe Chen,Ziao Kuang,Junsheng Zhu,Etsuko Kobayashi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出STC-MPM框架，将软组织切割中的有限应变变形、历史驱动连续损伤与可配置失效后处理耦合，无需预置切割界面即可联合分析切口形成与组织响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人自动化中的切割常面对高顺应性软组织：刀片运动可能导致材料变形或位移，而非直接推进切口；渐进失效还会改变载荷传递及后续刀具-组织相互作用。因此，计算描述需将切口形成与演化中的力学响应联系起来，而不能独立于材料失效预先指定切口。

Method: STC-MPM耦合有限应变变形、历史驱动的连续损伤模型和可配置的失效后处理策略。损伤仅在刀片过程区内拉伸应变历史超过材料阈值时触发；渐进退化先改变应力传递，再应用失效后策略，同时剩余组织继续变形、移动并与刀具相互作用。采用延迟粒子失活的数值手术刀研究追踪插入与拔出过程。

Result: 在相同刀片运动下，降低损伤速率上限不改变首次记录损伤时间，但会延迟并提高峰值反作用力，延迟粒子失活，并增大最大插入时的固定队列位移统计量。匹配对比表明，损伤起始后的失效演化会改变后续刀具载荷与记录到的材料运动。

Conclusion: STC-MPM支持在无预插入切割界面的情况下，对切口形成及伴随的组织响应进行联合分析。

Abstract: Cutting is a recurring operation in robotic au tomation, from food preparation to surgical tissue resection. For highly compliant targets, blade motion may deform or displace the material rather than advance the intended cut, while progressive failure changes load transfer and subsequent tool tissue interaction. A computational description must therefore connect cut formation to the evolving mechanical response, rather than specifying the incision independently of material failure. We present STC-MPM (Soft Tissue Cutting with the Material Point Method), a framework that couples finite-strain deformation, history-driven continuum damage, and configurable post-failure treatment. Damage initiates only when a tensile strain history exceeds a material threshold within the blade process zone. Progressive degradation changes stress transmission before the post-failure policy is applied, while the remaining tissue continues to deform, move, and interact with the tool. Numerical scalpel studies using delayed particle deactivation follow this response through insertion and withdrawal. Under identical blade motion, reducing the damage-rate limit leaves the first recorded damage time unchanged but delays and increases peak reaction force, delays particle deactivation, and increases the fixed-cohort displacement statistic at maximum insertion. The matched comparison illustrates how post-initiation failure evolution alters subsequent tool loading and recorded material motion. STC-MPM thus supports joint analysis of cut formation and accompanying tissue response without a pre-inserted cutting interface.

</details>


### [62] [Arm-wise Compositional Generalization in Dual-Arm Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06184)
*Zaibin Zhang,Binghao Ran,Yuhan Wu,Zhongbo Zhang,Yifan Wang,Junwei Jiang,Junlan Xiao,Wangcheng Shi,Li Kang,Yiran Qin,Zhenfei Yin,Lijun Wang,Huchuan Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 ACG-Bench 双机械臂组合泛化基准与 AE-VLA 策略，通过 SkillLoRA 和臂间注意力等设计提升未见技能重组条件下的泛化成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多臂协作中的泛化可视为以新方式组合已熟悉的原子技能；但现有评测难以揭示哪些训练与架构选择能在不同协调要求下支持这种能力，因此需要一个统一测试平台。

Method: 构建 ACG-Bench，包含 8 个任务族、23 个任务-条件对，其中 6 个域内条件、17 个未见组合，覆盖重排序、同步、二者组合及跨任务组合；所有方法使用相同的单臂原子提示，成功需同时满足任务目标、物理里程碑以及指定顺序或时序约束。以 π_0.5 为统一 VLA 骨干，在共享源数据和评测协议下比较数据增强与架构策略，重点研究臂 token 分组、技能特定 LoRA 适配器（SkillLoRA）和臂间注意力（AWA），并组合为 AE-VLA。

Result: 仿真中 AE-VLA 泛化成功率为 21.53%，高于 Single π_0.5 的 2.94%、MA-VLA 的 3.06% 和两个独立控制 π_0.5 策略的 5.53%。在物理 SO101 机器人上，AE-VLA 在五个未见条件下平均成功率为 39.00%，最强基线为 10.00%。

Conclusion: 结果表明技能条件化参数与注意力结构具有互补性，并为设计能超越固定训练流程、实现双机械臂组合泛化的策略提供了实证指导。

Abstract: Generalization in multi-arm collaboration can be studied as composing familiar atomic skills in new ways across arms. However, existing evaluations offer limited insight into which training and architectural choices support this ability under different coordination requirements. We introduce \textbf{ACG-Bench}, a benchmark for \emph{Arm-wise Compositional Generalization} that provides a common testbed for studying skill recomposition in dual-arm policies. It contains 23 task--condition pairs across 8 task families, with 6 in-domain conditions and 17 unseen compositions covering reordering, synchronization, their combination, and cross-task composition. All methods receive the same per-arm atomic prompts, and success requires achieving the task goal while satisfying physical milestones and specified order or timing constraints. Using $\pi_{0.5}$ as a common vision-language-action backbone, we compare representative data-augmentation and architectural strategies with shared source data and a common evaluation protocol. Our architectural study examines arm-token grouping, skill-specific LoRA adapters (SkillLoRA), and arm-wise attention (AWA), highlighting the complementarity of skill-conditioned parameters and attention structure. Combining these choices yields \textbf{AE-VLA}, which achieves 21.53\% generalization success in simulation, compared with 2.94\% for Single $\pi_{0.5}$, 3.06\% for MA-VLA, and 5.53\% for two independently controlled $\pi_{0.5}$ policies. On physical SO101 robots, AE-VLA reaches 39.00\% mean success across five unseen conditions, compared with 10.00\% for the strongest baseline. These findings provide empirical guidance for designing dual-arm policies that generalize beyond fixed training routines.

</details>


### [63] [Controllable and Photorealistic Pedestrian Risky Motion Generation for End-to-End Driving Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06171)
*Siyuan Liu,Miao Li,Haibao Yu,Haohong Lin,Qing Zhou,Bingbing Nie,Ding Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ControlPed框架，将轨迹级冲突合成与3D高斯泼溅结合，生成真实感、运动可控的车辆-行人安全关键场景，用于端到端自动驾驶安全评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于轨迹的场景生成器无法合成原始视觉观测，而基于视频的方法缺乏可控性，难以在罕见、安全关键的车辆-行人交互中开展传感器级端到端评估。

Method: 基于HazardPed数据集（包含10,352段交通视频、422条冲突轨迹、HD地图和857条标注3D人体运动），ControlPed首先生成冲突轨迹，再通过文本条件运动扩散将其提升为3D人体运动序列，最后利用可动画3DGS化身渲染多视角传感器观测。

Result: 在88个渲染的真实感场景中，七个领先端到端驾驶模型性能显著下降，平均HDScore从88.8骤降至47.4，暴露出危险行人行为下的主要失效模式。

Conclusion: ControlPed弥合了轨迹级可控性与视频级真实感之间的鸿沟，并将发布数据集与测试基准，以促进车辆-行人交互安全评估。

Abstract: Evaluating end-to-end autonomous driving under rare, safety-critical vehicle-pedestrian interactions requires photorealistic, sensor-level scenarios. However, trajectory-based scenario generators cannot synthesize raw visual observations, whereas video-based approaches lack controllability. To bridge this gap, we present ControlPed, a novel framework that combines trajectory-level conflict synthesis with 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to generate photorealistic, motion-controllable safety-critical scenarios. Built upon HazardPed, a dataset derived from 10,352 traffic videos comprising 422 conflict trajectories, HD maps, and 857 annotated 3D human motions, ControlPed first generates conflict trajectories, lifts them into 3D human motion sequences via text-conditioned motion diffusion, and finally renders multi-view sensor observations using animatable 3DGS avatars. Safety evaluation in 88 rendered photorealistic scenarios reveals that seven leading end-to-end driving models suffer a severe performance drop, with their mean HDScore plunging from 88.8 to 47.4, exposing major failure modes under dangerous pedestrian behaviors. The dataset and testing benchmarks will be released to facilitate safety assessment of vehicle-pedestrian interactions.

</details>


### [64] [Talk, Render, Act: Integrating Social Gesture and Digital Face with Synchronized Speech for Conversational Humanoid Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06153)
*Jin Jiang,Kun Li,Jiancong Ma,Shengcai Liao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TRABot 是一个面向人形机器人全身交互的智能体框架，统一生成语音、面部动画和身体手势，并在实体 G1 机器人上实现实时流式多模态交互。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富有表现力的人形交互需要语音、面部动画和身体手势协同一致；但许多全身人形机器人只有语音和手势而缺乏有表现力的面部，且说话人脸动画与机器人手势生成通常分别独立开发。

Method: 提出 TRABot，包含运动原子构建、对话生成、运动规划和面部动画等专用智能体。首先，将 G1 重定向后的长时 BEAT2 运动切分为具有自然手势边界、经人工验证的交际功能和可行轨迹的 Robot-Ready Semantic Motion Atoms。其次，提出 Semantic-Conditioned Compositional Planner，在给定有序语义功能序列和估计回复时长后，选择已批准的运动原子，在考虑过渡和中性恢复的前提下生成最长可行动作序列。最后，在实体 G1 人形机器人上部署 Streaming Face-Speech-Body Integration 系统，将流式对话音频、音频驱动面部动画和语义规划身体运动整合到统一的实时交互循环中。

Result: 定量和定性实验表明，在自然度、表现力和多模态一致性方面，TRABot 在所有比较条件中取得最佳整体性能。

Conclusion: TRABot 通过统一整合语音、面部动画和语义规划的身体手势，为实现自然、富有表现力且多模态连贯的人形机器人交互提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Expressive humanoid interaction requires speech, facial animation, and body gestures to form a coherent response. However, many full-body humanoid robots produce speech and gestures without a visually expressive face, while talking-face animation and robot gesture generation are typically developed separately. We present Talk, Render, Act (TRABot), an agent-based framework comprising specialized agents for motion-atom construction, dialogue generation, motion planning, and facial animation. First, to produce natural and semantically meaningful gestures, we construct Robot-Ready Semantic Motion Atoms by segmenting long-form, G1-retargeted BEAT2 motion into units with natural gesture boundaries, human-verified communicative functions, and feasible trajectories. Second, to preserve semantic order and coordinate body motion with the spoken response, we introduce a Semantic-Conditioned Compositional Planner. Given an ordered semantic function sequence and an estimated response duration, the planner selects approved atoms to realize the longest feasible action sequence while accounting for transitions and neutral recovery. Finally, we deploy a Streaming Face-Speech-Body Integration system on a physical G1 humanoid, combining streaming dialogue audio, audio-driven facial animation, and semantically planned body motion in a unified real-time interaction loop. Quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that TRAbot achieves the best overall performance among all compared conditions in terms of naturalness, expressiveness, and multimodal coherence.

</details>


### [65] [Robotizing Human Videos with Physically Consistent Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06137)
*Ching-Lam Cheng,Shengfeng He,Bin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作通过交互几何重建与深度感知合成，将人类操作视频转化为机器人兼容的抓取视频，并与机器人演示协同训练，在RoboTwin任务和真实部署中提升成功率与鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类视频可提供规模化操作数据，但人手与机器人夹爪之间的具身差异阻碍直接利用。现有视频编辑方法用渲染机器人替换人手，却因交互重建与合成不准确，导致抓取不一致和机器人-物体遮挡不合理。

Method: 从交互几何与场景可见性两方面解决：1）交互感知接触重建模块结合手-物分割与网格级接触预测，恢复稠密3D接触，并转换为时序稳定的平行夹爪抓取；2）深度感知合成模块利用场景与机器人深度，强制物理一致的机器人-物体遮挡。生成视频保留人类演示的交互结构并适配机器人，再与机器人演示协同训练。

Result: 在相同人类视频与机器人数据下，与仅机器人训练和原始Masquerade流程比较，方法在四个RoboTwin任务和两种Diffusion Policy视觉编码器上取得最高平均成功率，且在分布外场景变化下增益尤其显著。真实部署表明，当任务几何可观测时，协同训练提升了对视觉干扰的鲁棒性；但深度敏感抓取仍受单相机设置限制。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模交互几何与场景可见性，可更有效地将人类视频转化为机器人可用操作数据，并借协同训练提升泛化与鲁棒性；但单相机设置下深度敏感抓取仍是局限，未来需改进深度感知或多视角设置。

Abstract: Human videos offer scalable manipulation data, but the embodiment gap between human hands and robot manipulators limits their direct use. Existing video-editing methods replace hands with rendered robots, yet inaccurate interaction reconstruction and compositing can produce inconsistent grasps and implausible robot-object occlusions. We address these failures from two complementary physical aspects: interaction geometry and scene visibility. First, an interaction-aware contact reconstruction module combines hand-object segmentation with mesh-level contact prediction to recover dense 3D contacts, then converts them into temporally stabilized grasps for parallel-jaw grippers. Second, a depth-aware compositing module uses scene and robot depth to enforce physically consistent robot-object occlusions. The resulting videos preserve the interaction structure of human demonstrations in a robot-compatible form and are co-trained with robot demonstrations. Using identical human videos and robot data, we compare against robot-only training and the original Masquerade pipeline. Across four RoboTwin tasks and two Diffusion Policy visual encoders, our method achieves the highest average success rates, with especially strong gains under out-of-distribution scene variation. Real-world deployment further shows that the proposed co-training approach improves robustness to visual distractors when the task geometry is observable, while performance on depth-sensitive grasps remains limited by the single-camera setup.

</details>


### [66] [I-BFM: Reward-Conditioned Robust Humanoid Interaction via Unsupervised Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06129)
*Ziqi Han,Yitang Li,Junhan Sun,Fanrong Dong,Yaojie Shen,Lei Ye,Zetong Jing,Yongqi Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Xue Wang,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出首个面向人形机器人-物体交互的行为基础模型 I-BFM，通过无监督强化学习与前后向表示学习人-物-接触耦合动力学，使单一策略无需任务特定优化即可执行多种全身操作并具备强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为基础模型已能实现多样全身控制，但将这种通用性扩展到物理交互仍很困难；现有方法多依赖任务特定策略或参考轨迹跟踪，难以在交互失败、外部扰动和长时程任务中保持泛化与鲁棒。

Method: I-BFM 使用前后向表示与无监督强化学习，学习人形机器人、物体及其接触之间耦合动力学的共享表示；给定下游任务奖励，同一策略可直接以潜在命令为条件执行闭环交互，而无需任务特定策略优化。为适应不同时间尺度，训练同时使用短时程交互目标和长时程目标目标。

Result: 单一 I-BFM 策略可完成搬运、推动和踢击，并支持目标到达、运动跟踪、风格控制与长时程任务链。在 Carry 任务中，名义成功率 94.3%，机器人跌倒后仍保持 89.3% 成功率，而基于规划的基线仅 1.3%。在 Unitree G1 真机实验中，展示了多样移动操作、交互失败与外部扰动后的快速恢复，以及无需任务特定重训的任务链能力。

Conclusion: I-BFM 将行为基础模型扩展到人形机器人-物体交互，证明了通过学习耦合动力学共享表示与多时间尺度目标，可用单一策略实现通用、鲁棒且可迁移的闭环物理交互，并显著优于传统规划基线。

Abstract: Behavioral foundation models (BFMs) have recently shown that a single humanoid policy can support diverse whole-body control, but extending such generality to physical interaction remains challenging. We introduce I-BFM, to our knowledge the first BFM for humanoid-object interaction. Rather than relying on task-specific policies or reference tracking, I-BFM learns a shared representation of the coupled dynamics among the humanoid, objects, and their contacts using forward-backward representations and unsupervised reinforcement learning. Given a downstream task reward, the same policy can be directly conditioned on a latent command to execute closed-loop interaction without task-specific policy optimization. To improve interaction control over different time scales, we further train the policy with both short-horizon interaction targets and longer-horizon goal targets. A single I-BFM policy performs carrying, pushing, and kicking, while also supporting goal reaching, motion tracking, stylistic control, and long-horizon task chaining. More importantly, it remains effective after large deviations from nominal execution: on Carry, I-BFM achieves 94.3% nominal success and retains 89.3% success after robot falls, compared with 1.3% for a planning-based baseline. Real-world experiments on a Unitree G1 further demonstrate diverse loco-manipulation behaviors, rapid recovery from interaction failures and external disturbances, and task chaining without task-specific retraining.

</details>


### [67] [Radar2Plan: Benchmarking 4D Radar for End-to-End Open-Loop Ego-Trajectory Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06121)
*Ling Yao,Yichun Xiao,Jin Jin,Yihan Zhang,Fangqiang Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Radar2Plan 是首个基于真实 4D 雷达数据、面向自动驾驶开环自车轨迹规划的模块化基准，统一评估相机、4D 雷达与 LiDAR 在多种规划器及恶劣天气/光照条件下的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恶劣天气和低光照是移动自主中鲁棒自车轨迹规划的主要挑战；4D 雷达虽能在恶劣条件下可靠感知并提供径向速度，但感知鲁棒性未必转化为下游规划鲁棒性，且现有 4D 雷达基准多聚焦感知而非轨迹规划。

Method: 提出模块化基准 Radar2Plan，通过通用接口连接传感器编码器、场景表示与规划头，支持不同传感器与规划器配置的受控比较；使用 DSERT-RoLL 和 MAN TruckScenes 数据集，在统一协议下评估 7 种相机、4D 雷达、LiDAR 组合、4 种代表性规划基线及 12 种天气与光照条件。

Result: 实验表明，仅使用 4D 雷达即可支持有竞争力的自车轨迹规划，并在恶劣条件下保持鲁棒性能；传感器配置比较显示 4D 雷达对其他模态具有互补价值，同时揭示规划性能依赖传感器组合与规划架构。

Conclusion: Radar2Plan 是首个专用于评估真实 4D 雷达用于自动驾驶规划的基准；其模块化设计可扩展至更多数据集、传感模态和规划器，为基于雷达的自动驾驶规划研究提供灵活基础。

Abstract: Adverse weather and poor illumination remain major challenges for robust ego-trajectory planning in mobile autonomy. 4D radar offers reliable sensing under adverse conditions and direct radial-velocity measurements. However, sensing robustness does not necessarily translate into robust downstream planning, while existing 4D radar benchmarks focus primarily on perception rather than trajectory planning. We present Radar2Plan, a modular benchmark for open-loop ego-trajectory planning using real-world 4D radar data. Radar2Plan connects sensor encoders, scene representations, and planning heads through common interfaces, enabling controlled comparisons between different sensor and planner configurations. Using DSERT-RoLL and MAN TruckScenes, we evaluated seven combinations of camera, 4D radar, and LiDAR in four representative planning baselines and 12 weather and illumination conditions under a unified protocol. To our knowledge, Radar2Plan is the first benchmark dedicated to evaluating real-world 4D radars for autonomous driving planning. Experiments show that 4D radar alone supports competitive ego-trajectory planning and robust performance under adverse conditions. Sensor-configuration comparisons further demonstrate its complementary value to other modalities, while revealing dependencies on sensor combination and planning architecture. The modular design also supports additional datasets, sensing modalities, and planners, providing a flexible foundation for future research on radar-based autonomous driving planning.

</details>


### [68] [Conditional Trajectory Peaks: Single-Pass Multimodal Policies over Action Chunks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06104)
*Di Wu,Rongtian Shen,Ping Liu,Xuhua Chen,He Zheng,Lingfeng Zhang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Conditional Trajectory Peaks (CTP) 单次前向策略框架，联合预测完整动作块候选、概率质量和轨迹尺度，并通过 DAPS 与 ETBT 兼顾多模态行为、重规划一致性和高效推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态模仿学习要求在同一观测下生成多样且可执行的未来轨迹，并在重规划周期中保持行为一致；现有方法难以同时满足多模态建模、闭环一致性与低延迟推理。

Method: CTP 单次预测完整动作块候选、概率质量与轨迹尺度；Distribution-Aware Peak Specialization (DAPS) 使用轨迹级后验责任以及质量和尺度调制的重叠约束来专门化轨迹峰；Evidence-Gated Trajectory Belief Transport (ETBT) 通过可交换候选集之间的几何对应维持跨块一致性，同时允许当前策略证据覆盖历史约束。

Result: Push-T 覆盖率达 91.40%；D3IL 的 Avoiding、Aligning、Sorting-2 成功率分别为 100.0%、79.72%、84.44%；LIBERO 平均成功率 97.25%；真实双臂双盘任务保持两种放置模式并在 50 次试验中全部成功；扶正瓶子和放笔入架任务成功率与 π_{0.5} 相当，推理延迟从 218.24 ms 降至 75.80 ms。

Conclusion: 单遍轨迹建模能够同时实现多模态行为、闭环一致性与高效推理，在仿真和真实机器人任务中均验证了有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal imitation learning requires diverse executable futures under the same observation and consistent behavior across replanning cycles. We present Conditional Trajectory Peaks (CTP), a single-pass policy framework that jointly predicts complete action-chunk candidates, probability masses, and trajectory scales. Distribution-Aware Peak Specialization (DAPS) specializes trajectory peaks using trajectory-level posterior responsibilities and mass- and scale-modulated overlap constraints. Evidence-Gated Trajectory Belief Transport (ETBT) maintains cross-chunk consistency through geometric correspondence between exchangeable candidate sets, while allowing current policy evidence to override historical constraints. CTP achieves a coverage score of 91.40% on Push-T; success rates of 100.0%, 79.72%, and 84.44% on D3IL Avoiding, Aligning, and Sorting-2, respectively. On LIBERO, CTP achieves an average success rate of 97.25%. In real-world dual-arm experiments, CTP preserves both placement modes in a two-plate task, succeeding in all 50 trials. On bottle uprighting and pen placement into a holder, it maintains success rates comparable to $\pi_{0.5}$ while reducing policy inference latency from 218.24 ms to 75.80 ms. These results demonstrate that single-pass trajectory modeling can combine multimodal behavior, closed-loop consistency, and efficient inference.

</details>


### [69] [Propagating Elevation-Map Uncertainty Through the Contact Maximum in Closed Form](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06103)
*Aleš Kučera,Karel Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出一种闭式计算刚性接触最大值矩的方法，用Clark成对递归和高斯二次型恒等式替代一阶线性化与Monte Carlo，并通过竞赛深度校准修正标准差，在rover路径段上实现高精度风险规划和5.5微秒GPU规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风险感知路径规划依赖不确定高程图上路径代价的均值和标准差。但刚性接触需要对多个不确定栅格取最大值，一阶传播因只在单个单元处求导而损失精度，Monte Carlo则每次抽样都需完整路径评估，计算代价高。

Method: 对接触最大值采用Clark成对递归闭式求矩；跟踪每个接触相对共享地图单元的协方差，并用精确高斯二次型恒等式传播平滑剩余项；最后用仅基于两条设计轨迹拟合的contest-depth calibration修正聚合标准差不足。

Result: 在317条留出rover路径段上，以同一信念的Monte Carlo为参考，全部预注册标准均满足。校正Clark fold将均值中位误差从线性化的2.3%降至0.19%，并在90% CVaR上取得所有测试方法中最低误差；GPU基准规划耗时5.5微秒。精度增益集中于争议接触处。路径选择很少改变，但该方法在98%的决策中选出参考最优路径，线性化为93%至96%。在第二数据集上，均值可迁移，风险数值不可迁移。

Conclusion: 该方法为刚性接触风险传播提供了精确、高效的闭式方案，在争议接触处显著提升均值与CVaR精度，并以极低计算开销改善路径选择；但风险数值的跨数据集泛化仍受限，需进一步校准与验证。

Abstract: Risk-aware planners score paths on uncertain elevation maps using the path cost's mean and standard deviation. Modeling rigid contact, however, requires computing a maximum over several uncertain cells. First-order propagation loses accuracy here by differentiating at only a single cell, while Monte Carlo sampling requires a full path evaluation per draw. We compute the moments of that contact maximum in closed form using Clark's pairwise recursion. By tracking each contact's covariance against the shared map cells, we propagate the smooth remainder using exact Gaussian quadratic-form identities. A contest-depth calibration, fitted once on two design traverses, closes the aggregate standard-deviation shortfall that remains. On 317 held-out rover path segments, scored against a Monte Carlo reference from the same belief, every pre-registered criterion was met. The corrected Clark fold cuts the median error of the mean from linearization's 2.3% to 0.19% and attains the lowest error in the conditional value at risk (CVaR) at the 90% level of every method tested. A benchmark plan costs just 5.5 microseconds on a GPU. These accuracy gains concentrate at contested contacts. While they seldom change which path is chosen on this terrain, the fold still selects the reference-best path in 98% of decisions against linearization's 93 to 96%. On a second dataset the mean transfers, though the risk number does not.

</details>


### [70] [Execution-Aligned Progressive Noise for Consistent Asynchronous Replanning in Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06090)
*Di Wu,Ping Liu,Xuhua Chen,He Zheng,Lingfeng Zhang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出执行对齐渐进噪声（EAPN），通过在块间与块内引入结构化随机性，解决实时生成式机器人策略在连续异步重规划中的模式切换与动作块不连续问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续异步重规划对实时生成式机器人策略至关重要，但独立随机初始化会导致动作块之间出现模式切换，并破坏执行连续性。

Method: EAPN在重规划步骤间传播共享噪声轨迹，并将其与实际执行位移对齐，形成执行对齐的块间相关性；同时在每个动作块内建模沿动作时间的时间相关性。该方法还将对齐后的随机历史与已提交动作上下文结合，用于条件化后续生成，使新动作块从与执行一致的生成状态继续，而非从独立噪声重新开始。

Result: 在D3IL、Kinetix、LIBERO及真实世界操作任务上评估EAPN。D3IL上提升了多模态行为一致性；Kinetix上平均成功率达88.59%；LIBERO上保持鲁棒，在长推理延迟下仍维持较强任务性能；真实机器人实验中，Object Storage成功率为90.0%，双臂Cloth Folding成功率为96.7%。

Conclusion: EAPN能在异步重规划下实现可靠连续执行，有效缓解模式切换与动作块不一致问题，并提升多任务与真实机器人操作中的稳定性和成功率。

Abstract: Continuous asynchronous replanning is essential for real-time generative robot policies, but independent stochastic initialization can cause mode switching and inconsistent continuation across action chunks. We propose Execution-Aligned Progressive Noise (EAPN), which introduces structured stochasticity at both inter-chunk and intra-chunk levels. Across replanning steps, EAPN propagates a shared noise trajectory and aligns it with the actual execution displacement, establishing execution-aligned inter-chunk correlation. Within each action chunk, it models temporal correlation along action time. The aligned stochastic history is further combined with committed action context to condition subsequent generation, allowing new chunks to continue from execution-consistent generative states rather than restart from independent noise. We evaluate EAPN on D3IL, Kinetix, LIBERO, and real-world manipulation tasks. EAPN improves multimodal behavior consistency on D3IL and achieves an average success rate of 88.59% on Kinetix.
