On LIBERO, it remains robust and maintains strong task performance even under long inference delays. Real-robot experiments further achieve 90.0% success on Object Storage and 96.7% on bimanual Cloth Folding, demonstrating reliable continuous execution under asynchronous replanning.

</details>


### [71] [Adaptive Mean Flow for Responsive Closed-Loop Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06089)
*Aksel Vaaler,Marco Job,Christian Holden,Olav Egeland*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Adaptive Mean Flow（AMF），一种基于流的机器人模仿学习方法，利用 Mean Flow 加速 Flow Matching，并通过使用上一时刻轨迹的加噪版本作为条件，实现平滑、响应式、完全闭环的机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散和流匹配策略性能高且能建模连续多模态动作分布，但迭代去噪导致预测延迟高，难以支持高频闭环控制，并可能引起动作抖动和不稳定。常见做法是预测动作块并顺序执行，但会降低响应性且可能无法利用最新状态。

Method: AMF 采用 Mean Flow（Flow Matching 的加速形式）来降低预测延迟。为保持预测平滑性和一致性，在预测新动作时使用上一步轨迹的损坏版本作为条件，并让信噪比随轨迹时间参数增加，从而抑制相邻预测间的剧烈变化，同时保留对未来步骤的适应能力。

Result: 在大量仿真和真实机器人任务上评估 AMF，与基线方法相比性能显著提升。

Conclusion: AMF 能够实现平滑、响应式且完全闭环的机器人控制，并在多种任务上优于现有基线；代码已公开。

Abstract: Diffusion- and flow-based robot policies have recently become widespread in robotic Imitation Learning (IL) due to their high performance and ability to model continuous and multimodal distributions. However, the iterative denoising procedure used by these models introduces significant prediction latency, hindering high-frequency closed-loop robot control and leading to jittery, unstable motion when frequent updates to the robot's action predictions are used. Therefore, it is common practice to train models to predict chunks of actions that can be executed sequentially without feedback, even when this reduces responsiveness and may mean the most recent state information is not used. In this article, we present Adaptive Mean Flow (AMF), a flow-based IL method that enables smooth and responsive, fully closed-loop robot control. AMF uses Mean Flow, which is an accelerated form of Flow Matching (FM), to minimize prediction latency. To ensure smoothness and consistency across predictions, AMF uses a corrupted version of the trajectory from the previous step when predicting new robot actions, with the signal-to-noise ratio increasing over the time parameter of the trajectory. This discourages large changes in the prediction from one step to the next, while allowing freedom to adapt the predictions for future steps. We evaluate AMF across a wide range of simulated and real robot tasks and demonstrate significantly improved performance compared with baselines. Code: this https URL .

</details>


### [72] [Do VLAs Understand and Adapt to the Objects They Handle, or Simply Replay Learned Behaviors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06078)
*Xinnuo Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究检验VLA的泛化是源于对物体物理属性的全局理解并据此调整动作，还是仅重放已学动作而偶然成功。通过线性探测、RSA和LIBERO质量干预实验，发现VLA对物理属性编码弱且不可靠用于运动自适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分真正的泛化与偶然鲁棒性：若策略理解质量、易碎性、可变形性、摩擦、大小等物理属性，就能适应未见设置并调整运动；否则只是重放已学动作。

Method: 对七个VLA的激活进行线性探测和表征相似性分析（RSA），比较物理属性与非物理属性（语义类别、材料、声音、价格）的可解码性，并与基座VLM及预训练/微调影响对比；在LIBERO受控案例中增加域内物体质量，并通过语言或视觉信号提示变化，观察动作与任务成功率。

Result: 几乎每个模态流中，物理属性比非物理属性更难解码；机器人预训练削弱语言流中物理属性的线性编码；预训练和下游微调均未增强物理属性差异与激活距离的对齐。LIBERO中多数VLA对更重物体采用与原始质量相似的抬升行为，导致成功率下降；少数例外仅对词汇或视觉线索反应，而非对质量本身。

Conclusion: VLA对物理属性编码较弱，且不能可靠利用这些信息调整运动，因此其在新设置中的成功更多反映偶然鲁棒性而非基于物理理解的真正泛化。

Abstract: This paper asks whether VLA generalization is grounded in a global understanding of objects' physical properties that enables policies to adapt their motion to unseen setups, or if policies simply replay the motions they've learnt that happen to succeed in new setups. The former reflects genuine generalization; the latter reflects incidental robustness. We first examine awareness of physical properties in seven VLAs by applying linear probing and representational similarity analysis (RSA) to their activations. We find that physical properties, including mass, fragility, deformability, friction and size are less decodable than non-physical properties such as semantic category, material, sound and price in nearly every modality stream. Compared with their base VLMs, robot pre-training weakens the linear encoding of physical properties in the language stream. Neither pre-training nor downstream fine-tuning strengthens the alignment between physical-property differences and activation distances. We then ask whether the weak physical information present in these activations shapes the actions a VLA generates. In a controlled LIBERO case study, we increase the mass of an in-domain object and signal the change through language or vision. Most VLAs use similar lifting behaviour for the heavier and original-mass objects, leading to task success declines. The few exceptions change their behaviour in response to lexical or visual cues rather than to mass itself. These results suggest that VLAs encode physical properties weakly and do not reliably use them to adapt their motion.

</details>


### [73] [MagServo: Uncertainty-Resilient Hierarchical Magnetic Servoing via Learned Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06046)
*Yuhan Tan,Yameng Zhang,Pei Liu,Yao Zhong,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MagServo 是一种基于分层学习的 6-DoF 磁伺服框架，直接利用学习到的潜在磁特征进行控制，无需显式位姿估计或解析磁模型，在物理实验中实现亚毫米/亚度精度，并在未见磁源配置下保持鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有磁导航方法通常依赖显式位姿估计或原始磁测量，导致控制性能易受建模误差、测量噪声和外部扰动影响，难以实现高精度、强鲁棒的 6-DoF 伺服。

Method: MagServo 通过掩码重建学习不确定性鲁棒的磁表示，并捕捉机器人运动与潜在磁状态转移之间的状态相关交互动力学，无需解析磁模型或显式雅可比监督；在此基础上构建分层控制器，结合非线性模型预测控制进行粗接近，并利用局部雅可比逆进行精细调节。

Result: 物理实验表明，MagServo 在 6-DoF 位姿到达任务中达到亚毫米和亚度精度，平均终端误差为 0.386 mm 和 0.479°；在复杂轨迹跟踪中优于基于定位的控制基线，且在未见的磁源配置下无需重训练仍保持鲁棒性能；另提供真实机器人实验视频。

Conclusion: MagServo 证明直接使用学习到的潜在磁特征进行分层伺服，可规避显式建模与位姿估计，实现高精度、鲁棒且泛化性强的 6-DoF 磁导航控制。

Abstract: Magnetic navigation provides contact-free and line-of-sight-independent feedback for robotic systems, yet existing approaches typically rely on explicit pose estimation or direct use of raw magnetic measurements, making accurate control susceptible to modeling errors, measurement noise, and disturbances. This work presents MagServo, a hierarchical learning-based framework for robust 6-DoF magnetic servoing directly using the learned latent magnetic feature. MagServo learns uncertainty-resilient magnetic representations through masked reconstruction and captures state-dependent interaction dynamics between robot motion and latent magnetic transitions without analytical magnetic models or explicit Jacobian supervision. Based on the learned dynamics, a hierarchical controller combines nonlinear model predictive control for coarse approach with local Jacobian inversion for precise fine regulation. Extensive physical experiments demonstrate submillimeter and subdegree accuracy, achieving mean terminal errors of 0.386 mm and 0.479 degree for 6-DoF pose reaching. MagServo further outperforms a localization-based control baseline in complex trajectory tracking and maintains robust performance under unseen magnetic-source configurations without retraining. A supplementary video of the real-robot experiments is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [74] [Recon2Servo: Robotic Ultrasound Visual Servoing via Learned Image-to-Motion Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06010)
*Yameng Zhang,Pei Liu,Dianye Huang,Yizhao Qian,Zhongyu Chen,Xiangyu Chu,K. W. Samuel Au,Zhongliang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Recon2Servo：一种直接从2D B超图像学习图像到运动推断的6自由度超声探头视觉伺服框架，用于自主机器人超声中的目标视图对齐与动态跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声视觉伺服对自主机器人超声至关重要，但仅凭2D B模式图像实现6自由度探头控制仍很困难，因为平面外运动线索有限且存在歧义；现有方法多依赖解剖先验或手工视觉特征，跨成像目标泛化能力不足。

Method: 受无跟踪器3D超声重建启发，Recon2Servo直接由B模式图像学习图像到运动映射。采用带低秩适配（LoRA）的DINOv3编码器和双向关系模块，估计当前图像与目标图像之间的相对探头位姿，以引导迭代闭环目标视图对齐；框架结合监督式相对位姿学习、重建引导的闭环自适应以及有界残差位姿校正，以提升伺服过程中的运动推断。

Result: 在公开数据集和自建数据集上评估，自建数据来自12名健康志愿者并使用不同超声系统采集，结果表明该方法在重建体积伺服中有效。补充视频还展示了在人体前臂上的真实机器人目标视图对齐与动态跟踪演示。

Conclusion: Recon2Servo表明可从B模式图像直接学习6自由度探头运动，无需依赖解剖先验或手工特征，为自主机器人超声的目标视图伺服与动态跟踪提供了可行且具泛化潜力的方案。

Abstract: Ultrasound visual servoing is essential for autonomous robotic ultrasound, yet 6-DoF probe control from 2D B-mode images remains challenging due to limited and ambiguous out-of-plane motion cues. Existing methods typically rely on anatomical priors or handcrafted visual features, limiting their generalizability across imaging targets. Inspired by trackerless 3D ultrasound reconstruction, we propose Recon2Servo, a visual servoing framework that learns image-to-motion inference directly from B-mode images for 6-DoF probe control. A DINOv3 encoder with low-rank adaptation and a bidirectional relation module estimate the relative probe pose between current and target images to guide iterative closed-loop target-view alignment. The framework combines supervised relative-pose learning, reconstruction-guided closed-loop adaptation, and bounded residual pose correction to improve motion inference during servoing. Evaluations on a public dataset and an in-house dataset collected from 12 healthy volunteers using different ultrasound systems demonstrate its effectiveness in reconstructed-volume servoing. Additional real-robot demonstrations of target-view alignment and dynamic tracking on a human forearm are provided in the supplementary video: this https URL .

</details>


### [75] [P3: Persistent Particle Planning for Constrained Diffusion Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06002)
*Hikmet Simsir,Mahyar Fardinfar,Ozgur S. Oguz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出持久粒子规划（P3）：一种用于扩散控制的序贯蒙特卡洛框架，通过在重规划步骤间维护带权候选计划种群，在测试时约束下实现可行、一致且高效的轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型能提供表达力强的轨迹先验，但在测试时约束下，连续控制决策需要保持计划可行性与前后一致性；现有方法往往需要从头重采样、容易路线切换，或仅对单个采样计划做约束优化，效率与可靠性不足。

Method: P3将扩散去噪与重规划统一视为一个Feynman-Kac粒子系统，维护一组带权候选计划。每个控制步中，对候选轨迹进行偏移和部分重新加噪，并在最新观测下细化；随后通过约束感知加权与重采样选择后续方案，且无需重新训练扩散模型。理论上在理想化修复下分析重新加噪深度与计划保留可靠性。

Result: 理论表明，重新加噪深度控制被保留计划沿原路线前进的可靠性；保留一条稀有且分离良好的路线所需计划数远少于从头采样重新发现。多种测试时约束配置的实验显示，种群复用减少路线切换，在无约束违背下提高成功率；由于P3细化早前计划而非重新绘制，每次重规划所需去噪迭代更少。迷宫导航任务中，P3比重新生成种群的方法以及通过约束优化修正单个采样计划的方法规划更快。

Conclusion: P3为扩散控制的测试时约束提供了一种持久粒子规划方案，兼顾可行性、一致性与效率，并已开源代码和预训练模型。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide expressive priors over trajectories, but adapting these priors to test-time constraints requires maintaining feasibility and consistency across successive control decisions. We introduce Persistent Particle Planning (P3), a sequential Monte Carlo framework for diffusion control that maintains a weighted population of candidate plans across replanning steps. At each control step, P3 shifts and partially re-noises the candidate trajectories, refines them under the latest observation, and uses constraint-aware weighting and resampling to select among alternative continuations without retraining the diffusion model. We consider denoising and replanning as one Feynman--Kac particle system and analyze it under an idealized repair. We prove that the re-noising depth controls how reliably a kept plan stays on its route, and that keeping a rare, well-separated route takes far fewer plans than rediscovering it by sampling from scratch. Experiments under multiple test-time constraint configurations show that population reuse reduces route switching and improves success without constraint violations. Because P3 refines earlier plans instead of redrawing them, it also needs fewer denoising iterations per replan. On maze-navigation tasks, it plans faster than both regenerated populations and methods that correct a single sampled plan by constrained optimization. Code and pretrained models are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [76] [Infant simulator with an embodied caregiver: Generating infant-perspective touch and vision during social interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05997)
*Noemi Vaculinova,Matej Hoffmann*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MuJoCo中照护者使能的MIMo扩展，将婴儿模型从孤立模拟转为可控的二元互动平台，用于回放照护者-婴儿互动并生成婴儿第一人称多模态观测；触觉示例表明其可区分接触来源并统计触觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展机器人模拟器通常孤立建模婴儿，忽略照护者-婴儿二元互动中身体接触和面对面交流对婴儿感觉运动流的影响，限制了对照护者中介经验的研究。

Method: 在MuJoCo中扩展MIMo为照护者使能平台：提供与MIMo形态兼容的关节照护者模型，基于人体测量参数化并可缩放至记录的照护者；提供自然照护者-婴儿抱持与安抚交互回放工作流；记录并可视化婴儿第一人称多模态经验（触觉与视觉）；触觉方面引入来源感知接触日志，区分自我、照护者和环境产生的接触，并聚合成触觉率统计，与人工编码可比。

Result: 系统可回放二元照护者-婴儿交互并生成密集的婴儿视角观测；触觉分析中，来源感知接触日志能够区分自我、照护者和环境接触，并生成与人工编码可比的触觉率统计。

Conclusion: 该平台不仅用于触觉分析，更旨在作为触觉和第一人称视觉等多模态二元数据集的生成器，以建模社会互动的发展。

Abstract: Early development unfolds in caregiver-infant dyads, where infants' sensorimotor streams are shaped by physical contact and face-to-face interaction. Yet developmental robotics simulators commonly model infants in isolation, limiting the study of caregiver-mediated experience. We present a caregiver-enabled extension of the Multi-Modal Infant Model (MIMo) in MuJoCo that turns MIMo into a controllable platform for replaying dyadic interaction and generating dense infant-perspective observations. The system provides (i) an articulated caregiver model compatible with MIMo morphologies, parameterized from anthropometrics and optionally resized to a recorded caregiver; (ii) a workflow to replay naturalistic caregiver-infant holding and soothing interactions; and (iii) logging and visualizing the infant's first-person multimodal experience (we show touch and vision). We showcase the tool on touch by introducing origin-aware contact logging that disambiguates self-, caregiver-, and environment-generated contact and supports aggregation into touch-rate statistics comparable to manual coding. While we showcase tactile analysis, the platform is intended more broadly as a generator of multimodal dyadic datasets (touch and egocentric vision) for modeling the development of social interaction.

</details>


### [77] [How (and How Not) to Use Data Augmentation in VLA Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05994)
*Bram Grooten,Joaquin Vanschoren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究VLA模型RL后训练中的图像增强策略，发现仅增强critic、保持actor输入干净可显著提升视觉OOD泛化；增强actor则会导致训练崩溃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在真实机器人任务中表现良好，但对大幅视觉分布外偏移泛化不足；RL后训练虽能提升鲁棒性，仍有较大改进空间。

Method: 系统研究图像增强对VLA后训练的影响，对比增强模块、位置、类型与强度；提出仅在RL更新时增强critic，actor在rollout和更新时均使用干净输入。

Result: 在LIBERO-Plus上，π0.5和GR00T N1.5的OOD成功率分别提升7.8和10.0个百分点；增强actor会使训练完全崩溃，并总结了不同增强类型与强度的实用建议。

Conclusion: VLA的RL后训练中，图像增强应仅作用于critic，避免污染actor输入，这是提升视觉OOD泛化并稳定训练的关键，为VLA后训练泛化改进提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models currently demonstrate strong performance in a wide range of real-world robotics tasks. However, they often still lack the generalization ability to handle large visual out-of-distribution shifts. Post-training of VLAs with reinforcement learning (RL) has been shown to benefit robustness, but significant room for improvement remains. In this work, we systematically study the effect of image augmentation on VLA post-training. We find that it is crucial to augment only the critic module during RL updates, while leaving the actor's input clean during both rollouts and updates. For $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T N1.5 this raises out-of-distribution success on LIBERO-Plus by $7.8$ and $10.0$ points respectively, while augmenting the actor collapses training entirely. We investigate a range of augmentation types and strengths, and provide practical recommendations for improving generalization in VLA post-training.

</details>


### [78] [A State Based Dispatch Controller for Hospital Delivery Robots with Shared Human and Infrastructure Resources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05971)
*Krzysztof Siwek,Aleksandra Świetlicka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究构建位置感知的机器人配送调度模型，将机器人准入与转运人员支持、共享电梯、充电和清洁耦合，在医院请求回放中比较不同调度策略，发现电梯等共享资源约束可超过调度差异，机器人通过截止时间准入仍可能因人工交接和设施共享而延迟服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人配送研究常将取货行程和人工支持排除在调度模型外，从而高估运输能力；需要刻画机器人、人员与共享设施耦合的真实服务流程，区分准入选择性、完成与恢复可用性。

Method: 建立位置感知调度模型，将机器人准入与转运支持及共享电梯、充电、清洁耦合；回放33,079条医院请求，缺失内容、截止时间、人员配置和完成时间作为显式情景假设；在每情景30次配对重复中用两组网格比较人工调度、资源感知控制器、邻近度与工作量基准，并探索更简单的截止时间准入规则。

Result: 取货行程增加共享电梯群需求；某高人员配置情景下，将假设电梯容量从2提高到4，使仅人工配送的迟到率从55.11%降至3.18%，其影响超过调度策略差异；直接策略比较和机器人覆盖率可区分准入选择性与系统服务水平，说明机器人可通过截止时间准入测试，却因员工交接和共享设施延迟服务。

Conclusion: 贡献在于可复现地评估耦合调度工作流，并清晰分离准入、完成和恢复可用性；结果为条件性比较，并非医院效益或释放临床能力的估计。

Abstract: Robot delivery studies can overstate transport capacity when travel to pickups and human support fall outside the modeled schedule. We formulate a location aware dispatch model that couples robot admission to transporter support and shared elevators, charging, and cleaning. The model replays 33,079 observed hospital requests; missing contents, deadlines, staffing, and completion times remain explicit scenario assumptions. Two full grids compare human dispatch, a resource aware controller, and a proximity and workload benchmark in 30 paired replications per scenario. An exploratory extension tests a simpler deadline admission rule under the same operating model. Pickup travel increases demand on a pooled elevator bank. In one high staffing case, raising assumed elevator capacity from two to four reduces human only lateness from 55.11\% to 3.18\%, exceeding the dispatch differences. Direct policy comparisons and robot coverage distinguish admission selectivity from system service. The analysis explains why a robot can pass a deadline admission test yet delay service through staff handoffs and shared facilities. Its contribution is a reproducible evaluation of coupled dispatch workflows, with a clear separation between admission, completion, and return to availability. The results are conditional comparisons, not estimates of hospital benefit or released clinical capacity.

</details>


### [79] [From Social Reasoning to Embodied Interaction: An Agentic Framework for Social Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05964)
*Ziyu Cheng,Yuewen Guo,Zhirui Liu,Dong Zhang,Haotao Lu,Jingyi Yu,Ye Shi,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ARISE统一框架，在Sophia人形机器人上结合智能体推理与交互式社交具身，实现多模态理解、长期记忆、主动/反应式交互，并将社交意图转化为与语音和面部表情协调的机器人原生手势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然人机面对面交互要求机器人理解动态社交情境、判断何时参与，并通过协调的物理行为表达意图；现有方法难以闭环：基础模型智能体多缺乏具身，虚拟智能体不受真实机器人物理约束，而物理社交机器人往往只部分解决社交推理或具身表达。

Method: ARISE在Sophia人形机器人上统一智能体推理与交互式社交具身，集成多模态上下文理解、长期记忆、反应式与主动式交互以决定何时及传达什么，并通过流式执行将社交意图转化为与语音和机械面部表情协调的机器人原生手势。

Result: 在Sophia上的广泛评估表明：感知交互质量高，具身行为表达力强且协调良好，流式执行带来显著延迟降低。

Conclusion: 结果凸显了联合推理“传达什么、何时参与、如何物理表达社交意图”对于人形机器人自然交互的重要性。

Abstract: Natural face-to-face human--robot interaction requires a robot to understand an evolving social situation, decide when to engage, and express its intent through coordinated physical behavior. Yet existing approaches rarely close this loop: foundation-model agents provide increasingly capable multimodal reasoning and memory but remain largely disembodied, while expressive virtual agents do not face the physical constraints of real robots, and physical social robots typically address social reasoning and embodied expression only partially. We present ARISE, a unified framework that bridges Agentic Reasoning and Interactive Social Embodiment on the Sophia humanoid robot. ARISE integrates multimodal context understanding, long-term memory, and reactive and proactive interaction to determine when and what to communicate, and translates social intent into robot-native gestures coordinated with speech and mechanical facial expressions through streaming execution. Extensive evaluations on Sophia demonstrate strong perceived interaction quality, expressive and well-coordinated embodied behavior, and substantial latency reductions through streaming execution. These results highlight the importance of jointly reasoning about what to communicate, when to engage, and how to physically express social intent for natural interaction with humanoid robots. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [80] [Mulligan: Performance-Guided Data Collection for Efficient On-Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05882)
*Lars Ankile,Perry Dong,Rohan Bhowmik,Aneesh Muppidi,David D. Yuan,Shuran Song,Chelsea Finn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Mulligan 将监督部署中每轮初始状态分布作为决策变量，优先从已观察到的失败与未尝试状态开始收集，并用价值函数利用包括失败在内的全部数据，从而在固定监督回合预算下提升高精度操作任务的学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人从人类演示学习时，新增演示收益随策略提升而递减；在监督部署中，均匀采样初始状态会把操作员时间浪费在策略已能处理的状态上，且失败往往集中于少量初始状态。

Method: 方法将初始状态分布纳入决策，每轮从观察到的失败状态和未尝试状态启动回合；同时将交互式模仿学习与在全部数据（含模仿学习丢弃的失败数据）上训练的价值函数结合，并可用基于价值的动作选择（HiL-IDQL+Mulligan）。

Result: 在三个真实世界任务、2550 个留出盲测回合和两个仿真任务上，在匹配收集预算下 Mulligan 优于均匀初始状态采样；与基于价值的动作选择结合后，真实任务最终成功率提升 10-34 个百分点；有操作员介入时，人机团队完成 98% 的收集回合，并在策略学习过程中保持产出。

Conclusion: 结果表明，主动决定初始状态采样分布并利用失败数据中的价值信息，可显著提高监督部署与交互式模仿学习的数据效率和最终任务成功率。

Abstract: Learning from human demonstrations is a reliable way to teach robots new tasks, but the gains from each additional demonstration shrink as the policy improves. Continued improvement can instead come from supervised deployment, where an operator places the objects and intervenes when the policy fails. We ask how to maximize improvement from a fixed budget of supervised episodes on high-precision manipulation tasks with wide ranges of object placements. We observe that failures can concentrate in a small subset of initial states, so uniform collection spends much of the operator's time on states the policy already handles. Mulligan makes the initial-state distribution a decision, starting each round's episodes at observed failures and untried states. To further improve data efficiency, we augment interactive imitation learning with a value function trained on all data, including failures that imitation discards. Across three real-world tasks evaluated on 2,550 held-out, blinded episodes and two simulated tasks, Mulligan outperforms uniform initial-state sampling at matched collection budgets, and combined with value-based action selection, HiL-IDQL+Mulligan, improves final real-task success by 10-34 percentage points. With operator interventions, the human-robot team completes 98% of collection episodes, remaining productive while the policy learns. Videos, code, and data are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [81] [Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Collision-Free Locomotion of an Underactuated Biped](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05855)
*Jagannath Prasad Sahoo,Saurabh Kumar,Surya Prakash S.K.,Samiran Datta,Abhay Dwivedi,Amit Shukla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种分层强化学习框架，用高层策略生成身体速度指令、低层策略以PD关节目标跟踪，使无髋/踝滚转的四关节腿部欠驱动双足机器人能兼顾平衡与避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双足机器人绕障会破坏平衡，欠驱动平台上该耦合尤为严重，传统规划器难以直接输出可行运动指令。

Method: 高层观测机器人姿态、36条射线近距测量、移动障碍状态及滚动时域局部目标，每10控制步输出(vx, vy, omega_yaw)；低层为速度条件策略，经PD控制关节目标；两层用SAC联合训练。冻结任务无关步态后，与A*、RRT*、APF经典规划器在同一指令接口组合成基线。

Result: 在随机PyBullet环境各100次试验中，提出方法静态/动态成功率98.0%/88.0%，规划器混合最高仅78.0%/68.0%；路径长度与A*参考差距在4%以内；消融表明各观测通道和奖励项均有实质贡献。

Conclusion: 分层RL可将避障决策与平衡步态解耦，在欠驱动双足平台上优于经典规划器混合方案，且步态可冻结复用、观测与奖励设计有效。

Abstract: A bipedal robot cannot deviate from its path to avoid an obstacle without disturbing its balance, and this coupling is most severe on underactuated platforms such as the biped considered here, which has four actuated joints per leg and no hip or ankle roll. This paper presents a Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (HRL) framework in which a High-Level (HL) policy observes the robot pose, 36 raycast proximity measurements, moving-obstacle states, and a receding-horizon local goal, and outputs a body-velocity command $(v_x, v_y, \omega_{yaw})$ every ten control steps, while a velocity-conditioned Low-Level (LL) policy tracks each command through PD-controlled joint targets. Both policies are trained jointly with Soft Actor-Critic (SAC). Because the converged gait is task-agnostic, it is frozen and driven by classical planners over the same command interface, yielding three controlled baselines: SAC+A*, SAC+RRT*, and SAC+APF. Across 100 evaluation trials per method in randomized PyBullet environments, the proposed method reaches the goal in 98.0% of static and 88.0% of dynamic trials, against at most 78.0% and 68.0% for the planner hybrids, with path lengths within 4% of the A* reference, and ablations confirm that each observation channel and reward term contributes materially to this performance.

</details>


### [82] [Dual-Rate Force-Image Control with Model-Based Orientation Limits for Robotic Ultrasound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05839)
*Tyler Foster,Qiang Zhang,A B M Tahidul Haque,Anh Thu Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究针对机器人超声中快速力环与慢速延迟图像反馈耦合导致的旋转扰动问题，推导闭式方向角速率极限，并在双速率控制器中验证其主要价值是速率设计规则而非在线预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像引导的超声探头旋转会通过慢速、延迟的图像反馈扰乱接触力：在力环观察到图像响应之前，旋转已改变接触力。因此需要限制旋转速率以保证力安全。

Method: 推导有限时域内旋转引起的估计力偏差上界，同时考虑快速力环的扰动抑制，得到闭式方向速率极限；该极限依赖局部接触刚度、力环增益、保守旋转-力增益界、偏差预算和预测时域。在双速率控制器中实现该模型，采用基于时间戳的延迟重建和基于关节力矩的力估计，并在弯曲明胶体模上进行配对控制器比较和组件消融。

Result: 相比无约束图像引导，所提速率受限控制器将第一秒RMS估计力误差降低0.40 N，但线索收敛时间增加0.94 s。接近解析预测上限的固定速率帽对力误差无可分辨差异，且收敛快0.32 s，表明模型的主要实际价值是速率设计规则而非在线预测。延迟重建在测试延迟下无可分辨影响。单受试腘动脉扫描证明了可行性，但异质组织上的图像线索受噪声限制。

Conclusion: 该工作说明，旋转速率限制能有效降低机器人超声图像引导中的力扰动，但会牺牲收敛速度；解析速率极限主要用于指导速率设计，固定速率上限即可接近其力安全效果并更快收敛，且延迟重建在当前测试条件下影响不显著。

Abstract: Robotic ultrasound couples a high-rate contact-force loop with slower, delayed image feedback, so image-guided ultrasound probe rotation can perturb contact force before the resulting image response is observed. We derive a closed-form orientation-rate limit that bounds the modeled rotation-induced estimated-force excursion over a finite horizon while accounting for disturbance rejection by the fast force loop. The limit depends on local contact stiffness, force-loop gains, a conservative rotation-to-force gain bound, the excursion budget, and the prediction horizon. We implement this model in a dual-rate controller with timestamp-based delay reconstruction and joint-torque-based force estimation, and evaluate it on a curved gelatin phantom using paired controller comparisons and component ablations. Relative to unconstrained image guidance, the proposed rate-limited controller reduced first-second root-mean-square (RMS) estimated-force error by 0.40 N while increasing cue-convergence time by 0.94 s. A fixed rate cap near the analytically predicted ceiling produced no resolvable difference in force error and converged 0.32 s faster, indicating that the principal practical value of the model is the rate-design rule rather than online prediction. Delay reconstruction had no resolvable effect at the tested latency. A single-subject popliteal scan demonstrated feasibility, although the image cue was noise-limited on heterogeneous tissue.

</details>


### [83] [Transporting Unsecured Stacked Payloads with a Quadrupedal Robot via Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05819)
*Nobuo Namura,Masayuki Hiromoto,Kento Uemura,Hironobu Sasaki,Kanata Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PAMORT，一种面向四足机器人运输未固定堆叠箱体的载荷自适应多目标强化学习方法，通过本体感觉在线调节运动性能与载荷稳定性之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人在崎岖地形运输未固定载荷时，激进运动会破坏载荷稳定，即使机器人自身稳定；且无专用载荷传感器或主动承载机构时，需在运动性能与载荷稳定之间取得平衡。

Method: 在无边缘躯干安装板上运输未固定堆叠箱体；PAMORT先训练以偏好向量为条件、加权运动与载荷稳定奖励组的多目标基础策略，再冻结该策略并训练权重调节器，利用本体感觉在线自适应偏好。

Result: 仿真中，PAMORT在不同载荷配置下总体运输成功率与单目标基线相当或更优，并泛化到未见过的三箱堆叠（仅用两箱训练）。Unitree Go2真实实验中零样本迁移至训练难度及以上的斜坡和台阶，八个任务平均成功率0.850，基线为0.675。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅依赖本体感觉信息即可在线调整运动-载荷权衡，实现稳健的未固定载荷运输。

Abstract: Transporting unsecured payloads with legged robots over uneven terrain requires balancing locomotion performance and payload stability, since aggressive motion can destabilize the payload even when the robot remains stable. We study quadrupedal transportation of unsecured stacked boxes on an edgeless torso-mounted board without dedicated payload sensors or active carrier mechanisms. To address this trade-off, we propose Payload-Adaptive Multi-Objective Reinforcement learning for Transportation (PAMORT). PAMORT trains a multi-objective base policy conditioned on a preference vector that weights locomotion and payload-stability reward groups, then trains a weight adjuster on the frozen policy to adapt this preference online from proprioception. In simulation, PAMORT achieves comparable or better overall transportation success than a corresponding single-objective baseline across different payload configurations, including an unseen three-box stack, despite training only with two boxes. Real-world experiments on a Unitree Go2 demonstrate zero-shot transfer to slopes and steps at or beyond the training difficulty, with mean success rates of 0.850 for PAMORT and 0.675 for the baseline across eight tasks. These results demonstrate robust unsecured-payload transportation with online adaptation of the locomotion--payload trade-off from proprioceptive information.

</details>


### [84] [What the Guard Misses, the Robot Executes: Implied Harm in VLA Instructions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05818)
*Sripad Karne,Arjun Balaji*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究评估VLA模型无法拒绝指令时，现有文本监控与激活监控能否识别“任务无害但意图有害”的请求；发现隐含有害意图大量漏检，且机器人训练会削弱线性探针的可分性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于VLA会直接执行指令而不能拒绝，安全筛查只能依赖外部监控器；因此需要测试监控器在保持机器人任务不变、仅改变有害意图表述明确度时，能否识别出于有害原因请求的普通任务。

Method: 固定任务，仅改变有害意图的表述明确程度；测试π0.5的执行情况及文本guard与激活监控效果；在基础语言模型和视觉语言模型中训练线性探针区分有害与无害指令，并比较机器人训练前后两个模型家族。

Result: π0.5在所有明确度下均完成任务，频率与无害对照相当；文本guard能标记几乎所有直白请求，但很少标记隐含请求：隐含有害运行中最多95%完成任务且未触发警报，按机器人指令重校准后仍达90%；激活监控未能弥补该差距。线性探针在基础LM/VLM中近乎完美分离有害与无害指令，但机器人训练后分离能力下降，隐含有害意图下降最明显。

Conclusion: 当前监控机制对隐含有害意图的VLA请求存在严重盲区，机器人训练还可能削弱模型内部表征的可分性；需要针对隐性意图与机器人适配的安全监控方法。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) act on instructions without being able to refuse, so screening harmful requests falls to monitors. We test whether these monitors catch ordinary robot tasks requested for harmful reasons, holding the task fixed while varying only how explicitly the intent is stated. $\pi_{0.5}$ completes the task at every level of explicitness, as often as for harmless controls. Text guards flag nearly every blunt request but few implied ones: up to 95% of implied-harm runs end with the task done and no flag raised, and up to 90% even after recalibrating on robot instructions. Monitoring the model's activations does not close this gap. Linear probes separate harmful from harmless instructions almost perfectly in the base language and vision-language models, but this separation weakens after robot training in two model families, most for implied harm.

</details>


### [85] [Dynamics-Aware Adaptive Corridors with Feasibility-Perturbed Trust-Region SQP for Certified Nonholonomic Motion Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05796)
*Yang Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向带倒挡类车车辆的停车规划器：优化阶段每个迭代都精确满足离散动力学，并在采样点之间保持整车矩形无碰撞；在820个基准案例中818例成功，且所有评估迭代均可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于优化的停车规划器通常仅在时间采样点施加碰撞约束，因此车辆角点可能在采样间隔内切入障碍物，且求解器收敛前不存在可执行轨迹。

Method: 将每个时间区间用单个凸走廊覆盖，走廊两端包含车辆所有角点，并用扫掠裕度收缩以限制角点路径偏离弦；初始猜测碰撞时用分离半平面使走廊指向远离障碍方向；之后基于当前迭代的速度、曲率和步长生成航向对齐盒，并在接受步后重建。采用可行性扰动信赖域序列二次规划，通过反馈将每步投影到动力学上并精确验证，成本单调下降；走廊不再变化时，极限点为走廊约束问题的KKT点，或违反约束资格。

Result: 在820个基准案例中成功818例且无穿透，其中797例从初始猜测直接成功；7103个优化阶段迭代均可用；当99%初始猜测与障碍相交时成功率为96.5%；中位机动时间比同类认证的精确碰撞基线长0.7%。

Conclusion: 该方法为规划模型提供采样间无碰撞和动力学可行迭代保证，但这些保证不适用于物理车辆。

Abstract: Optimisation-based parking planners usually impose collision constraints only at the time samples, so a vehicle corner can cut an obstacle between samples, and no executable trajectory exists until the solver converges. We present a planner for car-like vehicles with reverse gear in which every iterate of the optimisation phase satisfies the discretised dynamics exactly and keeps the whole vehicle rectangle clear of obstacles between the samples. Each time interval receives one convex corridor that holds all vehicle corners at both ends and is shrunk by a sweep margin bounding how far the corner paths leave their chords. Separating half-planes give the corridors a direction out of obstacles when the initial guess is in collision; later, heading-aligned boxes are grown from the speed, curvature and step of the current iterate and rebuilt after accepted steps. A feasibility-perturbed trust-region sequential quadratic programming method projects each step onto the dynamics by feedback and verifies it exactly; the cost decreases monotonically, and once the corridors stop changing, limit points are Karush-Kuhn-Tucker points of the corridor-constrained problem or violate a constraint qualification. On 820 benchmark cases the planner succeeds in 818 without penetration (797 from the first initial guess), none of the 7103 evaluated optimisation-phase iterates is unusable, and it succeeds in 96.5% of the cases when 99% of the initial guesses intersect an obstacle. Its maneuvers take 0.7% longer in the median than those of a similarly certified exact-collision baseline. The guarantees hold for the planning model, not for a physical vehicle.

</details>


### [86] [DASH: A da Vinci Adapter for Serial-link and Humanoid Robots as an Accessible Platform for Surgical Robotics Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05792)
*Sara Wickenhiser,Junrong Zhou,Zekai Liang,Lizzie Peiros,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DASH，一种无需改造da Vinci Classic/Xi器械即可将其适配到通用串联臂和人形机器人平台的适配器系统，支持无线工具识别与器械特定运动学/耦合矩阵加载，并通过遥操作实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人微创手术具有明确临床获益，但专用平台成本高、基础设施要求高，限制了农村和资源匮乏机构的使用；已有通用机器人遥操作腹腔镜或体内操作的研究，但多依赖手持器械加被动连杆，而非原生机器人驱动。

Method: DASH包含两类适配器，分别面向串联机械臂和人形平台，无需修改da Vinci器械本身；适配器兼容多种机器人负载能力，可接合器械原生锁扣，并无线识别插入的工具，以加载器械特定运动学和耦合矩阵。该方法将da Vinci Classic和Xi器械适配到可融入临床工作流的通用机器人上。

Result: 遥操作实验证明了DASH的有效性，并显示其有利于为手术机器人平台提供研究接入。

Conclusion: DASH可在不修改da Vinci器械的情况下将其集成到通用机器人平台，降低对专用手术机器人平台的依赖，扩展手术机器人研究的可及性，并有望改善资源受限场景中的应用条件。

Abstract: Robotic minimally invasive surgery offers well-documented clinical benefits, but the cost and infrastructure requirements of purpose-built platforms limit access in rural and lower-resourced facilities. Recent work has teleoperated general-purpose robots for laparoscopic tasks and in vivo procedures, but relied on handheld instruments coupled through passive linkages rather than native robotic actuation. Instead, we adapt da Vinci Classic and Xi instruments onto general-purpose robots that can integrate in clinical workflows. We present DASH, a da Vinci Adapter for Serial-link and Humanoid platforms, consisting of two types of adapters that require no modification to the instruments themselves. Each adapter is compatible with many robotic platform load capacities, engages the instrument's native latch, and wirelessly identifies inserted tools to load instrument-specific kinematics and coupling matrices. Teleoperated experiments demonstrate the efficacy of DASH and its benefits for enabling research access to surgical robotic platforms.

</details>


### [87] [Bilinear Flow Policy: Distributional Extrapolation for Goal-Conditioned Visuomotor Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05765)
*Wonsuhk Jung,Sundhar Vinodh Sangeetha,Chen Xu,Abhishek Gupta,Masha Itkina,Shreyas Kousik,Haruki Nishimura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文提出 Bilinear Flow Policy（BFP），面向目标条件模仿学习（GCIL）中目标超出示范分布时的分布外外推问题，结合转导式检索与双线性条件流匹配，在保持多模态行为的同时提升未见目标上的泛化，并给出理论误差界与预部署诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 flow matching 的 GCIL 虽能建模多模态行为并适应多样用户目标，但当目标落在示范支持集之外时往往失败；核心挑战是在不破坏多模态性的前提下外推到未见目标，即“分布性外推”（distributional extrapolation）。

Method: BFP 是一种生成式视觉运动策略。对于未见观测-目标对，它先通过预训练视觉特征和一种新型可学习 anchor-selection 算法检索一个训练样本作为 anchor，并将未见对转导式重写为“anchor + 残差项”；残差需紧凑编码当前观测-目标对与 anchor 的差异，且 anchor 需使该差异对相应动作分布具有预测性。随后，双线性条件流联合建模 anchor 与残差如何决定多模态动作分布。理论上，在适当假设下证明未见目标处的动作分布误差可由分布内 flow-matching 误差上界控制，最多相差问题相关因子。

Result: 在五个仿真操作任务中，BFP 的分布外成功率是 GCIL 策略的 2.63 倍，是最强外推目标基线的 1.36 倍；在两个真实世界任务中，BFP 相比 GCIL 提升 32%。理论还给出实际可用的预部署诊断，用于预测哪些已训练策略能够良好外推以及可外推到哪些未见目标。

Conclusion: BFP 通过转导式 anchor-残差分解与双线性流匹配，有效实现目标条件模仿学习的分布性外推，在仿真与真实任务中显著优于现有方法，并将理论误差界转化为可部署的外推能力诊断工具。

Abstract: Goal-conditioned imitation learning (GCIL) with flow matching is a promising framework that can represent multimodal behaviors while adapting to diverse, user-specified goals, yet often fails when goals lie outside the demonstration support. To extrapolate to such unseen goals without collapsing multimodality - a problem we call distributional extrapolation - we introduce Bilinear Flow Policy (BFP), a generative visuomotor policy that combines transductive retrieval with a bilinear conditional flow. Given an unseen observation-goal pair, BFP retrieves an "anchor" training example and transductively reformulates the unseen pair as this familiar anchor plus a residual term. For this decomposition to guide action prediction, the residual must compactly encode how the current observation-goal pair differs from the anchor, and the anchor must be chosen so that this difference is predictive of the corresponding action distribution. BFP achieves this with pretrained visual features and a novel learned anchor-selection algorithm. The novel bilinear flow then models how the anchor and the residual jointly determine the multimodal action distribution. We prove that, for bilinear flow under suitable assumptions, action distribution error at unseen goals is bounded by the in-distribution flow-matching error up to problem-dependent factors. Across five manipulation tasks in simulation, BFP achieves 2.63x the out-of distribution success rate of a GCIL policy and 1.36x that of the strongest extrapolation-targeted baseline. On two real-world tasks, BFP improves over GCIL by 32%. Finally, our theory yields practical, pre deployment diagnostics for predicting which trained policies will extrapolate well and to which unseen goal.

</details>


### [88] [Human-in-the-Loop Neuro-Symbolic Drift Anticipation for Reliable Visual SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05757)
*Junhyun Nam,Wonse Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 HDS（Hybrid DeepSEE），一种人在回路（HITL）的神经符号框架，用于视觉 SLAM（V-SLAM）中的主动漂移预判。它结合神经漂移风险估计与符号约束推理，并利用 LLM 将人类定性上下文转化为可解释符号约束，以提升 OOD 环境下的可靠性与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动模型虽具预测能力，但其“黑箱”特性在分布外（OOD）环境中常产生物理不一致的输出，影响 V-SLAM 漂移预判的可靠性。

Method: HDS 将神经漂移风险估计与符号约束推理集成；采用 LLM 作为推理桥梁，把人类提供的定性上下文翻译为可解释的符号约束，形成人在回路的神经符号架构，用于主动预判 V-SLAM 漂移。

Result: 摘要未给出具体实验数据或量化结果；仅声称在此基础上提出更优的漂移预判框架，能够增强 Visual SLAM 的可靠性与一致性。

Conclusion: HDS 通过神经符号与 HITL+LLM 约束推理提升 V-SLAM 漂移预判的可解释性和 OOD 鲁棒性；但其“更优”与“增强可靠性/一致性”仍需实验验证。

Abstract: This paper introduces Hybrid DeepSEE (HDS), a Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) neuro-symbolic framework for proactive drift anticipation in Visual SLAM (V-SLAM). While data-driven models offer predictive power, their "black-box" nature often yields physically inconsistent outputs in out-of-distribution (OOD) environments. To address this, HDS integrates neural drift risk estimation with symbolic constraint reasoning. By utilizing a Large Language Model (LLM) as a reasoning bridge, the framework translates qualitative human context into interpretable symbolic constraints. Building upon this architecture, we propose a superior drift anticipation framework that ensures enhanced reliability and consistency in Visual SLAM

</details>


### [89] [Demonstration-Calibrated Port-Hamiltonian Retuning for Manipulation Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05755)
*Yulong Yang,Fan Wu,Christine Allen-Blanchette,Amit Chakraborty*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PHRetune 是一种无需评估 rollout 或增益搜索的离线方法：从示范学习端口哈密顿模型，为冻结策略闭式推导下游阻抗控制器增益，提升扩散策略与 VLA 操作成功率，并改善运动平滑性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略和 VLA 操作策略常通过下游阻抗控制器部署，其刚度与阻尼增益影响任务成功，但通常继承自数据采集而非针对部署策略选择。经验增益扫描虽可提升性能，却需要反复评估 rollout。

Method: 从示范中学习端口哈密顿模型，估计策略预测动作对应的 effort 与 energy。将策略应用于记录的示范观测，并把预测与示范导出的 effort/energy 预算比较，据此闭式推导单一增益缩放，调整下游控制器，同时保持策略及其动作表示不变。增益在评估前固定，无需先验机械臂模型、任务奖励、策略重训练或额外运行时计算。

Result: 在 LIBERO 套件上，PHRetune 使 Diffusion Policy 成功率最高提升 9.4 个百分点，推导增益达到经验增益扫描中的最高成功率。在四个真实操作任务中，PHRetuned Diffusion Policy 优于名义策略、其他增益调优方法和策略重训练基线。同一流程在每项任务上提升 SmolVLA 和 OpenVLA-OFT 成功率，并降低两个 VLA 骨干的加速度与 jerk。

Conclusion: 该工作表明可离线、无搜索地为冻结策略推导控制器增益，在仿真与真实任务中提升操作成功率，并对 VLA 策略改善运动平滑性。

Abstract: Diffusion and VLA policies for manipulation are often deployed through downstream impedance controllers. The stiffness and damping gains of these controllers affect task success, yet are commonly inherited from data collection rather than selected for the deployed policy. Although empirical gain sweeps can improve performance, they require repeated evaluation rollouts. We introduce PHRetune, an offline method that derives controller gains for a frozen policy without evaluation rollouts or gain search. Our approach learns a port-Hamiltonian model from demonstrations to estimate the effort and energy associated with the policy's predicted actions. The policy is applied to recorded demonstration observations, and its predictions are assessed against demonstration-derived effort and energy budgets. From this comparison, we derive a single gain scale in closed form, adjusting the downstream controller while preserving the policy and its action representation. The gains are fixed before evaluation, without requiring a prior manipulator model, task rewards, policy retraining, or additional runtime computation. Across LIBERO suites, PHRetune improves Diffusion Policy success by up to 9.4 percentage points, with the derived gains achieving the highest observed success rates in empirical gain sweeps. On all four real-world manipulation tasks, PHRetuned Diffusion Policy outperforms the nominal policy, alternative gain-tuning methods, and a policy-retraining baseline. The same procedure improves success with SmolVLA and OpenVLA-OFT on every task, while reducing acceleration and jerk for both VLA backbones.

</details>


### [90] [The Unexpired Plan: A Free Monitor for Accelerated Diffusion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05747)
*Yi Zhao,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究训练无关扩散策略加速中的监控器设计，主张监控器由“统计量”与“触发后响应”两部分构成，并按闭环成功率而非特征距离评估。方法利用分块策略尚未过期的上一计划，免费估计相对被替换策略的偏差，并采用不复位/吸收式响应，在保障闭环性能的同时实现每次调用1.55–3.09×的计算加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练无关加速依赖内部相似性信号认证捷径，但已有门控多用特征距离评估，且每次拒绝后重新武装；这种响应即使给oracle完整前向与精确局部动作误差也会损失20分。两个加速器可各自通过门控并将每次复用记为已认证，但在同一任务上一个完成所有回合、另一个零完成，说明认证信号不等于闭环成功。因此需要研究控制循环中真正可负担的监控统计量与响应策略。

Method: 在114种加速器配置和四个策略族上，以闭环成功率为代价评估监控器；将监控器拆为统计量与触发响应，比较重新武装与吸收/持续生效响应。统计量应衡量与加速器所替换策略的偏差，而分块策略的最后计划尚未过期，可免费读取。另在接触丰富的第五策略族上，于所有设计选择冻结后运行验证，该族被选为预期调度会失败的场景。

Result: 每次调用加监控后单次计算降低1.55–3.09×；同覆盖率下任何需要参考策略的检查将不得不停止加速。重新武装响应下oracle损失20分，吸收同一统计量仅损失1分但失去大部分速度。四个策略族中三个仅靠调度已满足预设±2点裕度。监控器价值体现在三处：第四族中挽救候选选择；四个未选中配置；第五接触丰富族中，无监控臂在同速下不满足裕度，而监控臂满足。

Conclusion: 监控器的有效性取决于稀有触发的统计量与不复位响应；应衡量相对被替换策略的偏差，而非仅特征距离，并以闭环成功率定价。如此可在训练无关扩散策略加速中实现有认证的复用与显著计算节省，同时保持任务成功裕度。

Abstract: Training-free acceleration of a diffusion policy is accepted when an internal similarity signal reports that the shortcut changed nothing. We price every monitor a control loop can afford in closed-loop success rather than feature distance, over $114$ accelerator configurations and four policy families: two accelerators each clear their own gate's bar and log every reuse as certified, while on one task one finishes every episode and the other none. A monitor is two designs, not one --- the statistic it reads, and what it does when that statistic fires. Published gates re-arm after every rejection, and under that response even an oracle handed every forward pass and the exact local action error loses twenty points; absorbing the same statistic costs one point, and most of its speed. What makes a response that never forgets affordable is a statistic that rarely fires, and what it must measure is deviation from the policy the accelerator replaced --- which a chunked policy has already paid for, its last plan not yet expired and free to read. Guarding every call this way cuts per-call compute by $1.55$--$3.09\times$, where any reference-requiring check at the same coverage would have to stop accelerating altogether. On three of our four families the schedule alone already holds the pre-stated $\pm2$-point margin. What the monitor is measurably worth shows in three places: on the fourth family, where it rescues the candidate selection landed on; on four configurations it did not select; and on a contact-rich fifth family, chosen where the schedule was expected to fail and run after every design choice was frozen, where no unmonitored arm at its speed holds the margin and the monitored one does.

</details>


### [91] [FreeSpeed: Training-Free Speed Control for Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05734)
*Yuxuan Hu,Shilin Shan,Qiheng Wang,Jinghan Yang,Junqiao Fan,Hao Wan,Jianfei Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FreeSpeed 是一个免训练模块，通过动作块的方向不一致性自适应调整机器人策略的执行速度，在保持任务成功率的同时支持在线变速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署机器人策略时需要在线控制执行速度，但模仿学习策略继承了示教速度，测试时直接变速会引入不可恢复的分布外观测并降低成功率。作者观察到动作块的方向不一致性可反映任务阶段关键性，从而指示动作步长可被多激进地修改而不损害任务成功。

Method: FreeSpeed 对预训练策略输出的动作块进行后处理：先按请求速率重采样预测的动作块，再以相邻动作之间的方向不一致性作为主要信号进行重缩放，自适应决定执行速度能多接近请求速度，从而在不牺牲成功率的前提下实现灵活变速。

Result: 在 3 类策略家族和 50 个仿真任务中，FreeSpeed 支持在线变速，在保持各任务成功的设置下实现 0.22x–2.53x 的执行速率；在 4 个真实世界操作任务中，平均成功率达 94.0%，与冻结策略的 93.8% 相当，同时实现 0.38x–1.97x 的执行速率。

Conclusion: FreeSpeed 是一种无需训练、可即插即用的执行速度在线调节方法，利用动作块方向不一致性实现任务成功与变速范围的平衡，并在仿真和真实机器人任务中验证了有效性。

Abstract: Online control of execution speed is essential for deploying robot policies in real-world scenarios, as robots may need to speed up under time constraints or slow down to facilitate human interaction and improve safety. However, imitation-learned policies inherit the execution speed of their demonstrations, and test-time speed modification can introduce unrecoverable out-of-distribution observations, reducing task success. We observe that the directional inconsistency of action chunks reflects task-phase criticality, indicating how aggressively action step lengths can be modified while preserving task success. Based on this observation, we introduce FreeSpeed, a training-free module that post-processes action chunks from pretrained policies. FreeSpeed resamples each predicted chunk at the requested rate, then uses directional inconsistency between adjacent actions as the primary signal for rescaling. This signal adaptively determines how closely the execution speed can approach the requested speed, allowing flexible speed adjustment within the evaluated limits without compromising task success. Across three policy families and 50 simulated tasks, FreeSpeed supports online speed changes, with realized execution rates spanning 0.22x to 2.53x among settings that preserve per-task success. Across four real-world manipulation tasks, FreeSpeed achieves an average success rate of 94.0%, matching the frozen policy's 93.8%, while realizing execution rates from 0.38x to 1.97x.

</details>


### [92] [End-to-End Safe Social Navigation via Multi-Task Reinforcement Learning and Probabilistic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05733)
*Tommaso Van Der Meer Andrea Garulli,Antonio Giannitrapani,Renato Quartullo,Alberto Vaglio,Alexandre Alahi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出轻量端到端强化学习框架JESSI，直接从原始LiDAR扫描输出运动可行控制命令，结合Dirichlet连续动作空间、确定性边界与注意力感知模块，实现安全、可解释、社交感知的自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主社交导航需平衡效率、物理安全与社交合规；现有RL方法常依赖不现实假设，如已知行人的位置和速度，因此需要更现实、安全且可解释的端到端方法。

Method: JESSI基于JAX构建，端到端地将LiDAR扫描映射为运动学可行控制指令；使用Dirichlet参数化连续动作空间与确定性边界增强安全性；集成注意力感知模块提取概率化人类状态以支持可解释社交决策；并以多任务范式联合优化RL策略与监督感知信号。

Result: 大量仿真和差速驱动机器人真实部署表明，联合优化RL策略与监督感知信号可提升社交行为；JESSI在导航成功率和社交行为上均优于当前最优基线，并能平衡二者。

Conclusion: JESSI能够在不依赖行人位置速度先验的情况下，实现安全、可解释且社交合规的端到端导航，兼顾高成功率与优越社交行为，并具备真实机器人部署可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous social navigation requires balancing efficiency, physical safety, and social compliance. Reinforcement Learning (RL) methods provide a viable and effective solution but often rely on unrealistic assumptions, such as the knowledge of humans' position and velocity. In this paper, we introduce JESSI (JAX-based E2E Safe Social Interpretable navigation), a lightweight end-to-end RL framework that maps raw LiDAR scans directly to kinematically feasible control commands. JESSI enhances safety via Dirichlet-parameterized continuous action spaces and deterministic bounding, while an integrated attention-based perception module extracts probabilistic human states for interpretable, socially aware decision-making. Through extensive simulations and real-world deployment on a differential-drive robot, we demonstrate that jointly optimizing the RL policy with a supervised perception signal in a multi-task paradigm enhances social behavior. Ultimately, JESSI is able to balance high navigation success rates and superior social behaviors compared to state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [93] [When to Switch: Reliable Action-Chunk Extension for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05719)
*Seonghoon Yu,Dongwon Kim,HyungRok Jung,Yoonjae Baek,Byung-kwan Lee,Suha Kwak,Jeany Son*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RACE，通过预测并条件化操作子技能切换时机，可靠扩展动作块长度，减少VLA策略调用停顿并提升机器人操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型推理开销大，导致机器人必须在策略调用间停顿，形成stop-and-go执行，既打断平滑运动又延长任务完成时间。延长动作块可减少调用和停顿，但预测更长未来会使长块执行不可靠。

Method: 分析长动作块内动作误差，发现误差集中于操作子技能切换处并随块长急剧增长；据此提出RACE，利用辅助一步去噪过程预测子技能切换时机，并以此条件化动作生成，降低切换处误差，支持更长动作块可靠执行。

Result: 仿真基准中，RACE在同块长下优于微调；使用2倍长动作块时成功率超过近期SOTA和高效VLA；使用4倍长动作块仍具竞争力。真实机器人上使用4倍长动作块，将stop-and-go空闲时间减少约5倍，且成功率高于同块长微调。

Conclusion: 显式建模并条件化子技能切换时机，是可靠扩展动作块、缓解VLA推理停顿并提升长时程操作性能的有效途径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models serve as unified policies for robotic manipulation, yet their expensive inference forces robots to pause between policy calls, resulting in stop-and-go execution that interrupts smooth motion and prolongs task completion. Extending the action chunk reduces policy calls and hence these pauses, but predicting farther into the future makes long-chunk execution unreliable. To understand where this unreliability arises, we analyze action errors within long chunks and find that they concentrate around transitions between manipulation subskills, growing sharply with chunk length. This suggests the importance of transition timing, i.e., when to switch subskills within a chunk. Motivated by this observation, we introduce RACE (Reliable Action-Chunk Extension), a framework that predicts the transition timing from an auxiliary one-step denoising pass and conditions action generation on it. By learning and conditioning on transition timing, RACE reduces errors at subskill transitions and enables reliable execution of longer action chunks. Across simulation benchmarks, RACE outperforms fine-tuning at the same chunk length; with 2x longer chunks, it surpasses recent state-of-the-art and efficient VLAs in success rate, and with 4x longer chunks, it remains competitive. On a real robot, RACE uses 4x longer chunks, which reduces the idle time caused by stop-and-go execution by about 5x, while achieving a higher success rate than fine-tuning with the same chunk length. Code and a real-robot demo are available at this https URL

</details>


### [94] [Virtual model control for compliant reaching under uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05695)
*Yi Zhang,Daniel Larby,Fumiya Iida,Fulvio Forni*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究虚拟模型控制（VMC）在力控机械臂到达任务中的应用，提出六种虚拟模型设计方法，并在8自由度力控人形机器人上验证其在障碍与不确定性环境中的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VMC此前主要用于腿式机器人运动控制，但具有推广到其他力控机器人系统的潜力；本文旨在探索其用于力控机械臂复杂到达任务的可行性。

Method: 提出六种面向到达任务的虚拟模型设计方法，以应对含障碍和不确定性的环境；使用力控8自由度人形机器人在真实世界中开展实验。

Result: 通过三个实验评估VMC控制器在可预测性、对外力敏感性和对已知/未知障碍适应性方面的表现；结果表明该方法虽牺牲精度与轨迹最优性，但能在不确定性下实现复杂到达运动。

Conclusion: VMC可直观且可扩展地设计复杂到达运动，适用于力控机器人在不确定环境中的到达任务。

Abstract: Virtual Model Control (VMC) is an approach to design a controller for force-controlled robots in complex uncertain environments. While this method was primarily investigated for legged robot locomotion in the past, it can be more generally applicable to other types of robotic systems. This paper investigates the VMC framework for reaching tasks in a force-controlled robotic arm. We propose six different approaches to designing virtual models in order to achieve reaching tasks in environments with obstacles and uncertainties. A force-controlled 8 degree-of-freedom humanoid robot was used to validate the proposed approach in the real world. We conducted three experiments to test the performance of VMC controllers in terms of predictability, sensitivity to external force, and adaptability against known and unknown obstacles. Experimental analyses show that, even though the proposed approach needs to sacrifice accuracy and trajectory optimality, it enables us to design complex reaching motions under uncertainties, in an intuitive and extendable manner.

</details>


### [95] [Benchmarking Generative Trajectory Models for Active-Inference Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05692)
*Yulin Li,Mohsen A. Jafari,Andrea Matta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GenAIF，用单一生成式轨迹模型从演示和动作干预中学习，同时提供目标条件策略分布与观测似然，以实现主动推断控制；在 MuJoCo 操作任务中，扩散模型控制最强，CVAE 短时预测相近且推理更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统动力学难以显式建模，而轨迹演示为主动推断控制提供了可行路径；需要明确生成式控制模型应满足的关键能力，并比较不同生成模型在控制、预测与信念更新中的表现。

Method: GenAIF 让一个生成式轨迹模型从演示和实测动作干预中学习，输出目标条件策略分布及状态到观测的似然映射；据此提出三项要求：有用的动作提议、施加动作下准确预测、用于信念更新和期望信息增益的概率观测证据。在多种物理条件的 MuJoCo 操作任务中，对比 diffusion、autoregressive Transformers、CVAE 和 flow matching，并进行隐藏动力学实验，用冻结模型测试未宣布倾斜变化后的适应。

Result: Diffusion 在测试动力学中控制性能最强；CVAE 具有可比的短时预测能力且推理快得多。正确条件化起决定作用，轨迹复用可进一步节省计算。隐藏动力学实验中，冻结模型在未宣布倾斜变化后能迅速调整信念，但随后出现不稳定，表明持续推断仍是挑战。

Conclusion: 结果支持在 GenAIF 中使用共享生成式轨迹模型，将动作提议、受控预测和观测证据统一连接起来。

Abstract: Learning from trajectory demonstrations offers a route to active-inference control of complex systems whose dynamics are difficult to model explicitly. We introduce generative active-inference control (GenAIF), in which one generative trajectory model learns from demonstrations and measured action interventions to supply a goal-conditioned policy distribution and a state-to-observation likelihood mapping. From this control design, we derive three model requirements: (i) useful action proposals, (ii) accurate prediction under imposed actions, and (iii) probabilistic observation evidence for belief updating and expected information gain. We benchmark diffusion, autoregressive Transformers, conditional variational autoencoders (CVAEs), and flow matching in a MuJoCo manipulation task with multiple physical conditions. Diffusion delivers the strongest control across the tested dynamics, while CVAE combines comparable short-horizon prediction with much faster inference. Correct conditioning is decisive, and trajectory reuse offers further computational savings. With the same frozen models, a hidden-dynamics experiment demonstrates prompt belief adaptation after an unannounced tilt change; subsequent instability identifies sustained inference as a remaining challenge. These findings support the use of shared generative trajectory models to connect action proposal, controlled prediction, and observation evidence within GenAIF.

</details>


### [96] [Dataset-Free Compliant Humanoid Loco-Manipulation with Dynamic Online Posture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05678)
*Seungho Yeom,Zhenyu Wu,Jaeyoung Huh,Diego Williams,Yuheng Zhi,Soofiyan Atar,Michael Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OCLO 是一种无需人体运动数据、仅由两个末端执行器目标指令驱动的人形 loco-manipulation 系统，通过在线姿态生成与全身柔顺学习实现平衡与操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人形 loco-manipulation 控制器多依赖人体运动数据学习全身协调与姿态，导致策略依赖外部数据源；而仅给定末端目标时，全身姿态并不唯一确定。

Method: OCLO 仅接收两个末端执行器目标，利用解析可达性先验在线生成骨盆高度与躯干朝向，并通过策略闭环采样和任务无关代价进行细化；同时使用弹簧-阻尼模型根据测得力偏移末端参考，学习全身柔顺，使腿、腰、骨盆顺应外部负载。

Result: 仿真中，可达性先验将指令参考获取成功率从 37.8% 提升至 77.8%；细化降低所有评估任务的末端朝向误差；同一姿态模块在 5 项任务中的 4 项上改进预训练 SONIC 控制器；无柔顺训练时策略受扰更易失去平衡而非牺牲跟踪。在 Unitree G1 上，OCLO 在导致消融实验失败的末端扰动下保持平衡，并完成 7 项 loco-manipulation 任务，包括蹲姿行走和从低处拾取箱子。

Conclusion: OCLO 表明无需人体运动数据、仅用末端目标即可实现人形全身 loco-manipulation，并通过在线姿态先验与柔顺训练提升成功率、姿态精度、抗扰平衡能力和任务多样性。

Abstract: Most humanoid loco-manipulation controllers require human motion data to learn whole-body coordination and posture, leaving policies reliant on external sources to provide this data. We present OCLO (Online-posture Compliant LOco-manipulation), a humanoid loco-manipulation system trained without human motion data and commanded only through two end-effector targets. Because these targets do not uniquely determine whole-body posture, OCLO generates pelvis height and torso orientation online using an analytic reachability prior, further refined through policy-in-the-loop sampling with a task-agnostic cost. OCLO also learns whole-body compliance by displacing end-effector references according to measured forces through a spring-damper model, encouraging the legs, waist, and pelvis to yield to external loads. In simulation, using the reachability prior leads to a 77.8% success rate in acquiring the commanded reference, a vast improvement over the 37.8% success rate accomplished without the prior. Further, refinement reduces end-effector orientation error across all evaluated tasks. The same posture module improves a pretrained SONIC controller on four of five tasks. Without compliance training, policies tend to lose balance under disturbances rather than sacrifice tracking. On a Unitree G1, OCLO maintains balance under end-effector disturbances that cause its ablations to fail and performs seven loco-manipulation tasks, including crouched walking and picking up a box from a low surface. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [97] [Learning Coordinated Visuomotor Box-Pushing from Solo Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05677)
*Ryusei Matsumoto,Mei Kinjo,Yoko Sasaki,Keisuke Okumura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究无通信、机载去中心化的多机器人模仿学习，以大规模协同推箱为测试平台，探索如何利用单机器人遥操作数据，并系统考察数据集构建策略与轻量策略架构；最多40个机器人的实验表明，仅靠被动观察其他运行机器人难以获得有效协同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人模仿学习，尤其是无通信、机载去中心化的视觉运动策略，具有吸引力，但其实现仍缺乏充分理解，主要瓶颈在于集体演示数据难以收集：单个操作者无法同时控制多个机器人。同时，许多协同任务不同于耦合式协作操作，其系统效率主要来自最小化机器人间干扰。因此，作者希望研究能否利用单机器人遥操作采集的数据，服务于大规模协同推箱任务。

Method: 作者以大规模协同推箱为测试平台，在无通信、机载去中心化的设定下，系统研究数据集创建策略和轻量级策略架构，并利用单机器人遥操作数据进行学习；实验规模扩展至最多40个机器人，重点检验仅通过被动观察其他正在运行的机器人能否获得有效协同。

Result: 在最多40个机器人的实验中，仅通过被动观察其他正在运行的机器人，难以获得有效的协同行为。这一结果揭示了多机器人模仿学习中的一个具体瓶颈。

Conclusion: 单机器人遥操作数据有望用于大规模协同推箱等任务，但被动观察不足以实现有效多机器人协同。数据集创建策略与轻量策略架构是关键因素，而实验揭示的瓶颈为后续多机器人研究提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Multi-robot imitation learning, particularly in settings where visuomotor policies are deployed in a communication-free, onboard decentralised style, represents an attractive paradigm. However, its realisation remains insufficiently understood, largely due to the difficulty of collecting collective demonstrations, since a single operator cannot control many robots simultaneously. Meanwhile, unlike coupled collaborative manipulation, many coordinated tasks achieve system-wide efficiency primarily through minimising inter-robot interference. This structure motivates us to study whether data collected by a teleoperated single-robot can be leveraged for large-scale coordinated box-pushing as a testbed. We systematically investigate dataset creation strategies and lightweight policy architectures. In particular, experiments with up to 40 robots highlight the difficulty of acquiring effective coordination solely through passive observation of other operating robots, revealing a concrete bottleneck for multi-robot research.

</details>


### [98] [Bayesian Data Augmentation for DNN Retraining with Binomial Outcomes in Vision-Based UAV Landing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05674)
*Ashik E Rasul,Hyung-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种贝叶斯数据增强框架，结合高保真车辆动力学的逼真仿真器，迭代重训练停机坪检测DNN，以优化无人机视觉着陆性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在GPS拒止或杂乱城市环境中，视觉着陆对无人机任务可靠性至关重要；真实着陆点非结构化且多变，要求感知系统具有强泛化能力。合成数据增强虽可扩展，但DNN重训练计算昂贵，试飞验证困难，限制了模型充分微调，因此需要优化的重训练流程。

Method: 部署贝叶斯数据增强框架，集成具有高保真车辆动力学的逼真仿真器，以着陆性能为目标函数，迭代重训练停机坪检测DNN；并在不同环境条件和车辆状态下进行仿真实验验证。

Result: 在逼真仿真器中的实验表明，所提框架提升了着陆性能，并使预测着陆结果的置信区间更紧。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯数据增强与高保真仿真结合的迭代重训练流程，可有效优化停机坪检测器，提高无人机视觉着陆的性能与预测可靠性。

Abstract: In GPS-denied or cluttered urban environments, vision-based landing is essential for reliable UAV missions. Real-world landing sites are often unstructured and highly variable, requiring strong generalization by the perception system. Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) trained with synthetic data augmentation offer a scalable solution for learning landing-site features across diverse vehicle and environmental states. However, computationally expensive DNN retraining, along with challenging performance validation via test flights, limits exhaustive model fine-tuning and necessitates an optimized retraining pipeline. In this work, we deploy a Bayesian data augmentation framework integrated with a photorealistic simulator featuring high-fidelity vehicle dynamics to iteratively retrain the helipad detector DNN, maximizing landing performance as the objective function. We validate our framework with experiments in a photorealistic simulator under different environmental conditions and vehicle states, demonstrating improved landing performance and tighter confidence intervals on predicted landing outcomes.

</details>


### [99] [Spacecraft Rendezvous Trajectory Generation with Modular Constraints via Diffusion Model Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05642)
*Mariko A. Storey-Matsutani,Richard Linares*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于扩散模型的RPO轨迹生成框架，通过组合独立训练的能量模型来灵活满足多种任务约束，无需重新训练即可适配新的约束组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在轨服务、卫星检查、主动碎片清除等新兴任务需要适应多种场景的轨迹设计方法，尤其是交会与近距离操作（RPO）中需灵活配置任务约束。

Method: 首先针对接近锥、传感器视线等不同约束，利用优化轨迹分别训练独立的能量扩散模型；推理时将这些学习到的能量模型相互组合，或与解析定义的能量场组合，以施加特定约束组合。

Result: 验证了学习接近锥模型与学习传感器视线模型组合，以及学习接近锥模型与合成避障模型组合，约束满足率与单约束模型相差不超过1个百分点或更高。

Conclusion: 该组合扩散框架可为RPO轨迹设计提供模块化方法，支持新约束组合的重构，而无需重新训练模型。

Abstract: Emerging mission classes such as on-orbit servicing, satellite inspection, and active debris removal require trajectory design methods that are adaptable to a variety of mission scenarios. We present a diffusion-based trajectory generation approach for rendezvous and proximity operations (RPO) that enables flexible configuration of mission constraints. First, individual energy-based diffusion models are trained to satisfy distinct constraints such as approach cone and sensor line-of-sight from a set of optimized trajectories. Then, at inference time, the learned energy models can be composed with one another, or with an analytically defined energy field, to enforce specific constraint combinations. We validate this framework with the composition of a learned approach cone model and a learned sensor line-of-sight model, as well as a learned approach cone model and synthetic obstacle avoidance model, both of which yield constraint satisfaction rates that are within 1 percentage point of the single-constraint models or higher. These results indicate that our compositional diffusion framework can provide a modular approach to RPO trajectory design and enable reconfiguration for new constraint combinations without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [100] [FOCUS: Fine-Grained Open-Vocabulary Change Detection for Uncertainty-Aware Semi-Static Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05639)
*Can Xu,Mingfeng Yuan,Mahan Mohammadi,Steven L. Waslander*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FOCUS：一种面向半静态环境的物体级变化检测与地图维护框架，通过融合几何与3D高斯渲染外观的概率推理，递归估计物体状态为PERSISTED、REPLACED或REMOVED。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期运行的自主机器人需持续更新环境记忆；在半静态环境中，物体可能被几何和语义相似的新实例原地替换，仅靠几何或粗粒度语义难以检测身份变化。

Method: 将半静态记忆维护建模为概率推断，融合几何似然与由3D高斯地图渲染的外观似然；使用递归三状态估计器判断每个地图物体是PERSISTED、REPLACED还是REMOVED；针对3DGS渲染不完美，将渲染外观证据概率化建模而非直接作为变化分数，并利用初始建图会话的无变化回放自动校准模型参数。

Result: 在包含歧义原地替换的新Isaac Sim仓库基准和真实世界TorWIC数据集上评估；物体级替换F1从概率基线的0.20提升至0.84，并能无需人工重新调参迁移到真实数据。

Conclusion: FOCUS通过不确定性感知的概率融合与自动校准，有效提升半静态环境下物体级变化检测和地图维护性能，并具备真实场景迁移能力。

Abstract: Autonomous robots operating over long periods must keep their environmental memory up to date as the world changes between visits. In semi-static environments, an object may be replaced in place by a different but geometrically and semantically similar instance, making the identity change difficult to detect from geometry or coarse semantics alone. We present FOCUS, an uncertainty-aware framework for object-level change detection and map maintenance. We formulate semi-static memory maintenance as probabilistic inference that fuses geometric likelihood with appearance likelihood rendered from a 3D Gaussian map. A recursive three-state estimator maintains whether each mapped object is PERSISTED, REPLACED, or REMOVED. To account for imperfect 3DGS rendering, we model the rendered appearance evidence probabilistically rather than using it as a direct change score, with the model parameters automatically calibrated from a change-free replay of the initial mapping session. We evaluate our method on a new Isaac Sim warehouse benchmark with ambiguous in-place replacements and on the real-world TorWIC dataset. It improves object-level replacement F1 from 0.20 to 0.84 over a probabilistic baseline and transfers to real-world data without manual parameter retuning.

</details>


### [101] [Emergency Obstacle Avoidance Maneuvers in Differential-Drive Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05607)
*Kautik Mandve,Nilay Kant*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究差速驱动机器人在不同接近速度下紧急避障机动的性能与里程计误差，发现反向自旋机动的编码器航向偏差随速度显著增大，并与避障成功率下降相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 紧急避障要求机器人在可用距离内制动或转向，因此需要理解接近速度如何影响差速驱动机器人的机动性能与里程计误差。

Method: 通过足迹间隙分析和归一化轮运动指标，对五种制动与转向机动进行运动学描述；在瓷砖地面上开展540次区组随机试验（保留539次），指令接近速度为45、55、65 cm/s；使用俯视相机提供独立位姿参考，并在完整运动记录上比较编码器、陀螺仪与相机的航向变化。

Result: 在最低与最高速度之间，两种反向自旋机动的平均编码器偏差增加4.7–5.1°，而弧线机动和制动辅助枢轴转向的变化小于0.4°；在调整距离、速度、机动、日期和转向方向后，较大的编码器偏差与较低的避障成功率相关；反向自旋的陀螺仪偏差约为3°；还估计了90%成功率所需反应距离及其不确定性，以量化机动权衡。

Conclusion: 结果表明应按速度对紧急机动进行经验性表征，并将编码器误差与恢复运动及测量窗口不匹配区分开来。

Abstract: Emergency obstacle avoidance requires a mobile robot to brake or change its heading within the available distance. This paper investigates the dependence of maneuver performance and odometric error on approach speed for a differential-drive robot. A footprint-clearance analysis and a normalized wheel-motion index provide a kinematic description of five braking and turning maneuvers. The principal experiment comprises 540 block-randomized trials on tile, of which 539 are retained, at commanded approach speeds of 45, 55, and 65 cm/s. An overhead camera provides an independent pose reference. When encoder, gyro, and camera heading changes are evaluated over complete motion records, the mean encoder discrepancy increases by 4.7-5.1 degrees for the two reverse-spin maneuvers between the lowest and highest speeds; the arc and brake-assisted pivot change by less than 0.4 degrees. Larger encoder discrepancy is associated with lower avoidance success after adjustment for distance, speed, maneuver, day, and turn direction. Gyro discrepancies remain approximately 3 degrees for the reverse spins. Estimated reaction distances for 90% success and their uncertainty quantify the maneuver trade-offs. The results support speed-specific empirical characterization of emergency maneuvers and distinguish encoder error from recovery motion and measurement-window mismatch.

</details>


### [102] [Building A Multi-Sensor Platform For Autonomous Driving Research: Challenges and Lessons Learned](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05604)
*Paulo Ricardo Marques de Araujo,Eslam Mounier,Qamar Bader,Emma Dawson,Aboelmagd Noureldin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文总结自动驾驶研究中柔性多传感器平台开发与部署的挑战和经验，并给出时间同步、传感器标定与可靠性设计等缓解策略，为后续平台设计提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠且多样的数据集需求不断增长，新环境和传感配置对解决真实场景挑战至关重要；因此许多团队自建多传感器采集平台，但无论传感器类型如何，机械设计、传感器标定、电源管理和时间同步等基础问题都会出现。

Method: 基于平台开发与部署实践，反思所遇挑战并分享关键洞见；同时总结采用的缓解策略，包括使用GNSS授时和NTP协议进行系统级时间同步、针对不同传感器配置的定制标定流程，以及提升现场部署可靠性和数据完整性的设计实践。

Result: 形成了多传感器平台在机械设计、标定、供电与时间同步等方面的经验总结和缓解策略，旨在提升系统可靠性与数据完整性。

Conclusion: 这些经验可为未来多传感器平台设计提供指导，增强自动驾驶测试的可复现性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper reports on the challenges encountered and lessons learned during the development and deployment of a flexible multi-sensor platform for autonomous driving research. It aims to serve as a reference for researchers developing new multi-sensor systems. As the need for reliable, diverse datasets increases, novel environments and sensing configurations are essential to tackle real-world operational challenges. Consequently, many research groups create custom multi-sensory data collection platforms, where fundamental issues, such as mechanical design, sensor calibration, power management, and time synchronization, arise regardless of sensor types. We reflect on these challenges and share key insights to guide future platform designs, enhancing reproducibility and robustness in autonomous vehicle testing. We also summarize the mitigation strategies we adopted, for instance, system-wide time synchronization using GNSS timing and NTP protocols, custom calibration routines for different sensor configurations, and design practices to improve reliability and data integrity during field deployments.

</details>


### [103] [TacGELLO: Tactile Contact Feedback for a 3D-Printed Teleoperation Leader](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05583)
*Younsoo Kim,Sangmin Song,Sarath Kodagoda,Marc Carmichael,Karthick Thiyagarajan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TacGELLO 利用 3D 打印被动主臂的现有舵机，为遥操作提供夹爪触觉接触反馈和关节负载提示，并在 UR3 实验中实现低跟踪误差与更精确的闭合控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动主臂虽然能传递操作者运动，但无法回传远端接触或负载线索，导致操作者难以感知抓取接触与提升阻力。

Method: TacGELLO 复用 3D 打印主臂的现有舵机：在夹爪扳机处提供触觉接触反馈，在手臂关节处提供基于电流的负载线索；AnySkin 检测到接触后触发扳机位置保持，而跟随端关节电流变化会调整主端舵机输出限幅，以在提升时产生阻力提示。

Result: 在单人操作者进行的六次 UR3 会话中，发送指令跟踪误差为 0.15--0.36° RMSE，估计轨迹滞后为 82--98 ms；62 个分割的 TacGELLO 抓取区间中有 60 个记录到触觉起始。基于触觉或宽度接触参考后的中位指令闭合量为 TacGELLO 0.7--2.1 mm，原界面为 13.0--13.9 mm。

Conclusion: TacGELLO 无需额外主端硬件即可为被动主臂补充触觉接触与负载反馈，并显著降低接触后的指令闭合量，从而提升遥操作抓取控制的反馈质量与精度。

Abstract: Passive leader arms transmit an operator's motion without returning remote contact or load cues. TacGELLO uses the existing servos of a 3D-printed leader for tactile contact feedback at the gripper trigger and current-based load cues at the arm joints. AnySkin contact detection activates a trigger position hold, while changes in follower joint current adjust leader-servo output limits to command resistance during lifting. Across six UR3 sessions with one operator, sent-command tracking error was 0.15--0.36$^\circ$ RMSE, with estimated trajectory lag of 82--98\,ms. Tactile onset was logged in 60 of 62 segmented TacGELLO grasp intervals. Median commanded closure after tactile or width-based contact references was 0.7--2.1\,mm with TacGELLO and 13.0--13.9\,mm with the original interface.

</details>


### [104] [GR-LIO: A Local Ground-Aware LiDAR-Inertial Odometry System Using Body-to-Ground Height](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05546)
*Zhixin Zhang,Yang Song,Liang Zhao,Nathan Shankar,Barry Lennox,Pawel Ladosz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于滤波器的局部地面感知LiDAR-惯性里程计框架，将局部地面平面几何显式引入状态估计，以提升定位精度与计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LIO系统在陆地自主移动机器人中广泛应用，但局部地面提供的几何约束尚未被充分利用；论文旨在挖掘地面平面几何信息以改善状态估计。

Method: 将局部地面平面由机器人姿态和机体到地面高度参数化，并在状态估计中连续传播；基于该B-G几何模型实现高效可靠的地面分割，将地面点通过点到面约束加入滤波器更新；引入平面运动更新，把传播的局部地面平面作为额外几何约束以抑制垂直漂移；针对初始未知的B-G高度，设计高效初始化策略并进行在线标定持续优化。

Result: 在多个公开基准数据集和自采集真实场景数据集上评估，覆盖多样化运行场景；实验表明该方法在定位精度和计算效率上持续优于代表性LIO方法。

Conclusion: 显式利用局部地面平面几何的滤波式LIO框架能够有效提升地面机器人状态估计的精度、效率与鲁棒性，并抑制垂直漂移。

Abstract: LiDAR-inertial odometry (LIO) is widely used for state estimation in ground-based autonomous mobile robots. However, the geometric constraints provided by the local ground surface remain largely underexploited in existing LIO systems. This paper proposes a filter-based local ground-aware LIO framework that explicitly incorporates local ground plane geometry into the state estimation process to improve both localization accuracy and computational efficiency. Specifically, a local ground plane is parameterized by the robot orientation and the body-to-ground (B-G) height and continuously propagated within the state estimation process. Based on the proposed B-G geometry model, a propagated local ground plane enables efficient and reliable ground segmentation. The segmented ground points are then incorporated into the filter update through point-to-plane geometric constraints, improving both state estimation accuracy and efficiency. Furthermore, a planar motion update is introduced to exploit the propagated local ground plane as an additional geometric constraint, effectively suppressing vertical drift and improving estimation robustness. To address the initially unknown B-G height, an efficient initialization strategy is developed, followed by an online calibration procedure for continuous refinement. The proposed system is evaluated on several public benchmark datasets and self-collected real-world datasets covering diverse operating scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms representative LIO methods in terms of both localization accuracy and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [105] [Neural Barriers: An Online Certifiable Learning-enhanced Adaptive High Order Safety Critical Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05542)
*Lishuo Pan,Mattia Catellani,An Cao,Lorenzo Sabattini,Nora Ayanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可认证的在线学习增强鲁棒自适应控制障碍函数，结合Neural ODE与保形预测，在未知时变模型扰动下提供概率安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统控制障碍函数依赖高保真模型，而载荷、风扰等会引起显著模型摄动，使空中机器人等实际系统的安全认证失效。

Method: 使用Neural ODE在线学习未知时变扰动，并用保形预测量化自适应不确定性；不确定性高时采取保守策略，数据增多后自适应降低控制器保守性；在模型与轨迹满足Lipschitz光滑假设下给出安全保证。

Result: 方法在未知时变模型扰动下保证所有时刻安全，并提供概率安全界；能随数据积累有效适应并减少保守性。

Conclusion: 结果表明该方法有望成为模型摄动下机器人系统的实用安全控制器。

Abstract: Control barrier functions are an effective model-based tool to formally certify the safety of a system. However, transferring their theoretical guarantees to real-world robotics systems requires high model fidelity. For example, payloads or wind disturbances can cause significant model perturbations to an aerial vehicle, leading to safety compromises. In this work, we propose a certifiable online learning-enhanced robust adaptive control barrier function, which adapts to disturbances using a Neural ODE and quantifies its adaptation uncertainty with conformal prediction. Our approach guarantees safety at all time under unknown time-varying model disturbances. It adopts a conservative strategy when the adaptation uncertainty is high; and efficiently adapts to reduce controller conservativeness as it receives more data. Our approach provides a provable safety guarantee with a probability bound under suitable Lipschitz smoothness assumptions on the underlying model and trajectory. These results demonstrate the potential of our method as a practical safety controller for robotics system operating under model perturbations.

</details>


### [106] [Robust 2D Traversability Mapping for Construction AMRs via Failure-Mode-Aware Fusion of LiDAR Geometry and Monocular Semantics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05505)
*Manoj Karnekar,Om Mandhane,Gautham Ramkumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向活跃施工工地的实时、失效模式感知多模态可通行性流水线：以LiDAR为几何安全主估计，单目语义经类别与置信度门控选择性校正，并在Jetson AGX Orin上部署；同时发布多模态施工工地数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AMR在活跃工地导航时，纯几何LiDAR可通行性地图会漏检湿泥、积水混凝土等视觉危险但几何平坦区域，又会把减速带、坡道等可行驶突变几何误判为幻影障碍。

Method: 在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin上构建实时多模态流水线：LiDAR投影至密集语义掩码，形态学修复稀疏性，结合Patchwork++进行失效模式感知融合；语义作为类别与置信度门控的选择性纠正信号；表示保留平地、地形、岩石地形等可通行类别并标记施工危险。

Result: 发布来自定制AMR的多模态施工工地数据集：两个活跃工地、四条闭环ROS 2序列（RGB、深度、LiDAR、IMU、GPS-RTK、里程计），以及28类、506帧语义标注；系统可纠正互补的几何失效模式并生成局部AMR代价地图。摘要未给出定量精度或实时帧率指标。

Conclusion: LiDAR主导几何安全、视觉语义选择性纠正的失效模式感知融合，可弥补纯几何可通行性映射在施工工地中的互补缺陷，为局部代价地图提供更鲁棒的可通行性估计。

Abstract: Autonomous Mobile Robots (AMRs) on active construction sites face severe navigational challenges: geometry-based traversability mapping (e.g., LiDAR) misses visually hazardous but geometrically flat surfaces like wet mud and ponding concrete, while abrupt geometry on drivable speed-breakers and inclines produces phantom obstacles. We propose a real-time, failure-mode-aware multimodal traversability pipeline on an NVIDIA Jetson AGX Orin, where LiDAR is the primary geometric safety estimate and monocular semantics act as a selective, class- and confidence-gated corrective signal. The representation retains distinct traversable classes, namely flat road, terrain, and rocky terrain, while flagging construction hazards. We also release a multimodal construction-site dataset from a custom AMR: four closed-loop ROS 2 sequences from two active sites (RGB, depth, LiDAR, IMU, GPS-RTK, odometry) plus 506 annotated frames across 28 semantic classes. By projecting LiDAR onto dense semantic masks, resolving sparsity via morphological in-painting, and applying failure-mode-aware fusion with Patchwork++, the system corrects complementary geometric failure modes for a local AMR costmap.

</details>


### [107] [Robust Surgical Robotic Instrument Tracking via Sequential Multi-Cue Fusion and Sim-to-Real Self-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05491)
*Hanyang Hu,Zekai Liang,Florian Richter,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种由跟踪器引导的自训练框架，将合成图像上预训练的模型自适应到无标注真实手术视频，通过EKF/RTS平滑生成伪标签以微调关键点检测器，从而提升手术机器人器械跟踪的精度与速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人器械的高效鲁棒跟踪对机器人辅助微创手术很重要，但手术场景复杂且器械几何形状特殊，跟踪仍具挑战。基于关键点的方法效率高，但依赖可靠的特征检测；而真实世界精确标注获取成本高、难以规模化，限制了检测器的监督改进。

Method: 引入tracker-guided self-training框架：给定机器人关节状态，使用不确定性感知EKF，通过比较投影模型特征与检测到的关键点、轴边界和掩码线索，递归校正器械位姿及可观测关节角；随后用RTS smoother细化轨迹，并将轨迹投影为伪标签，用于微调特征检测器，无需昂贵的位姿标注。

Result: 在真实世界视频实验中，自训练在所有评估的关键点指标上均带来一致提升，所得模型在准确率和运行时方面均优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 该方法能够利用无标注真实视频有效改进手术器械关键点检测与跟踪，代码和数据将在发表后公开。

Abstract: Efficient and robust tracking of surgical robotic instruments is important for robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery, yet remains challenging due to the complexity of surgical scenes and the unconventional geometry of surgical instruments. Keypoint-based approaches are efficient, but their performance depends on reliable feature detection. Improving these detectors with real-world supervision is difficult because accurate real-world annotations are costly to obtain at scale. To address this limitation, we introduce a tracker-guided self-training framework that adapts a model pretrained on synthetic images to unlabeled real-world videos. Given measured robot joint states, an uncertainty-aware EKF recursively corrects the instrument pose and the observable joint angles by comparing projected model features with detected keypoints, shaft boundaries, and mask-derived cues. An RTS smoother subsequently refines the resulting trajectory, which is projected into pseudo-labels for fine-tuning the feature detector without laborious pose annotations. Experiments on real-world videos demonstrate consistent improvements from self-training across all evaluated keypoint metrics, and the resulting model outperforms prior approaches in both accuracy and runtime. The code and data will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [108] [FLEX-WAM: Flexible Block-Causal World-Action Models for Long-Horizon Imagination and Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05483)
*R. Khorrambakht,Joseph Amigo,Félix Lebel,Leon Seetoo,Jean Ponce,Zhenzhen Li,Ludovic Righetti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 FLEX-WAM：一种灵活高效的块因果世界-动作模型，统一动作条件未来预测、可行动作生成与想象规划，支持流式长时程 rollout 和实时部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联合视频-动作模型通常依赖计算量大、固定时域的主干网络，不适合流式推理，也难以进行稳定长时程开环 rollout；同时联合训练可能生成对指令动作响应弱的看似合理的未来。

Method: FLEX-WAM 采用块因果、可 KV 缓存的架构，结合轴向注意力与块级扩散强制（blockwise diffusion forcing），支持变长上下文、非因果预测时域，以及逐帧或逐块的无限自回归生成，从而无需重训练即可实现实时 rollout 和部署时延-吞吐权衡。针对动作响应弱的问题，在状态-动作扩散噪声网格上平衡状态与动作 flow-matching 梯度贡献，并使用 Forward-Dynamics elasticity 作为高效训练时动作响应代理来调节世界模型采样。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上，FLEX-WAM 取得更优的多步预测质量与时延，并能产生稳定的联合状态-动作 rollout 数千步。作为 MCTS 中的联合动作提议器与模拟器，它完全在想象中解决长时程 PushT 和全部五个 OGBench Puzzle-4x4 任务。在基于 OpenArm 的双臂机器人上，单一 checkpoint 同时作为 play policy 和预期结果预测器，实时识别并收集模型-现实不匹配以供未来自改进。

Conclusion: FLEX-WAM 展示了统一、高效且可扩展的世界-动作建模能力，可用于长时程想象规划、实时策略推理以及真实机器人上的模型-现实不匹配收集与自改进。

Abstract: World--action models (WAMs) promise a unified model that predicts action-conditioned futures, generates feasible actions, and supports planning in imagination.
