However, existing joint video--action models often use computationally heavy, fixed-horizon backbones ill-suited to streaming inference and stable long-horizon open-loop rollouts. We introduce FLEX-WAM, a Flexible and Efficient Block-Causal World--Action Model for unified simulation and policy inference. FLEX-WAM supports variable-length contexts and non-causal prediction horizons, as well as infinite autoregressive generation frame by frame or block by block. Its block-causal, KV-cacheable architecture combines axial attention and blockwise diffusion forcing to enable efficient real-time rollout and deployment-time latency--throughput tradeoffs without retraining. Joint training can nevertheless produce plausible futures that weakly respond to commanded actions. We address this failure mode by balancing state and action flow-matching gradient contributions across the state--action diffusion-noise grid and regulating world-model sampling using Forward-Dynamics (FD) elasticity, an efficient training-time proxy for action responsiveness. Across simulated and real-world datasets, FLEX-WAM achieves superior multi-step prediction quality and latency while producing stable joint state--action rollouts for thousands of steps. As a joint action proposer and simulator within MCTS, it solves long-horizon PushT and all five OGBench Puzzle-4x4 tasks entirely in imagination. On a bimanual OpenArm-based robot, a single checkpoint jointly serves as a play policy and expected-outcome predictor, enabling real-time identification and collection of model--reality mismatches for future self-improvement.

</details>


### [109] [Social Navigation for Tour-guide Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05455)
*Vikram Shree,Jose Nino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于导游机器人社会导航的力模型，将障碍物、用户位置和用户朝向等社会力纳入；设计用户跟随实验，以A*最短路径为基线，通过每轮主观指标评估可跟随性、安全感和智能感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 让导游机器人在用户跟随场景中表现得更具社交性，需要显式建模障碍物、用户位置与朝向等因素对人机交互的影响。

Method: 构建力基础社会导航模型，考虑障碍物、用户位置和用户朝向等社会力；实验中被试在已知地图中跟随自主机器人到达目的地，途中完成简单分心子任务；每名被试参与多轮实验，每轮使用不同社会力配置以及A*最短路径基线；采用每轮主观指标进行评价。

Result: 摘要未报告具体实验结果，仅提出实验评估方案与待研究构念，包括可跟随性、感知安全性和感知智能性。

Conclusion: 拟通过主观指标分析各社会力对机器人社交性的影响，并验证所提力模型相较最短路径基线的效果。

Abstract: We propose a force-based model for social navigation of a tour-guide robot. Social forces due to various factors like obstacles, user position and heading, have been accounted for in the model. We claim that each one of these forces makes the robot more sociable to the user and we design an experimental setup for evaluation. In the experiment, the user follows an autonomous robot to a destination in a known map, while undertaking a few simple sub-tasks in the middle, which serve as distractions. For each participant, we run several rounds of the experiment, each with different forces and a shortest path, A*-search baseline model. Using per-round subjective indicators, we propose to study the effect of our force model on constructs such as: follow-ability, perceived safety, and perceived intelligence.

</details>


### [110] [EvoMem-VLA: State-Evolution Memory for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05418)
*Yuheng Na,Zhide Zhong,Junjie He,Junfeng Li,Haodong Yan,Jiaan Wang,Jiaguan Zhu,Yangyang Zheng,Tianyu Huang,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 EvoMem-VLA，通过显式编码历史状态间的变化构建状态演化记忆，使视觉-语言-动作策略能在长时程、依赖记忆的任务中跟踪任务进展，并在多个仿真与真实机器人基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型主要依赖当前观测，一旦任务相关证据离开视野便会丢失；已有压缩历史特征或稀疏视觉关键帧的方法仅提供孤立快照，难以让策略理解过去交互中发生了什么变化以及下一步应执行什么动作。

Method: EvoMem-VLA 构建状态演化记忆，显式编码并保留历史状态之间的观测变化。具体引入条件式 delta tokenization，将有序帧对编码为方向性、源条件化的 delta token，并关联对应状态证据。共享 VLM 主干支持任务自适应路由：普通长时程任务走直接动作路由，多阶段任务走子任务路由，生成可执行子任务作为动作生成的额外输入。每个仿真基准使用单个联合训练策略。

Result: 在 RMBench 上成功率达 80.7%，RoboMME 上达 82.0%，跨两种机器人本体的四个真实任务上达 83.8%；三个评估设置均显著优于此前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 显式建模状态演化与交互结果的变化表征，可弥补孤立快照的不足，提升 VLA 在长时程、记忆依赖任务中的决策能力与泛化性能。

Abstract: Most vision-language-action (VLA) models rely on current observations and lose task-relevant evidence once it leaves view, limiting performance on long-horizon, memory-dependent tasks. Existing efforts incorporate compressed historical features or sparse visual keyframes. However, isolated snapshots can leave the policy uncertain about what changed during past interactions and which action should follow. To overcome this limitation, we propose EvoMem-VLA, which constructs state-evolution memory by explicitly encoding and retaining observed changes between historical states. These change representations preserve evidence of interaction outcomes, allowing the policy to track task progress beyond isolated snapshots. Specifically, we introduce conditional delta tokenization to encode ordered frame pairs into directional, source-conditioned delta tokens, each associated with its corresponding state evidence. A shared VLM backbone supports task-adaptive routing: normal long-horizon tasks follow a direct action route, whereas multi-stage tasks use a subtask route that generates an executable subtask as an additional input for action generation. With a single jointly trained policy for each simulation benchmark, EvoMem-VLA achieves success rates of 80.7\% on RMBench, 82.0\% on RoboMME and 83.8\% across four real-world tasks spanning two robot embodiments. These results represent substantial improvements over the previous state of the art in all three evaluation settings.

</details>


### [111] [Nudge Before You Push: Physics-Aware Navigation via Tactile Probing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04924)
*Xianyao Li,Fang Xu,Ruitong Tian,Bowen Sun,Xiao Hu,Yang Ye,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TANav 通过短促轻推测量容器推动阻力，结合 TacPhys 力序列质量估计与重复巡逻规划，在给定搬运边界下实现更安全、更高效的导航与推运决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉上完全相同的容器可能装载不同重量的物品，因而需要不同的搬运或推运决策；仅靠视觉或峰值力阈值难以可靠判断能否安全推动，且重复巡逻中还需要权衡探测成本与路径收益。

Method: TANav 使用短暂轻推测量推动阻力；TacPhys 将力序列（可选结合 RGB-D 与运动学）映射为质量估计，用于推动授权。重复巡逻规划器联合优化探测成本与路径成本，必要时请求第二次接触，并跨访问复用观测结果。

Result: 仿真中，TacPhys 接近仅基于阻力的 Bayes 参考，在相当低风险工作点下将漏推率从 28.7% 降至 5.5%（相对峰值力阈值）。重复巡逻仿真中，TANav 恢复 oracle 路径节省的 90%，人工干预较始终绕行减少一半以上，边界违规率从 4.3% 降至 2.9%（相对仅 RGB-D 探测）。在带 Hall 阵列指尖的四足机械臂上，离线零样本 MAE 为 0.28–1.07 kg（容器最大 2.82 kg）；3 kg 力上升校准获得 93.5% 汇总离线准确率（非立方体回合为 86.4%）；单独原始峰值规则在未见箱体上实现 20 次在线决策中 15 次正确。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于接触力与推动阻力的主动探测和规划可显著减少误推与人工干预，并在真实机器人上实现可用的零样本质量估计与在线推动授权。

Abstract: Visually identical containers can conceal loads that require different handling decisions. We present TANav, which uses a brief nudge to measure push resistance for navigation under a site-defined handling boundary. TacPhys reads the force sequence, with optional RGB-D and kinematics, into a mass estimate for push authorization. A repeated-patrol planner weighs probe and route costs, requests a second contact when useful, and reuses observations across visits. In simulation, TacPhys approaches a resistance-only Bayes reference and reduces missed pushes from 28.7% to 5.5% relative to peak-force thresholding at comparable low-risk operating points. In repeated-patrol simulation, TANav recovers 90% of the oracle's path saving, more than halves human interventions relative to always-detour, and reduces boundary violations from 4.3% to 2.9% relative to RGB-D-only probing. On a quadruped manipulator with a Hall-array fingertip, offline zero-shot MAE is 0.28-1.07 kg on containers up to 2.82 kg. Force-rise calibration at 3 kg gives 93.5% pooled offline accuracy (86.4% on non-cube episodes); a separate raw-peak rule gives 15 of 20 correct online decisions on unseen boxes.

</details>


### [112] [PreAct-Nav: Agentic Reasoning Before Action for Urban Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04916)
*Jing Xie,Shouwei Ruan,Yubin Wang,Yuxiang Zhang,Junwei Yang,Songchang Jin,Dianxi Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PreAct-Nav，一种面向城市导航的具身智能体框架，通过中时段子目标、动作条件世界模型预测与视觉语言模型推理，将远距离目标转化为稳健的局部决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市导航要求智能体基于自我中心观测进行局部决策以实现长时程目标。现有方法常难以将远距离目标转化为连贯的局部动作，因其依赖对瞬时观测或有限历史的语言推理，缺乏对动作后果与未来状态的前瞻能力，尤其在大规模复杂城市路线中表现受限。

Method: PreAct-Nav 在冻结策略上加入前瞻推理：以持久的中时段子目标锚定局部决策；维护导航记忆模块，保存当前子目标与相关经验；引入预测世界沙箱，使用动作条件世界模型（AC-WM）根据候选动作预测世界动态；由视觉语言模型（VLM）推理器在当前子目标下解释预测，决定保留或修正动作；执行后利用真实观测评估结果、纠正不一致假设并更新记忆，以继续或重新制定子目标。

Result: 大量评估表明，PreAct-Nav 通过记忆更新与视觉预测改进了动作选择；在更长路线以及转弯更多的路线上提升更明显。

Conclusion: PreAct-Nav 通过将前瞻推理、子目标记忆与世界模型预测结合，弥补了城市导航中远距离目标到局部决策的转化缺口，提升了复杂城市环境中的导航稳健性。

Abstract: Urban navigation requires embodied agents to pursue long-horizon goals through local decisions based on egocentric observations. However, existing agentic navigation methods often struggle to translate distant goals into coherent local decisions in large-scale physical environments. Their reliance on linguistic reasoning over transient observations or limited history constrains anticipation of the consequences of actions and future conditions, despite the importance of such foresight for navigating long and complex urban routes. To bridge this gap, we propose PreAct-Nav, an agentic navigation framework that equips frozen policies with anticipatory reasoning for robust urban navigation. Our central idea is to anchor local decisions in persistent medium-horizon subgoals, assess the consequences of predicted actions before execution, and continually update the reasoning context using actual outcomes. At its core, a navigation memory module maintains the active subgoal and relevant experience across decisions, translating distant goals into actionable intermediate objectives. We further introduce a predictive world sandbox that uses an action-conditioned world model (AC-WM) to forecast world dynamics conditioned on candidate movements. A vision-language model (VLM) reasoner interprets these predictions under the current subgoal to retain or revise actions. After execution, real observations are used to assess outcomes, correct inconsistent assumptions, and update memory to continue or reformulate the subgoal. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that the proposed PreAct-Nav improves action selection through memory updates and visual prediction, with more pronounced gains on longer routes and routes with more turns.

</details>


### [113] [$R^2$-WAM: Repair-and-Reject Post-Training for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04913)
*Ruiyan Xu,Haisheng Su,Sixu Lin,Zhaokun Yue,Chengming Hu,Xin Jin,Guiliang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出R²-WAM两阶段修复-拒绝后训练框架：先提升世界动作模型预测未来与输入动作的一致性，再利用预测结果筛选次优动作样本进行负微调，实现无需额外环境交互的策略精炼。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型可通过预测采样动作的后果来精炼策略，但视觉上合理的预测可能并未真实反映输入动作，从而误导策略精炼，因此需要解决预测与动作不一致的问题。

Method: R²-WAM包含修复与拒绝两阶段。修复阶段引入运动学对齐分数，衡量预测运动与示教运动的一致性，使预测视频忠实反映输入动作；拒绝阶段使用修复后的视频模型比较采样动作与示教动作的想象结果，对预测任务进度低于示教参考且超过规定裕度的样本进行选择性负微调。该方法不增加环境交互，也不改变推理流程。

Result: 在RoboTwin 2.0的干净与随机设置中平均成功率达93.8%；在长时真实世界Fold Shirt任务中平均成功率为87.5%，而Fast-WAM为0%。

Conclusion: R²-WAM将视频预测从表征学习扩展到基于后果的策略精炼，并验证了其在仿真与真实长时任务中的有效性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) emerge as a promising foundation for policy refinement by predicting the consequences of sampled actions. However, visually plausible predictions can mislead policy refinement if they fail to reflect the input actions. To address this mismatch, we introduce $R^2$-WAM, a two-stage repair-and-reject post-training framework that first improves the consistency of predicted futures with input actions, then uses these futures to select inferior action samples for negative fine-tuning. The repair stage grounds imagination in observed robot behavior through a kinematic alignment score that measures agreement between predicted and demonstrated motion, enabling the predicted video to faithfully reflect its input actions. Using the repaired video model, the rejection stage compares imagined outcomes of sampled and demonstrated actions, selectively applying negative fine-tuning to samples whose predicted task progress falls below the demonstrated reference by a prescribed margin. Together, the two stages extend video prediction from representation learning to consequence-based policy refinement without additional environment interaction or changes to the inference procedure. $R^2$-WAM achieves 93.8% average success on RoboTwin 2.0 across clean and randomized settings. On the long-horizon real-world Fold Shirt task, it achieves 87.5% average success, compared with 0% for Fast-WAM.

</details>


### [114] [GJK-CBF: Control Barrier Functions for Convex Rigid Body Collision Avoidance on SE(3)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04910)
*Yi-Hsuan Chen,Shuo Liu,Wei Xiao,Michael Otte,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 GJK-CBF：利用 GJK 算法获取当前见证点对，并直接由见证点对的相对刚体运动构造控制障碍函数梯度，从而避免可微优化开销，实现一般凸体间的实时避碰。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 凸体间避碰是机器人学基础问题；CBF 适合实时安全滤波，但一般凸体的精确分离度量通常依赖优化，现有 CBF 往往通过可微优化求梯度，增加计算负担。

Method: 采用 Gilbert-Johnson-Keerthi（GJK）算法得到实现最小距离或穿透深度的见证点对，并据此构造 CBF，即 GJK-CBF；其梯度直接来自见证点对的相对刚体运动，无需 diffOpt。方法适用于 2D 和 3D 环境中的广泛凸体对，只要每一一交互中至少一个凸体严格凸。

Result: 在多种场景中验证，包括 2D/3D 多机器人位置交换、3D 垂直狭缝导航以及机械臂应用；所有场景均实现无碰撞运动，并减少几何近似带来的保守性，尤其是在狭窄环境中。

Conclusion: GJK-CBF 为凸体避碰提供了一种无需可微优化、可扩展至 2D/3D 与机械臂的实时安全滤波方法，能在狭窄环境中降低保守性并保持无碰撞。

Abstract: Collision avoidance among convex bodies is a fundamental problem in robotics. Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) provide a practical framework for real-time safety filtering due to their computational efficiency. For general convex bodies, exact separation measures, such as distance or scaling factor, are typically computed through optimization. Existing CBF formulations often obtain the required gradient via differentiable optimization (diffOpt), adding computational overhead. In contrast, we leverage the Gilbert-Johnson-Keerthi (GJK) algorithm to obtain the current witness pair---the pair of points realizing the minimum distance or penetration depth---and formulate a CBF, termed GJK-CBF, whose gradient is constructed directly from the relative rigid-body motion of the witness pair, without resorting to diffOpt. This formulation applies to both 2D and 3D environments across a broad class of convex body pairs, provided that at least one in each one-to-one interaction is strictly convex. The proposed GJK-CBF is validated in various scenarios, including multi-robot position swapping in both 2D and 3D, navigation through a vertical slit in 3D, and its applicability to manipulators. The results demonstrate collision-free motion across all scenarios while reducing the conservativeness introduced by geometric approximations, particularly in narrow environments.

</details>


### [115] [Tackling Sim-to-Real Mismatch Through Sampling-Based Disturbance Observers: From Analytical Models to Learned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04896)
*Tianqi Zhu,Jun Yang,Jianliang Mao,Cong Li,Shihua Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出采样型扰动观测器SDOB，将扰动观测器原理扩展到模拟器和学习世界模型，通过状态推演或成本查询接口估计扰动，补偿sim-to-real失配并提升控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人控制器日益依赖解析模型、模拟器、成本查询接口和学习世界模型，但物理部署常偏离名义假设，且即使模型准确也会出现额外扰动。经典扰动观测器通常依赖显式被控对象模型，难以适用于更广泛的模型形式。

Method: SDOB采用基于采样的扰动观测方法，通过状态rollout或成本query接口扩展DOB原理。它将可观测扰动分为两个通道：状态效应扰动，即反馈状态与预测状态不一致；成本扰动，即相同查询状态因感知环境变化而获得不同成本。

Result: 在传统模型和学习模型上的多种仿真与真实机器人实验表明，SDOB能够有效补偿sim-to-real失配，并改进控制性能。

Conclusion: SDOB把扰动观测思想推广到模拟器和学习世界模型等更广泛模型接口，为存在未建模扰动和模型失配的机器人控制提供了有效补偿方案。

Abstract: Robotic controllers increasingly rely on analytical models, simulators, cost-query interfaces, and learned world models. However, physical deployment can deviate from nominal assumptions, and additional disturbances may arise even when the model itself is accurate. In control systems, disturbance observers (DOB) are widely used to estimate such unmeasured effects from nominal models and measured feedback. Classical DOB formulations are generally built around explicit plant models. This paper develops the sampling-based disturbance observer (SDOB), extending the DOB principle to a broader range of models, including simulators and learned world models, through state-rollout or cost-query interfaces. SDOB separates two observable channels: state-effect disturbances, for which the feedback state differs from its prediction, and cost disturbances, for which the same query state receives different costs as the perceived environment changes. Diverse simulation and real-robot experiments across traditional and learned models demonstrate the effectiveness of SDOB in compensating for sim-to-real mismatch and improving control performance.

</details>


### [116] [ExStereo: Lifting 2D Vision-Language-Action Models to 3D with Explicit Stereo Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04805)
*I-Chun Arthur Liu,Jason Chen,Gaurav S. Sukhatme,Daniel Seita*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ExStereo立体感知模块，通过立体图像重建场景几何并以动作-立体交叉注意力将3D信息注入预训练2D VLA，在仿真与真实双臂平台上均优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维感知对机器人高精度操作至关重要，但从单目RGB观测恢复度量深度和精确3D物体位置本质上是病态问题；然而许多视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型仅依赖RGB输入进行感知，缺乏显式3D信息。

Method: 基于立体匹配基础模型，设计ExStereo模块：从双目图像对重建场景几何，将多视角观测渲染为显式立体表示并提取立体特征；提出动作-立体交叉注意力机制，使动作专家输出的动作令牌选择性地关注立体令牌，从而在3D场景条件下生成机器人动作；并在任务后训练前引入中间训练阶段，利用大规模立体数据与自监督学习目标学习鲁棒的3D表示。

Result: 将ExStereo微调至两个公开VLA模型π0.5和SmolVLA，在仿真环境和真实双臂PiPER平台上进行评估，微调后的VLA在两种设置下均持续超越基线。

Conclusion: 实验证明立体感知可有效增强VLA的3D场景理解能力，提升机器人操作性能。

Abstract: Three-dimensional perception is critical for robotic manipulation, particularly for high-precision tasks, as recovering metric depth and precise 3D object positions from monocular RGB observations is inherently ill-posed. However, many Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models rely solely on RGB observations for perception. Leveraging recent advances in foundation models for stereo matching, we introduce ExStereo, a stereo module that augments pre-trained 2D VLAs with 3D perception. ExStereo reconstructs scene geometry from stereo image pairs and renders multi-view observations as an explicit stereo representation for stereo feature extraction. The action tokens from the action expert selectively attend to the resulting stereo tokens through our proposed action-stereo cross-attention mechanism, enabling the policy to generate robot actions conditioned on 3D scene information. To learn robust 3D representations, we introduce a mid-training stage before task-specific post-training, using a self-supervised learning objective on large-scale stereo data. We validate our approach by fine-tuning two publicly available VLAs, $\pi_{0.5}$ and SmolVLA, and evaluate them in simulation and on a real-world bimanual PiPER platform. Across both settings, VLAs fine-tuned with ExStereo consistently outperform baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of stereo perception for robotic manipulation. Our project website is at: this https URL .

</details>


### [117] [Distributed Cascade Force Control of Soft-Tactile-Based Multi-robot System for Object Transportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04775)
*Duy Anh Nguyen,Nhat Minh Dinh Le,Nhan Huu Nguyen,Pham Duy Hung,Van Anh Ho,Trung Dung Ngo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需机器人间通信的分布式级联力控系统（DCFC），使多机器人协同将刚性物体推向期望的运动目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人推动刚性物体时，通常依赖机器人间通信，且物体质量未知、目标可能运动，给协调控制带来挑战。

Method: 机器人配备360度视觉软触觉传感器以检测接触位置和合力；基于平面运动刚体动力学设计分布式级联控制：内环结合接触力与定位，保证接触并施加期望力；外环在无机器人间通信条件下协调多机器人沿期望方向推动物体，且不依赖未知物体质量；使用Lyapunov稳定性理论验证系统稳定性与收敛性。

Result: 通过仿真和真实世界实验验证了所提控制方法的性能，实验结果表明多机器人能够成功协调，将物体推向运动中的期望方向。

Conclusion: DCFC可在无机器人间通信、物体质量未知条件下实现多机器人协同推动刚性物体至运动目标，并具有理论稳定性保证与实验有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a distributed cascade force control system (DCFC) for multiple robots with the aim of pushing a rigid object towards a desired moving target without their inter-robot communication. These mobile robots are equipped with 360-degree vision-based soft tactile sensors utilized to determine contact location and resultant impact force. By investigating the dynamics of moving rigid objects on the flat, we proposed a distributed cascade control. The inner loop control incorporates contact force and positioning, ensuring the robots' pushing contact and application of the desired force to the object. The outer loop control coordinates the robots to push the object in a desired direction without inter-robot communication, regardless of the unknown object mass. The stability and convergence of the system are verified using the Lyapunov stability theory. We also conducted simulation and real-word experiments to validate the performance of the proposed control method, and the experimental results showcase the successful coordination of multiple robots in pushing an object towards a moving desired direction.

</details>


### [118] [Learning Task and Motion Plans from Real Demonstrations with Hybrid Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04771)
*Zuleika Redondo Garcia,Andreu Matoses Gimenez,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合流匹配规划器，用单一网络同时生成符号计划（掩码离散流匹配）与运动轨迹（连续流匹配），通过少量演示学习和闭环重规划实现长时程移动操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程移动操作需要任务计划与运动执行；现有生成式规划器依赖数千条固定基座机械臂脚本演示，且以开环方式执行，难以适应少量数据与真实移动操作场景。

Method: 方法为混合流匹配规划器：掩码离散流匹配生成符号计划，连续流匹配生成运动轨迹。利用两条数据性质补偿小数据集：演示从任意中间动作续接仍构成演示，从而倍增训练样本并支持每步动作后重规划；同类物体可互换，将单目标演示扩展为所有排列目标的演示。采用改进的离散计划训练与生成方案，并在运动学仿真与真实移动机械臂上闭环执行。

Result: 基础实现于68%留出场景生成有效计划；改进离散计划训练与生成后提升至76%；每步重规划将运动学仿真任务完成率从40%提升至53%。该方法任务完成率与仅运动流匹配策略相当，同时额外提供符号计划；在任务完成率和计划有效性上优于既有混合扩散方案，并在真实移动机械臂上验证。

Conclusion: 结论：混合流匹配可从少量演示学习长时程移动操作，通过离散-连续联合生成与闭环重规划提升计划有效性和任务完成率，兼具符号规划与运动生成能力，并具备真实机器人可行性。

Abstract: Long-horizon mobile manipulation requires a task plan and the motion that executes it. Generative planners trained on demonstration produce both in one pass, requiring neither a symbolic domain nor search. To date, however, they have relied on thousands of scripted demonstrations of fixed-base arms and executed open loop. This paper presents a hybrid flow matching planner: a single network generates the symbolic plan with masked discrete flow matching and the motion trajectory with continuous flow matching. Unlike prior generative planners, we aim to learn from a much smaller set of demonstrations and to execute the plan in closed loop. Two properties of the data compensate for the small dataset. A demonstration resumed from any of its intermediate actions is itself a demonstration, which multiplies the training samples and enables replanning after every action. Objects of the same kind are interchangeable, which turns demonstrations of one goal into demonstrations of every permuted goal. Our base implementation produces valid plans on 68% of held-out scenes; a training and generation scheme for the discrete plan raises this to 76%, and replanning after every action raises the task completion rate from 40% to 53% in a kinematic simulation. The planner matches the task completion rate of motion-only flow matching policies while additionally providing the symbolic plan, and it outperforms previous hybrid diffusion formulations on both task completion and plan validity. We validate the planner on a real mobile manipulator. Videos and project page: this https URL

</details>


### [119] [Flow Policies as Actions of Skill-Level World Models: Learned and Symbolic Abstractions for Long-Horizon Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04767)
*Andreu Matoses Gimenez,Andrei-Carlo Papuc,Chris Pek,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该文用流匹配策略从演示中构建技能级动作，并比较四种动作抽象在世界模型规划中的效果；符号标签在短技能序列任务上成功率高，但长任务受搜索而非世界模型限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制频率动作使长任务需要大量预测步，导致搜索空间增大且误差累积；技能级动作可缩短序列，但符号技能词表通常依赖领域知识和标注演示。

Method: 训练流匹配策略，将噪声种子与观测（可选代码或标签）映射为完整技能执行，使一次技能执行对应一次世界模型转移；提出四种任务知识递增的动作抽象：压缩种子、两种从演示学习的离散代码、符号标签；用统一的世界模型训练与规划框架，在最多14个连续技能的模拟积木重排任务上评估。

Result: 符号标签在需要不超过6个技能的任务中成功率超过90%，之后下降；无标签时，物体中心学习代码在单技能任务上与符号标签相当，在2至5个技能任务上保留其一半到四分之三的成功率；消融表明标签优势很大程度来自规划器知道哪些动作适用，而非标签本身；超过6个技能后，限制成功的是搜索而非世界模型。

Conclusion: 技能级动作可从策略输入中构建，无需显式符号词表；短序列任务中符号标签有效，但长序列规划瓶颈在搜索；学习到的物体中心代码可作为无标签替代，但随技能数增加性能下降。

Abstract: Latent world models enable robots to plan by predicting the consequences of actions. Planning long tasks with control-rate actions requires many prediction steps, which enlarges the search space and accumulates error. Skill-level actions shorten these sequences, but a symbolic skill vocabulary requires domain knowledge and labeled demonstrations. We construct skill-level actions from the inputs of a flow-matching policy trained on demonstrations segmented into complete skills. The policy maps a noise seed and an observation, optionally with a code or label, to a complete skill execution, so one execution is one world-model transition. On this mechanism we propose four action abstractions with increasing task knowledge: a compressed seed, two discrete codes learned from the demonstrations, and a symbolic label. We evaluate them with a common world-model training procedure and planning framework on simulated block rearrangement tasks that require up to 14 sequential skills. The symbolic label succeeds in over 90% of the tasks that require up to six skills and degrades beyond. Without any label, an object-centric learned code matches it on single-skill tasks and retains half to three quarters of its success on tasks of two to five skills. Ablations attribute much of the label's advantage to its planner knowing which actions are applicable, rather than to the label itself. Beyond six skills the search, not the world model, limits success. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [120] [PatternDex: Learning Interaction Patterns to Guide Reinforcement Learning of Bimanual Dexterous Manipulation of Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04765)
*David Minkwan Kim,Runfa Blark Li,Beckham Po-Ju Lee,Nikolay Atanasov,Truong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PatternDex，通过从人-物演示中学习物体主导的交互模式，并将其作为目标机器人可执行动作的引导，实现双臂灵巧手高成功率操作铰接物体并跨本体泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双臂灵巧手操作铰接物体时存在本体差异（embodiment gap），导致策略难以直接迁移，且在新机器人上训练成本高。作者观察到手部运动与物体运动之间的相关性主要由物体决定，而非由手决定，因此可从人-物演示中学习这种相关性。

Method: PatternDex 学习手-物运动相关性，并将其表示为 token 序列，称为交互模式（interaction pattern）。基于该模式，估计适配目标机器人的手腕运动和接触点，并用这些估计作为引导训练强化学习策略。由于引导适配目标本体，策略只在目标机器人可执行的动作空间中探索。学习到的交互模式可复用，新机器人仅需简单微调。

Result: 在 ARCTIC 数据集的人类演示上，使用 Allegro 手平均成功率达 92.8%，而最先进基线为 52.2%。仅经微调后，在另外三种机器人手上成功率均超过 70%。学习到的策略还成功迁移到真实世界打开微波炉任务。

Conclusion: PatternDex 通过物体中心的交互模式学习与本体自适应引导，有效缓解跨本体差异，实现高成功率双臂灵巧操作，并展现出良好的复用、微调和真实世界迁移能力。

Abstract: In this paper, we develop a method that enables bimanual dexterous hands to manipulate articulated objects with a high success rate without suffering from an embodiment gap. We observe that the correlation between hand motions and object motions is dictated by the object rather than the hands and can be learned from human-object demonstrations. Based on this observation, we propose PatternDex, a method that learns this correlation and represents it as a token sequence, which we call an interaction pattern. From this pattern, PatternDex estimates the wrist motions and contact points that fit the target robot, and then trains a reinforcement learning policy that exploits these estimates as guidance. Since the guidance fits the target embodiment, the policy explores only the actions that the target robot can execute and thus achieves high success rates. PatternDex also requires only simple fine-tuning to train a new robot, since it can reuse the learned interaction pattern. We evaluate PatternDex with bimanual dexterous hands on human demonstrations from the ARCTIC dataset. PatternDex achieves, on average, a 92.8% success rate with Allegro hands, while the state-of-the-art baseline achieves 52.2%. Also, it achieves success rates above 70% with three other robot hands after fine-tuning alone. Furthermore, we verify that the learned policy transfers well to a real-world task of opening a microwave. Videos and additional results are available at this https URL

</details>


### [121] [DNPush: Distributed Nonparallel Pushing Force Control of Multi-Robot Systems for Object Transportation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04764)
*Duy Anh Nguyen,Pham Duy Hung,Trung Dung Ngo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DNPush，一种无需机器人间通信的分布式非平行推挤力控制器，仅凭局部力测量、预设力权重和局部期望运输方向，即可协调多机器人搬运物体并收敛到唯一旋转平衡，使物体速度方向与期望运输方向一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人协同搬运通常依赖机器人间通信、物体模型、接触位置、质心估计或力臂信息，难以在分布式和未知物体条件下实现稳定协调；本文旨在仅用局部测量和局部期望方向实现无通信的非平行推挤协同运输。

Method: DNPush为每个机器人利用局部力测量、预设力权重和局部表达的期望运输方向，在线无需对象模型、接触位置、质心估计和力臂大小。控制器通过互正弦律根据局部力方向失配调节非平行力幅值。在给定接触条件下，采用Lyapunov分析和LaSalle不变性原理证明系统收敛到唯一旋转平衡：垂直力分量相互抵消，法向力净力矩为零；物体速度收敛到期望运输方向，并证明凸多边形存在合适接触区域。

Result: 仿真分别验证了互正弦缩放机制、五机器人协调和主动旋转阻尼；硬件实验中，使用软触觉差速驱动机器人，在5次固定运输方向试验中速度方向误差为0.61°±0.79°，并展示三机器人带路点运输。

Conclusion: DNPush表明，无需机器人间通信和物体/接触先验信息，仅靠局部力测量与互正弦力调节即可实现多机器人协同物体运输，且具备理论收敛保证和硬件可行性。

Abstract: This letter proposes DNPush, a Distributed Nonparallel Pushing force controller for cooperative object transport without inter-robot communication. Each robot uses local force measurements, a preassigned force weight, and a locally expressed desired transport direction, without requiring an object model, contact locations, a CoM estimate, or moment arm magnitudes online. DNPush regulates nonparallel force magnitudes from local force direction misalignments through a reciprocal-sine law. Under the stated contact conditions, Lyapunov analysis with LaSalle's invariance principle proves convergence to a unique rotational equilibrium, where perpendicular force contributions cancel and the net torque from normal forces is zero. Object velocity converges to the desired transport direction, and suitable contact regions exist for convex polygons. Simulations isolate the reciprocal-sine scaling mechanism, five-robot coordination, and active rotational damping. Hardware experiments with soft tactile differential-drive robots achieve a velocity direction error of 0.61 deg +/- 0.79 deg over five trials with a fixed transport direction and demonstrate object transport through waypoints with three robots.

</details>


### [122] [Lie Rarely, Lie Big: Stealthy Insider Attacks on LLM Robot Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04744)
*Sribalaji C. Anand,George J. Pappas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究LLM多机器人团队中单个被攻陷机器人如何利用团队验证预算限制隐蔽地破坏共享建图结果；推导报告被验证概率下界与地图误差上界，并用LLM诚实机器人实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当多机器人把规划与互信委托给LLM智能体时，单个失陷机器人可能污染团队共享结果。该问题在可物理核验测量、但核验会消耗任务预算的多机器人勘测任务中尤其关键，需刻画验证预算与攻击损害之间的权衡。

Method: 将受控机器人建模为系统论意义上的隐蔽对手，其约束来自团队自身检测器而非能量上限。基于通信图度数与验证预算推导报告被验证概率下界；将任意隐蔽攻击造成的地图误差上界表示为关于对手偏差分布的线性规划，其解给出隐蔽预算与破坏之间的兑换率。

Result: 第一，攻击者报告被验证的概率有下界，取决于通信图度数与验证预算。第二，地图误差上界由该线性规划值给出：低于临界验证水平时，最坏隐蔽攻击是在最不可能被核验的记录上偶发全幅谎言；高于该水平时，更优策略是让小幅偏差藏入噪声。LLM诚实机器人实验中，两界在攻击实际花费的预算处均成立；LLM重查哪些记录无偏，但重查多少不可预测。

Conclusion: 验证预算与通信结构共同决定LLM多机器人系统对隐蔽失陷的鲁棒性。LLM核验行为在对象选择上无偏、在强度上不可预测，因此任务规划需显式考虑验证预算并设计自适应检测策略。

Abstract: When a team of robots delegates planning and mutual trust to LLM agents, a single compromised robot can corrupt the shared outcome. We study this threat in a grounded task: a multi-robot survey in which measurements can be verified against the physical world, but every verification costs budget that would otherwise advance the mission. We treat the compromised robot as a stealthy adversary in the system-theoretic sense: it is limited not by an energy bound but by the team's own detectors. We then derive two bounds. First, the probability that the adversary's reports are verified is bounded below in terms of the degrees in the communication graph and the verification budget. Second, the map error caused by any stealthy adversary is bounded above by the value of a linear program over the adversary's bias distributions; its solution is an exchange rate between stealth budget and damage: below a critical verification level the worst stealthy attack tells rare, full-magnitude lies on the records least likely to be verified, and above it the better purchase is small biases hidden in the noise. In experiments where the honest robots are LLM agents, both bounds hold at the budget the attack actually spent. The experiments also show that which records an LLM robot re-checks is unbiased, but how much it re-checks is unpredictable.

</details>


### [123] [Robot Learning with Visual Predicted Force](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04741)
*Haonan Chen,Feiyang Wu,Yuxiang Ma,Mustafa Mete,Pengfei Ye,Junxuan Shen,Cheng Zhu,Aurora Ruggeri,Kelvin Cheung,Jiayuan Mao,Edward Adelson,Jiajun Wu,Robert D. Howe,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种无需力/触觉传感器的力感知操作框架：利用Fin Ray柔性夹爪形变进行视觉力预测，并在推理时依据预测力选择动作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统力感知操作依赖专用力或触觉传感器，部署成本高且受限；作者希望仅凭视觉从柔性夹爪形变中推断力，以实现接触丰富操作中的力感知。

Method: 采用两阶段训练：首先用标定数据训练视觉力估计器，并用其为任务演示标注力估计；然后基于力增强演示训练动作-力提议策略，联合生成候选机器人动作及其对应力。测试时采样候选动作与预测力，执行预测力最接近演示目标力的动作。

Result: 在浆果采摘、空罐抓取、手内重定向和插头插入任务上评估，结果表明视觉力预测可引导推理阶段的动作选择，完成接触丰富操作，且部署时无需力或触觉传感器。

Conclusion: 视觉力预测可作为专用力/触觉传感的替代方案，支持无传感器部署下的力感知操作，并通过推理时动作选择提升接触丰富任务表现。

Abstract: Force-aware manipulation typically relies on specialized force or tactile sensors. We show that force-aware manipulation can instead be achieved through visual force prediction from the deformation of a compliant Fin Ray gripper. Our approach trains two models. First, we train a visual force estimator on calibration data and use it to annotate task demonstrations with force estimates. Second, we train an action--force proposal policy on these force-augmented demonstrations to jointly generate candidate robot actions and their associated forces. At test time, we sample candidate actions and the forces they are expected to produce, then execute the action whose predicted force is closest to a target from the demonstrations. We evaluate our approach on berry picking, empty-can grasping, in-hand reorientation, and plug insertion. Our results show that visual force prediction can guide inference-time action selection for contact-rich manipulation without requiring force or tactile sensors at deployment.

</details>


### [124] [Emergent Underactuation in Fully Actuated Multirotors by Minimizing a Homogeneous Allocation Cost](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04726)
*Antonio Franchi,Barbara Bazzana*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作表明：当仅给定位置轨迹时，最小化正齐次分配代价会使全驱动多旋翼表现出类欠驱动行为；但全驱动在扰动抑制与瞬态力生成中仍具重要价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全驱动通常因可独立跟踪位置与姿态而受重视，但在仅需位置轨迹的任务中，最优名义运动为何可能呈现欠驱动特性尚缺乏统一解释；同时需说明全驱动平台在扰动与运动变化下的优势。

Method: 将执行器输出代价通过逐纤维下确界（fiberwise infimization）提升至力旋量空间，构造正齐次下确界分配代价；限制到单位零力矩力集合上定义方向性代价地形，分析最小方向与所需世界坐标系力的对齐关系，并推导最优性间隙分解。

Result: 最小化方向与所需力幅值无关；将任一固定最小方向与所需世界坐标系力对齐，可最小化瞬时与积分零力矩分配代价，所得机体力保持在固定一维子空间且无横向分量。进一步给出精确最优性间隙分解，量化其他姿态轨迹的额外代价并刻画最小代价类。当扰动或期望运动变化改变所需力方向时，全驱动平台可立即利用横向权限生成修正力，再定向至新最小代价族；结构欠驱动平台则须先再定向才能生成该力。

Conclusion: 齐次分配代价最小化解释了最优名义运动为何可类欠驱动，而全驱动仍对扰动抑制与瞬态力生成具有关键价值。

Abstract: Full actuation is commonly valued for independent position and attitude tracking. This work establishes a complementary principle: when only a position trajectory is prescribed, minimizing a positively homogeneous allocation cost selects underactuated-like behavior on a fully actuated multirotor. This cost class includes norms and power laws, including standard minimum-norm pseudoinverse allocation. We use fiberwise infimization to transport the actuator-output cost to wrench space, yielding a homogeneous infimal allocation cost. Restriction to unit zero-moment forces defines a directional landscape whose minimizing directions are independent of force magnitude. We show that aligning any fixed minimizing direction with the required world-frame force minimizes instantaneous and integrated zero-moment allocation costs. The resulting body force remains in a fixed one-dimensional subspace without transverse components. We derive an exact optimality-gap decomposition quantifying the additional cost of every other attitude trajectory and characterizing the minimum-cost class. When a disturbance or prescribed-motion change modifies the required-force direction, a fully actuated platform can immediately generate the modified force using transverse authority and reorient toward the new minimum-cost family. A structurally underactuated platform must reorient before generating that force. Thus, homogeneous allocation-cost minimization explains why optimal nominal motion can be underactuated-like, while full actuation remains valuable for disturbance rejection and transient force generation.

</details>


### [125] [Test-Time Training as Residual Memory for Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04701)
*Haoxuan Wang,Gengyu Zhang,Ramana Rao Kompella,Gaowen Liu,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TTT-RM 将测试时训练（TTT）重新定义为残差记忆，用快速权重补足有界记忆库，专门保存当前观测与检索记忆无法恢复的任务相关历史信息，从而提升长时程机器人操作的可扩展性与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作依赖过去事件，但保留完整历史代价高；现有方法或使用有界记忆库存储部分观测，或通过 TTT 将交互历史压缩为固定大小参数状态，却未显式区分当前策略上下文已可恢复的信息与必须跨上下文持续保留的信息，导致记忆容量利用不充分、可扩展性受限。

Method: TTT-RM 学习一个历史解码器，从当前观测和检索到的记忆重建历史表示，并将重建残差作为 TTT 的学习目标；该残差表示当前上下文未能解释、需额外持久保存的历史信息。TTT 的慢权重针对残差目标优化，使在线快速权重更新随时间编码互补历史信息；随后查询快速权重状态生成残差记忆表示，用于条件化动作生成，并与有界记忆库互补。

Result: 在记忆密集型仿真基准和真实世界任务中，TTT-RM 在多种记忆设计下均取得一致提升，优于多种基线，并支持在三分钟、八阶段的 stowing 任务中持续执行。

Conclusion: TTT-RM 通过将 TTT 作为残差记忆，显式补足当前上下文不可恢复的历史信息，为长时程机器人操作提供了一种可扩展且有效的记忆增强策略。

Abstract: Memory is essential for long-horizon robotic manipulation, where successful actions may depend on past events that are no longer recoverable from the current observation. As episodes grow longer, however, retaining the full history becomes increasingly costly, creating a fundamental scalability challenge for memory-augmented policies. Existing approaches address this challenge by either storing selected past observations in a bounded memory bank or compressing interaction history into a fixed-size parametric state through Test-Time Training (TTT). Yet these formulations do not explicitly distinguish between historical information that can already be recovered from the policy's current context and information that must persist beyond it. We introduce TTT-RM, which repurposes TTT as Residual Memory, using fast weights not to generically compress history but to complement a bounded memory bank by preserving task-relevant historical information that cannot be recovered from the policy's current context. Concretely, TTT-RM learns a history decoder that reconstructs historical representations from the current observation and retrieved memory. The resulting reconstruction residual captures what this context fails to explain and serves as the learning target for TTT. The TTT slow weights are optimized against this residual target so that online fast-weight updates learn to encode complementary historical information over time. The fast-weight state is then queried to produce a residual memory representation that conditions action generation. Extensive experiments on memory-intensive simulation benchmarks and real-world tasks show that TTT-RM consistently improves across multiple memory designs, outperforms diverse baselines, and supports sustained execution on a three-minute, eight-stage stowing task.

</details>


### [126] [RoboIRS: Inference-Time Internal Representation Steering for Generalist Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04681)
*Jiuzhou Lei,Chang Liu,Dayou Li,Zhiyuan Zhang,Xiao Liang,Yu She,Zhiwen Fan,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboIRS：一种推理时内部表征引导方法，利用成功与失败轨迹训练线性分类器、选择结果相关干预位置并推导任务特定引导方向，无需更新策略参数，即可提升 VLA 与世界动作模型在分布外任务上的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作模型（VLA）和世界动作模型（WAM）在分布外任务变化下常出现性能退化，但仍保留部分任务能力；目标是恢复这种能力，而非重新训练策略。

Method: RoboIRS 使用成功和失败的 rollout 训练线性分类器，选择与任务结果相关的内部干预位置，并推导任务特定的引导方向；在推理阶段直接干预冻结策略的内部表征，不更新策略参数。

Result: 在 15 个仿真任务和冻结的 pi0.5 策略上，平均成功率从 44.4% 提升至 66.2%，优于其他推理时干预基线且推理时间增加很少；在真实机器人操作中使用同一 pi0.5 策略验证有效；在 Cosmos Policy 世界动作模型上，平均成功率从 35.4% 提升至 55.4%。

Conclusion: 直接引导机器人策略的内部表征可在推理时提升机器人策略性能，并适用于 VLA 与世界动作模型。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action models (WAMs) often degrade under out-of-distribution task variations despite retaining partial task capability. To recover such capability, we propose RoboIRS, an inference-time internal representation steering method that uses successful and failed rollouts to train linear classifiers, select outcome-relevant intervention locations, and derive task-specific steering directions without updating policy parameters. On 15 simulation tasks with a frozen $\pi 0.5$ policy, RoboIRS improves the average success rate from 44.4% to 66.2%, outperforming alternative inference-time intervention baselines while adding little inference time. We further validate RoboIRS on real-robot manipulation using the same $\pi 0.5$ policy and demonstrate its applicability to a world-action model Cosmos Policy, where the average success rate improves from 35.4% to 55.4%. These results show that directly steering internal robot-policy representations can improve the performance of robot policies at inference time. Project website is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [127] [PerturBot: Breaking Shortcut Priors in Vision-Language-Action Models with Perturbative Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04616)
*Mingyu Liu,Chonghao Sima,Tianjian Feng,Hanqing Wang,Cong Chen,Hao Chen,Chunhua Shen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文指出VLA策略会依赖模态捷径，即视觉、词汇或运动线索在成功演示中足以预测专家动作，而忽略决定动作的任务证据。提出Perturbot训练干预与GroundingFscore离线诊断评分，使策略更依赖任务证据并保持推理不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略可能在复杂任务中忽略应决定其行为的证据。靠近腕部相机的物体可替代指令目标；熟悉名词可触发训练中配对的操作，即使动词改变；夹爪未夹到物体也可能抬起。这类模态捷径来自成功演示中的规律。仅增加同类演示可提升任务成功率，却不会消除捷径。

Method: Perturbot通过三种方式改变被缩放的内容：施加任务保持的腕部视角扰动；用决策相关描述丰富指令；加入随机和失败轨迹片段，并按片段实际包含的行为重新标注。GroundingFscore是一种离线评分，用于诊断策略对模态捷径的依赖严重程度。

Result: 摘要未给出具体数值结果。其主张是：Perturbot使任务相关证据更易被使用，并使捷径本身不再充分；它可补充scaling且不改变推理。任务成功率显示策略是否改进，而GroundingFscore揭示策略是否健康扩展，即依赖任务证据而非捷径。

Conclusion: Perturbot与GroundingFscore共同构成训练与评估框架，用于将VLA决策与捷径先验解耦，同时保持策略对任务相关证据的响应。

Abstract: A vision--language--action (VLA) policy can complete complex tasks while ignoring the evidence that should determine its actions. An object held near the wrist camera can displace the instructed target. Language and action show the same pattern: a familiar noun can trigger the operation it was paired with in training even after the verb changes, and a gripper that closed on nothing may lift anyway. We call these dependencies modality shortcuts: regularities in successful demonstrations make visual, lexical, or motor cues sufficient to predict expert actions without the task evidence needed for the underlying decision. More demonstrations of the same kind can raise task success while leaving these shortcuts intact. We propose Perturbot which makes task-relevant evidence easier to use and shortcuts insufficient on their own: it applies task-preserving wrist-view perturbations, enriches instructions with decision-relevant captions, and adds random and failed trajectory segments relabeled with the behavior they contain. It complements scaling by changing what is scaled, and leaves inference unchanged. Moreover, we propose GroundingFscore, an offline score that diagnoses how severely a policy relies on modality shortcuts. Task success rate shows whether a policy improves, while GroundingFscore reveals whether the policy scales healthily, relying on task evidence rather than shortcuts. Together, Perturbot and GroundingFscore provide a training-and-evaluation framework for disentangling VLA decisions from shortcut priors while preserving responsiveness to task-relevant evidence.

</details>


### [128] [An End-to-End Framework for Modelling Pneumatic Soft Robots Based on Differentiable Finite Element Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04612)
*Shaohong Zhong,Yao Yao,Perla Maiolino,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端可微分有限元（FEM）框架，用于气动软机器人的建模与系统辨识，并支持轨迹优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软机器人因非线性、复杂动力学和复杂几何而难以精确建模与系统辨识，现有方法在精度、计算效率和速度之间存在权衡，限制了其在精确机器人任务中的应用。

Method: 构建可微分FEM框架：自动将CAD转换为体素化四面体粘弹性FEM网格以表征几何与驱动机制；融合易获取的点云数据与可微分FEM，以最小实验设置辨识材料和动态驱动参数；利用模型可微分性及机器人动力学与接触交互梯度进行轨迹优化。

Result: 在复杂波纹管状气动软机器人上验证，建模精度高，最大位置误差小于3 mm，并成功应用于路径跟踪和抓取等真实运动规划任务。

Conclusion: 该框架能够为下游任务生成可靠模型，有效简化软机器人建模与系统辨识，并借助梯度实现高效标定与轨迹优化。

Abstract: Soft robots present significant modelling challenges due to their non-linearity, complex dynamics and potentially intricate geometries. These difficulties in accurate system identification and dynamics modelling limit their applications in precise robotics tasks. Prior modelling approaches typically suffer from trade-offs in accuracy, computational efficiency, or speed. The differentiable finite element method (FEM) offers a promising balance between these desiderata for soft robot modelling, and enables the use of gradients for efficient calibration and trajectory optimisation. In this paper, we propose an end-to-end differentiable FEM-based framework designed to streamline modelling and system identification for pneumatic soft robots, enabling the generation of reliable models for downstream tasks. The framework automates the conversion of computer-aided designs into voxelised tetrahedral viscoelastic FEM meshes that accurately represent the robot's geometry and actuation mechanism. By integrating easily acquired point cloud data with differentiable FEM, the framework achieves precise identification of material and dynamic actuation parameters using a minimal experimental setup. Additionally, the differentiable nature of the model facilitates trajectory optimisation by leveraging gradients from robot dynamics and contact interactions. We validate the framework by modelling a complex bellow-shaped pneumatic soft robot and demonstrate its efficacy in real-world motion planning tasks. Experimental results indicate high modelling accuracy, with a maximum positional error of less than 3 mm, and successful application in tasks such as path following and grasping.

</details>


### [129] [Exploiting Hierarchical Controller Structure in Contextual Parameter Learning for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04609)
*Sebastian Hirt,Lukas Theiner,Jan Peters,Rolf Findeisen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种上下文贝叶斯优化框架，用于分层控制系统的跨层联合参数学习；该方法分别观测并建模任务性能、实现质量与控制代价，利用多输出高斯过程和解析聚合优化闭环目标。在人形机器人推箱的loco-manipulation任务中，联合调节质心预测控制器与全身控制器，训练和适应阶段的平均经验遗憾均最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层控制架构将复杂控制问题分解为多层级，但整体闭环性能依赖分布在各层的参数；独立调节不同层控制器会忽略跨层交互，因此需要联合优化。

Method: 提出上下文贝叶斯优化框架，不只把闭环性能建模为标量黑箱，而是保留任务性能、实现质量和控制代价的独立观测；用相关多输出高斯过程建模这些性能分量，并解析评估其已知聚合为整体闭环目标。该形式利用分层控制的三点：层级暴露的信息性性能量、不同控制层参数间的耦合、以及这些关系随工况的变化。

Result: 在人形机器人loco-manipulation的物理推箱任务中，联合调优质心预测控制器和全身控制器，并在箱质量变化下评估。所提方法在训练和适应阶段的平均经验遗憾均低于所考虑基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，结合多输出建模的上下文贝叶斯优化可有效处理分层控制中的跨层耦合与工况变化，实现优于基线的联合参数学习。

Abstract: Hierarchical control architectures are widely used to decompose complex control problems into interacting control levels and are particularly important in robotics, where planning, whole-body motion, and lower-level control must be coordinated across different levels of abstraction and time scales. Their overall closed-loop performance, however, depends strongly on parameters distributed across the hierarchy, such that tuning controllers on different levels independently may neglect relevant cross-layer interactions. We propose a contextual Bayesian optimization framework for joint parameter learning in hierarchical control systems. Rather than modeling closed-loop performance only as a scalar black-box function, we retain separate observations of task performance, realization quality, and control effort. A correlated multi-output Gaussian process models these performance components, while their known aggregation into the overall closed-loop objective is evaluated analytically. The formulation exploits three complementary consequences of hierarchical control: informative performance quantities exposed by the hierarchy, coupling between parameters of different controller levels, and variations of these relations with operating conditions. We evaluate the approach for humanoid loco-manipulation, jointly tuning a centroidal predictive controller and a whole-body controller for physical box pushing under varying box mass. The proposed method achieves the lowest mean empirical regret during both training and adaptation among the considered baselines.

</details>


### [130] [ForeAct3D: Policy-Grounded Future World Modeling for VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04607)
*Zhe Tao,Feiran Wang,Gaowen Liu,Ramana Rao Kompella$,Yan Yan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForeAct3D 提出一种策略接地的未来世界建模框架，通过动作条件化的语义3D未来预测与物理一致性约束增强VLA策略，在仿真和真实操作任务中显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略从共享特征预测未来观测，但预测与策略实际执行动作解耦，且未对场景演化施加物理约束，导致操作成功所依赖的接触与物体运动预测不可靠。

Method: 在VLA策略表示上用可学习几何查询解码深度、语义分割和相机位姿，形成当前与未来语义3D场景状态；未来查询以策略生成的动作块为条件，使预测与计划交互对齐；通过物理一致性闭合约束背景静态性和实例级刚性，并将腕部相机位姿锚定到末端执行器运动学；训练时这些目标塑造用于动作生成的共享表示，推理时无需未来预测。

Result: 无机器人预训练下，LIBERO平均成功率98.3%，CALVIN平均任务长度3.73，并在所有套件上超过基策略；消融表明语义3D监督、物理一致性和动作条件化各自提升操作性能，动作条件化显著改善未来物体定位；真实世界空间放置、物体插入和顺序操作将平均成功率从6.7%提升到37.8%。

Conclusion: 将未来世界建模与策略动作及物理约束耦合，可有效学习更强的操作表征，且推理时无需未来预测；该方法在仿真与真实任务中一致优于基策略，验证了策略接地的语义3D未来监督对VLA操作的价值。

Abstract: Robots need to anticipate how their actions will change the world, since manipulation success hinges on the resulting contacts and object motions. However, existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies that predict future observations from shared features leave the forecast decoupled from the actions the policy will actually execute, and impose no physical constraints on how the scene may evolve. We introduce ForeAct3D, a framework for policy-grounded future world modeling within VLA policies. Learnable geometric queries decode depth, semantic segmentation, and camera pose from the policy representation into current and future semantic 3D scene states, and the future queries are conditioned on the policy-generated action chunk to ground the forecast in the planned interaction. A physical-consistency closure relates the two states through background staticity and instance-level rigidity, and anchors the wrist-camera pose to end-effector kinematics. These objectives shape the shared representation used for action generation during training, and no future prediction is required at inference. Without robot pretraining, ForeAct3D achieves 98.3\% average success on LIBERO and an average task length of 3.73 on CALVIN, outperforming its base policy on every suite. Ablations show that semantic 3D supervision, physical consistency, and action conditioning each improve manipulation performance, and that action conditioning substantially improves future object localization. Real-world experiments on spatial placement, object insertion, and sequential manipulation further raise average success from 6.7\% to 37.8\% over the base policy. The project page and code are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [131] [RiskFly: Frustum-Aligned Spatio-Temporal Risk Fields for One-Stage Agile Flight in Dynamic Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04566)
*Luxia Ai,Haopeng Chen,Yuchao Mei,Guohao Zhang,Wenbing Tao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RiskFly 是一种单阶段学习规划器，在动作空间内预测风险，通过双流观测和辅助时空风险场回归，实现未知、杂乱、动态环境中的敏捷飞行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于可微分特权成本的一阶段学习规划器速度快且无需专家，但编码器仅接收标量轨迹成本，缺乏空间和时间结构，导致避障退化为晚期反应式机动；因此需要让规划器知道危险将在何处、何时出现，而不仅知道轨迹危险。

Method: 提出 RiskFly。其双流观测将短深度序列与视锥对齐的倒置球面距离图序列配对，角度单元与末端状态提议一一对应。辅助头在特权最近接近点（CPA）目标监督下回归视锥对齐的时空风险场。该自预测风险场沿实例化五次轨迹在其到达时间可微分查询，并进入训练目标，使表征监督与规划梯度在同一空间汇合。部署时丢弃特权信号，规划器仅依赖机载深度和本体感觉，无需地图。

Result: 大量仿真和资源受限平台上的零样本真实飞行表明，在端到端延迟相当的情况下，RiskFly 取得了更高的成功率。

Conclusion: 将风险预测与动作/规划空间对齐，可增强一阶段规划器的前瞻避障能力，并在真实资源受限平台上有效部署。

Abstract: Agile flight in unknown, cluttered, and dynamic environments requires a planner that knows where and when danger will appear, not only that a trajectory is dangerous. One-stage learning-based planners trained with differentiable privileged costs are fast and expert-free, but the only signal reaching their encoder is a scalar trajectory cost with no spatial or temporal structure, so avoidance degrades into late reactive maneuvers. We present RiskFly, a one-stage planner that predicts risk in the same space in which it acts. A dual-stream observation pairs a short depth sequence with a frustum-aligned inverted spherical range-map sequence, whose angular cells match the end-state proposals one to one. An auxiliary head regresses a frustum-aligned spatio-temporal risk field, supervised by a privileged closest-point-of-approach (CPA) target. This self-predicted field is queried differentiably along the instantiated quintic trajectory at its own arrival times, and also enters the training objective, so representation supervision and planning gradients meet in a single space. Privileged signals are discarded at deployment, and the planner runs map-free from onboard depth and proprioception. Extensive simulation and zero-shot real-world flights on resource-constrained platforms show higher success rates at comparable end-to-end latency.

</details>


### [132] [Leveraging Cost-effective Robotics for K-12 STEM Education through Water Quality Monitoring Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04565)
*Rishi Mukherjee,Andrew Ruiz,Travis Henderson,Resha Tejpaul,Kris Simonson,David Mulla,Brian McNeil,Nikolaos Papanikolopoulos,Junaed Sattar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 介绍Jar Jar ROV低成本开源ROV平台，面向中学生公民科学水质监测，并在全州100多名学生中大规模部署；学生构建、编程和部署ROV，获得近1.1万条有效水质数据，但电子产品组装和数据上传环节存在参与度下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: K-12学生参与真实科学研究仍具挑战，环境科学与机器人交叉领域尤其缺乏可行方案；需要低成本、开源、可扩展平台，推动中学生开展公民科学水质监测。

Method: 设计Jar Jar ROV低成本开源ROV平台；组织100多名中学生构建、编程并在本地湖泊部署；采用教育框架评估参与度；建立基层监测网络，采集温度、pH、溶解氧和浊度等水质数据。

Result: 动手活动参与度高，ROV构建获导师满分平均评价；生成近11,000条经验证测量数据；发现关键“参与度鸿沟”，学生在电子组装和数据上传等复杂任务中兴趣骤降。

Conclusion: 贡献了将机器人融入环境教育的可验证、可扩展模型，以及数据驱动的改进路线图；未来需降低技术门槛，强化数据采集与科学发现之间的联系，赋能下一代公民科学家。

Abstract: Engaging K-12 students in authentic scientific research remains a significant challenge, particularly at the intersection of environmental science and robotics. We introduce the Jar Jar ROV, a low-cost, open-source Remotely Operated Vehicle (ROV) platform designed for citizen science-based water quality monitoring by middle school students. This paper presents the design of the platform and the results of a large-scale deployment with over 100 students across a US state who built, programmed, and deployed the ROVs in local lakes. The educational framework yielded high student engagement in hands-on activities, with ROV construction earning a perfect average score from mentors. Scientifically, the program established a grassroots monitoring network, generating nearly eleven thousand validated measurements of temperature, pH, dissolved oxygen, and turbidity. However, our evaluation identified a critical "engagement gap," with student interest declining sharply during more complex tasks such as electronics assembly and data uploading. This paper contributes both a validated, scalable model for integrating robotics into environmental education and a data-driven roadmap for future improvements. These enhancements focus on lowering technical barriers and creating a more intuitive link between data collection and scientific discovery, addressing a key challenge in empowering the next generation of citizen scientists.

</details>


### [133] [Online Target-less Radar-LiDAR-Camera Extrinsic Calibration via Joint Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04552)
*Gunhee Shin,Yunsoo Kim,Chanhyuk Lee,Wanhee Kim,Minwoo Lee,Sungwoo Han,Jeongwoo Woo,Hyuntai Chin,Minha Park,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向雷达-激光雷达-相机的在线无目标联合外参标定框架，通过联合优化、自适应雷达噪声滤波和跨帧对应累积，提高三传感器标定一致性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 雷达、激光雷达与相机融合可在复杂和恶劣条件下实现鲁棒感知，但其性能高度依赖三者间准确的外参标定。现有无目标方法多针对单传感器对设计，简单组合成对结果无法保证三传感器一致性；同时雷达测量稀疏且含噪，使涉及雷达的传感器对标定不可靠。

Method: 构建针对每个传感器对的残差，并联合优化所有传感器对的外参以最小化总体残差；引入自适应雷达噪声滤波器，利用距离相关边界抑制虚假雷达回波；采用对应累积策略，跨帧聚合稀疏的雷达对应关系。

Result: 在覆盖多样城市环境的自建雷达-激光雷达-相机数据集上验证，该方法相较最先进的相机-激光雷达基线，在所有传感器对上均降低了标定误差。

Conclusion: 所提联合标定框架能够有效实现雷达-激光雷达-相机系统的在线无目标外参标定，并提升多传感器标定的一致性与准确性。

Abstract: Fusing radar, LiDAR, and camera enables robust perception in diverse and adverse conditions, but the fusion performance critically depends on accurate extrinsic calibration among the three sensors. In this paper, we address the problem of online target-less extrinsic calibration for the radar-LiDAR-camera system. Existing target-less methods are mostly designed for a single sensor pair, and composing the pairwise results does not guarantee consistency across the three sensors. Moreover, the sparse and noisy radar measurements make the radar-involving pairs unreliable. To tackle these challenges, we propose a joint calibration framework that constructs residuals for each sensor pair and optimizes the extrinsics of all pairs together to minimize the overall residual. Furthermore, we introduce an adaptive radar noise filter that rejects spurious radar returns using a range-dependent margin, and a correspondence accumulation strategy that aggregates sparse radar correspondences over frames. We validate our method on an in-house radar-LiDAR-camera dataset covering diverse urban environments, where it reduces calibration errors across all sensor pairs over a state-of-the-art camera-LiDAR baseline.

</details>


### [134] [NM-LIO: Multiple LiDAR-Inertial Odometry Addressing LiDAR Measurement Noise Discrepancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04551)
*Gunhee Shin,Seungjae Lee,Minho Oh,Dongkyu Lee,Jaeyoung Lee,Youngwoo Seo,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出噪声感知多LiDAR-惯性里程计NM-LIO，通过建模各LiDAR测量噪声并估计残差不确定性，解决多LiDAR噪声差异问题，在公开数据集上实现高精度里程计估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多LiDAR-惯性里程计虽精度和可靠性高，但不同LiDAR间测量噪声存在差异，现有方法通常忽略该差异，从而显著影响定位精度。

Method: 集成噪声模型以量化每个LiDAR的测量噪声；基于该噪声估计残差不确定性，使测量模型能够捕捉不同LiDAR间的噪声差异。

Result: 在公开多LiDAR数据集上评估，并与当前先进方法比较，结果表明所提方法通过考虑噪声差异，能在多种环境中准确估计里程计。

Conclusion: NM-LIO有效处理多LiDAR系统中的测量噪声不一致问题，提升多LiDAR-惯性里程计在复杂环境下的精度与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Multiple LiDAR-inertial odometry methods have been widely applied in robotic applications owing to their enhanced accuracy and reliability. However, adopting multiple LiDAR systems can be challenging due to the discrepancies in measurement noise across different LiDARs. Existing methods have typically overlooked the noise discrepancies, which can significantly affect accuracy. In this paper, we propose Noise-aware Multiple LiDAR-Inertial Odometry (NM-LIO) that addresses the noise discrepancies. We integrate a noise model to quantify the measurement noise of each LiDAR. Additionally, we estimate the uncertainty of the residuals based on the measurement noise, allowing the measurement model to capture the noise discrepancies. Our proposed method is evaluated on a public multiple LiDAR dataset and compared with state-of-the-art methods. The experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method can accurately estimate the odometry in various environments by accounting for the noise discrepancies.

</details>


### [135] [DreamFormer: Dream Imitation with a Transformer World Model for Language-Conditioned Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04540)
*Mostafa Kotb,Cornelius Weber,Muhammad Burhan Hafez,Stefan Wermter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamFormer 是一种基于世界模型的智能体，通过在潜空间中想象专家演示来学习语言条件多任务技能，在 CALVIN 长时程基准上优于 LUMOS，并在零样本迁移上近乎翻倍于行为克隆基线 HULC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法中，离线行为克隆存在协变量偏移，且长时程想象训练代价高；同时，语言条件多任务技能获取需要既能利用专家演示又能适应自身误差的策略。

Method: DreamFormer 先从未结构化 play 数据学习任务无关的 Transformer 世界模型；再在想象中 on-policy 训练策略，用内在奖励对齐智能体 rollout 与专家演示的潜空间表示，从而缓解离线行为克隆的协变量偏移。为降低长时程想象成本，它将高分辨率多视角机器人观测编码为单个输入 token，避免空间下采样和先前 Transformer 世界模型的多 token 表示。

Result: 在长时程 CALVIN 基准上，单环境评估中 DreamFormer 每五任务链平均完成 2.52 个任务，优于可比模型 LUMOS 的 2.34；在零样本迁移到未见环境时，性能为 1.30，近乎是行为克隆基线 HULC 的 0.67 的两倍，同时想象 rollout 保持可计算。

Conclusion: 世界模型学到的动力学比直接克隆的策略更易迁移，这与生物智能体中内部模型的作用一致：环境动力学模型可支持在先前未遇情境中的行为。

Abstract: We introduce DreamFormer, a model-based agent that acquires language-conditioned, multi-task skills by imitating expert demonstrations within the latent imagination of a learned world model. DreamFormer first learns a task-agnostic Transformer world model from unstructured play data, then acquires task-specific behaviors by optimizing an intrinsic reward that aligns agent-generated rollouts with expert demonstrations in latent space. Since the policy is trained on-policy inside imagination, it is exposed to its own errors during training, mitigating the covariate shift inherent to offline behavioral cloning. To make long-horizon imagination affordable, DreamFormer encodes a high-resolution multi-view robotic observation into a single input token, avoiding both spatial downsampling and the multi-token representations used by prior Transformer world models. On the long-horizon CALVIN benchmark, DreamFormer outperforms LUMOS, the comparable model-based agent, on single-environment evaluation (2.52 vs 2.34 average tasks completed per chain of five) while keeping imagination rollouts tractable. Against HULC, the behavior cloning baseline, it nearly doubles performance on zero-shot transfer to an unseen environment (1.30 vs 0.67), indicating that dynamics learned by the world model transfer more readily than a directly cloned policy. This is consistent with the role attributed to internal models in biological agents, where a model of environment dynamics supports behavior in situations not previously encountered.

</details>


### [136] [WasserMan: Benchmark for Underwater Manipulation Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04536)
*Danil Belov,Artem Erkhov,Sergei Parsegov,Pavel Osinenko*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出水下浮动基座接触操作视觉运动学习多任务仿真基准 WasserMan，并系统评估多种模仿学习策略与执行因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下操作需耦合视觉决策、接触力与推进器控制的浮动基座，然而缺乏面向浮动基座水下接触操作视觉运动学习的多任务仿真基准。

Method: WasserMan 提供十个专家可解任务、两种车-臂平台、双臂配置和水下动力学；九项任务含学习策略评估。在六项任务上以三次训练和相同采样窗口预算比较 ACT、扩散策略（DP）与行为克隆，并在三项任务上评估预训练 SmolVLA；配套版本化任务、演示与逐回合证据。

Result: 测试中，去除积分作用可能阻止任务完成；即使专家回放成功，改变动作接口也可降低学习策略成功率；水流会任务依赖地改变完成度与驱动努力。

Conclusion: WasserMan 支持将专家可行性、学习完成度与执行努力分离评估的协议，为水下浮动基座接触操作视觉运动学习提供可复现基准。

Abstract: Underwater manipulation couples visual decisions, contact forces and a thruster-controlled floating base. We introduce WasserMan, to our knowledge the first multi-task simulation benchmark for visuomotor learning of floating-base underwater contact manipulation. It provides ten expert-solvable tasks, two vehicle-arm platforms, a bimanual configuration and underwater dynamics. Nine tasks have learned-policy evaluations. We compare ACT, diffusion policies (DP) and behavioral cloning on six tasks, with three training runs and equal sampled-window budgets. Pretrained SmolVLA adds evaluations on three tasks. In the tested settings, removing integral action can prevent completion, while changing action interfaces can reduce learned-policy success despite successful expert replay. Currents produce task-dependent changes in completion and actuation effort. Versioned tasks, demonstrations and per-episode evidence support a protocol separating expert feasibility, learned completion and execution effort.

</details>


### [137] [Reachability-Guided Sequential Quadratic Programming-Guarded Model Predictive Path Integral for Safe Nonlinear Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04406)
*Alexandre Didier,Jason J. Choi,Namhoon Cho,Claire J. Tomlin,Melanie N. Zeilinger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ReSQ-MPPI，一种基于可达性信息的生成-精炼架构，将离线 HJR 安全引导、在线 MPPI 采样与全阶 MPC 的 SQP 约束精炼结合，以提升安全与性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以同时兼顾长时域性能优化与硬状态/输入约束：采样型 MPC（如 MPPI）适合非线性非凸环境，但有限采样与无约束加权平均可能产生不安全控制；确定性非线性 MPC 可显式处理约束，但实时安全与性能依赖热启动和局部收敛；HJR 能提供严格安全证书，但离线值函数计算通常仅适用于降阶模型。

Method: ReSQ-MPPI 使用离线计算的降阶模型 HJR 值函数引导在线 MPPI 采样，使其偏向安全且有前景的轨迹候选；随后用少量 SQP 迭代在全阶 MPC 问题中精炼 MPPI 解。关键是将标准 MPPI 推断视为无约束加权最小二乘问题，并用带约束的 MPC 精炼替代：当 MPPI 更新可行时恢复该更新，否则以最小修改使其满足约束。

Result: 在杂乱导航与自动驾驶赛车仿真中，ReSQ-MPPI 相比独立 MPPI、MPC 以及基于可达性过滤的采样控制基线，提升了安全性和性能。

Conclusion: ReSQ-MPPI 通过可达性引导采样与约束精炼，有效结合 HJR 的安全证书、MPPI 的非凸探索能力和 MPC 的硬约束处理，在复杂环境中实现更安全且高性能的机器人控制。

Abstract: Safe robot control often requires combining long-horizon performance optimization with hard state and input constraints, but existing approaches tend to address this tradeoff partially. Sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) methods such as model predictive path integral (MPPI) are effective in handling nonlinear and nonconvex environments, yet their finite-sample rollouts and unconstrained weighted-average update can return an unsafe control. Deterministic nonlinear MPC can explicitly incorporate constraints, but its real-time safety and performance depends strongly on warm starts and local convergence. Hamilton--Jacobi reachability (HJR) provides rigorous safety certificates, but offline value-function computation remains practical only for reduced-order models. We propose ReSQ-MPPI, a reachability-informed generation--refinement architecture that combines these complementary strengths. An HJR value function computed offline for a reduced-order model guides online MPPI sampling toward safe, promising trajectory candidates. The MPPI solution is then refined by a small number of sequential quadratic programming (SQP) iterations in a full-order MPC problem. A key observation is that the standard MPPI inference step is an unconstrained weighted least-squares problem; ReSQ-MPPI replaces it with a constrained MPC refinement that recovers the MPPI update when it is feasible and minimally modifies it otherwise. Simulations in cluttered navigation and autonomous racing environments demonstrate that ReSQ-MPPI improves safety and performance over standalone MPPI, MPC, and reachability-filtered sampling-based control baselines.

</details>


### [138] [AgenticTactileVLA: Contact-Guided Execution-Time Supervision for Generalizable Dexterous Manipulation without VLA Retraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04391)
*Elizaveta Semenyakina,Ivan Snegirev,Mikhail Kiselev,Miguel Altamirano Cabrera,Artem Lykov,Hajira Amjad,Dzmitry Tsetserukou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 AgenticTactileVLA 执行时监督器，无需触觉传感器或重训 VLA，利用手指位置与电机力矩反馈自适应抓取，提升固定 VLA 对未见物体的操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略虽能预测可迁移的操作方案，但在真实物体上常执行失败：同一抓取兼容的物体在几何和柔顺性上存在差异，且闭合阶段遮挡导致视觉反馈退化。因此需要将部分物体特定适应从预测阶段转移到物理交互阶段。

Method: 固定 VLA 提供接近与手部目标；监督器决定保持透明、微调手指弯曲、保留或释放修正配置、交还 VLA 重试，或选择柔顺手控策略。其以手指位置和电机努力反馈作为本体感受接触证据，不依赖触觉传感器，也不重训 VLA。在 Unitree G1 与 BrainCo Revo2 手上，对 VLA 训练中留出的五个物体进行随机匹配方块评估，并包含消融、保留审计与薄壁杯研究。

Result: 共享预算下，基础 VLA 完成率 61.3%，无条件闭合至堵转控制为 72.0%，监督器为 84.0%；增益在每个物体上均为正，并在中等位姿扰动下保持。消融显示增益不能仅由延长闭合解释；选择性触发将修正回合减少 65.7%，完成率无可检测变化。保留审计中，接受预测保留的留出案例占 88.9%，柔顺物体暴露保守误拒。薄壁杯研究显示可上下文路由至柔顺控制，并匹配始终柔顺参考。

Conclusion: 接触引导的执行时适应可通过调整物理实现方式，提升固定 VLA 对留出物体的物体级泛化能力，且无需针对特定物体重新训练。

Abstract: Vision-language-action policies may predict a transferable manipulation strategy yet fail to realize it reliably on the encountered object: objects compatible with the same grasp differ in geometry and compliance, and visual feedback degrades under closure occlusion. AgenticTactileVLA is presented as an execution-time supervisor that shifts part of object-specific adaptation from prediction to physical interaction. A fixed VLA provides the approach and hand targets; the supervisor decides whether to remain transparent, refine finger flexion, retain or release the corrected configuration, return control to the VLA for retry, or select a compliant hand-control regime. It uses finger-position and motor-effort feedback as proprioceptive contact evidence and requires neither tactile sensors nor VLA retraining. On a Unitree G1 with a BrainCo Revo2 hand, a randomized matched-block evaluation on five objects held out from VLA training yields 61.3% completion for the base VLA, 72.0% for unconditional close-to-stall control, and 84.0% for the supervisor under a shared budget; the gain is positive on every object and persists under moderate pose perturbations. Ablations show the gain is not explained by extended closure alone, and that selective triggering reduces correction episodes by 65.7% with no detected change in completion. A retention audit shows acceptance predicts retention in 88.9% of held-out cases, while compliant objects expose conservative false rejection. A thin-walled-cup study demonstrates contextual routing to compliant control, matching an always-compliant reference. These results suggest that contact-guided execution-time adaptation can improve the object-level generalization of a fixed VLA to held-out objects by adapting physical realization without object-specific retraining.

</details>


### [139] [Frame-Level Temporal Alignment for Human-to-Robot Visual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04372)
*Xizhe Zhang,Jingfeng Zhang,Zirun Zhou,Hong Jia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FLTA框架，利用全局进度先验和局部时序顺序先验进行帧级时序对齐，无需帧级对应标注即可将人类视频预训练的视觉编码器适配到机器人操作，在仿真和真实任务中显著提升成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将人类视频预训练的视觉表征迁移到机器人操作时，需要学习人类与机器人演示间的可靠对应。但配对演示在执行速率和非关键帧比例上存在差异，导致相同相对时间戳的帧可能对应不同任务阶段，从而使对应学习失败。

Method: 提出帧级时序对齐（FLTA）框架，使用两种时序先验适配预训练视觉编码器。它无需帧级对应标注即可学习共享任务进度表征，允许不同相对时序位置的帧匹配。全局进度先验结合归一化时序位置与视觉相似度构建软对应目标；局部时序顺序先验惩罚向后跳转，同时允许停留和不同前进速率以适应执行速率差异。在ResNet-50和ViT编码器上验证。

Result: 在平均仿真成功率上，相比最佳基线分别相对提升46.93%（ResNet-50）和65.96%（ViT）；在真实机器人操作任务中取得更高成功率。结果表明，有效的人-机器人适配更取决于选择哪些参数更新，而非更新参数的数量。

Conclusion: FLTA通过时序先验对齐有效解决了人类-机器人演示间的时序错位问题，提升了迁移效果，为机器人操作中的视觉表征适配提供了新思路。

Abstract: Transferring visual representations pretrained on human videos to robot manipulation requires learning reliable correspondences between human and robot demonstrations. However, paired demonstrations can differ in execution rate and in the proportion of non-key frames that do not directly reflect task progress. Frames at the same relative timestamp may therefore represent different task stages, which can cause correspondence learning to fail. To address these issues, we propose Frame-Level Temporal Alignment (FLTA), a framework that uses two temporal priors to adapt visual encoders pretrained on human videos for robot manipulation. It learns shared task-progress representations without frame-level correspondence annotations, allowing frames at different relative temporal positions to match. A global progress prior combines normalized temporal positions with visual similarity to construct a soft correspondence target. A local temporal order prior penalizes backward transitions while allowing stays and varying forward rates to accommodate execution-rate differences. With ResNet-50 and ViT encoders, our method achieves relative improvements of 46.93% and 65.96%, respectively, over the best baselines in average simulation success rates and achieves higher task success rates on real-world manipulation tasks. These results also suggest that effective human-robot adaptation depends less on the number of parameters updated than on which parameters are selected. Our project page is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [140] [Human Behavior-Informed Crash Scenario Generation with Real-World Crash Priors for Autonomous Vehicle Safety Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04366)
*Mingxing Peng,Xusen Guo,Long Chen,Xintao Yan,Siyu Teng,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CrashSim 是一种由人类行为信息与真实碰撞先验引导的生成式多智能体交通仿真框架，用于生成真实且可扩展的碰撞场景，以提升自动驾驶安全评估的可靠性；并构建了 nuCrash 数据集，闭环评估显示其比 nuScenes 更能区分不同规划器的安全能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆的安全评估依赖对罕见碰撞的真实仿真，但现有碰撞场景生成方法虽能提高碰撞发生率，却往往无法真实复现碰撞前演化过程以及现实世界中的碰撞类型分布，导致评估可靠性不足。

Method: 提出 CrashSim：利用真实世界碰撞先验引导生成式多智能体交通仿真；先验捕获碰撞前演化方式与碰撞类型分布，使有限碰撞数据可指导自然驾驶情境下真实且可扩展的场景生成。进一步构建包含 4000 多个碰撞与近碰撞场景的 nuCrash 数据集，并使用 LLM 辅助评估代理分析规划器失败。

Result: 与竞争方法相比，CrashSim 更贴近真实碰撞前行为、碰撞动力学、碰撞几何与碰撞类型分布；对五个 AV 规划器进行闭环评估表明，nuCrash 比 nuScenes 更有效暴露规划器安全能力差异；LLM 辅助评估代理可提供能力级诊断和有针对性改进指导。

Conclusion: CrashSim 实现了真实且可扩展的碰撞场景生成，可为自动驾驶车辆提供更具信息量的安全评估，并支持规划器能力诊断与改进。

Abstract: Reliable safety evaluation of autonomous vehicles (AVs) is essential to improving road safety, yet it depends critically on realistic simulation of rare crashes. Existing crash scenario generation methods can increase collision occurrence, but often fail to realistically reproduce how crashes evolve before impact or the distribution of crash types observed in the real world. Here, we present CrashSim, a human behavior-informed crash scenario generation framework that uses real-world crash priors to guide generative multi-agent traffic simulation for more reliable AV safety evaluation. These priors capture how real-world crashes evolve before impact and how different crash types are distributed, allowing limited crash data to guide realistic and scalable scenario generation across naturalistic driving contexts. We evaluate CrashSim against competing methods, showing that it more closely reproduces real-world pre-impact behavior, collision dynamics, collision geometry and crash-type distributions. We further use CrashSim to construct nuCrash dataset, containing over 4,000 crash and near-crash scenarios. Closed-loop evaluation of five AV planners shows that nuCrash more effectively exposes differences in planner safety capabilities than nuScenes. An LLM-assisted evaluation agent further analyzes planner failures to provide capability-level diagnoses and targeted improvement guidance. Together, CrashSim enables realistic and scalable crash generation for more informative AV safety evaluation.

</details>


### [141] [TacOT: Learning Contact-Rich Dexterous Manipulation from Human Demonstrations via Tactile-Guided Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04363)
*Xingting Li,Yifan Han,Zijian Lin,Wei Hou,Chuqiao Lyu,Shoujie Li,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TacOT：一种触觉引导最优传输框架，用于将人类演示迁移到机器人富接触灵巧操作中，无需预定义帧级人-机器人配对。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人到机器人技能迁移方法多依赖视觉外观或运动相似性，但相似运动可能对应不同接触状态与力模式，导致人-机器人对应不可靠。触觉动力学能提供交互感知线索，区分运动相似但接触状态不同的操作过程。

Method: TacOT 使用动作-触觉动态时间规整（action-tactile DTW）识别人-机器人演示中交互动力学一致的对应关系，并用这些对应关系引导共享策略表示空间中的软最优传输对齐，从而让人体演示为机器人策略学习提供富接触监督。

Result: 在四个真实世界灵巧操作任务中，TacOT 相比动作引导最优传输（action-guided OT）将闭环成功率在分布内任务上最多提升 17 个百分点，在针对性人-机器人分布外迁移下最多提升 20 个百分点。进一步分析表明，触觉引导对应能选出接触动力学更一致的演示对，并产生更好反映交互状态演化的潜在表示。

Conclusion: 触觉动力学可作为有效语义信号，用于在富接触灵巧操作中建立可靠的人-机器人对应关系。

Abstract: Learning contact-rich dexterous manipulation from human demonstrations provides a scalable source of interaction data, yet transferring such skills to robots remains challenging due to unreliable human--robot correspondence. Existing human-to-robot transfer methods typically rely on visual appearance or motion similarity, which may associate similar motions with different contact states and force patterns. Tactile dynamics provide interaction-aware cues to distinguish manipulation processes with similar motions but different contact states. We introduce tactile-guided optimal transport (TacOT), a framework for human-to-robot contact-rich manipulation. TacOT leverages action--tactile dynamic time warping to identify human--robot demonstration correspondences with consistent interaction dynamics and uses these correspondences to guide soft optimal transport alignment in a shared policy representation space. This enables human demonstrations to provide contact-rich supervision for robot policy learning without requiring predefined frame-level human--robot pairing. Across four real-world dexterous manipulation tasks, TacOT improves closed-loop success rates over action-guided OT by up to 17 points on in-distribution tasks and 20 points under targeted human-to-robot out-of-distribution transfer. Further analyses show that tactile-guided correspondence selects demonstration pairs with more consistent contact dynamics and produces latent representations that better reflect interaction-state evolution. These results demonstrate that tactile dynamics provide an effective semantic signal for establishing reliable human-to-robot correspondence in contact-rich dexterous manipulation.

</details>


### [142] [Asynchronous Tracking, Optical Communication and 3D Motion Capture using Event-based Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04342)
*Ziwei Wang,Angus Apps,Holly Battisson,Iain Guilliard,Timothy Molloy,Robert Mahony*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ATOC系统，将LED智能信标调制、事件相机检测、光学跟踪与事件数据解调集成于单条流水线，可同时跟踪并解调多个LED信标，把传统视觉标记转化为鲁棒光通信通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无线通信虽普遍简单，但存在接收方难以定位信号源、时钟同步困难、信号广播而非定向等固有局限；光通信具有定向性和空间局部性，可互补这些不足。事件相机具备高时间分辨率、高动态范围和低延迟，适合作为协作机器人系统中高速光通信的接收器。

Method: ATOC集成LED智能信标调制、基于事件的检测、光学跟踪和事件数据解调，形成统一流水线；通过同时跟踪与解调多个LED智能信标，实现定向、空间局部的机器人间光通信。

Result: 在系列实验室研究中验证了ATOC，并展示两个应用：智慧城市演示和3D运动捕捉系统。

Conclusion: ATOC可将普通视觉标记扩展为鲁棒通信通道，适用于多种高影响力机器人应用，表明事件相机光通信在协作机器人中具有实用潜力。

Abstract: Inter-robot communication is a key enabling technology in cooperative robotics applications. While wireless communication is ubiquitous, simple, and effective, it has inherent limitations: the receiving robot cannot easily localise the source of an incoming signal, clock synchronisation is challenging, and signals are broadcast to all nearby devices rather than targeted recipients. Optical communication provides a complementary channel that is inherently directional and spatially localised, directly addressing these limitations. Event cameras are bio-inspired dynamic vision sensors that respond to changes in image intensity with high temporal resolution, high dynamic range, and low latency, making them well-suited as receivers for high-rate optical communication in cooperative robotic systems. In this paper, we propose the Asynchronous Tracking and Optical Communication (ATOC) system, which integrates LED smart-beacon modulation, event-based detection, optical tracking, and event-data demodulation in a single pipeline. By simultaneously tracking and demodulating multiple LED smart beacons, ATOC transforms a conventional visual marker into a robust communication channel suitable for a wide range of high-impact robotics applications. We validate ATOC in a suite of laboratory studies and two 'applications': a smart-city demonstration and a 3D motion-capture system.

</details>


### [143] [Real-Time Conformal-Seeded Hybrid Inverse Kinematics for Offset Redundant Manipulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04266)
*Duc Cuong Vu,Van Tung Nguyen,Duc Hai Nguyen,Manh Cuong Nguyen,Vu Trung Tran,Minh Nhat Vu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于共形校准种子选择的混合逆运动学框架，用于偏移冗余7自由度人形机械臂，结合近似解析候选生成、学习排序与Levenberg-Marquardt数值精修，实现<40us实时性与可达目标100%成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析逆运动学（AIK）计算成本低但仅适用于简化模型，偏移结构缺乏精确闭式解；数值逆运动学（NIK）精度高却对初值敏感、收敛性差。因此需要兼顾速度、精度与鲁棒性的逆运动学方法。

Method: 采用两阶段混合框架：首先用近似解析模型枚举有限候选关节解；其次用轻量学习预测器评估候选精修难度，并通过分裂共形预测将其转化为校准上界作为选择分数；最后选取最佳种子，在完整运动学模型上用Levenberg-Marquardt求解器精修。

Result: 在可达目标评估中成功率达100%，单次求解时间低于40us；通过工作空间大规模随机仿真与人形机械臂运动规划实验验证了方法有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架有效融合快速候选生成、校准难度排序与精确数值精修，为偏移冗余机械臂提供实时、高成功率的逆运动学解决方案，适用于人形机器人运动规划。

Abstract: This paper presents a conformal-seeded hybrid strategy for solving inverse kinematics of offset, redundant 7-DoF robot arms of the humanoid class. Analytical inverse kinematics (AIK) provides closed-form solutions with very low computational cost. However, for offset kinematic structures, the exact closed-form solution is generally unavailable, and practical AIK must rely on an approximate or simplified kinematic model. In contrast, numerical inverse kinematics (NIK) can achieve high-precision solutions on the full kinematic model. However, its convergence is highly sensitive to initialization. To overcome these limitations, we propose a two-stage hybrid inverse kinematics framework with conformal-calibrated seed selection. First, an approximate analytical model efficiently enumerates a finite set of candidate joint solutions. Second, we rank these candidates using a lightweight learned predictor of post-refinement difficulty, wrapped by split-conformal prediction into a calibrated upper bound that serves as the selection score. The best-ranked seed is then refined using a Levenberg-Marquardt solver on the full kinematic model.
