The proposed method combines fast candidate generation, learned seed ranking with a calibrated difficulty bound, and accurate numerical refinement, achieving real-time performance of less than 40us and a success rate of 100% in our evaluation on reachable targets. We validate the approach through large-scale stochastic simulation across the workspace and experimental demonstrations with motion planning on a humanoid robot arm. Demonstration videos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [144] [Grounded in Time: A Multi-Source Dataset and Benchmark for Temporal Grounding in Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04255)
*Yi Wang,Yang Yang,Guangqi Xu,Sumin Lin,Ning Kang,Pengxiang Lu,Xiaotong Chen,Zeyu Xue,Ping Deng,Xing Liu,Chenguang Yang,Zhenyu Lu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 GiT 数据集与基准，用于评估机器人在历史依赖场景中基于过去事件进行操作的语义推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前观测不足以决定动作时，机器人需从过去交互推断任务相关状态；现有记忆增强 VLA 模型基准对应用导向、依赖历史的语义推理任务覆盖不足。

Method: 构建 GiT（Grounded in Time）数据集与基准，涵盖生物实验室、家庭和工业场景；包含真实机器人和 UMI 风格演示，覆盖 18 个双臂任务，并提供仿真数据与基于 ManiSkill 的评测套件，覆盖 9 个任务；含细粒度子任务标注和反事实任务对，其中相似当前观测需依据先前事件采取不同动作。

Result: 在仿真和部分真实任务上评估代表性端到端 VLA 模型，发现历史依赖操作仍有较大提升空间。

Conclusion: GiT 为历史依赖的机器人操作提供数据、标注和评测基准，支持策略学习与针对性评估，并揭示当前 VLA 模型在利用历史信息方面的不足。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation often requires inferring task-relevant states from past interactions when the current observation alone is insufficient to determine the appropriate action. Despite progress in benchmarking memory-augmented vision-language-action (VLA) models, application-oriented tasks requiring history-dependent semantic inference remain underrepresented. We introduce GiT (Grounded in Time), a dataset and benchmark for grounding manipulation decisions in past events across biolaboratory, household, and industrial scenarios. It includes real-robot and Universal Manipulation Interface (UMI) style demonstrations covering 18 bimanual tasks, together with simulation data and a ManiSkill-based evaluation suite covering nine tasks. Fine-grained subtask annotations and annotated counterfactual task pairs, in which similar current observations require different actions depending on prior events, support policy learning and targeted evaluation of history use. Evaluations of representative end-to-end VLA models in simulation and on selected real-world tasks reveal substantial room for improvement in history-dependent manipulation. The dataset and benchmark are available at the project page.

</details>


### [145] [ROOT: Discovering Rewards for User-Specified Embodied Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04250)
*Eren Sadikoglu,Aditya Taparia,Xinyuan Liu,Ransalu Senanayake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ROOT 通过观测引导的持久实验树搜索与视频-语言模型反馈，迭代优化奖励函数，使具身控制策略更符合用户指定的行为风格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身强化学习中的奖励设计困难，尤其 LLM 生成奖励函数虽能从自然语言合成奖励，却常无法捕捉人类关心的步态、姿态、运动风格等细微行为属性；这些行为往往更易被视觉识别而非直接编码为标量奖励。

Method: 提出 ROOT（Reward Optimization via Observable Trees），将奖励设计建模为观测引导的搜索过程；维护一棵持久实验树，节点存储奖励程序、训练所得策略、rollout 观测，以及由视频-语言模型蒸馏的行为洞察，用于诊断行为失败并指导后续奖励细化，而非仅依赖标量训练统计。

Result: 在 7 个任务、4 种具身平台（仿真 Hopper、HalfCheetah、Ant、Unitree Go2 及真实 Unitree Go2）上评估。相比现有 LLM 奖励生成方法，ROOT 更符合用户意图，运动完整性准确率最高达 86.8%；Vid-LLM 行为对齐评分从 3.56/5 提升至 4.14/5，相对基线提升 16.5%；人类评估中 ROOT 在 51–63% 的成对比较中被偏好。

Conclusion: ROOT 表明，结合观测引导的实验树搜索与视频-语言模型的行为诊断，可有效发现更对齐用户意图的奖励函数，并提升仿真与真实具身控制策略的行为质量。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for embodied control remains constrained by the difficulty of reward specification. Although recent large language model (LLM)-based methods can synthesize reward functions from natural-language descriptions, they often fail to capture subtle behavioral properties that humans care about, such as natural gait, posture, and movement style. This limitation arises because many desired behaviors are easier to recognize visually than to encode in a reward function. We introduce Reward Optimization via Observable Trees (ROOT), a framework for discovering reward functions that align learned policies with user-specified embodied behaviors. Rather than relying solely on scalar training statistics, ROOT casts reward design as an observation-guided search over a persistent experiment tree that stores reward programs, trained policies, and rollout observations, together with behavioral insights distilled by a video-language model, to diagnose behavioral failures and guide subsequent reward refinements. We evaluate ROOT on seven tasks across four embodiments: simulated Hopper, HalfCheetah, Ant, Unitree Go2, and as well as the real-world Unitree Go2. ROOT produces behaviors that better align with user intent than those generated by existing LLM-based reward-generation methods, achieving up to 86.8% locomotion-completeness accuracy and improving Vid-LLM behavioral alignment from 3.56/5 to 4.14/5, a 16.5% improvement over baselines. Human evaluations further support these results, with ROOT preferred in 51-63% of pairwise comparisons.

</details>


### [146] [Attention-Based Surface Representation Learning for Robot State Prediction and Open-Ended Surface Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04240)
*Oleg Kushnarev,Alexander Belyaev*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种可扩展地形表面类别的机器人地形分类方法，通过预测下一状态与注意力机制，在Belyaev-Kushnarev和BorealTC数据集上分别达到98.56%和94.8%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地面机器人在户外环境中需要理解地形属性以保证可靠运行；但多数基于感知的研究将问题设定为训练时类别数固定的分类，无法方便地加入新表面类别。

Method: 将新表面类别表示为可训练向量以便后续添加并用于更高层任务；采用基于预测机器人下一时刻状态的学习范式，并结合注意力块进行地形分类。

Result: 在Belyaev-Kushnarev数据集上分类准确率提升至98.56%，在BorealTC上提升至94.8%。

Conclusion: 该方法实现了表面类别的可扩展表示，并显著提升地形分类精度，可为更高层任务提供支持。

Abstract: For ground robots operating in outdoor environments, understanding the properties of the underlying terrain is essential for ensuring reliable operation. In most perception-based studies, this problem is formulated as categorical classification with a fixed number of classes defined during training. We propose an approach that enables new surface classes to be added as trainable vectors, which can subsequently be used to address higher-level tasks. By employing a learning paradigm based on predicting the robot's next state in time and using attention blocks, we improved classification accuracy to 98.56% on the Belyaev-Kushnarev dataset and 94.8% on BorealTC.

</details>


### [147] [Humanoid Rickshaw Pulling: Whole-Body Locomotion under Coupled Wheeled Loads](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04238)
*Yangzhi Yang,Xiansheng Lin,Zhaoming Xie,Xiaobin Xiong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种人形机器人拉人力车的全身控制框架：训练时用特权教师获取车辆状态、交互力与负载属性，蒸馏为历史条件学生并从本体感知隐式推断耦合动力学，再经强化学习微调；在Unitree G1上实现拉起、持续拉行、转向和停止，最大负载人力车质量达115 kg且无需按负载重新调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人可通过拉被动轮式车辆来运输远超自身重量的载荷，而非直接背负。但这会形成耦合运动问题：机器人必须保持持续的上肢接触，同时适应由负载、车辆和地形带来的未知且随构型变化的力。

Method: 面向人形机器人拉人力车的全身控制框架，在不确定负载动力学下跟踪指令车辆运动，并保持平衡与稳定抓握。训练阶段，特权教师利用车辆状态、交互力和负载属性；将其动作与隐变量蒸馏到历史条件学生策略中，使其从本体感知响应隐式推断耦合动力学，随后进行强化学习微调。与No History和Only History基线比较。

Result: 所得策略实现准确车辆跟踪，同时降低车辆振荡、躯干倾角和驱动成本。行为分析表明Unitree G1能推动人力车，并产生与步态同步的全身反应以稳定横向和滚转运动。拉行还重新分配关节出力，在多数测试负载-速度条件下，其机器人归一化运输成本代理低于空载行走。硬件上，单一策略可完成起步、持续拉行、转向和停止，适用于刚性负载和人类乘客，处理高达115 kg的负载人力车质量且无需针对负载重新调参。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过协调且持续的人形机器人-车辆交互，可实现鲁棒的重载运输。

Abstract: Humanoid robots could transport payloads substantially heavier than themselves by pulling passive wheeled vehicles instead of carrying the load. This capability, however, creates a coupled locomotion problem: the robot must maintain persistent upper-body contact while adapting to unknown, configuration-dependent forces arising from the payload, vehicle, and terrain. We present a whole-body control framework for humanoid rickshaw pulling that tracks commanded vehicle motion while preserving balance and stable grasps under uncertain load dynamics. During training, a privileged teacher exploits vehicle states, interaction forces, and load properties. Its actions and latent are distilled into a history-conditioned student that implicitly infers coupled dynamics from proprioceptive responses, followed by reinforcement-learning fine-tuning. Comparisons with \emph{No History} and \emph{Only History} baselines show that the resulting policy achieves accurate vehicle tracking while reducing vehicle oscillation, torso tilt, and actuation cost. Behavioral analysis shows that Unitree G1 propels the rickshaw and generates gait-synchronized whole-body reactions that stabilize its lateral and roll motions. Moreover, pulling redistributes joint effort and yields a lower robot-normalized cost-of-transport proxy than unloaded walking over most tested load--speed conditions. On hardware, a single policy performs starting, sustained pulling, turning, and stopping with both rigid payloads and human passengers, handling a loaded rickshaw mass of up to 115~kg without load-specific retuning. These results demonstrate robust heavy-load transportation through coordinated and persistent humanoid--vehicle interaction.

</details>


### [148] [Continual Humanoid Motion Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04231)
*Zhewen He,Hao Huang,Geeta Chandra Raju Bethala,Chong Yu,Tao Chen,Anthony Tzes,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Similarity-guided LoRA-PNN，用于人形全身运动的持续学习：在无历史数据回放的顺序任务流中，通过渐进神经网络防遗忘，并用相似度引导的低秩适配复用知识，实现高前向迁移、高精度与显著参数/时间节省，并部署于 Unitree G1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形全身控制器通常离线训练后冻结，学习新技能会侵蚀已掌握技能，产生灾难性遗忘；因此需要在不回放历史数据的顺序任务流中持续学习单一控制器。

Method: 提出 Similarity-guided LoRA-PNN：一种渐进神经网络（PNN）策略，按构造避免灾难性遗忘，并通过轻量低秩适配（LoRA）跨技能复用知识；使用两级运动相似度度量，由动态时间规整（DTW）结合最优传输（optimal transport）聚合，决定基于哪个先验技能扩展以及分配多少新容量。

Result: 在六类顺序学习的技能上，相似度引导的 LoRA 策略取得最佳前向迁移（0.125 vs. 0.079）和最高平均准确率，同时节省最高 94.5% 可训练参数与 40.8% 训练时间；所得控制器达到 96.13% sim-to-sim 迁移，并部署到实体 Unitree G1。

Conclusion: Similarity-guided LoRA-PNN 能在人形全身运动持续学习中有效防止遗忘、促进知识复用与正向迁移，并兼顾参数与训练效率，且具备良好的仿真到仿真和实机部署能力。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body controllers can now track a diverse set of dynamic motions, but they are typically trained offline and then frozen, so teaching such a controller a new skill tends to erode the skills it already mastered. We study continual learning for humanoid whole-body motion, where a single controller must acquire skills from a sequential task stream without revisiting past data. We introduce Similarity-guided LoRA-PNN, a progressive neural network (PNN) policy that prevents catastrophic forgetting by construction while reusing knowledge across skills through lightweight low-rank adaptation. A two-level motion-similarity measure, built from dynamic time warping aggregated by optimal transport, decides which prior skill to build on and how much new capacity to allocate, yielding strong forward transfer and large efficiency gains. Across six sequentially learned skill categories, our similarity-guided LoRA policy attains the best forward transfer (0.125 vs. 0.079) and the highest average accuracy among all methods, while saving up to 94.5% of trainable parameters and 40.8% of training time. The resulting controller reaches 96.13% sim-to-sim transfer and is deployed on a physical Unitree G1. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [149] [Bi-manual Stabilization of the Cervical Spine for Safe Physical Human-Robot Interaction in Rolling Maneuvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04220)
*Elizabeth Peiros,Moira Bohley,Lucas Yager,Derek Chen,Rebecca Fellbaum,Chris D'Ambrosia,Ananya Rao,Xiao Liang,Michael C. Yip*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种用于双臂机器人在滚动操作中稳定颈椎的算法与控制方法；实验表明其旋转对准能力达到人类水平，带预测时轴向对准与人类新手相差仅0.21°。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颈椎脆弱且活动度高，在战场、灾害或运动事故中搬运、翻滚或移动伤者时，极易因操作不当造成灾难性损伤，因此需要安全可靠的颈椎稳定技术。

Method: 提出面向双臂机器人的算法与适配方法，研究在安全约束内最大化抓握力的技术，并设计控制策略以实现正确的颈部旋转；实验中还结合预测来提升对准性能。

Result: 双臂机器人能够维持目标旋转对准，达到人类级表现；带预测的机器人轴向对准与人类新手相差在0.21°以内。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该算法与控制策略可使双臂机器人在滚动操作中稳定颈椎，并在旋转对准上达到与人类新手相当的水平，有望用于安全的患者搬运。

Abstract: The neck, specifically the cervical spine, is a highly fragile and mobile part of the body with a high risk of catastrophic injury. Whether someone is injured on a battlefield, in a natural disaster, or in an athletic event, great care is always taken with the cervical spine when transporting, rolling, or moving the patient. In this work, we present an algorithm and adaptations for bi-manual robots to stabilize the cervical spine during rolling maneuvers. We investigate techniques to maximize grip strength within safety bounds and present a control approach for proper neck rotation. We found that with our approach, a bi-manual robot is capable of achieving human-level performance in maintaining target rotational alignments. The axial alignment for the robot with prediction was within .21 degrees of human novices.

</details>


### [150] [Autoware in Construction: Gap Analysis and LiDAR Perception Toward Off-Road Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04187)
*Yu Otsuki,Teja Emmey,Sena Matsushita,Akiro Harada,Takeshi Miura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文总结将Autoware自动驾驶系统扩展至建筑工地大型自卸卡车过程中的经验，识别公共道路与越野施工场景在感知、建图、定位、规划与控制等模块的差距，重点介绍LiDAR感知流水线开发与实地测试发现，并提出面向建筑车辆端到端自动驾驶的路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Autoware原主要面向公共道路应用，但正扩展至建筑、农业等越野环境；建筑工地具有非结构化地形、扬尘、持续变化的场地条件和施工特有物体，挑战传统自动驾驶系统的基本假设，因此需要研究其适配问题。

Method: 基于Autoware栈，面向建筑工地大型自卸卡车，逐模块分析公共道路与越野施工应用在传感、建图、定位、感知、规划和控制方面的差距，讨论潜在解决方案，并开展基于LiDAR的感知流水线开发与实地测试。

Result: 识别出公共道路与施工场景之间在Autoware各模块上的多项差距；提出潜在解决思路；获得LiDAR感知流水线开发及实地测试的相关发现。

Conclusion: 提出面向建筑车辆端到端自动驾驶的路线图，为Autoware在越野施工场景中的进一步扩展提供方向。

Abstract: Autoware is an open-source autonomous driving software platform widely adopted by researchers and industry developers. Originally developed primarily for public-road applications, including passenger vehicles, taxis, and buses, Autoware is increasingly being extended to off-road environments such as construction and agricultural sites. Construction sites, however, differ fundamentally from public roads and challenge many assumptions underlying conventional autonomous driving systems. They are characterized by unstructured terrain, airborne dust, continuously evolving site conditions, and construction-specific objects. This paper presents lessons learned from ongoing efforts to extend an Autoware-based autonomous driving system to large dump trucks operating at construction sites. We identify gaps between public-road and off-road construction applications across the sensing, mapping, localization, perception, planning, and control modules of the Autoware stack. We then discuss potential solutions for addressing these gaps, with particular emphasis on ongoing LiDAR-based perception pipeline development and findings from field testing. Finally, we propose a roadmap toward end-to-end autonomous driving for construction vehicles.

</details>


### [151] [Terrain-Dependent Intra-Cycle Leg Timing for Effective Locomotion on Granular Slopes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04144)
*Long Zhao,Xingjue Liao,Feifei Qian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究通过六足机器人在可倾斜颗粒床上的实验与建模，揭示颗粒斜坡运动性能下降主要源于锚定延迟和后滑增加，而非过度下陷，并构建失效相图用于风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沙丘等颗粒斜坡因剪切强度降低和重力诱导的各向异性屈服，对腿式机器人运动构成根本挑战，需要理解坡度相关颗粒阻力及失效机制。

Method: 使用六足机器人在可倾斜颗粒床上系统测量运动速度，以及随坡度变化的法向和剪切颗粒阻力；基于测量建立简化机器人-地形交互模型，预测锚定时机、步长和机器人速度，并扩展到一般地形以构建失效相图。

Result: 法向贯入阻力随倾角几乎不变，而剪切阻力随坡度增大显著下降；模型表明坡度引起的性能损失主要受锚定延迟和后滑增加控制，而非过度下陷；失效相图识别出下陷型和滑移型失效区域，可对颗粒斜坡运动进行定量风险评估。

Conclusion: 该物理信息框架提供了对地形相关失效机制的预测性理解，并为机器人在可变形斜坡上更安全、更鲁棒地运行提供指导。

Abstract: Locomotion on granular slopes such as sand dunes remains a fundamental challenge for legged robots due to reduced shear strength and gravity-induced anisotropic yielding of granular media. Using a hexapedal robot on a tiltable granular bed, we systematically measure locomotion speed together with slope-dependent normal and shear granular resistive forces. While normal penetration resistance remains nearly unchanged with inclination, shear resistance decreases substantially as slope angle increases. Guided by these measurements, we develop a simple robot-terrain interaction model that predicts anchoring timing, step length, and resulting robot speed, as functions of terrain strength and slope angle. The model reveals that slope-induced performance loss is primarily governed by delayed anchoring and increased backward slip rather than excessive sinkage. By extending the model to generalized terrain conditions, we construct failure phase diagrams that identify sinkage- and slippage-induced failure regimes, enabling quantitative risk estimation for locomotion on granular slopes. This physics-informed framework provides predictive insight into terrain-dependent failure mechanisms and offers guidance for safer and more robust robot operation on deformable inclines.

</details>


### [152] [Reward-DAgger: Robot-Gated Interactive Imitation Learning with General-Purpose Progress-Based Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04054)
*Ryan Li,Yigit Korkmaz,Erdem Bıyık*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Reward-DAgger 是一种机器人门控式交互模仿学习框架，利用通用奖励模型的密集进度信号判断何时需要人工干预，从而在跨任务、环境和策略架构下实现失败检测与恢复教学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用机器人策略在未见环境中性能下降，因此需要检测失败并教授恢复行为。现有运行时监控方法通常依赖任务和策略特定的训练或超参数调优，难以跨任务部署，并在迭代更新策略时引入额外开销。

Method: 提出 Reward-DAgger：使用通用奖励模型提供的密集进度信号作为门控依据，决定人工干预时机。该方法对底层策略架构不可知，无需访问策略内部，且门控机制无需针对新任务重新调参。

Result: 实验表明，Reward-DAgger 在失败检测准确率与延迟的权衡上优于现有运行时监控基线。在八个仿真和真实任务中，它持续提升下游策略在交互学习过程中的自主成功率，并取得较高的人工投入回报，在多数设置下优于基线。相同门控配置无需任务特定超参数调优即可跨任务、环境和策略架构迁移。

Conclusion: Reward-DAgger 提供了一种通用、免策略内部访问且跨任务可迁移的运行时失败检测与人工干预门控方案，能有效提升交互模仿学习中的策略自主成功率与人工教学效率。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot learning have enabled generalist control policies capable of completing a wide range of tasks. However, their performance degrades when deployed in unseen environments, making it critical to detect failures and teach recovery behaviors. Existing runtime monitoring methods often require task- and policy-specific training or hyperparameter tuning, limiting cross-task deployment and introducing additional overhead during iterative policy updates. We present Reward-DAgger, a robot-gated interactive imitation learning framework that uses dense progress signals from a general-purpose reward model to determine when human intervention is needed. Our approach is agnostic to the underlying policy architecture, requires no access to policy internals, and can be applied across tasks without retuning the gating mechanism. Our results show that Reward-DAgger achieves a better failure-detection accuracy-latency tradeoff than existing runtime monitoring baselines. Across eight simulated and real-world tasks, Reward-DAgger consistently improves the downstream policy's autonomous success rate throughout interactive learning and achieves strong return on human effort, outperforming the baselines in most settings. Importantly, the same gating configuration is used across tasks without task-specific hyperparameter tuning, demonstrating transfer across tasks, environments, and policy architectures. Code and videos are available at this https URL .

</details>


### [153] [A Biomimetic Gaze Control to Restore Head-Neck Movements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04022)
*Colin Rubow,Haohan Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于眼动与前庭眼反射（VOR）的颈部外骨骼控制器及个性化工具，并在人体中验证，较旧模型运动更自然且运动意图检测准确率平均提升24%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动力颈部外骨骼多用手持设备控制，对颈部肌无力人群不直观或不可行；眼动可与头部协调完成日常任务，有望无需手控，但先前眼动控制模型缺乏生物行为基础与个性化手段，导致头颈运动不自然。

Method: 设计新型眼动追踪控制器，利用前庭眼反射模拟自然眼-头协调；开发个性化工具，通过滑块调节控制器参数；开展系统技术设计与人体用户验证。

Result: 与先前眼动追踪模型相比，新控制器提供更自然的速度曲线和个性化自然头部运动，并将准确运动意图检测平均提高24%。

Conclusion: 该系统为研究眼动控制器中的个性化与用户偏好、最终恢复患者头颈运动能力提供了有力平台。

Abstract: Powered neck exoskeletons are developed to restore head-neck motions for those with neck muscle weakness. Current methods using hand-held input devices to control these robotic systems are unintuitive or non-viable for these populations. Alternatively, the eyes coordinate with the head to accomplish many daily tasks. Therefore, movements of the eyes can be a useful signal to control the neck exoskeletons without needing to use the hands. However, previous control models lack biobehavioral basis as well as means to personalize the control behavior, causing unnatural head-neck movements. This paper presents a novel eye-tracking controller design leveraging the vestibulo-ocular reflex to emulate natural eyehead coordination. Personalization tools were also designed, consisting of sliders to allow users to adjust the parameters of this controller. Here, we present the technical design and validation of this system in human users. Compared to the previous eye-tracking model, the new controller provides natural head motions personalized to the user through more natural velocity profiles and a 24 percent average increase of accurate motion intent detection. This system provides a powerful platform for studying eye-tracking controllers in the context of personalization and preference, and ultimately restoring the lost head-neck mobility in patient populations.

</details>


### [154] [Return-to-Home Feasible Micro-Aerial Vehicle Exploration for 3D Gaussian Splatting Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04013)
*Prajit Krisshnakumar,Fan Yang,Koichiro Niinuma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向微型无人机室内主动三维重建的拓扑优先框架：以3DGS作为高质量重建目标，用轻量TSDF/占据栅格构建保守碰撞检测与稀疏Voronoi骨架路线图，并在飞行时间预算和返航可行性约束下选择视点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型无人机可用于快速室内三维重建与时效性态势感知，但受严格飞行时间预算和安全约束限制，必须显式保证带保守裕度的返航（RTH）可行性，因此需要兼顾重建质量、导航安全与计算效率。

Method: 框架将高保真渲染与导航解耦：稠密3DGS地图作为重建目标，轻量TSDF/占据栅格作为碰撞检测与在线稀疏3D Voronoi骨架路线图构建的支架。由于从TSDF几何直接提取路线图在噪声和不完整在线融合下不稳定，使用雕刻自由空间一致性与稠密可见性检查来验证节点和边，抑制墙后幻影结构并稳定规划。视点选择在路线图上采用飞行时间预算感知目标，并引入轻量滚动时域前瞻以改善非短视探索。

Result: 在ReplicaCAD、Gibson和HM3D等逼真室内仿真基准上评估，报告重建覆盖率/误差、RTH成功率和计算开销。结果表明，在相同飞行时间预算下，性能优于或可媲美近期基于GS的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该拓扑优先主动重建框架能够在严格飞行时间与安全约束下实现稳健的室内三维重建和返航可行性保障，并通过解耦渲染与导航、路线图验证及预算感知视点选择取得有竞争力的效果。

Abstract: Micro aerial vehicles (MAVs) enable rapid indoor 3D reconstruction for inspection and time-critical situational awareness, but must operate under strict flight-time budgets and safety constraints that require explicit return-to-home (RTH) feasibility with a conservative margin. We present a topology-first active reconstruction framework that decouples high-fidelity rendering from navigation. A dense 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) map is optimized as the reconstruction target, while a lightweight Truncated Signed Distance Field (TSDF)/occupancy scaffold supports conservative collision checking and online construction of a sparse 3D Voronoi skeleton roadmap. Since directly extracting roadmaps from TSDF geometry can be unstable under noisy and incomplete online fusion, we validate nodes and edges using carved free-space consistency and dense visibility checks, which suppress behind-wall phantom structure and stabilize planning. Viewpoints are selected on the roadmap using a flight-time-budget-aware objective and a lightweight receding-horizon lookahead to improve non-myopic exploration behavior. We evaluate on photorealistic indoor simulation benchmarks (ReplicaCAD, Gibson, and HM3D), reporting reconstruction coverage/error, RTH success, and computational cost. Results demonstrate improved or competitive performance relative to recent GS-based baselines under identical flight-time budgets.

</details>


### [155] [SUAVE: Unified Video-Action Models via Masked Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04009)
*Rhythm Syed,Jean Mercat,Sedrick Keh,Kushal Arora,Paarth Shah,Aykut Onol,Mengchao Zhang,Tony Dear*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SUAVE 提出一种基于掩码扩散 Transformer 的单词汇表统一动作-视频模型，将语言、视频与动作离散化为共享序列中的 token，通过选择掩码实现世界模型、策略或视频-动作模型，并在仿真与真实机器人上验证其长时程预测、闭环控制与跨域鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 具备语义 grounding 但只预测动作、不预测未来观测；基于视频扩散的 WAM 能想象但将语言作为冻结条件且处于连续隐空间；统一模型则要么逐 token 自回归解码，要么保持视频连续并外挂动作头。

Method: 提出 SUAVE：Single vocabulary Unified Action-Video modEl。使用掩码扩散 Transformer，在语言条件下生成视频和动作，三种模态均表示为共享序列中的离散 token。推理时通过选择不同掩码位置，同一网络可切换为世界模型、机器人策略或视频-动作模型。无动作联合训练时，未标注视频的动作位置填入 mask token 并从损失中排除。

Result: 仿真与真实实验表明：单个 SUAVE 可预测长时程视频并作为策略，在静态与动态操作任务上与专用世界模型和专门动作策略相当；在真实机器人 RTX 5090 上，1,030 ms 内生成子目标图像和覆盖 1 秒运动的动作 chunk，实现每秒 2.5 次动作的闭环控制。机器人视频预训练与人类视频联合训练显著提升策略性能和零样本分布偏移鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，掩码扩散是统一视频-动作建模的一种实用且通用的基础范式。

Abstract: Vision-language-action models (VLAs) inherit strong semantic grounding from pretrained vision-language backbones but are typically optimized for predicting actions rather than future observations. They can see and act, but they do not imagine the future before acting. World action models (WAMs) built on video diffusion backbones can imagine but treat language as frozen conditioning on a continuous latent space. Unified models bring these modalities into one architecture, but they either decode autoregressively, one token at a time, or keep video continuous with an auxiliary action head. In this work, we present SUAVE, a Single vocabulary Unified Action-Video modEl in which a masked diffusion transformer generates video and actions conditioned on language, with all three modalities represented as discrete tokens in a shared sequence. Choosing which tokens to mask at inference turns the same network into a world model, a robot policy, or a video-action model. For action-free co-training, the action positions of unlabeled video are filled with mask tokens and excluded from the loss. Simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate two findings. First, a single SUAVE model predicts long-horizon video and acts as a policy, competitive with dedicated world models and specialized action policies on static and dynamic manipulation tasks. On a real robot, our model generates subgoal images and an action chunk spanning one second of motion in 1,030 ms on an RTX 5090 GPU, sustaining closed-loop control at 2.5 actions per second. Second, pretraining on robot video and co-training on human video substantially improves policy performance and zero-shot robustness to distribution shift. Together, these results show that masked diffusion is a practical and versatile foundation for unified video-action modeling.

</details>


### [156] [REDIRECT: A 1% Fix for Bad Robot Habits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03997)
*Yu Zhang,Jiazhuo Li,Yancong Wei,Kangkang Dong,Xiaojun Zhu,Houde Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REDIRECT 仅用 episode 级好坏标签，在极低更新预算下定位并修复机器人示范数据中的局部不良行为，从而显著恢复任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人会从混合质量遥操作数据中的少数缺陷片段习得坏习惯；正常与问题示范大部分行为共享，仅局部动作延续不同。全量重训代价高，而在小更新预算下仅用干净数据微调恢复有限。作者探究能否仅用全量训练样本-反向传播预算的 1% 来修复该局部差异。

Method: REDIRECT 不使用帧级标注或额外交互，仅利用 episode 级的保留/问题标签，定位问题分支，为原始观测分配一致的保留动作延续，并锚定共享行为，从而将更新预算集中在行为差异上。

Result: 在三个随机化 ManiSkill 任务和三个随机种子上，REDIRECT 将平均成功率从 68.2% 提升至 90.3%，恢复干净重训差距的 88.8%，而同计算量微调仅恢复 22.1%；在 PiPER 机械臂的杯子插入和毛巾折叠任务上，恢复干净仅训练差距的 86.7%-92.9%。

Conclusion: 局部机器人坏习惯可通过将更新预算用于修复行为差异而非重新学习共享行为来有效修复。

Abstract: Robots can acquire bad habits from a few defective moments in otherwise useful teleoperation. In mixed-quality robot data, normal and problematic demonstrations share most task behavior and differ only at a local action continuation. Full retraining is costly, while fine-tuning on clean data alone offers limited recovery under a small update budget. We ask whether the local difference can instead be fixed with only 1% of the full-training sample-backward budget. Using only episode-level retained/problematic labels, REDIRECT localizes the branch, assigns a coherent retained continuation to the original observations around it, and anchors shared behavior, without frame-level annotations or additional interaction. Across three randomized ManiSkill tasks and three seeds, REDIRECT raises the mean success rate from 68.2% to 90.3%, recovering 88.8% of the clean-retraining gap versus 22.1% for matched-compute fine-tuning. On a PiPER arm, it recovers 86.7-92.9% of the clean-only gap across cup insertion and towel folding. Local robot habits can therefore be repaired by spending the update budget on the behavioral difference rather than relearning shared behavior.

</details>


### [157] [Sparse Calibration-Based Personalization of Kernel-Based Gait Phase and Speed Estimation Using Wearable IMUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03931)
*Myeongju Cha,Pilwon Hur*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于稀疏校准的个性化核方法，用于从可穿戴IMU实现步态相位与行走速度的联合估计。方法构建了28速度参考库，并用3个校准速度生成用户特异性基线，再结合基线中心化运动学偏差的主成分模型个性化核函数。离线验证显示个性化核降低相位误差；单参与者在线试点中误差数值更低且可在嵌入式硬件实时运行，但速度效应不显著，相位差异经多重比较校正后也不显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人群平均核函数难以适应个体步态差异，而可穿戴步态相位与速度联合估计需要兼顾个性化精度、稀疏校准和嵌入式实时性。

Method: 基于20名受试者运动数据集，从双侧大腿和小腿轨迹构建28速度参考库；不直接使用群体平均核，而是用3个校准速度估计用户特异性基线，并建立基线中心化运动学偏差的主成分（PC）模型，从而生成个性化核；随后进行离线验证和单参与者在线试点评估。

Result: 离线验证中，个性化核相对群体核降低了相位误差。在线单参与者试点中，个性化核产生数值上更低的平均相位误差和速度误差，并能在嵌入式硬件上实时运行。但速度效应不显著，且相位成对差异经多重比较校正后不再显著。

Conclusion: 该试点评估证明了可穿戴实现的可行性，同时表明存在参考数据集与在线数据集之间的不匹配问题，并提示需要更大规模队列验证。

Abstract: This paper presents sparse calibration-based personalization for kernel-based gait phase and walking speed co-estimation from wearable inertial measurement units (IMUs). A 28-speed reference library was constructed from bilateral thigh and shank trajectories in a 20-subject locomotion dataset. Rather than directly applying population-average kernels, the method combines a user-specific baseline estimated from three calibration speeds with a principal-component (PC) model of baseline-centered kinematic deviations. Offline validation showed that personalized kernels reduced phase error relative to the population kernel. In a single-participant online pilot evaluation, the personalized kernels produced numerically lower mean phase and speed errors and ran in real time on embedded hardware. However, the speed effect was not significant, and pairwise phase differences did not remain significant after multiple-comparison correction. The pilot evaluation establishes wearable implementation feasibility while indicating reference-to-online dataset mismatch and the need for larger-cohort validation.

</details>


### [158] [MOSAIC-SV: Real-Time Adaptive Identification of Vessel Dynamics for the Control and Deployment of Aquatic Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03898)
*Wensen Liu,Jerry Peng,Shravani Vedagiri,Aaron M. Johnson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 MOSAIC-SV：一种可部署的实时自适应水动力学辨识与控制系统，无需专门辨识试验，仅从规格表工程先验出发，在闭环任务每个控制步重估控制充分模型并让控制器基于当前估计规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的水面/水下机器人控制依赖水动力模型，但此类模型辨识成本高，且强依赖于船体、载荷和测量工况，难以跨条件复用；因此需要免专用试验、可在线适应并能直接支撑闭环控制的方案。

Method: 以规格表工程先验为起点；用物理可行的无迹卡尔曼滤波器在闭环每个控制步重估水动力、扰动和执行器参数；通过命令相关的 consider 投影，在当前命令无法区分执行器效能与外力时抑制修正；由模型预测路径积分控制器基于当前估计进行规划，形成辨识-控制闭环。

Result: 仿真 CyberShip II 中，在静态失配和瞬态变化下恢复标定模型的航行性能；当惯量或阻尼先验错一个数量级时，航行时间与未缩放先验自身相比仍保持在20%以内。BlueBoat 双推进器双体船实航中，比冻结工程先验至少快25%，自运动预测误差至少低56%，且包含未建模载荷场景；同一系统概念还可部署于6.3吨双外挂机单体船。

Conclusion: MOSAIC-SV 表明无需专门辨识试验，仅凭工程先验即可在闭环中实时辨识并控制水动力模型，提升航速与预测精度，并对不同船体、载荷和尺度具备可部署性与泛化潜力。

Abstract: Model-based control of an aquatic robotic platform depends on a hydrodynamic model that is costly to identify and specific to the hull, payload, and conditions it was measured in. Here, we present MOSAIC-SV, a deployable real-time adaptive dynamics identification and control system that identifies a control-sufficient dynamics model from a spec-sheet engineering prior, without dedicated identification trials, and re-estimates it at every control step of a closed-loop mission while the controller plans on it. A physically admissible unscented Kalman filter re-estimates hydrodynamic, disturbance, and actuator parameters at every control step of the closed-loop mission; while a command-dependent consider projection withholds corrections the current command cannot attribute between actuator effectiveness and external force; and a model predictive path integral controller plans on the current estimate. In simulation on a CyberShip II plant, MOSAIC-SV recovers the transit performance of the calibrated model under static mismatch and transient changes, and stays within 20% of its own transit time at the unscaled prior when its inertia or damping prior is wrong by an order of magnitude. In on-water field trials on the Blue Robotics BlueBoat, a twin-thruster catamaran, MOSAIC-SV transits at least 25% faster and predicts its own motion with at least 56% less error than its frozen engineering prior, including under an unmodeled payload. The same MOSAIC-SV system concept was also feasibly deployed on a 6.3-tonne dual outboard monohull.

</details>


### [159] [GOTT: Object-centric Dexterous Manipulation with a Reusable Cross-Embodiment Primitive](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03861)
*Yulin Liu,Lai Wei,Yen-Jen Wang,Akash Sharma,Pieter Abbeel,Henrik I. Christensen,Haozhi Qi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出GOTT，一个以跨本体接触获取原语为核心的“接近-获取-移动”框架，可将操作意图转化为多指机器人行为并提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型与大规模人类数据提供丰富操作意图，但难以转化为多指机器人行为；灵巧手缺少可复用、跨任务与跨本体可靠建立接触的低层原语。

Method: GOTT接收机器人无关的物体轨迹与reach specification，先将手靠近任务相关接触区域；随后共享闭环接触获取原语从近似初始化建立稳定接触，姿态条件控制器跟踪期望物体运动。reach specification可来自未来感知规划、外部模型或人类演示，而原语与跟踪后端保持不变。

Result: 仿真与真实实验表明，GOTT能在多样物体、臂手平台以及已见/未见手部形态上鲁棒建立接触，并相较开环抓取执行一致提升端到端任务成功率。

Conclusion: GOTT提供了一种可复用的跨本体接触获取原语和统一框架，有效桥接高层操作意图与灵巧手底层控制，提升多指机器人操作的泛化性与可靠性。

Abstract: Foundation models and large-scale human data provide rich sources of manipulation intent, but translating this intent into multi-fingered robot behavior remains difficult. Dexterous hands still lack a reusable low-level primitive that reliably establishes contact across tasks and embodiments. We propose GOTT, a reach-acquire-move framework built around a single cross-embodiment contact-acquisition primitive. Given a robot-agnostic object trajectory and a reach specification, GOTT first brings the hand near a task-relevant contact region. The shared closed-loop primitive then establishes stable contact from this approximate initialization, and a pose-conditioned controller tracks the desired object motion. Reach specifications may come from future-aware planning, external models, or human demonstrations, while the primitive and tracking backend remain unchanged. Simulation and real-world experiments show that GOTT is able to establish robust contact across diverse objects, arm-hand platforms, and seen and unseen hand morphologies. It also consistently improves end-to-end task success over open-loop grasp execution.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [H-JEPA: End-to-End Learning of Hierarchical World Models for Visual Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06805)
*Wancong Zhang,Basile Terver,Michael Rabbat,Yann LeCun,Randall Balestriero*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: H-JEPA提出分层动作条件JEPA世界模型，在不同抽象层级与预测时域上分别学习潜空间并进行自顶向下规划，显著提升长时程规划成功率并降低规划计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有任务无关JEPA世界模型仅在单一时间尺度或共享潜空间中以多时域进行预测与规划，难以处理跨时间尺度和抽象层级的长时程规划。

Method: H-JEPA端到端训练动作条件JEPA层级；每层在自身学习到的潜空间中预测更远的未来；规划自顶向下：顶层优化目标进展，其预测作为下层的子目标；当数据因素按分离时间尺度演化时，高层丢弃快速不可预测细节，保留慢变任务相关状态；并可用逆动力学监督扩展到真实机器人视频。

Result: 在四个仿真导航与操作环境中，分层规划优于扁平JEPA；在Visual AntMaze上，三层层级将成功率从18%提升至73%，且规划计算更少；消融表明增益来自时间分解和高层目标表示；在DROID真实机器人视频上，逆动力学监督下层级提升离线规划保真度并降低规划计算。

Conclusion: 分层潜世界模型可有效分解时间尺度与抽象层级，为长时程规划和真实机器人视频离线规划提供更高效、更准确的方案。

Abstract: Long-horizon planning with latent world models requires reasoning across timescales and levels of abstraction. Existing task-agnostic JEPA world models predict and plan at a single timescale or with multiple horizons in one shared latent space. We introduce H-JEPA, an end-to-end recipe for training a hierarchy of action-conditioned JEPAs in which each level predicts farther ahead in its own learned latent space. Planning proceeds top-down: the top level optimizes progress toward the goal, and each level's predictions become subgoals for the planner below it. When factors in the data evolve at separated timescales, higher levels discard fast, unpredictable detail and retain slower task-relevant state. Across four simulated navigation and manipulation environments, hierarchical planning improves over a flat JEPA; on Visual AntMaze, a three-level hierarchy raises success from 18% to 73% using less planner compute. Ablations attribute these gains to both temporal decomposition and higher-level goal representations. With inverse-dynamics supervision, the approach extends to diverse real-robot videos from DROID, where hierarchy improves offline planning fidelity at lower planner compute.

</details>


### [161] [EpicWorldModel: Exploration-driven Planning with Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05996)
*Bowen Feng,Julian Ost,May Mei,Anirudha Majumdar,Felix Heide*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 EpicWorldModel，一种面向部分可观测与固有不确定性环境的随机 JEPA 潜在世界模型；它用流匹配预测多模态未来，并以流预测方差引导 CEM 规划，在潜在规划任务中取得最佳或相当性能，较 LeWorldModel 成功率最高提升 22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 JEPA 的潜在世界模型本质上是确定性的，在完全可观测场景中有效，但当历史观测与动作对应多种合理未来（如遮挡导致）时不再适用。因此，需要一种能建模部分可观测下固有不确定性、并利用该不确定性进行规划的随机 JEPA 框架。

Method: 联合训练 EpicWorldModel 预测器与其潜在表示空间；当条件历史中缺少目标相关场景内容时，使用流匹配目标直接预测多个潜在未来状态。将流预测方差作为探索信号，其动机来自该方差与预测熵上界的关系；并把该不确定性信号集成到基于 Cross-Entropy Method（CEM）的规划中，以平衡目标达成与对遮挡目标可能区域的探索。

Result: 在系列潜在规划实验中，EpicWorldModel 取得最佳或相当的性能，相比 LeWorldModel 成功率最高有 22% 的经验提升。

Conclusion: EpicWorldModel 能有效处理部分可观测下的多模态未来预测，并将不确定性转化为规划探索指导，从而提升潜在规划的成功率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Latent world models based on Joint-Embedding Predictive Architecture (JEPA) are deterministic by design. While successful in fully observable scenarios, this paradigm breaks down when past observations and actions lead to multiple plausible future possibilities, e.g., due to occlusion. We introduce EpicWorldModel, a framework to train stochastic JEPAs for environments and tasks with inherent uncertainty under partially observability. We jointly train the EpicWorldModel predictor with its latent representation space to directly predict multiple potential future states using a flow-matching objective, when the goal-relevant scene content is absent from the conditioning history. We show that flow predictive variance, motivated by its relation to an upper bound on predictive entropy, serves as a useful exploration guidance for planning. By incorporating this uncertainty signal into Cross-Entropy Method (CEM)-based planning, our approach balances goal-reaching with exploration of uncertain regions where occluded goals are most likely to be located. We demonstrate the effectiveness of EpicWorldModel through a series of latent planning experiments with the best or on-par performance across tasks, showing up to 22% empirical improvement in success rate over LeWorldModel.

</details>


### [162] [Reachability-Aware Diffusion Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05969)
*Hikmet Simsir,Kutay Demiray,Ozgur S. Oguz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RADPO：一种无模型的安全感知在线扩散策略优化方法，结合折扣首次命中可达性价值与累计成本预算反馈，在无需学习动力学模型、无需通过评论家求动作梯度、无需对反向扩散采样器求导的情况下改善连续控制安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略能为连续控制强化学习提供表达力强的动作分布，但安全感知的在线扩散策略优化仍研究不足，尤其是如何在不依赖显式动力学模型的情况下利用预测可达性信息。

Method: RADPO学习折扣首次命中可达性价值，用于刻画成本事件的折扣风险，并对更早发生的成本事件赋予更大权重，以此塑造奖励；另设类对偶乘子，根据实际回合成本相对给定预算调整塑形强度；扩散演员通过对由奖励评论家评分的候选动作进行加权去噪回归来改进。

Result: 理论上，论文建立可达性价值的性质，并证明累计可达性惩罚可作为未来折扣累计成本的保守替代。实验上，在10个连续控制安全任务中，RADPO取得有竞争力的奖励-成本权衡，并在多个任务上较基线显著减少约束违规。

Conclusion: 将可达性信息与累计预算反馈相结合，是安全感知扩散策略的一条可行路径，且理论与实证结果均支持其有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion policies provide expressive action distributions for continuous-control reinforcement learning. However, safety-aware online diffusion policy optimization remains underexplored, particularly methods that use predictive reachability information without an explicit dynamics model. We propose Reachability-Aware Diffusion Policy Optimization (RADPO), a model-free method that combines predictive first-hit safety estimation with cumulative-cost budget feedback. RADPO learns a discounted first-hit reachability value that captures the discounted risk of a cost event, assigns larger weight to events that occur sooner, and uses this signal to shape the reward. A separate dual-like multiplier adjusts the shaping strength according to realized episodic costs relative to a prescribed budget. The diffusion actor improves through weighted denoising regression on candidate actions scored by the reward critic. Our approach requires neither a learned dynamics model, action gradients through the critics, nor differentiation through the reverse diffusion sampler. We establish theoretical properties of the reachability value and show that accumulated reachability penalty provides a conservative surrogate for future discounted cumulative cost. Across ten continuous-control safety tasks, RADPO achieves competitive reward-cost trade-offs, with substantial reductions in constraint violations on several tasks relative to the compared baselines. Our theoretical and empirical analysis supports that combining reachability with cumulative budget feedback is a viable approach to safety-aware diffusion policies.

</details>


### [163] [OGAM: Connecting Systematic Testing to Runtime Assurance through Object-Grounded Attention Monitoring for VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05878)
*Haki Darwish,Xiangyu Yin,Changwen Li,Rongjie Yan,Francisco Gomes de Oliveira Neto,Chih-Hong Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出对象接地注意力监控（OGAM），将有限基准测试中发现的注意力分歧转化为运行时保障信号，在VLA策略失败完全展开前进行在线早停干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略仅暴露于少量规范指令，而穷尽式部署测试不可行；需要连接系统测试与运行时保障，以在有限测试套件之外检测并阻止失败。

Method: 通过动作模板与对象的两两组合生成场景接地指令，并单独测试保义复述；在每个动作查询中，将梯度加权视觉注意力经对象掩码投影，按指令角色分组，跨任务与策略比较。用动态时间规整将注意力轨迹与成功参考对齐，以克服速度差异；在成功回合上做共形校准，设定持续偏差的早停阈值，名义误停目标为α=0.05。

Result: 87个基准外案例中，OpenVLA、OpenVLA-OFT、UniVLA与π0.5均表现问题行为：24条可行指令无一完成，不可行或危险请求也触发行为替换。OGAM在四种策略上停止87-100%的失败回合，中位时间为5-12秒，处于20秒预算内，观测误停率为3-5%，且无需失败标签训练。

Conclusion: 有限测试可识别支持在线干预的注意力模式，使系统能在失败完全展开前阻止失败，从而将有限测试与运行时保障有效衔接。

Abstract: Benchmarks expose vision-language-action (VLA) policies to few canonical instructions, while exhaustive deployment testing is impossible. We introduce Object-Grounded Attention Monitoring (OGAM), connecting systematic testing to runtime assurance: testing reveals attention divergence between successful and failed executions, and OGAM uses this signal to stop failures beyond the finite suite. We generate scene-grounded instructions through pairwise combinations of action templates and objects, and separately test meaning-preserving paraphrases. All 87 out-of-benchmark cases reveal problematic behavior across OpenVLA, OpenVLA-OFT, UniVLA, and $\pi_{0.5}$: none completes any of the 24 feasible instructions, while infeasible or hazardous requests also trigger behavior substitution. At each action query, we project gradient-weighted visual attention through object masks and group it by instruction role for comparison across tasks and policies. Dynamic time warping aligns this course with a successful reference despite speed differences; conformal calibration on successful episodes sets the early-stopping threshold for sustained deviations, with a nominal false-stop target of $\alpha=0.05$. Across four policies, OGAM stops 87-100% of failed episodes at median times of 5-12s within a 20s budget, with observed false-stop rates of 3-5%, without failure-labeled training. Finite testing thus identifies attention patterns that support online intervention before failure fully unfolds.

</details>


### [164] [Beyond In-Distribution Preservation: Recovering Generalization in Quantized VLAs via Vulnerability-Oriented Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05745)
*Shen Ruan,Wenchang Gao,Jin Wang,Siao Liu,Zhoxizhuoma,Dongchun Ren,Xin Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了后训练量化VLA策略在环境扰动下的鲁棒性与泛化性，发现量化策略虽保持分布内性能却对细微环境变化脆弱，并提出PIVOT-Q——一种脆弱状态感知的在策略蒸馏框架，仅用7.4%的蒸馏预算即可在所有设置下超越全状态蒸馏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练量化虽在标准评估下保持VLA性能，但其是否保留全精度模型的鲁棒性与泛化性尚不明确；且动作差异集中于少量rollout状态，教师指导在高差异状态提升泛化、在低差异状态反而有害，因此需要选择性干预而非全局蒸馏。

Method: 提出PIVOT-Q（Policy-Induced Vulnerability-Oriented Tuning），一种脆弱性感知的在策略蒸馏（OPD）框架。它利用冻结的全精度策略作为教师，在量化学生rollout中通过短时域折扣累积差异识别脆弱状态，施加阶段平衡的稀疏监督，并引入Behavioral Anchor防止不必要的改变。

Result: 在七种LIBERO-Plus环境变化下，跨多个VLA骨干与量化方法实现一致的性能恢复；在所有设置下持续优于全状态蒸馏，且仅使用其状态级蒸馏预算的7.4%。

Conclusion: 有效的后量化恢复需对脆弱状态进行选择性干预而非全局蒸馏；PIVOT-Q证明了脆弱性感知稀疏蒸馏的有效性与高效性。

Abstract: Post-training quantization has been shown to preserve VLA performance under standard evaluation conditions, but whether it preserves the full-precision model's robustness and generalization remains underexplored. In this study, we systematically study the robustness and generalization of post-quantized VLA policies under environmental disturbances. Empirical results show that quantized policies can become fragile to subtle environmental variations despite retaining comparable in-distribution performance. We further observe that action discrepancies are concentrated in a small subset of rollout states, while teacher guidance has opposite effects depending on discrepancy: it improves generalization at high-discrepancy states but can degrade it at low-discrepancy states. These findings reveal that effective post-quantization recovery requires selectively intervening on vulnerable states rather than globally distilling the student. We therefore propose Policy-Induced Vulnerability-Oriented Tuning (PIVOT-Q), a vulnerability-aware On-Policy Distillation (OPD) framework that selectively corrects vulnerable states encountered during quantized-student rollouts using the frozen full-precision policy as a teacher. PIVOT-Q identifies vulnerable states using discounted accumulated discrepancies over a short horizon, applies phase-balanced sparse supervision, and uses a Behavioral Anchor to prevent unnecessary changes. Experiments under seven LIBERO-Plus environmental variations demonstrate consistent recovery across multiple VLA backbones and quantization methods. Notably, PIVOT-Q consistently outperforms full-state distillation across all settings while using only 7.4% of its state-level distillation budget. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [165] [Direction-Conditioned Policies for Online Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05087)
*S K Swaminathan,Damiya Gondha,Theyanesh Eswaramoorthy Rajahkrishnan,Aritra Hazra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出方向条件策略（DCP），对对比强化学习（CRL）做小幅修改：在线训练时选已访问状态作为路点，并让策略以表征空间中的方向与距离为条件；部署时直接对最终目标使用同一接口，无需选路点或规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CRL 学到的表征可估计目标可达性，但其策略仍以原始目标为条件，未能直接利用 critic 所编码的几何结构。

Method: DCP 围绕 CRL 的小修改构建：在线训练中选取历史访问状态作为 waypoint，并将策略条件化为这些状态在表征空间中的方向与距离。部署时，DCP 将相同接口直接应用于最终目标，不需要 waypoint 选择或规划。

Result: 在九个导航与操作任务中，DCP 在七个任务上取得更高最终成功率，并在七个任务上花更多时间靠近目标。受控迷宫实验进一步表明，DCP 更准确地捕捉最短路径几何，且所提供的方向对 actor 行为具有因果影响。作者指出 waypoint 覆盖与排序是探索的限制，并表明学习候选生成可在两个受控迷宫中改善目标到达。

Conclusion: DCP 通过将策略条件化于表征空间中的方向与距离，更直接地利用 CRL critic 的几何信息，从而提升目标到达表现；其部署无需规划，但探索效果受 waypoint 覆盖与排序限制。

Abstract: Contrastive Reinforcement Learning (CRL) learns representations that estimate goal reachability, yet its policy remains conditioned on raw goals and therefore does not directly exploit the geometry encoded by its critic. We introduce Direction-Conditioned Policies (DCP), a method built around a small modification to CRL: DCP selects previously visited states as waypoints during online training and conditions the policy on their direction and distance in representation space. At deployment, DCP applies the same interface directly to the final goal, requiring neither waypoint selection nor planning. Across nine navigation and manipulation tasks, DCP attains higher final success rates than CRL on seven tasks and spends more time near the goal on seven. Controlled maze experiments further show that DCP captures shortest-path geometry more accurately and that the supplied direction causally influences the actor's behavior. We identify waypoint coverage and ranking as limits to exploration, and show that learned candidate generation improves goal reaching in two controlled mazes.

</details>


### [166] [A Bird's-Eye View of Iterative Reward Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04364)
*Logan Mondal Bhamidipaty,Lauren Robson,Linda Petrini,Shengrui Lyu,Kamal Ndousse*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出统一基准 BIRD，用于在一致的配置、反馈条件和策略训练预算下比较与消融基于 LLM 的迭代奖励设计方法，并发现简单设计选择组合即可显著优于已有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习奖励函数设计通常依赖大量专业知识和试错；现有基于 LLM 的自动化方法虽能生成并迭代改进奖励代码，但因实现细节、反馈假设和评估环境不同而难以公平比较，也难以判断哪些设计真正有效。

Method: 提出迭代奖励设计基准 BIRD，将已有方法映射到统一的配置与评估空间，从而支持直接比较算法、消融个体设计选择，并在匹配的反馈条件和策略训练预算下原型验证新组件。

Result: 在 MuJoCo、Meta-World、Assistax 和 HumanoidBench 上，识别出一小组能持续提升性能的简单设计选择；将这些选择组合后，性能显著优于所评估的先前方法。

Conclusion: 结果凸显简单基线的有效性，并呼吁进一步研究在何种条件下增加算法复杂度才能改进迭代奖励设计；代码已公开。

Abstract: Designing effective reward functions in RL typically requires substantial expertise and trial and error. Recent work automates this process with LLM-based systems that generate and iteratively improve reward code using policy feedback. However, these methods are often hard to compare because they differ in implementation details, feedback assumptions, and evaluation environments. To address this, we introduce a Benchmark for Iterative Reward Design (BIRD) that expresses existing methods in a unified configuration and evaluation space. This lets us compare algorithms directly, ablate individual design choices, and prototype new components under matched feedback conditions and policy-training budgets. Across MuJoCo, Meta-World, Assistax, and HumanoidBench, we identify a small set of simple design choices that consistently improve performance. Combining these choices yields significantly better performance than the evaluated methods from prior work. Our results highlight the strength of simple baselines and motivate further study of when additional algorithmic complexity improves iterative reward design. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [167] [On Learning Optimal Corners in Orthogonal Partially Observable Cooperative Guard Art Galleries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06777)
*Yassin Ben Mansour,Edwin Meriaux*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文为CADENCE算法提出两种可学习的角落选择启发式，在保持形式化覆盖与连通性保证的同时，提升多智能体部署效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CADENCE虽能求解部分可观测协同警卫艺术画廊问题（POCGAGP）并给出覆盖与连通性保证，但未指定各智能体应部署到哪个有效角落，而该选择显著影响效率。

Method: 提出两种保持CADENCE形式化保证的学习式角落选择启发式：一是基于网格编码对候选角落打分的CNN；二是基于可见性图、用深度Q学习（DQN）训练的GATv2网络。

Result: 在50×50至250×250的随机正交环境中进行7,500次运行，所提方法在达到全覆盖所需步数和峰值智能体数量上均优于基线CADENCE，且增益随规模增大而增加；在智能体利用率上优于增量自部署（ISDA）基线，同时提供ISDA不具备的保证。

Conclusion: 学习式角落选择可在不牺牲CADENCE形式化性质的前提下，提升其速度和智能体利用率。

Abstract: The CADENCE algorithm solves the Partially Observable Cooperative Guard Art Gallery Problem (POCGAGP) with formal coverage and connectivity guarantees, but leaves unspecified which valid corner each agent should be deployed to, a choice that strongly affects efficiency. We introduce two learned corner-selection heuristics that preserve these guarantees: a CNN scoring candidates on a grid encoding, and a GATv2 network trained with Deep Q-Learning (DQN) on a visibility graph. Across 7,500 runs on random orthogonal environments (50x50 to 250x250), our heuristics outperform baseline CADENCE in both steps to full coverage and peak agent count, with gains growing with scale, and improve on Incremental Self-Deployment (ISDA) baselines in agent utilization while providing guarantees ISDA lacks. Learned corner selection thus improves CADENCE in speed and agent utilization at no cost to its formal properties.

</details>


### [168] [Orchestrating Level-$K$ Policies Against Unknown Opponents in Partially-Observable Dynamic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04937)
*Addison Kalanther,Sanika Bharvirkar,Daniel Bostwick,Chinmay Maheshwari,Shankar Sastry*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 论文将 Level-K 策略库的部署建模为部分可观测马尔可夫博弈中的动态编排问题，并发现基于强化学习的编排器优于基于分类的编排器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Level-K 推理会生成针对不同推理水平对手的专用策略层级。当对手水平未知时，常见部署规则先估计其水平再选择对应响应；但在动态、部分可观测博弈中，该选择会重复进行并影响后续状态与观测，而选择最可能对手水平对应的响应未必能最大化当前历史下的期望回报。

Method: 作者将部署问题形式化为在部分可观测马尔可夫博弈中对固定预训练策略库的动态编排，并比较两类方法：基于分类的编排器（CBO，分别用离线数据或 on-policy 数据聚合训练）与以最大化期望折扣回报为目标的强化学习编排器（RLBO）。实验在追逃场景中进行。

Result: on-policy 训练提升了分类与回报，但 RLBO 的回报高于 on-policy 和离线 CBO。给定预训练策略库，RLBO 还能以更少训练步数达到与直接在追捕者动作空间上训练的策略相当的性能。

Conclusion: 这些发现支持将 Level-K 策略层级视为可供编排的资源，而不是部署时的固定处方。

Abstract: Level-$K$ reasoning generates a hierarchy of policies specialized to opponents with different reasoning levels. When an opponent's level is unknown, a common deployment rule estimates that level and selects the corresponding response. In a dynamic, partially-observed game, this selection is repeated, with each choice shaping subsequent states and observations. The response associated with the most likely opponent level need not maximize expected return from the current history. We formulate this deployment problem as dynamic orchestration of a fixed, pretrained policy library in a partially observable Markov game. We compare classification-based orchestrators (CBOs) trained using offline data or on-policy data aggregation with a reinforcement-learning-based orchestrator (RLBO) trained to maximize expected discounted return. In pursuit-evasion experiments, on-policy training improves classification and return, yet RLBO achieves higher return than the on-policy and offline CBOs. Given a pretrained library, RLBO also reaches performance comparable to a policy trained directly over the pursuers' action space with fewer training timesteps. These findings support treating a hierarchy of level-$K$ policies as a resource for orchestration, not a prescription for deployment.

</details>


### [169] [Nonlinear Density-Driven Optimal Control (D2OC) for Multi-Agent Spatial Coverage via Sequential Convex Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04545)
*Julian Martinez,Kooktae Lee*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出密度驱动最优控制（D2OC）的非线性扩展，用序列凸规划将基于Wasserstein密度的多智能体空间覆盖推广到离散时间控制仿射非线性系统，并给出有限时域误差界与滚动时域实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统D2OC难以直接处理非线性动力学、有限预测时域和输入约束，而非线性模型预测控制虽性能好但计算成本高，因此需要一种兼具分布驱动特性与计算效率的非线性多智能体覆盖方法。

Method: 在每个控制更新步，将非线性动力学在预测时域内局部线性化，构造严格凸二次规划，保持Wasserstein重心结构并直接纳入输入约束；同时分析约束控制偏差与非线性泰勒余项对局部线性预测精度的影响，建立显式有限时域误差界，并给出用于滚动时域实现的两步特例。

Result: 独轮车与四旋翼集群仿真表明，该方法覆盖性能与非线性模型预测控制相当，同时显著降低计算时间。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持D2OC去中心化、密度驱动特性的同时，为非线性多智能体空间覆盖提供了一种计算高效且具理论刻画的方法。

Abstract: This paper presents a nonlinear extension of Density-Driven Optimal Control (D2OC) for multi-agent spatial coverage with prescribed density distributions. Rather than assigning individual target locations, D2OC drives the collective spatial distribution of agents toward a desired density through a Wasserstein-based objective. We extend this framework to multi-step finite-horizon control for discrete-time control-affine nonlinear systems using sequential convex programming. At each control update, the nonlinear dynamics are locally linearized over the prediction horizon, yielding a strictly convex quadratic program that preserves the Wasserstein barycentric structure while directly incorporating input constraints. We further characterize the effect of constrained control deviations and nonlinear Taylor remainders on the accuracy of the local linear prediction, establishing an explicit finite-horizon error bound and a two-step specialization for receding-horizon implementation. The resulting method retains the decentralized, distribution-driven nature of D2OC while providing a computationally efficient optimization procedure for nonlinear multi-agent systems. Simulations with unicycle and quadrotor teams show coverage performance comparable to nonlinear model predictive control, while substantially reducing computation time. These results demonstrate a tractable and theoretically characterized framework for density-driven spatial coverage under nonlinear dynamics.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Tactile Perception through Fluid-Solid Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03773)
*Arman Goshtasbi,Minke Berghuis,Aida Parvaresh,Saravana Prashanth Murali Babu,Robert W. Style,Ahmad Rafsanjani*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 提出一种基于流固耦合、传感点无电子元件的软触觉传感器；仅用两个外部压力传感器，通过机器学习解码压力模式，实现1D/2D触觉定位与力估计，并适用于水下和磁干扰环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软触觉传感器多依赖嵌入式电子器件，易受干扰并受环境限制；需要一种简单、鲁棒且传感点无电子元件的触觉感知方案。

Method: 利用流体-固体相互作用，设计充满流体的弹性体通道，仅连接两个外部压力传感器。触摸不同区域使黏性流体位移，产生可编码触摸位置与力的压力模式。采用集成特征提取、软聚类和自适应神经模糊推理的机器学习框架进行解码。在1D线性传感器中验证单点触摸定位与力估计；通过空间填充曲线布线通道，将同一原理扩展到2D触觉映射，并保持相同的最小硬件配置。

Result: 在1D线性传感器上实现单点触摸定位和力估计；通过空间填充曲线成功扩展到2D触觉映射，且硬件配置不变；在水下及磁干扰等传统电子传感器易失效环境中仍保持有效。

Conclusion: 该无电子传感点的软触觉传感方法可行、简单且鲁棒，为受限或恶劣环境下的触觉感知提供了新途径。

Abstract: Soft tactile sensors elevate robotic touch through enhanced flexibility and adaptability, yet most existing designs depend on embedded electronics that are susceptible to interference and environmental limitations. In this work, we leverage fluid-solid interactions to develop a class of soft tactile sensors that operate entirely without electronics at the sensing site. The sensor comprises a fluid-filled elastomeric channel connected to only two external pressure sensors. Touching different regions of the elastomeric surface displaces the viscous fluid, producing distinct pressure patterns that encode both touch position and force. These signals are decoded through a machine learning framework that integrates feature extraction, soft clustering, and adaptive neuro-fuzzy inference to achieve accurate localization and force estimation. We validate this concept through single-point touch localization and force estimation in a linear (1D) sensor and extend the same sensing principle to 2D tactile mapping by routing the channel across the surface using space-filling curves, while maintaining the same minimal hardware setup. This simple approach remains effective in environments where conventional electronic sensors often fail, such as underwater or in the presence of magnetic interference.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [171] [Exact Optimal Transport by Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04085)
*Dmitry Kamenetsky*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该文系统比较精确离散最优传输（即最小代价二分完美匹配，用 Jonker-Volgenant 求解）与熵正则近似方法 Sinkhorn/Greenkhorn。在中等规模稠密二维问题上，精确匹配在速度和精度上均优于近似方法；同时提出基于 kNN 池与 Blossom 对偶的可行下界证书，并用多机器人任务分配验证精确离散计划更省成本。作者承认 Sinkhorn 族在大 n 稠密场景仍有优势，未予挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平衡单位质量离散最优传输本质是 n 源到 n 目标的最小代价分配问题，可由工业级匹配引擎精确求解，但实践中常默认采用 Sinkhorn 及其加速变体 Greenkhorn。作者希望厘清精确法何时优于近似法，并为稀疏精确路线提供可验证的 LP 对偶下界证书。

Method: 将问题建模为二分图完美匹配，使用 Jonker-Volgenant 精确求解。实验在稠密二维实例上比较精确匹配与 Sinkhorn/Greenkhorn 的速度和质量。证书构造：给定 kNN 池匹配及其 Blossom 对偶，做一次 O(n^2) 的对偶可行性 clip 得到稠密可行下界；再结合每次迭代均有效的 Sinkhorn 对偶势形成下界。最后进行多机器人任务分配合理性检验，其中离散计划本身是交付物。

Result: 精确匹配在中等 n 区间更快且更准：n=500 时 0.01 s，n=8000 时 11.5 s。达到 1% 质量目标，Greenkhorn 约需 10-80 分钟，比精确法慢 4e2-6e4 倍；普通 Sinkhorn 还慢 20-650 倍，外推呈近似幂律。Greenkhorn 相对 Sinkhorn 在多数收敛单元仅加速 1.0-1.5 倍。kNN 池下界证书在全部 45 个配置上有效，且随 k 单调收紧。多机器人中，每轮精确分配耗时 0.1-68 ms，Sinkhorn 加硬化流程耗时 0.12-15.9 s，15 轮累计多出 6-27% 行驶距离。

Conclusion: 在中等规模稠密二维及其他需要离散交付计划的场景中，精确匹配可能优于熵正则近似方法；所提对偶 clip 能提供有效且随 k 收紧的下界证书。Sinkhorn 族在大 n 稠密区域的优势仍成立，本文未涉足该范围。代码、数据与结果以 MIT 许可开源。

Abstract: Balanced discrete optimal transport between n sources and n targets of unit mass is exactly the minimum-cost assignment problem-a bipartite perfect matching-and is therefore solvable exactly by industrial matching engines in milliseconds to seconds. We ask when the exact approach beats the standard approximate alternatives, entropic Sinkhorn and its accelerated variant Greenkhorn, and make the sparse-exact side certified by a textbook LP dual-feasibility clip. Three contributions. (i) Measurement: on dense 2-D instances, exact matching (Jonker-Volgenant) is faster and strictly more accurate than either approximate method throughout the moderate-n regime (0.01 s at n=500 to 11.5 s at n=8000); reaching a 1% quality target on the same hardware requires roughly 10-80 min for Greenkhorn (factors 4e2-6e4 over exact; plain Sinkhorn is 20-650x slower still), a rough power-law projection beyond the measured range. Greenkhorn's measured speedup over plain Sinkhorn is only 1.0-1.5x on most converged cells. (ii) A simple kNN-pool gap certificate: given a pool matching and its Blossom dual, a one-pass O(n^2) clip produces a dense-feasible lower bound; combined with the Sinkhorn dual potential (valid at every iterate, not just at convergence), the bound is valid on all 45 measured configurations and tightens monotonically with k. (iii) A multi-robot task-allocation sanity check where the discrete plan is the deliverable: per-round exact assignment costs 0.1-68 ms, while a Sinkhorn-plus-hardening pipeline costs 0.12-15.9 s and accumulates 6-27% extra travel over 15 rounds. The Sinkhorn family's large-n dense regime is acknowledged and left untouched. Code, data, and results under MIT: this https URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Budget-Constrained Fault-Tolerant Mutual Visibility for Autonomous Robots under the Mobility Fault Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04402)
*Prakhar Shukla,Animesh Maiti,Shivam Kumar,Subhash Bhagat*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究在受限移动预算与任意移动故障下，不透明自主移动机器人集群的相互可见性问题；提出确定性分布式算法，在SSYNC、非刚性移动、无坐标系一致且仅共用一个固定参考点的发光机器人模型中，实现非故障机器人对包括故障机器人在内所有机器人的无遮挡可见，同时满足预算、无碰撞并使用12种光色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人能量有限且可能永久失效，遮挡导致可见性受限，需在无全局坐标系和严格同步保证下设计容错协调算法。

Method: 采用发光机器人模型，在SSYNC与非刚性移动下，仅依赖公共固定参考点；为每个机器人设定有限移动预算，允许任意数量机器人因移动故障永久静止；设计确定性分布式算法协调非故障机器人运动。

Result: 算法在任意数量移动故障下仍能求解问题，保证非故障机器人最终相互可见，尊重每个机器人移动预算，实现无碰撞移动，并仅使用12种光色。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了资源受限且故障频发条件下相互可见性的可行分布式解法，表明仅需有限光通信颜色与公共参考点即可实现容错协调。

Abstract: We investigate the mutual visibility problem for a swarm of $n\ge3$ autonomous mobile robots under the budget-constrained mobility fault model. The robots are opaque, so if three robots are collinear, the middle robot obstructs the visibility between the other two. Each robot is assigned a finite movement budget, reflecting its limited energy, that bounds the total distance it may traverse during the execution. Moreover, an arbitrary number of robots may become permanently immobile due to mobility faults. The objective is to design a distributed algorithm that enables the non-faulty robots to coordinate their movements so that, within a finite time, every non-faulty robot attains unobstructed visibility of all robots in the system, including the faulty ones, while respecting the prescribed movement budget. We consider luminous robots operating under the $\mathsf{SSYNC}$ model with non-rigid movements, without any agreement on their local coordinate systems, and equipped only with a {\it common fixed reference point}. We present a deterministic distributed algorithm that solves the problem despite an arbitrary number of mobility faults. The algorithm guarantees mutual visibility for the non-faulty robots, respects the movement budget of every robot, provides collision-free movements for the robots, and uses only 12 light colors.

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [173] [Scenario-Based Compositional Statistical Model Checking for Safety Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05571)
*Abhinav Pomalapally,Arya Raeesi,Kevin Kai-Chun Chang,Beyazit Yalcinkaya,Sanjit A. Seshia*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向安全与co-safety规范的基于场景的组合式统计模型检验框架，可高效分析由原语场景构建的复合场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SMC方法对每个复合场景独立分析，需大量昂贵仿真，且当场景共享结构时会产生显著冗余计算。

Method: 将复合场景分解为原语场景，将规范分解为子规范，独立验证各原语；利用重要性采样与核密度估计组合统计估计，并通过并行化和轨迹重用降低仿真成本。

Result: 实验表明，该框架能准确回答未见复合场景的验证查询，同时借助并行化与轨迹重用减少仿真开销。

Conclusion: 组合式SMC框架可有效支持复合场景下的安全与co-safety验证，并减少冗余计算。

Abstract: In safety-critical domains such as autonomous driving, systems must be evaluated across a large number of environment conditions, often represented as composite scenarios built from primitive scenarios. Existing statistical model checking (SMC) approaches analyze each composite scenario independently, requiring many expensive simulations and resulting in substantial redundant computation when scenarios share common structure. This work introduces a scenario-based compositional SMC framework for safety and co-safety specifications, enabling efficient analysis of composite scenarios. Our approach decomposes scenarios into primitives and specifications into sub-specifications, verifies each primitive independently, and composes the resulting statistical estimates using importance sampling and kernel density estimation. Our empirical evaluation shows that the proposed framework can accurately answer verification queries for previously unseen composite scenarios while reducing simulation cost through parallelization and trace reuse.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [174] [Beyond LLM Serving: Characterizing Vision-Language-Action Workloads for Embodied AI System Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05062)
*Seonghun Jung,Sieun Moon,Jiyoung Jeong,Jimin Lee,Jaehyuk Huh*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文刻画了四类代表性VLA模型在边缘GPU服务器与两种板载SoC上的推理与闭环运行特性，揭示动作张量维度、平台瓶颈、GPU频率及推理-执行重叠对延迟、能耗与精度的影响，并指出不存在跨部署SLO的Pareto最优配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型将多模态观测映射为底层机器人动作。机器人每个控制周期都有推理截止时间，超时会使其基于过期观测行动，降低任务成功率。因此需要设备端或近边缘执行，但单机器人batch-1推理偏离LLM服务系统的设计点；VLA虽由视觉语言、自回归和扩散式组件构成，其在batch-1控制下的运行时行为仍缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 在边缘GPU服务器和两种板载SoC上，对四种代表性VLA模型进行单次推理性能剖析，并使用43,200次闭环episode实验，分析各阶段瓶颈、平台差异及部署配置表现。

Result: 动作张量维度决定阶段是访存受限还是计算受限；平台平衡会改变该瓶颈；GPU频率缩放存在平台相关的能耗-延迟最优区间。闭环运行中，推理与动作执行重叠形成精度-速度-能耗权衡，且没有任何配置在所有部署SLO下Pareto占优。

Conclusion: 这些结果可为VLA模型架构、硬件与运行时策略的联合设计提供指导。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models translate multimodal observations into low-level robot actions. During robot operation, each control period sets an inference deadline, and overruns leave the robot acting on stale observations, reducing task success. Meeting this deadline motivates on-device or nearby edge execution, where a single robot requires batch-1 inference outside the design point of LLM serving systems. Although VLA architectures combine familiar vision-language, autoregressive, and diffusion-style components, their runtime behavior in this batch-1 control setting remains uncharacterized. We characterize four representative VLA models on an edge GPU server and two onboard SoCs, using single-inference profiling and 43,200 closed-loop episodes. Action tensor dimensionality determines whether a stage is memory- or compute-bound, platform balance can shift that bottleneck, and GPU frequency scaling yields a platform-dependent energy-latency sweet spot. In closed-loop operation, overlapping inference with action execution creates an accuracy-speed-energy tradeoff, and no configuration is Pareto-dominant across deployment SLOs. These results guide joint design of VLA model architectures, hardware, and runtime policies.

</details>
